veḷiyīṭu
tamiḻmaṇ patippakam
2, ciṅkārāvēlar teru
tiyākarāyar nakar, ceṉṉi -600 017
to.pē: 2433 9030

moḻi mīṭpiṉ mīḷ varavu
taṉit  tamiḻ vitti ūṉṟiyavar kālṭuvelār. ati muḷikkac ceytavar paritimāl kaliñar; ceṭiyāka vaḷarttavar niṟimaliyām maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ; maramāka vaḷarttu varupavaṉ yāṉē eṉṟa vīṟuṭiyār pāvāṇar.
kiṟittu perumāṉ camaya mīṭpar; kāralmārkkacu poruḷiyal mīṭpar; moḻimīṭpar yāṉē eṉṉum perumita mikkār pāvāṇar.
āriyattiṉiṉṟu tamiḻi mīṭpataṟkāka yāṉ arumpāṭu paṭṭu ilakkiya ilakkaṇa muṟiyōṭu kaṟṟa moḻikaḷ muppatu  eṉṟu eḻutiya perumitat tōṉṟal pāvāṇar.
māntaṉ tōṉṟiyatu kumarik kaṇṭattilēyē; avaṉ pēciya moḻiyē ulaka mutaṉmoḻi; āriyattiṟku mūlamum, tiraviṭattukkut tāyum tamiḻē eṉṉum mummaṇik koḷkiḷi nili nāṭṭiya maliyaṉṉa māṇpar pāvāṇar.
avar colliyavi eḻutiyavi aṉittum meymmiyiṉ pāṟpaṭṭaṉavē eṉa iṉṟu ulaka āyvup perumakkaḷāl ovvoṉṟāka meyppikkappaṭṭu varutal kaṇkūṭu.
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭit tam āyvu matukiyāl tēvanēya ūḻi ākkiya pukaḻum vēṇṭāp pukaḻ māmaṇi tēvanēyap pāvaṇar.
avar moḻiyāyvuc ceytikaḷ oru nūlil, ōr itaḻil, oru malaril, orukaṭṭuriyil, oru kaṭitattil, oru poḻivil ōr uriyāṭalil aṭaṅkiyavi alla. koṅku tēr vāḻkki añciṟit tumpiyāka avaṟṟi yellām tokuttu akara niralil tokukkappaṭṭa ariya tokuppē tēvanēyam ākum.
neṭṭa neṭuṅkālamākat tēvanēyattil ūṉṟiya yāṉ ataṉi akara niral tokiyākki veḷiyiṭal tamiḻulakukkup perumpayaṉām eṉṟu eṇṇiya kāliyil, tamiḻ moḻiyiyum tamiḻ maṇṇiyum tamiḻ iṉattiyum tāṅkip piṭittu ūkkum - vaḷarkkum - vaṇmiyarāy - pāvāṇarkku aṇukkarāy - avarāl uriyum pāṭṭum oruṅku koṇṭa peruntoṇṭarāyt  tikaḻnta ciṅka purivāḻ tamiḻttiru ve. kōvalaṅkaṇṇaṉār avarkaḷ tamiḻ viḻā oṉṟaṟkākac ceṉṉi vanta pōtu yāṉum,  muṉivar ku. tirumāṟaṉārum cantittu aḷavaḷāviya pōtu ikkarutti yāṉ urikka uṭaṉē pāvāṇar aṟakkaṭṭaḷi tōṟṟuvippatākavum ataṉ vaḻiyē tēvanēyam veḷik koṇaralām eṉavum kūṟi appoḻutēyē aṟakkaṭṭaḷi amittār.
tēvanēyar paṭippukaḷ aṉittilum uḷḷa collāyvukaḷit tiraṭṭi akara niral paṭuttip patiṉmūṉṟu tokutikaḷ ākkiṉēṉ. patippuk kuḻuvum amikkappaṭṭatu; acciṭum poṟuppu, pāvāṇar pala kālattup palavakiyāl veḷiyiṭṭa nūlkaḷiyum kaṭṭurikaḷiyum oruṅkē tokuttu orē nērattil veḷiyiṭṭa tamiḻmaṇ patippaka urimiyāḷar nāṭu moḻi iṉap pōrāḷi kō. iḷavaḻakaṉāriṭam oppaṭikkappaṭṭu veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. attēvanēyam tamiḻ āyvar, tamiḻ mīṭpar aṉivar kikaḷilum irukka vēṇṭum eṉṉum vēṇavāvāl mīḷpatippākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭukiṟatu.
pāvāṇar aṟakkaṭṭaḷi niṟuviya kōvalaṅkaṇṇaṉār pukaḻ uṭal eytiya niliyil, avar eṉṟum iṟavā vāḻviṉar eṉpati nilippaṭuttum vakiyil avarkkup paṭiyalākki ippatippu veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu.
moḻi iṉa nāṭṭup paṟṟāḷar aṉivariṭamum irukka vēṇṭiya nūl, pal patippukaḷ kāṇa vēṇṭum. varuṅkāla iḷiñarkku vaḻikāṭṭum oḷiviḷakkākat tikaḻa vēṇṭum. ataṟkut tūṇṭalum tulakkalumāka irukka vēṇṭiyavarkaḷ tamiḻ mīṭṭeṭuppup paṟṟumiyarum toṇṭarumāvar.
veḷiyīṭṭāḷarkkum parappunarkkum perunaṉṟiyuṭiyēṉ.
vāḻiya nalaṉē!								iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
vāḻiya nilaṉē!							      irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ



patippuri
20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiya  iṇiyaṟṟat tamiḻp pēraṟiñar moḻiñāyiṟu tēvanēyap pāvāṇar. ivar vaṭamoḻi ātikkattiliruntu tamiḻi mīṭṭeṭuppataṟkākat tam vāḻviṉ muḻup poḻutiyum celaviṭṭavar.
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkut tāymoḻit tamiḻ, intiya moḻikaḷukku mūlamoḻit tamiḻ, ulaka moḻikaḷukku mūtta moḻi tamiḻ eṉpatit tam paṉmoḻip pulamiyāl ulakukku aṟivittavar.
ivar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ, kaṭṭurikaḷ aṉittiyum tokuttu oru cēra tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭṭatit tamiḻ ulakam aṟiyum. 
mutumuṉivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār  pāvāṇar vaḻi nili aṟiñar. vāḻum tamiḻukku vaḷam pala cērppavar. pāvāṇariṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷi yellām tēvanēyam eṉṉum talippil tamiḻ ulakam payaṉkoḷḷum vakiyil tokuttut tantuḷḷār. ittēvanēyat tokuppukaḷ tamiḻarkaḷukkuk kiṭitta viraccuraṅkam. it tokuppukaḷi veḷiyiṭu vatil perumip paṭukiṟōm.
aṟiñarulakamum, āyvulakamum ivvaruntamiḻk karuvūlatti vāṅkip payaṉ koḷvīr.
- patippāḷar
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ

paṇṭi viḷiyāṭṭu viḻākkaḷ
(1) puṉal viḷiyāṭṭu
paṇṭik kālattil, āṟṟarukēyirunta nakaramāntarellārum, āṇṭutōṟum āṟṟiṟ putuveḷḷam vantavuṭaṉ oruṅkē ceṉṟu, oru pakaliṟ perumpakuti avveḷḷattil tiḷittāṭi iṉpuṟṟa viḷiyāṭṭu viḻā, puṉal viḷiyāṭṭu allatu puṉalāṭṭu eṉṟu peyar peṟṟatu. atu nīrviḻā, nīrāṭṭu, nīrāṭṭaṇi mutaliya peyar koṇṭum vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.
nīrāṭuvārellām, āṟṟiliṭuvataṟkup poṉṉālum veḷḷiyālum ceyta mīṉ mutaliya kāṇikkik karuvikaḷiyum, puṇi teppam parical mutaliya mitavik karuvikaḷiyum, kātalar mītu vāca neyyiyum vaṇṇa nīriyum teḷittaṟkut turutti kompu civiṟi mutaliya viḷiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷiyum, nīrāṭiyapiṉ vēṇṭum ūṇ uṭi akil mutaliyavaṟṟiyum, tattamakku iyaṉṟavāṟu, yāṉi kutiri tēr mutaliyavaṟṟil ūrntum kālnaṭiyāy naṭantum, koṇṭu celvar. kuṭivāriyāka āṅkāṅku amikkap peṟṟa kuṟṟilkaḷum putuk kaṭikaḷum, cērntu, oru viḻavūr pōlak kāṭciyaḷikkum.
nīntavallār caṟṟu āḻattilum allātār kariyiyaṭuttum nīrāṭuvatum, pūcuñ cuṇṇam cāntu kuḻampu mutaliyaṉavaṟṟiṉ ēṟṟattāḻvu paṟṟip peṇṭir ikalāṭuvatum, tam kaṇavaṉmār piṟa peṇṭiroṭu kūṭip puṉalāṭiṉāreṉṟu maṉik kiḻatti yar ūṭuvatum, pūcucāntam puṉintamāli uḻakkum nīrāṭṭu mutaliyavaṟṟāṟ kalaṅkal veḷḷam putumaṇam peṟuvatum, puṉalāṭṭu nikaḻccikaḷām.
nīrāṭiyavar mālikkālattil makiḻntum ayarntum maṉi tirumpuvar.
nīrāṭṭu viḻā uñci nakarattil 21 nāḷ nikaḻnta teṉap peruṅkati kūṟum.
ikkālattuk koṇṭāṭappeṟum āṭip patiṉeṭṭām perukku, paṇṭip puṉalāṭṭi oru puṭiyokkum.
(2) poḻil viḷiyāṭṭu
nakaravāṇar, iḷavēṉiṟ kālattil, ūrukkuc caṟṟut tolivi luḷḷa oru cōlikkuc ceṉṟu, taṉit taṉiyākavum kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamākavum oru pakal camittuṇṭu makiḻnta viḻā, poḻil viḷiyāṭṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. iḵtu ikkālattil uṟaviṉarum naṇparumāka oru cilar ceṉṟuṇṇum kāṭṭuṇāp pōṉṟa jkum.nrhiyia, aṭintapiṉ, aṭicil toḻilil īṭupaṭṭava roḻinta ēṉiyarellām vevvēṟu viṉipaṟṟi vevvē ṟiṭañceṉṟu viṭuvar. āṭavaruḷ periyōr vēṭṭiyāṭa vum, ciṟiyōr maramēṟutal kāykaṉi paṟittuṇṭal viḷiyāṭu tal mutaliya viṉi nikaḻttavum, pirintuviṭuvar. peṇṭiruḷ mūttōr aṭicil toḻilil amara; iḷiyōr,  malar koytu māli toṭukkavum, pāvi puṉintu pārāṭṭi makiḻavum, ūñcalamittu untiyāṭavum, ciṟṟiliḻittuc ciṟucōṟu camikkavum, āṅkāṅku akaṉṟuviṭuvar.
cilar kaṭṭamutu koṇṭu celvatumuṇṭu. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, aṉṉār aṉivarum iṉpa viḷiyāṭṭil īṭupaṭuvar.
naṇpakal uṇavuṇṭapiṉ, cila nāḻiki nēram iḷippāṟi mālik kālam vīṭu tirumpuvatu iyalpākum.
putitāy maṇanta kātalar, tāmē ceṉṟu āṭum poḻilāṭṭum, mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu potu nakara māntar koṇṭāṭum poḻilāṭṭum, vevvēṟām.


paṇṇāṅkuḻi viḷiyāṭṭu
I potuvaki
āṭṭiṉ peyar :  neṟkuttum paṇṇipōl vaṭṭamāṉa paḷḷam allatu kuḻitōṇṭi, atiṟ kaṟkaḷiyiṭṭu āṭum āṭṭu paṇṇāṅkuḻi eṉappaṭum. paṇṇiyeṉpatu paḷḷam. paṇṇi paṟittal kuḻitōṇṭutal.
paṇṇāṅkuḻi eṉṉum peyar, avvav viṭattip poṟuttup paṉṉāṅkuḻi, pallāṅkuḻi, paḷḷāṅkuḻi eṉa vevvēṟu vaṭivil vaḻaṅkum. perumpālum patiṉāṉku kuḻi vittu ivviḷiyāṭṭu āṭap peṟuvatāl, patiṉāṉku kuḻi eṉpatu muṟiyē patiṉāṅkuḻi paṉṉāṅkuḻi eṉat tirintatākac cilar koḷvar. āyiṉ, patiṉāṅkuḻi eṉa eṅkēṉum vaḻaṅ kāmi yāṉum, paṉṉāṉku eṉpatu ilakkiya vaḻakkātalāṉum, paṇṇāṅkuḻi, paḷḷāṅkuḻi eṉṉum vaṭivaṅkaḷē peru vaḻakkāy vaḻaṅkutalāṉum, patiṉāṉkiṟkuk kuṟintum kūṭiyum kuḻikaḷ vittuk koḷḷappaṭutalāṉum, paḷḷāṅ kuḻi eṉpataṟkup paḷḷamāṉa kuḻi eṉṟē potumakkaḷāṟ poruḷ koḷḷappaṭutalāṉum, paṇṇāṅkuḻi allatu paḷḷāṅkuḻi eṉpatē tiruntiya vaṭivamām.
āṭuvār toki :  iti iruvar āṭuvar.
āṭu karuvi:  nilattiṟ camamāṉa irupaṭukki variciyākak killappaṭṭa 10 allatu 14 allatu 16 kuḻikaḷum, avaṟṟuḷ avvintāy iṭuvataṟku vēṇṭiya kaḻaṟcikkāy (kaccakkāy) allatu puḷiyaṅkoṭṭi allatu kūḻāṅkaṟkaḷum, itaṟku vēṇṭuṅ karuvikaḷām.
cilar, eṉṟum eṅkum vacatiyāy āṭutaṟ poruṭṭu, vēṇṭiya aḷavu paḷḷañ cetukkap peṟṟa marakkaṭṭikaḷi vittiruppar.
āṭiṭam: itu vīṭṭuḷḷum vīṭṭu allatu mara niḻalilum āṭap peṟum. itu ēṉi vakikaṭkum okkum.
āṭumuṟi:  kuḻi varicikkoruvarāka iruvar variciyaṭuttu etiretir uṭkārntu, kuḻikkintāka ellāk kuḻikaḷilum kaṟkaḷip pōṭuvar. muntiyāṭupavar, tam variciyil ētēṉu moru kuḻiyiluḷḷa kaṟkaḷintiyum eṭuttu, valappuṟamākac cuṟṟik kuḻikkoṉṟākap pōṭṭuk koṇṭē pōtal vēṇṭum. kaṟkaḷip pōṭṭu muṭintapiṉ, kaṭicikkal pōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭutta kuḻiyiluḷḷa kaṟkaḷintiyum eṭuttu, ataṟkappāluḷḷa kuḻiyil ovvoṉṟākap pōṭṭuk koṇṭu pōtal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam pōṭumpōtu, tam variciyil iṭavalamākavum, etiri variciyil vala iṭamākavum pōṭṭuc cellavēṇṭum. kaṭiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭutta kuḻi, veṟumiyāka iruntāl ataṟkaṭutta kuḻiyiluḷḷa kaṟkaḷaṉit tiyum eṭuttut tammiṭam vittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum. ataṉpiṉ, etiriyār tam variciyiluḷḷa oru kuḻiyiliruntu toṭaṅki, muṉ coṉṉavāṟē āṭuvar. kaṭiciyākappōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭutta iru kuḻiyilum kal illāviṭiṉ, āṭupavar oṉṟum eṭukkāmalē niṉṟuviṭa vēṇṭum. ovvoruvarum tattam variciyiluḷḷa kuḻiyoṉṟil 4 kaṟkaḷ cērntuviṭiṉ, avaṟṟip pacu eṉṟu colli eṭuttuk koḷvar. cilaviṭaṅ kaḷil, 8kal cērntu viṭiṉ avaṟṟip paḻam eṉṟu colli eṭuttuk koḷvar.
iṅṅaṉam iruvarum māṟi māṟi āṭivarumpōtu, eṭuttuk koḷḷappaṭātu eñciyuḷḷa kaṟkaḷellām orē variciyiṟ pōyc cērntuviṭiṉ, āṭṭam niṉṟuviṭum. iruvarum tāntām eṭuttuvittirukkum kaṟkaḷi eṇṇuvar. kaṭici yil orē variciyiṟ pōyc cērnta kaṟkaḷellām, avvarici yāric cērum, mikutiyāṉa kaṟkaḷi eṭuttavar veṉṟavarāvar.
cilaviṭaṅkaḷil, orē āṭṭiyil veṟṟiyit tīrmāṉiyāmal, aintāṭṭiyiṉ piṉ allatu pattāṭṭiyiṉ piṉ tīrmā ṉippar. ammuṟippaṭi āṭumpōtu, muṉ āṭṭikaḷil tōṟṟavar veṉṟavariṭam tamakku vēṇṭiya kaṟkaḷik kaṭaṉ vāṅkik koḷvar. veṉṟavar kaṭaṉ koṭāviṭiṉ, irukkiṉṟa kaṟkaḷi avvintākak kuḻikaḷil pōṭṭuviṭṭu, kal illāta allatu aintu kal illāta kuḻikaḷi veṟumiyāka viṭṭuviṭa vēṇṭum. avveṟum kuḻikaṭkup pavvīkkuḻikaḷ eṉṟu peyar. avaṟṟil ovvoru kuccu iṭappaṭum. oruvarum avaṟṟil āṭal kūṭātu.
tōṟṟavarkku oru kuḻiyum nirampātapōtu (atāvatu aintu kaṟkum kuṟivāka irukkumpōtu) irukkiṉṟa kaṟkaḷi ov voṉṟākak kuḻikaḷil pōṭṭuviṭṭu, kal illāta kuḻikaḷip pavvīk kuḻikaḷāka viṭṭuviṭal vēṇṭum. aṉṟu etiriyārum tam variciyiluḷḷa ellāk kuḻikaṭkum ovvoru kallē pōṭavēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam ovvoru kallē pōṭṭu āṭum muṟikkuk kañci kāyccutal eṉṟu peyar. avvintu kal pōṭṭu āṭit tōṟṟavar, kañcikāycci āṭumpōtu vella iṭamuṇṭu. orumuṟi veṉṟavar maṟumuṟi muntiyāṭal vēṇṭum.
ōr āṭṭi muṭintapiṉ, veṟṟiyum tōlviyumiṉṟi iruvarum camamākak kaṟkaḷ vittiruppiṉ, aṭutta āṭṭiyil caripāṇṭi  āṭal vēṇṭum. mutalāvatu avvintākavum piṉpu pappattākavum kaṟkaḷiyeṭuttu, attaṉiyē pōṭṭu āṭuvatu, caripāṇṭiyāṭalākum. itil virintu viḷivu kāṇalām.
II kaṭṭuk kaṭṭal
āṭṭiṉ peyar : nāṉku mūlikkuṅ kaṭṭukkaṭṭi āṭum paṇṇāṅkuḻi viḷiyāṭṭuvaki, kaṭṭukkaṭṭal eṉappaṭum.
āṭuvār toki:  iruvar iti āṭuvar.
āṭu karuvi :  iru camavariciyākavuḷḷa patiṉāṉku kuḻikaḷum, kuḻikkintāka avaṟṟiṟku vēṇṭiya kaṟkaḷum, itaṟkuriya karuvikaḷām.
āṭumuṟi: āṭakar ovvoruvarum tattam variciyiṉ iṭakkōṭi (atāvatu iṭappuṟak kaṭici)k kuḻiyiṉiṉṟu toṭaṅki āṭal vēṇṭum. eṭutta kaṟkaḷiyellām ovvoṉṟākap pōṭṭapiṉ, kaṭiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻiyiṉiṉṟē kaṟkaḷiyeṭuttu āṭal vēṇṭum. kaṭiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻiyil ēṟkeṉavē oru kallēṉum iruntāltāṉ, atiṉiṉṟu eṭukka muṭiyum; illāviṭṭāl (atāvatu pōṭṭa piṉ oru kallēyiruntāl) eṭukka muṭiyātu. veṟuṅ kuḻiyit tuṭittu aṭutta kuḻiyiliruntēṉum, piṟa vakiyi lēṉum, kaṟkaḷi eṭuttu vittuk koḷḷutal ivviḷi yāṭṭil illi. oru mūlikkuḻiyil ēṟkeṉavē mūṉṟu kalliruppiṉ, nālāvatu kallip pōṭumpōtu, atik kuḻikkuḷ pōṭāmal kuḻikkup pakkamāka vittuviṭa vēṇṭum. ituvē kaṭṭukkaṭṭal eṉappaṭuvatu. kaṭṭuk kaṭṭiya kuḻiyil, kaṭṭiṉavarē mēṟkoṇṭu kalpōṭa muṭiyum;  aṭuttavar pōṭamuṭiyātu. iṅṅaṉam oruvarēyō iruvarumākavō nāṉku mūlikkum kaṭṭuk kaṭṭik koḷḷalām. kaṭṭiya kuḻiyil kal pōṭalāmēyaṉṟi eṭukka muṭiyātu. ātalāl, kaṭṭukkaṭṭiya kuḻikaḷil mēlum  mēlum kal cērntu koṇṭēyirukkum. ellāk kaṟkaḷum nāṉku mūlikkum pōyc cērntapiṉ avaravar kaṭṭiya kuḻikkaṟkaḷi yeṭuttu eṇṇal vēṇṭum. mikutiyāṉa kaṟkaḷiyuṭiyavar veṉṟava rāvar.
III aracaṉum mantiriyum cēvakaṉum
āṭṭiṉ peyar: aracaṉum mantiriyum cēvakaṉum eṉa mūvar āṭum paṇṇāṅ kuḻiyāṭṭuvaki, avar puṉipatavip peyariyē peyarākak koṇṭatu.
āṭukaruvi : kaṭṭukkaṭṭaṟkup pōṉṟē, iru camavarici yākavuḷḷa patiṉāṉku kuḻikaḷum, kuḻikkintāka avaṟṟiṟku vēṇṭiya kaṟkaḷum, iti āṭukaruviyām.
āṭumuṟi : iru varicik kuḻikaḷuḷḷum, naṭu iru mūṉṟu aracaṉuṭiyaṉa veṉṟum, iṭappuṟa īriraṇṭu mantiriyuṭiyaṉa veṉṟum, valappuṟa īriraṇṭu cēvakaṉuṭiyaṉaveṉṟum koṇṭu, aracaṉ mantiri cēvakaṉ eṉṟa variciyoḻuṅkil, āṭakar mūvarum āṭal vēṇṭum.
ovvoruvarum, tāntām āṭum ovvoru taṭaviyum, tattam kuḻiyiliruntē toṭaṅki āṭalvēṇṭum. āyiṉ, ellār kuḻikaḷilum varicippaṭi kaṟkaḷip pōṭalām. ovvoṉ ṟākak kaṟkaḷip pōṭṭu muṭinta piṉ, kaṭiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭutta kuḻiyiluḷḷa kaṟkaḷiyeṭuttu muṉpōl āṭavēṇṭum. kaṭiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭutta kuḻi veṟumiyāka iruppiṉ atit tuṭittu ataṟkaṭutta kuḻiyi luḷḷa kaṟkaḷiyellām eṭuttu vittukkoḷḷalām. ataṉpiṉ aṭuttavar āṭalvēṇṭum.  kaṭiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭutta iru kuḻikaḷum veṟumiyāka iruppiṉ, āṭupavar oṉṟum eṭukkāmalē niṉṟukoḷḷa vēṇṭum. veṟuṅkuḻikku aṭutta kuḻiyiluḷḷa kaṟkaḷi eṭukkumpōtu, yārum yār kāyiyum eṭukkalām.
avaravar kuḻikaḷiluḷḷa pacuvi avaravar eṭuttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
ellāk kāyum eṭuttukkoḷḷappaṭṭa piṉ, aracaṉ ēṉiyiruvarkkum ovvoṉṟu koṭuttuviṭavēṇṭum. ataṉpiṉ, avaṉukku 6 kuḻi nirampiṉāl (atāvatu 30 kāy iruntāl), avaṉukku veṟṟiyām. aṉṟi, yār mikunta kāy eṭukkiṉṟaṉarō avarukkē veṟṟiyām. veṉṟavar tamakkaṭutta mēṟpataviyum tōṟṟavar tamakkaṭutta kīḻppataviyum aṭital vēṇṭum. aracaṉ velliṉ taṉ pataviyik kāttuk koḷ vāṉ. āyiṉ, aracaṉum mantiriyum mūṉṟumuṟi toṭarntu paḻamāyiṉ, kīḻppatavi yaṭital vēṇṭum.
ulaka vāḻkkiyiluḷḷa ēṟṟiṟakkam ivviḷiyāṭṭāl uṇarttap paṭuvatu, kavaṉikkattakkatu.
IV acōkavaṉat tāṭṭam
āṭṭiṉ peyar : oruvar tamittirukkumpōtu tāmāy āṭik koḷḷum paṇṇāṅkuḻiyāṭṭuvaki, acōka vaṉattil taṉittiruntatākac collappaṭum cītiyoṭu toṭarpupaṭutti, acōkavaṉat tāṭṭam eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭukiṟatu.
āṭu karuvi : kaṭṭuk kaṭṭaṟkuriyaviyē itaṟkum.
āṭumuṟi : irukuḻi variciyuḷḷum, mēlvariciyil, muṟiyē oṉṟum iraṇṭum mūṉṟum nāṉkum aintum āṟum ēḻumāka mēṉmē lēṟṟa muṟiyilum, kīḻvariciyil, muṟiyē ēḻum āṟum aintum nāṉkum mūṉṟum iraṇṭum oṉṟumāka mēṉmē liṟakka muṟiyilum, iṭavalamākak kāykaḷip pōṭṭuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉamaṉṟi, mēl variciyil mēṉmē liṟakka muṟiyilum kīḻvariciyil mēṉmēlēṟṟa muṟiyilum pōṭṭuk koḷḷalām. eṅṅaṉamāyiṉum, iruvariciyilum ēṟṟiṟakka muṟi māṟi yiruttal vēṇṭumeṉpatē vitiyām.
toṭaṅkumpōtu kīḻ variciyiluḷḷa peruntokik kuḻiyi ṉiṉṟu (atāvatu 7 kāykaḷ uḷḷa kuḻiyiṉiṉṟu) toṭaṅkal vēṇṭum. kāykaḷik kuḻikkoṉṟākap pōṭṭuc cellum pōtu, piṟavaki yāṭṭukkaḷiṟ pōṉṟē kīḻvariciyil iṭa valamākavum mēl variciyil valiṭamākavum pōṭṭuc cella vēṇṭum. kāykaḷip pōṭṭu muṭintapiṉ, kaṭiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭutta peruntokik kuḻiyiṉiṉṟu kāy kaḷi eṭuttāṭavēṇṭum. atu kaṭiciyākap pōṭṭa kuḻikku aṭuttēyirukkum.
veṟuṅkuḻi amivatum, atit tuṭittu aṭutta kuḻiyi luḷḷa kāykaḷi eṭuttukkoḷvatum, ivviḷi yāṭṭil illi.
mēṟcoṉṉavāṟu (atāvatu eṉṟum oru variciyiluḷḷa perun tokik kuḻiyiṉiṉṟē kāyeṭuttu) oṉpatu muṟi toṭarntu āṭiṉ. āṭṭan toṭaṅku muṉ pōṭṭirunta vāṟē, avvak kuḻikaḷiṟ kāykaḷ amintuviṭum. aṅṅaṉam irāviṭiṉ āṭiṉatu tavaṟeṉṟu aṟintu koḷḷalām.
oruvar tamittāṭutaṟkēṟṟa viruppatti ivviḷiyāṭṭu uṇṭupaṇṇa vallatu.
kuṟippu :- paṇṇāṅkuḻi peṇpiḷḷikaṭkē ēṟṟa tāyiṉum, piyaṉ kaḷālum āṭappaṭuvatu uṇṭu.
paṇṇiyam
paṇ - paṇṇiyam = paṇṇappaṭṭa palvēṟu poruḷ.
koṭuppōr kaṭitoṟum paṇṇiyam paranteḻa  (maṇi : 7 : 124).
vaṭavar, meccattakkatu, vāṅkavum viṟṟaṟkum uriyatu eṉṟum ceyaṟki muṟiyil vevvēṟu mūlapporuḷ kūṟuvar.  (va.va : 199).
paṇṇi
uḻavar ceyyum payirttoḻilic ciṟuvar naṭittāṭum āṭṭu paṇṇi...  (ta.vi.muka.kū.)
paṇpaṭṭa moḻi
iyaṟkimoḻi allatu muḻittal moḻi muḻuvaḷarcci yaṭintapiṉ paṇpaṭṭa moḻi (Culticulate Language) allatu iḻittal moḻi (Articulate Speech) tōṉṟiṟṟu. muṟiyē cēymi aṇmi muṉmiyuṇarttum M.ī.C eṉṉum muccuṭṭukkaḷiṉiṉṟē iḻittal moḻi perumpālum uruvāyiṟṟu. am mūṉṟaṉuḷḷum muṉmic cuṭṭākiya ūkāramē tamiḻc coṟkaḷuḷ mukkāṟ paṅkiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa pakutiyip piṟappittuḷḷatu.  (ta.i.va.muṉṉuri.)
paṇpāṭu eṉṉum col viḷakkam
paṇpaṭuvatu paṇpāṭu. paṇpaṭutal cīrppaṭutal, allatu tiruntu tal. tiruntiya nilattip paṇpaṭṭa allatu paṇpaṭuttappaṭṭa nilameṉṟum, tiruntiya tamiḻip paṇpaṭṭa centamiḻ (taṉippāṭal) eṉṟum, tiruntiya vuḷḷattip paṇpaṭṭavuḷḷa meṉṟum, colvatu vaḻakkam.
paṇ eṉṉum peyarc coṟku mūlamāṉa paṇṇutal eṉṉum viṉic collum, ciṟappāka āḷappeṟumpōtu, palvēṟu viṉikaḷit tiruntac ceytaliyum palvēṟu poruḷkaḷic cevviyāy amittaliyum, kuṟikkum.
paṇṇutal = 1. nilattit tiruttutal.
(paṇṇappaṭṭa marutanilam paṇṇi).
2. 	ūrtiyit takutip paṭuttutal.
	pūnutal yāṉiyoṭu puṉitēr paṇṇavum  (puṟam.12)
3. 	cuvaṭittal (alaṅkarittal)
	paṭṭamo ṭilaṅkal paṇṇi  (cūḷā. kalyā. 14)
4.	iciyala kamittal.
	paṇṇal parivaṭṭaṇi yārāytal  (cīvaka. 657, uri)
5. 	paṇ amittal.
	marutam paṇṇiya karuṅkōṭṭuc cīṟiyāḻ  (malipaṭu. 534).
6. 	camittal.
pālu mitaviyum paṇṇātu peṟukuvir   (malipaṭu. 417).
cey eṉṉum viṉic colliṉiṉṟu, tirunta allatu aḻakāyc ceyyappeṟṟatu eṉṉum poruḷil, cey eṉṉum nilappeyarum ceyyuḷ eṉṉum iyaṟṟamiḻppāṭṭiṉ peyarum tōṉṟiyiruppatu pōṉṟē; paṇ eṉṉum viṉic colliṉiṉṟum, tirunta allatu iṉitāyc ceyyappeṟṟatu eṉṉum poruḷil, paṇṇi eṉṉum nilap peyarum paṇ eṉṉum icit tamiḻ amippiṉ peyarum; tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
paṇpāṭu pala poruṭku uriyatēṉum, nilamum makkaḷ uḷḷa mum paṟṟiyē peruvaḻakkākap pēcappeṟum. āṅkilattil Culture eṉṉum peyarc col ciṟappāka nilap paṇpāṭṭiyum uḷap paṇpāṭṭiyum kuṟippatu kavaṉikkat takkatu. cultivate eṉṉum viṉiccollum aṅṅaṉamē. ivviruvakip paṇpāṭṭuḷḷum, makkaḷit taḻuviya uḷappaṇpāṭē ciṟappākak koḷḷavum collavum peṟum.
cēlaṅ kallūrimēṉāḷ mutalvar pērā. irāmacāmik kavuṇṭar kuṭumpam paṇpaṭṭa tamiḻk kuṭumpattiṟkum, tāymoḻiyākiya tamiḻit talimiyākap pōṟṟum ceṭṭikuḷam paṇpaṭṭavūrukkum, tali ciṟanta eṭuttuk kāṭṭām.
uḷḷam paṇpaṭuvatu perumpālum kalviyālātalāl, paṇpāṭu kalvi mikutiyiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
tamiḻil taṉmiyik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷuḷ, iyalpu eṉpatu iyaṟkiyāṉa taṉmiyiyum, paṇpu eṉpatu paṇpaṭuttap peṟṟa nalla taṉmiyiyum, kuṟikkum.
paṇimoḻi
ettuṇik kaṭuñciṉattiyum eḷitil taṇikkum iṉimiyum tāḻmiyum kalanta vaciyac coṟkaḷ.  (ti.ma. 1258)
paṇiyam
pantayap poruḷ. vaṭṭiṉi eṉṟum peyar peṟum.  (ti.ma.933).
patti
patti - pakti (bh)
paku - paj (bh). pattaṉ - pakta (bh). iṅṅaṉam paku, pattaṉ, patti eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷum, paj, pakta, pakti eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkaḷum, orē mūlattiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyiruppatāl, vaṭacoṟkaṭkuk kāṭṭiya moḻipporuṭ kāraṇamē teṉcoṟkum okkum eṉa aṟika.  (va.va).
pakta = pakukkappeṟṟatu, pakutiyāṉatu, pāṟpaṭṭatu, pāṟpaṭṭavaṉ, paṟṟuḷḷavaṉ, pōṟṟupavaṉ, vaṇaṅkupavaṉ, vaḻipaṭupavaṉ, aṭiyāṉ (mā.vi.a.).
pakti = pakukkap peṟṟami, pakutiyāṉami, pāṟpaṭṭami, pōṟṟuki, vaṇaṅkuki, vaḻipaṭuki, tēvaṭimi (mā.vi.a.).
patti eṉṉum colliṉ vaṭiva varalāṟṟiyum, ataṉ potupporuḷi yum, paku eṉṉum colliṉ kīḻk kāṇka.
paṟṟu eṉpatu irutiṇip poruḷkaḷ mītumuḷḷa aḻuntiya āciyiyē kuṟikkum; patti eṉpatu iṟivaṉiṭattum talivaṉiṭattuṅ koṇṭa accattoṭu kūṭiya uṇmiyāṉa paṇi vaṉpiyē kuṟikkum.  (va.va)
pattup poruttaṅkaḷ
1. nāḷ (naṭcattiram), 2. kaṇam, 3. eṇṇikki (makēntiram), 4. peṇ nīṭci (tiri tīrkkam), 5. piṟavi (yōṉi), 6. ōri (irāci), 7. kōḷ (irāciyatipati), 8. vaciyam, 9. iṇakkam (vēti), 10. caraṭu (iraccu).  (ta.ti. 20)
patakkam
patakkam - pataka
paṭa - pata - patakkam = paranta aṇi. o.nō: paṭāki - patāki.  (va.va : 199).
patam - pata (i.vē)
pati - patam = nilattiṟ patiyum kālaṭi. patam - pātam - pāti = pātam paṭṭu uṇṭākum vaḻi.
pāti eṉṉum col vaṭamoḻiyil illi. āyiṉ, tiyūttāṉiyattil uṇṭu. OE peth, E. path.
pātam - pāta (i.vē.)  (va.va :199) (ti.ma. : 747)

(4)	poruḷum āṭcimēṟkōḷum : (meanings and illustrative quotations).
ceyaṟpeyar vaṭivu : (gerunidial form)
pati - tal = (ce.kuṉṟā.vi). 1. pativēṭṭil eḻututal, to register, enter in writing. 2. peyarp paṭṭiyiṟ cērttal, to take on the roll.
(ce. kuṉṟiya.vi.) 1. āḻtal, to sink in, as the foot or a wheel in mud; to enter, penetrate, as into a soft body. vaṇṭic cakkaram cēṟṟiṟ patikiṟatu. (ulaka vaḻakku). 2. tāḻntiruttal, (to be low-lying. as land; to be depressed, sunk, hollow, worn away. nilam patintirukkiṟatu. (u.va.). 3. taṅkutal, to settle, abid; to perch, roost, as a bird, paticeṉṟu patintaṉaṉ (taṇikippu piramaṉ. 2.) 4. niliyātal, to be permanent, as a post. eṉakku araṇmaṉiyiṟ patinta vēli yilli. (nāñ.u.va.) 5. ūṉṟutal, to be absorbed, engrossed, involved, as the mind in any object. maṉam pāṭattiṟ patikiṟātā? (u.va.). 6. muttiri, eḻuttaccu mutaliyaṉa aḻuntutal, to be imprinted, impressed, marked, stamped, engraven, indented. 7. atikāram peṟutal, to be invested with power, authority. (W.). 8. iṟaṅkutal, to be decline after meridinal transits; to be near setting, as a heavenly body; to descend, alight, as a bird. 9. vili taṇital, to be low, as price. viliyip patiyak kēṭṭāṉ. (u.va.) 10. amitiyātal, to be mild, gentle or tractalle; to become submissive, modest or humble. piyaṉ paḷḷikkūṭattiṟ patintirukkiṟāṉ. (u.va.) 11. añci yiṇaṅkutal allatu piṉvāṅkutal, to quail, flinch.
peyareccam : (adjectival participle)
i. kā. pe. patinta nilam low land.
ceyaṟpeyarum toḻiṟpeyarum : (gerund and verbal noun.)
patital, patiki, pativatu, the act of registering, sinking etc.
pativu, registration, sinking etc.
toḻiṟpeyarum, toḻilākupeyarum : (Verbal noun and metonymical verbal noun.)
pati = 1. patiki, penetration, transfixion, thrust. nuṇṇilivēl patikoṇṭu, (cīvaka. 1186). 2. patiyam, cluster of saplings planted temporarily. 3. nāṟṟu, sapling for transplantation. 4. pativiḷakku, lamp fixed on a pot while exercising devils (W.). 5. ciwÉl«, abode, residence (âth.), patiyiṟ kalaṅkiya mīṉ (kuṟaḷ. 1116), 6. vīṭu, home, house (tivā.). 7. kōyil, temple (caṅ. aka.) 8. kuṟi collum iṭam. an oracular shrine, patiyirunta patiyellām pativākac ceṉṟēṉ (nāñ.marumak.mā.) 9. ūr, town, city, village. patiyeḻu vaṟiyāp paḻaṅkuṭi (cilap. 1 : 15) 10. ñālam (pūmi), the earth. (tilava. tiva.). 11. kutiri, horse. (aka. ni).
pativu = 1. aḻuntuki, impression, indentation. 2. paḷḷam, lowness of a surface; depression. nilam pativāyirukkiṟatu. (u.va.) 3. patiyam. cluster of saplings planted temporarily. 4. viṇmīṉkaḷiṉ cāyvu declination of a heavenly body. (W.) 5. eḻuvataṟku muṉṉuḷḷa tiṅkaḷ (cantiraṉ) nili, situation of the moon before its rise. (W.) 6. patukkam, stooping, crouching, lurking as a thief or a beast ready to spring on its prey; ambush. 7. maṉam ūṉṟuki. engrossment, absorption in an object or pursuit. 8. kaṇakkup patiki, registering, entering, as in account. 9. pativu ceyyappaṭṭatu. that which is registered. 10. patiyap paṭṭatu. registry, entry. 11. tīrmāṉikkappaṭṭa celavu, allotment, as in a budget. intak kōvilil caṅku ūtuvataṟkup pativu ēṟpaṭavilli (nāñ.u.va.) 12. nilippu (nilivaram), permanence. intavūril avaṉ pativāyirukkiṟāṉ (u.va.). 13. vaḻakkam, custom, habit. 14. amiti, submission, obedience, humility.  pativāy naṭantukoḷ (u.va.) 15. vilittaṇivu. lowness of price. (W.).
patiyam = 1. nāṟṟu muṭiyi iraṇṭoru nāṭkuc cēṟṟil patittu vikki, temorary planting of a cluster of saplings in mud. 2. ūṉṟi naṭuñ ceṭi koṭi kiḷi mutaliyaṉa, slip, shoot, graft. 3. ilippāci, a species of duckweed. ceṟiyaṇip patiyattiṭi vaḷariḷañ cēlkaḷ (iraku. nāṭṭup. 7).
patiyam - patikam = patikam parikkuṅ kuḻal (maṟici. 17).

patam - patavu - patavi.
patavi1 = pe. 1. nili, station, situation, position, rank. 2. vaḻi, way, path, road. (tiv. iyaṟ. 2 : 89, arum.). 3. viṇṇulakam, worlds of the gods, lower states of bliss pataviyi yevarkkum nalkum paṇṇavaṉ (kamparā. kiṭkintā vāliva. 136). 4. vīṭṭulakam, final states of bliss (piṅ).
patavi2 = pe. paṇinta nīrmiyuḷḷavaṉ, person of real humility; pataviyāyp pāṇiyāṉī rēṟṟu (tiv. iyaṟ. 2 : 89).
patavu - patavitu - patavicu = pe. amiti, mildness, humbleness.

patam - pātam = pe. 1. nilattiṟ patiyum kāliṉ aṭip pakuti; foot, as a person or animal. pātak kāppiṉaḷ pintoṭi (cilap. 14 : 23). 2. viḷakkut taṇṭiṉ akaṉṟu vaṭṭamāṉa aṭippakuti, broad base of lamp - stand. 3. aṭi cuvaṭu, foot-print. 4. kālaṭiyaḷavu, foot-measure. 5. periyōr muṉṉili presence of a great person. catāciva tēva makārāyar pātattilē viṇṇappamum ceytu (S.I,I.i, 70).
āriya ilakkiyat toṭarpāl, pātam eṉṉuñ coṟkuk kāl eṉṟum aṭi eṉṟum tavaṟāṉa poruḷkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. eṭuttukkāṭṭu varumāṟu.
pātam = pe. 1. viṉippoṟikaḷ (karumēntiriyam) aintuṉuḷ oṉṟākiya kāl. 2. pīṭam mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ tāṅkukāl; leg, support, as of an article furniture. (W.). 3. ceyyuḷaṭi, (Pros.) unit of metrical measure; line of stanza vāṅkarum pātanāṉkum vakutta vāṉmīki (kamparā. pāla. nāṭṭup. 1.) 4. mali maram mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ aṭiyiṭam; base, as of a mountin, a tree (W.). 5. kāṟpaṅku quarter (cūṭā). muṉṉip pīṭattiṉ pātaṅ kuṟintu (mēruman. 1172). 6. vaṭṭattiṉ kāṟpaṅku; quadrant of a circle. 7. veḷḷik kāl (naṭcattira pātam); a fourth part of a duration of a naksatra. 
8. toṇṭu, vaḻipāṭu, ōkam, ōti eṉṉum nāṉku civacamaya neṟi (cariyāpātam, kiriyāpātam, yōkapātam, ñāṉapātam eṉṟa nāṉku civa camaya mārkkam) - ce. pa. ka. ka. ta. a. (Madras University Tamil lexicon).
pātak kāppu = pe. 1. ceruppu, slippers, sandals, clogs (W.). 
2. tiruvaṭip pātukāval; protection at the feet of a great person. pātak kāppiṉaḷ pintoṭi yākaliṉ (cilap. 14 : 23).
pātak kuṟaṭu = pe. kumiḻkoṇṭa kaṭṭi mitiyaṭi; knobbed wooden sandals.
pāta kaṭakam (pātak kaṭakam) = pe. pāṭakam, anklet (cūṭā.).
pāta kāṇikki (pātak kāṇikki) = pe. 1. kuruk koṭi; present to a spritual teacher lid at his feet. 2. cila vūrkaḷil puṉcey nilaṅkaḷukkākak kuṭikaḷāl kuṟunila maṉṉarukkuc celuttappaṭum oruvaki vari; a money payment made in certin villages by ryots of drylands to their landlords. 
pātac cāyi = pe. aṭiniḻal; human shadow.
pāta cakkaram (pātaccakkaram) = pe. pātattil varum oruvakip puṇ; a kind of festering sore in the foot.
pāta cālakam (pātaccālakam) = pe. kālaṇi vaki; a foot ornament. paranta mēkaliyuṅ kōtta pātacālakamum (kamparā, ayōt. kōlaṅ. 12).
pātacālakam - pātacālam (pātaccālam) = pe. kālaṇi vaki pāta cālatta meṉkāl (iraku. nāṭṭup. 48).
pāta tāmari (pātat tāmari) = pe. tiruvaṭit tāmari; lotus - like feet.
pāta pūci (pātappūci) = pe. kuru mutaliyōriṉ tiruvaṭikaḷik kaḻuvi malariṭṭu vaḻipaṭuki; worshipping the feet of a religious preceptor or revered person by washing and adorning them with flowers.
pātatūḷi (pātattūḷi) pe. periyōr aṭippoṭi dust of the feet of great persons ēttuvārkaḷuḻakkiya pātatūḷi paṭutalāl (tiv. periyāḻ. 4 : 4-6).
pāta mayakku = pe. 1. aṭi mayakku; aṭi muṟi māṟṟakkūṭiya ceyyuḷ; stanza whose lines are capable of transposition. 2. vēṟu pulavar pāṭiya mūvaṭikaḷōṭu tāṉ ōraṭi pāṭi muṭikkum miṟippā vaki; a kind of artificial stanza of four lines the first three of which are taken from works of other poets while the last is composed by the author. (yāp. vi. 96. pak. 504).
pāta muttiri = pe. āciriyaṉ tiruvaṭic cuvaṭu; imprint of a guru’s feet (W.).
pāta mūlam = pe. 1. kutikāl heel, (yāḻ. aka.) 2. vīṭu pēṟṟiṟkuk karaṇiya māṉatum (kāraṇamāṉatum) aṭikkalamākak karutappaṭuvatumāṉa tiruvaṭi; feet of a deity or sint considered as the source of the bliss and a refuge. niṉiyumiṉ piṇṭināta ṉalaṅkiḷar pātamūlam (cīvaka. 511).
pāta veṭippu = pe. pitta veṭippu; fissure-foot.
pātam - pāti = pe. 1. oṟṟiyaṭi vaḻi; foot-path. beaten track. 2. vaḻi, way, path, road (piṅ.). 3. muṟi way, method, mode, manner.

mūlamum tirivum (origin and derivation)
paḷ - paṭu - paṭi - pati. paḷ - paḷḷam.
paṭutal = tāḻa viḻutal, viḻutal, toṭutal, patital.
paṭuttal = paḷḷamātal, tāḻtal, viḻutal, paṭukkiyāyk kiṭattal, patital, pativu ceytal.
paṭital = tāḻttal, kīḻppaṭutal, paṇital, patital.
patital = āḻa iṟaṅkutal, poṟittal, pativu ytal.
iṉac col  (cognates and allied words)
1. tiraviṭam 
maliyāḷam - pati, pativu.
patikka, to impress. patiyuka, to be impressed, be pressed down. pati, being fixed in, pressed down. pativu, settlement, custom.
patam, softness, elasticity, yielding temer. patama, patuma. pliancy. patarmma, rottenness of rice through damp, softness of mind. patukka, to be soft, tender, pliable. patuppu, softness. patuppikka, to soften. patam, the right degree of ripeness, temperature, etc.
teluṅku - pataṉu, patuṉu, moisture, dampness, wetness; ripeness maturity temper.
kaṉṉaṭam - pata, proper or good state or condition, proper degree or temperature, the seasoning of any food, the right degree of a ripeness, keeness of edge or sharpness hata, proper condition.
tuḷuvam - pataṉuṉi, pataṇuṉi, to become soft.
kuṭakam - pata soft.
kōttam - patm, temper of iron.
mālattam - petke, to be soft. patō, sharp.
kuvī - pete. soft and damp.
pirākuvī - putēṉ, cold, cool. putī, coldness, frost.
2. āriyam
pataṉam - va. (Skt.) pataṉa.
patavi - va. (Skt.) patavī.
patam - pātam - va. (Skt.) pata, pāta. Gk. pod. L. ped, pedis, OE, OS fot, OHG. fuoz, ON. fotr. Goth, fotus, E. foot.
pāti - OE. poeth. OLG. pad, OHG. pfad, E.path.
camaṟkirutattiṟ pāti eṉṉum col illātatu kavaṉikkattakkatu. pata eṉṉum collē atiṟ pātiyik kuṟikkum.
ped eṉṉum ilattīṉ colliṉiṉṟē, pedal, pedate, pedestal, peduncle eṉṉum coṟkaḷ tirintuḷḷaṉa.
vati - vaci - va. (Skt.) va. iv vaṭacollē āṅkilattil was eṉṉum iṟanta kālat tuṇiviṉic collākat tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
“was. - The OE. weson, to be, is eognate with Goth, wisan; ON. vera. to be, abide; Skt. vas. to dwell.” (P.266)
“were = OE. woreon, where is for original S.” (P. 267).
eṉṟu iriccārṭu māricu (Richard Morris) tam āṅkilac coṟṟiripu varalāṟṟuc caṭṭakam (Historiacal Outlines of English Accidence) eṉṉum nūlil varintiruttal kāṇka. iv viṟanta kālac tuṇiviṉic col maṭṭumiṉṟi, is, are eṉṉum nikaḻkālat tuṇiviṉic coṟkaḷum iru eṉṉum tamiḻccol tiripē.
tamiḻt takaram vaṭamoḻiyil sakaramākat tirivati māsa eṉṉum collālum uṇarka. mati - mātam - māsa(va.) - mā(i).
ciṟappuk kuṟippu
pata, pāta eṉṉum coṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil vaḻaṅkuvatāl, tamiḻp pulavar palar avaṟṟi vaṭacolleṉṟē mayaṅki aṭi eṉṉum collip pātam eṉṉum poruḷil āṇṭuvarukiṉṟaṉar. aṭi vēṟu; pātam vēṟu. aṭi eṉpatu oru poruḷiṉ aṭippakutiyiyum ōr uyiriyiṉ kāliyum pātattiyum kuṟikkum potuc col. e-ṭu: aṭittaṭṭu, mayilaṭi, tiruvaṭi, aṭip pakuti yeṉpatu kīḻ iṭattiyum kīḻp poruḷiyuṅ kuṟikkum. e-ṭu : pantalaṭi, tēraṭi, ciṟiya tiruvaṭi periya tiruvaṭi. maravaṭi eṉpatu kālpōṉṟa aṭimaram. pātam eṉpatō nilattiṟ patiyum paranta aṭiyuṟuppiyē allatu pakutiyiyē kuṟikkum ciṟappuc col. e-ṭu : pātat tāmari, pātam vitta viḷakku. ākavē, patital eṉpati vērp poruḷākak koṇṭa patam, pātam eṉṉum iru coṟkaḷum tūya teṉ collēyeṉṟum, aviyē muṟiyē pata, pāta eṉa vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum eṉṟum aṟika. iṉi, pātam paṭuvatāl ēṟpaṭum vaḻiyik kuṟikkum pāti yeṉṉum col, āṅkilattil (path eṉa) iruppatiyum vaṭamoḻiyil iṉmiyiyum, nōkkuka.
va (vaci), eṉṉum camaṟkiruta viṉic coṟkum. ataṉ tirivāṉa  ‘was’ eṉṉum āṅkila iṟanta kāla viṉiccoṟkum, vati eṉṉum tamiḻccol mūlamāyiruppatiṉiṉṟu, tamiḻiṉ toṉmiyiyum muṉmiyiyum aṟika.
tiruttam
patam, pātam, patavi, pataṉam eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷiṉ tirivāṉa pata, pāta, patavī, pataṉa eṉṉum vaṭa coṟkaḷi, avaṟṟiṉ mūlamāṉa teṉcoṟkaṭkē mūlameṉac ceṉṉi yakara mutaliyiṟ kuṟittiruppatu, mūla vaḻu eṉa aṟika.
patikam
patikam - pratīka
pal - patu - pattu. patu - patikam = 1. oru poruḷpaṟṟi varum orē vakip pattuc ceyyuḷ.
2. pattu allatu patiṉoru kuṟippik kūṟum ciṟappup pāyiram.
ākkiyōṉ peyarē vaḻiyē elli
nūṟpeyar yāppē nutaliya poruḷē
kēṭpōr payaṉō ṭāyeṇ poruḷum
vāyppak kāṭṭal pāyirat tiyalpē.  (naṉ. 47)
kālaṅ kaḷaṉē kāraṇam eṉṟim
mūvaki yēṟṟi moḻinarum uḷarē.  ( 48).  (va.va : 199).
tolkāppiyam, naṉṉūl, cilappatikāram, maṇimēkali mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ ciṟappuppāyiram, perumpālum immuṟiyit taḻuvi amintaviyē.
mā.vi.a. prati - aka eṉpataṉ tiripeṉṟu pratīka eṉṉuñ collik kāṭṭi, nōkkiyatu. nōkku, tōṟṟam, mukam, muṟpakuti, ceyyuḷiṉ muṟpakuti mutaṟcol eṉṟu palvēṟu mēṟkoḷkaḷi iṭarppaṭṭup porutti, vēṟupaṭṭa karuttukkaḷit toṭarpupaṭuttiyiruppatu, ataṉ ceyaṟkit taṉmiyit teḷivākap pulappaṭuttukiṉṟatu.  (va.va : 199 -200).
panti
panti - paṅkti (a.vē.)
pakkam, pākam eṉṉum iraṇṭum paku eṉṉum orē mutaṉili yiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyirukkavum, vaṭavar avaṟṟuḷ muṉṉati āriya iyalpu vallolip pakarattilum (p), piṉṉatik kaṉippolip pakarattilum (bh), toṭaṅkiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. ataṟkēṟpa, pakkam eṉpataṟkup pakṣ eṉpatiyum, pakavaṉ, paṅku, pākam eṉpavaṟṟiṟkup paj (bhaj) eṉpatiyum, mūlamākak koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. iṅṅaṉam orumūlac coṟkaḷiyē vevvēṟu mūlattiṉavākak kāṭṭuvatu vaṭavar vaḻakkam.
pakṣ eṉpatu pakku eṉpataṉ tiripu. tātupāṭa eṉṉum nūl ataṟkup paṟṟu, koḷ, eṭu eṉṟē poruḷ kūṟum. āyiṉ, vilcaṉ akaramutali pakkam (pakṣa) eṉṉum coṟkēṟpap pakkañ cārtal eṉṟu poruḷurikkum.  (va.va.)
pakkam eṉṉum coṟkut tamiḻilum, pakuti, kūṟu, nūṟpakuti, nūl, puṟam (side), ēṭṭup puṟam (page), aruku, aṇmi, iṭam, vīṭu, nāṭu, vilāppuṟam, ciṟaku. iṟaku, ampiṟaku, ki, kiyaṇi, naṭpu, aṉpu, cuṟṟam, marapu (vamicam), cēṉippakuti, cēṉi, patiṉintu piṟināṭkālam, piṟināḷ, mēṟkōḷ (Proposition), tuṇiporuṭ kūṟṟu, taṉmi eṉap palaporuḷkaḷuṇṭu.
ki, ciṟaku eṉṉum coṟkaḷ pakkattik kuṟippatupōṉṟē pakkam eṉṉum collum kiyiyum ciṟakiyum kuṟikkum. vaṭavar ciṟakup poruḷiṉiṉṟu paṟavip poruḷi virittiruk kiṉṟaṉar eṉak karutalām.
pakkam - pakṣa - pakṣi = ciṟakiyuṭiyatu.
iṉi, caturakkaḷḷiyil taṅkum oruvakip paṟavi pakki eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. pakki yuvaṇaṅ kaḻuku (tiruppu.319) eṉa aruṇakiri nātarum pāṭiyuḷḷār.  va.va.
paka (bhaga) eṉṉum irukkuvētac coṟku, “dispenser”, gracious lord, patron (applied to gods, esp. to Savitri), R.V.” eṉṟu mā. vi. a. poruḷ vakukkiṉṟatu.
ākavē, pakavaṉ eṉṉum coṟku, pakuttaḷippavaṉ, paṭiyaḷappavaṉ eṉpatē mūlapporuḷām. āṇṭavaṉ paṭiyaḷappāṉ. eṉṟu aṭikkaṭi makkaḷ kūṟuvati nōkkuka.
namakkum paṭiyaḷappār nāriyōr pākar  (taṉippāṭal).
pakavaṉ eṉpatu pala teyvaṅkaṭkum potuppeyarāy vaḻaṅkiya tiṉālēyē, muḻumutal teyvamākiya kaṭavuḷik kuṟikka āti pakavaṉ eṉṟu aṭikoṭuttuk kūṟiṉār vaḷḷuvar.
ātipakavaṉ = ātippakavaṉ.
vali iṭimikātē pala kūṭṭuc coṟkaḷ akkālattu vaḻaṅki vantaṉa.  va.va.
e-ṭu : mārikālam, kār kālam.
panti eṉpatu patti eṉpataṉ melittal tiripē.
āyiṉ, vaṭavar pañcaṉ (c) eṉpataṉoṭu toṭarpupaṭuttip pac (c) eṉpati mūlamākak kāṭṭuvar. ataṉ viḷakkam varumāṟu:-
pac allatu pañc = viri, kiviri.
pañc - pañcaṉ = viritta kiyiluḷḷa aintuviral, aintu.
pañcaṉ - paṅkti = aimporuḷ tokuti allatu varici, tokuti, varici, kūṭṭam.
vaṭavar ēmāṟṟukkaliyik kāṭṭa iḵtoṉṟē amiyum.  (va.va : 194 - 195).
pantu viḷiyāṭṭu
tuṇiyālum kayiṟṟālum iṟukak kaṭṭappaṭṭa uruṇṭiyi eṟintāṭum āṭṭu, pantu eṉappaṭum (pantu = uruṇṭiyāṉatu).
I. pēyppantu
āṭṭiṉ peyar : pēyttaṉamāka oruvaṉmēloruvaṉ eṟintāṭum pantu, pēyppantu eṉap peyar peṟṟirukkalām.
āṭuvār toki : perumpālum nālvarkku mēṟpaṭṭa palar āṭuvatē ivviḷiyāṭṭiṟkēṟṟatām. āṭuvār toki perukapperuka iv āṭṭuc ciṟakkum.
āṭu karuvi : āṭuvār ettuṇiyarāyiṉum oru pantē iv āṭṭiṟkuriyatām.
āṭiṭam : poṭṭalum veḷinilamum iti āṭu miṭamām.
āṭu muṟi : palar iṭiyiṭṭu niṉṟu koṇṭirukka avaruḷ oruvaṉ panti mēlē pōṭṭup piṭittuk koṇṭu, pantē pantu eṉṟu urakkakkattuvāṉ. piṟar eṉṉa pantu eṉṟu kēṭpar. avaṉ pēyp pantu eṉpāṉ. yār mēlē eṉṟu oruvaṉ kēṭka, avaṉ uṉmēlē eṉṟu collikkoṇṭu avaṉmēl vaṉmiyāy eṟivāṉ. atu avaṉmēl paṭṭālum paṭum; paṭātupōṉālum pōm. pantu yār kippaṭṭatō avaṉ ati ōṅki yār mēlum eṟivāṉ. iṅṅaṉam viruppa muḷḷavari māṟi māṟi aṭittu āṭik koṇṭēyiruppar. yār eṟiyiṉum avaṉuṭiya valimikkut takkavāṟu vaṉmiyāy eṟivatē vaḻakkam.
āṭṭuttōṟṟam : kuraṅkeṟi viḷaṅkāyiṉiṉṟō, pēyc ceyalākak karutappaṭṭa oru pantu vīḻcciyiṉiṉṟō, iv āṭci tōṉṟiyirukkalām.
viḷā māttilirukkum kuraṅkik kallāleṟiya atu viḷaṅkāy koṇṭeṟivatu, kuraṅkeṟi viḷaṅkāyām.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : caṟṟut tolivil niṟkum allatu iyaṅkum irutiṇip poruḷkaḷmēlun tappātu eṟitaṟkēṟṟa payiṟciyi, iv viḷiyāṭṭu aḷikkum. vēṭṭiyāṭal, pōr ceytal, tiruṭaṉik kallāl aṭittal mutaliya viṉikaṭku ippayiṟci ēṟṟatām.
II. piḷḷiyār pantu
āṭṭiṉ peyar : piḷḷiyārik kuṟikkum oru kalliṉmēṟ panti eṟintāṭum āṭṭu piḷḷiyār pantu. itu tirucci vaṭṭārattiṟ piḷḷiyār viḷiyāṭṭu eṉa vaḻaṅkum.
āṭuvār toki : potuvāka, eṇmarkkuk kuṟiyāta palar iti āṭuvar.
āṭu karuvi : ēṟattāḻa āṟaṅkula nīḷamuḷḷa oru kallum, oru pantum, itaṟkuriya karuvikaḷām.
āṭiṭam : cuvaraṭiyum atiyaṭutta veḷi nilamum iti āṭumiṭamām.
āṭumuṟi : āṭuvōrellārum camat tokiyiṉavāṉa iru kaṭciyākap pirintukoḷvar. oru ceṅkalli allatu ciṟu nīḷak kallip piḷḷiyārākap pāvittu oru cuvaraṭiyil ciṟitu maṇalik kuvittu atil ati naṭṭu, oru kaṭciyār aiṅkacat tolivil etirniṉṟu ovvoruvarāy ovvoru taṭavi piḷḷiyārip paṭukiṭiyākac cāyttaṟkup pantālaṭikka, iṉṉoru kaṭciyār iru pakkattilum piḷḷiyārkkum avarukkum iṭiyil variciyāka niṉṟukoṇṭu, eṟiyap paṭṭa pantip piṭikka muyalvar. piḷḷiyārip paṭukiṭi yāyc cāytta panti yārēṉum antarattiṟ piṭittu viṭiṉum, eṟinta pantu piḷḷiyārmēṟ paṭāviṭiṉum, paṭṭum atip paṭukiṭiyāyc cāykkāviṭiṉum, eṟiyuṅ kaṭciyār āḷ māṟikkoṇṭēyiruntu aṉivaruntīrntapiṉ, etirk kaṭciyār aṭikkuṅ kaṭciyārākavum aṭitta kaṭciyār piṭikkuṅ kaṭciyārākavum, māṟal vēṇṭum.
piḷḷiyārip paṭukiṭiyāyc cāytta pantu piṭikkap paṭāviṭiṉ, piṭikka niṉṟa kaṭciyar aṉivarum uṭaṉē oṭip pōyc caṟṟut tolivil iṭiyiṭṭu niṟpar. aṭitta kaṭciyāraṉivarum nerukkamākak kūṭi niṉṟu, avaruḷ oruvaṉ pantit taṉ aṭi vayiṟṟiṉmēl vittu atu veḷikkut teriyāmal iru kiyālum pottik koṇṭum, piṟarum tām pantu vittiruppatāka etirk kaṭciyārukkut tōṉṟumāṟu taṉittaṉi naṭittukkoṇṭum, avariṭiyē pirintu celvar. etirkkaṭciyāruḷ yārēṉum oruvaṉ, uṇmiyāyp pantu vittiruppavaḷi aiyuṟātu avaṉukkup pakkamāka niṟpiṉ, pantu vittiruppavaṉ tiṭumeṉṟu avaṉmēl eṟintu viṭuvāṉ. atōṭu ōr āṭṭi muṭiyum. aṭutta āṭṭi āṭuvatu āṭakar viruppattip poṟuttatu.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : orukāl, piṟamatap pakimi paṟṟiyum koḷḷi yaṭittaṟ poruṭṭum, iṭik kālattiṟ cila aracarum koḷḷit talivarum teyvaccilikaḷi (vikkiraṅkaḷi) uṭittatum kavarntatum, iv viḷiyāṭṭut tōṟṟattiṟkutk kāraṇamāyiruntirukkalām. kaciṉi makamatu, mālikkāpūr, tirumaṅkiyāḻvār mutaliyōr ceyalkaḷ, iṅkuk kavaṉikkat takkaṉa.
pappaṭam
pappaṭam - parppaṭa (!)
appaḷittal = camaṉākat tēyttal. kavari appaḷittup pūcukiṟāṉ eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
appaḷi - appaḷam = camaṉākat tēyttamikkum uḻuttamā vaṭṭam.
mēvukapan tīrkkuṅkāṇ ōtumuḻun tappaḷam  (patārtta. 1426).
te. appaḷamu - appapamu - ka. appaḷa - pappaḷa. ma. pappaṭam.  va.va : 200
pampara viḷiyāṭṭu
I. oyākkaṭṭi
ciṟuvar, tam pamparaṅkaḷi vaṭṭattuḷāyiṉum, varampilā nilattilāyiṉum oruṅkē āṭaviṭṭu, yāratu nīṇṭa nēram āṭukiṉṟateṉṟu pārkkum āṭṭu ōyākkaṭṭiyām. itu mīṇṭum mīṇṭum orē vakiyāy āṭappeṟum.
II. uṭitta kaṭṭi
āṭṭiṉ peyar : āṭṭattil tōṟṟavaṉatu pamparatti uṭikkum āṭṭu uṭitta kaṭṭi eṉappaṭum.
āṭumuṟi : iruvarkku mēṟpaṭṭa ciṟuvar palar, oru vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuvil oru māṅkoṭṭiyi vittu, ovvoruvaṉākap pamparatti ataṉmēlēṟṟi, ati veḷiyēṟṟuvar. atu veḷiyēṟiyavuṭaṉ, ellārum oruṅkē virivākat taṉ taṉ pamparatti āṭṭik kimēl ēṟṟuvar. mikap pinti ēṟṟiyavaṉ taṉ pamparatti vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuvil vittal vēṇṭum.
pamparatti yāṭṭik kimēlēṟṟumpōtu, apiṭkōcu allatu ciṅkōcu eṉṟu collikkoḷvatāl, aṅṅaṉam ēṟṟuvataṟku, apiṭkōceṭuttal allatu ciṅ kō ceṭuttal eṉṟu peyar.
vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuviluḷḷa pamparatti ēṉiyōrellārum, muṉpu māṅkoṭṭiyi veḷiyēṟṟiyatu pōl veḷiyēṟṟi, ēṟattāḻa irupatu kacat toliviluḷḷa ellik kōṭ ṭiṟkuk koṇṭu pōvar. ovvoruvaṉāka avaṉavaṉ taṉtaṉ pamparatti ataṉmēlēṟṟit taḷḷiyē, atikkoṇṭu pōtal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam koṇṭu pōmpōtu, yārēṉum maṭṭi vīḻttiṉum cāṭṭi pōkkiṉum, kīḻēyirukkum pamparattiṟkup patilākat taṉ pamparatti vittuviṭal vēṇṭum. kīḻēyirunta pamparakkāraṉ ati eṭuttup piṟarpōl āṭṭuvāṉ.
pamparam talikīḻāy viḻutaṟku maṭṭi eṉṟum, pakkamāka uruṇṭupōtaṟkuc cāṭṭi eṉṟum peyar.
yār pamparam ellik kōṭṭiṟkuk koṇṭu pōkap paṭṭatō, atu uṭaṉē piṟarāl uṭikkappaṭum.
III. pamparak kuttu
āṭṭiṉ peyar : tōṟṟavaṉatu pamparattik kutti yāṭum āṭṭu pamparakkuttu eṉappaṭum.
āṭu muṟi : iruvarkku mēṟpaṭṭa ciṟuvar palar, oru vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuvil māṅkoṭṭi vittu veḷiyēṟṟi, apiṭkōceṭuttu, atil mikap pintiyavaṉ taṉ pamparatti vaṭṭattuḷ vitta piṉ, ēṉiyōrellām ovvoru vaṉākat taṉ taṉ pamparattāṟ kutti ati veḷiyēṟṟuvar. atu veḷiyēṟṟappaṭiṉ uṭaṉē mīṇṭum muṉpōl apiṭ kōceṭuttu, atiṟ pintiyavaṉ taṉ pamparatti vaṭṭattuḷ vittal vēṇṭum. ēṉiyarellām muṉpōṟkutti ati veḷiyēṟṟuvar.
maṭṭi vīḻttiyavaṉ pamparamum, cāṭṭi pōkkiyavaṉ pamparamum vaṭṭattuḷ ēṟkeṉavē vittiruppatuṭaṉ cērttu vikkappaṭum.
vaṭṭattuḷ āṭum pamparaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu veḷiyēṟiyāṭum pōtu, ati vaṭṭattuḷ vittirukkum pamparakkāraṉ cāṭṭiyāl taṉ kiyilēṟṟi āṭṭiṉ, atuvum vaṭṭattuḷ vikkappaṭal vēṇṭum.
vaṭṭattuḷ āṭum pamparaṅkaḷuḷ etiyēṉum, vaṭṭattuḷ vikkappaṭṭirukkum pamparakkāraṉ, taṉ kiyālaḻuttip patittuviṭiṉ, ati eṭuttal kūṭātu.
uḷḷē vikkappaṭṭirukkum pamparam mēlum mēlum kuttap paṭṭuc cētamākātapaṭi, ataṉ contakkāraṉ āṭṭikkoṇ ṭirukkum pamparakkāraruḷ oruvaṉiṭam, nī iṉṟu eṉ pamparatti veḷiyēṟṟiṉ, uṉ pamparam uḷḷirukkumpōtu nāṉ veḷiyēṟṟuvēṉ, eṉṟu oppantañ ceytukoḷva tuṇṭu.
pala pamparaṅkaḷ uḷḷē vikkappaṭṭuk kiṭappiṉ, avi yaṉittum veḷiyēṟṟappaṭṭa piṉputāṉ apiṭkōcu eṭukkappaṭum.
iṅṅaṉam mīṇṭum mīṇṭum vēṇṭum aḷavu toṭarntu āṭappeṟum.
vaṭṭattuḷ vikkappaṭṭirukkum pamparaṅkaḷuḷ naṇpaṉati veḷiyēṟṟa vēṇṭumeṉṟum, piṟaṉati veḷiyēṟṟak kūṭāteṉṟum, iruvēṟu nōkkuk koṇṭu ataṟkēṟpap pamparatti āṭṭuvatu vaḻakkam.
IV. iruvaṭṭak kuttu
vaṭṭattuḷ vaṭṭamāka iruvaṭṭam kīṟi avviraṇṭuḷḷum pamparattik kuttuvatu, iruvaṭṭakkuttu. itu perumpālum pamparak kuttup pōṉṟatē.
itaṉ uḷvaṭṭattil māṅkoṭṭi vittu veḷiyēṟṟi apiṭ kōceṭuttup pintiyavaṉ pamparatti ataṉuḷ vittapiṉ, atik kutti veḷiyēṟṟuvar. maṭṭiyum cāṭṭiyum uḷvikkappaṭum. uḷ vaṭṭattil āṭum pamparam veḷivaṭṭattuḷḷum vantu āṭalām. āṭum pamparatti aḻuttuvatu uḷvaṭṭattiltāṉ nikaḻum; āyiṉ, atik kuttuvatu iruvaṭṭattilum uṇṭu. veḷivaṭṭattuḷ āṭum pamparam tāṉāy nakarntu veḷiyēṟi viṭiṉ, ati uṭiyavaṉ eṭuttu āṭṭalām; aṉṟi uḷḷēyē ōyntuviṭiṉ ati eṭuttal kūṭātu. atuvum maṭṭipōṟ pāvikkappaṭum.
V. taliyāri
āṭuvār toki : mūvarkkuk kuṟiyāta ciṟuvar palar iti āṭuvar.
āṭu karuvi : āḷukkoru pamparamum, ēṟattāḻa irupatu kacam iṭiyiṭṭa iru cama aḷavāṉa vaṭṭaṅkaḷum, oru pōkāka (atāvatu camatūramāka) irupuṟamum nīṭṭappaṭṭa avaṟṟiṉ viṭṭaṅkaḷum, ivviḷiyaṭṭiṟ kuriya karuvikaḷām.
āṭu muṟi : mutalāvatu oru vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuviluḷḷa māṅ koṭṭiyip pamparattiṉ mūlamāy veḷiyēṟṟi, apiṭkō ceṭuttu atiṟ pintiyavaṉ pamparatti uḷ vittu, ati ēṉiyō rellām ovvoru vaṉāyp pamparamēṟṟi veḷi yēṟṟuvar. aṅṅaṉam veḷiyēṟṟumpōtu vaṭṭattiṟku valappuṟamāy veḷiyēṟṟal vēṇṭum; illā viṭiṉ mīṇṭum apiṭkō ceṭuttup pintiyavaṉ pamparatti uḷḷē vikka vēṇṭiyirukkum.
veḷiyēṟṟappaṭṭa pamparatti nērē etiruḷḷa vaṭṭattiṟkup pamparattiṉ mūlamāy aṭittuk koṇṭu pōvar. oruvaṉ iṭiyil maṭṭi vīḻttiṉum cāṭṭi pōkkiṉum, kīḻirukkum pamparattiṟkup patilākat taṉati vittuviṭal vēṇṭum. muṉpu kīḻirunta pamparakkāraṉ piṉpu piṟaroṭu cērntu āṭuvāṉ.
aṭittukkoṇṭu pōkappaṭum pamparam etirvaṭṭattuḷ cērnta vuṭaṉ, piṟa pamparakkārarellām tam cāṭṭikaḷik kaḻuttiṟ cuṟṟik koṇṭu, atip pampara āṇiyāl ovvoru taṭavi kuttuvar. kuttumpōtu cāṭṭi taḷarntu nilattit toṭiṉ, kuttappaṭum pamparakkāraṉ taṉ cāṭṭiyāl vaṉmiyāy aṭittu viṭuvāṉ.
piṉpu, iraṇṭām vaṭṭattiliruntu mutal vaṭṭattiṟku, muṉpōṉṟē ap pamparam aṭittukkoṇṭu pōkappaṭum. aṅku ati ivviru taṭavi kuttuvar.
ataṉpiṉ, atu iraṇṭām vaṭṭattiṟku mīṇṭuṅ koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭu, mummūṉṟu taṭavi kuttappaṭum. iṅṅaṉam iṅkumaṅku māka iyakkappaṭṭu, ovvoru muṟikkum ovvoru taṭavi kūṭutalākak kuttappaṭum.
ivvakiyil itu virumpiya aḷavu toṭarntu āṭappeṟum.  (ta.nā.vi.)
payirc ceṟivu vaki
putar kuttuc ceṭikaḷiṉ ceṟivu; poti peruñceṭi koṭi kaḷiṉ ceṟivu; potumpar maraṅkaḷiṉ ceṟi.  (col : 72).
payir vaki 
pūcaṉam allatu pūñcaṇam (pūccāḷam) nonta cōṟṟilum īramarattilum tōṉṟum nuṇpāci; pācam nīr mēlum īramuḷḷa iṭattilum tōṉṟum pacumiyāṉa nuṇpayir; pāci nīril aṭivari paṭarntuḷḷa pācam; kāḷāṉ īramuḷḷa iṭattil kuṭipōl tōṉṟum payir vaki; pul aṟuku kuci pōṉṟavi; pūṇṭu veṅkāyam veḷḷip pūṇṭu pōṉṟavi; ceṭi miḷakāyc ceṭi kattiric ceṭi pōṉṟavi; koṭi avarik koṭi pākaṟkoṭi pōṉṟavi; putar aṭarnta kuttuc ceṭi; payir nel kampu kēḻvaraku pōṉṟavi; maram mā puḷi pōṉṟavi.  col : 70

paricam vāṅkāmi
peṇṇiṟkup paricam kēṭpatu ōraḷavu vili kūṟuvatu pōṉṟiruttalāl, celvap peṟṟōr. ataṉik kēḷātiruttal ciṟappām. ēḻippeṟṟōrāyiṉ, maṇac celavu nōkki oru toki kēṭpatu kuṟṟamākātu.
iṉi peṇṇiṟkuk kēṭpataṟkup patilāka maṇavāḷap piḷḷikkuk kēṭpatu, iyaṟkikku māṟāṉatāyum, ek kāraṇattālum carimippaṭutta muṭiyātatāyum irukkiṉṟatu. ivvulakattil oruvaṉ peṟakkūṭiya pēriṉpap pēṟu peṇṇē akattaḻakum puṟattaḻakum oruṅkē yaminta arumip peṇṇirukkavum avaḷi viṭṭuviṭṭu, kāciṟkācippaṭṭu aḻakiliyi maṇappatu, illaṟa iṉpattiyum vāḻkki vacatiyiyum paṇattiṟku viṟpatu pōṉṟatē.  ta.ti. 50 - 51.

cītanīr poḷḷaṟ ciṟakuṭattu nillātu
vītalō niṟṟal viyappu  (naṉṉeṟi, 12).
puḷ - paḷ - paḷku. paḷkutal = patuṅkutal, paḷḷamāṉa iṭattil maṟital. paḷku - paḻku.
paḷ - paḷḷam = 1. tāḻviṭam, 2. tāḻnilam, 3. tāḻmaṭṭam, tāḻvu. kuḻivu. 5. kaṉṉattil viḻuṅkuḻivu. 6. āḻam. 7. kiṭaṅku. 8. kuḻi.
tuḷittal tōṇṭutal. tōṇṭum nilam mutaliṟ paḷḷamākum; piṉpu kiṭaṅkākum; ataṉpiṉ kuḻiyākum; kuḻi oṉṟi ūṭuruviṉ tuḷiyākum. tōṇṭat tōṇṭa āḻam mikum.
paḷ - paḷḷi. paḷḷik kiruttal = vitikaḷ cēṟṟiṟ patintu kiṭattal. nāñcil nāṭṭu vaḻakku).
paḷ - paḷḷi = kuḷḷam. paḷḷiyaṉ = kuḷḷaṉ, kuṟukip paruttavaṉ. paḷḷiyāṭu = kuḷḷamāṉa āṭṭuvaki.
kuḷḷam paḷḷampōl tāḻmaṭṭamāyiruttalāl, paḷḷi yeṉappaṭṭatu.
paḷḷi - paḷḷiyam = tāḻ maṭṭamāṉa (taṭṭiyāṉa) uṇkalam. ma. paḷḷiyam.
paḷḷiyam pōṭutal = ciṟu teyvattiṟkuk kīḻē paṭittal.
paḷḷi = 1. tāḻvu. 2. tāḻvāṉa iṭam, tāḻ maṭṭam. 3. tāḻvāṉa vīṭu allatu kuṭici. 4. tāḻvāṉa vīṭukaḷ cērnta ciṟṟūr. 5. tāḻvāṉa iṭiccēri. kāvum paḷḷiyum (malipaṭu. 451). o. nō: paṭu - pāṭu - pāṭi = iṭiccēri paṭuttal = tāḻvāyiruttal. 6. neṭitāy eḻuntu niṟkum niliyum kuṟitāy amarntirukkum niliyum iṉṟit tāḻvāy nila allatu aṭittaḷa maṭṭamāy nīḷakkiṭakkum nili. 7. aṅṅaṉaṅ kiṭattal, paṭuttal. 8. paṭukkum iṭam, vilaṅku tuyiliṭam. 9. paṭukkum pāyal, parappal, virippu, metti. 10. paṭuttut tūṅkutal, tūkkam. 11. paṭukkum aṟi. 12. paṭuttut taṅkum vīṭu. (maṭippaḷḷi = camiyal ceyyuma taṉi vīṭu). 13. aracaṉ vīṭākiya araṇmaṉi. 14. teyva vīṭākiya tirukkōvil. kaḻāakkāl paḷḷiyuḷ vittaṟṟāl (kuṟaḷ. 840); camaṇak kōyil, puttakkōyil. 15. tuṟaviyar taṅkum maṭam. 16. muṉiva rirukki. 17. aṟaccāli. 18. taṅkum iṭam. 19. iṭam. colliya paḷḷi niliyiṉa vāyiṉum (tol. eḻut. 100). 20. kōvilil allatu maṭattil naṭipeṟum tuvakkak kalviccāli. 
21. paṇimaṉi. taccaṉ viṉipaṭu paḷḷi (kaḷavaḻi. 15). 22. pala vīṭukaḷ cērnta pērūr, pala kāri vīṭukaḷ cērnta nakar allatu nakaram. 23. ūrp peyarīṟu. e-ṭu: kurāppaḷḷi, tirukkāṭṭup paḷḷi. te. gšÈ (b.), mataṉapalli, paṅkaṉappalli, paḷḷi, haḷḷi, māraṇṭahaḷḷi, paḻaṅkaṉṉaṭattil paḷḷiyeṉṟu vaḻaṅkiya ūrppeyar īṟukaḷellām, putuk (hoca) kaṉṉaṭattil haḷḷi eṉṟu māṟiviṭṭaṉa.
paḷḷi - va. pallī, palli (katācaritcākara) = ciṟu vīṭu, ciṟṟūr.
palli eṉṉum vaṭacol vētattil vaḻaṅkātu piṟkālac camaṟkiruttilēyē vaḻaṅkutalum pakaramutal eṭuppoli peṟāmiyum, vērccol iṉmiyum, vēṟu poruḷ koḷḷāmiyum, atu tamiḻt tiricol eṉpatit teḷḷat teḷivākak kāṭṭum.
paḷḷik kuṟippu, paḷḷi koṇṭāṉ, paḷḷi koḷḷutal, paḷḷi maṇṭapam, paḷḷi māṭam, paḷḷiyantulā,paḷḷiyampalam, paḷḷiyayartal, paḷḷiyaṟi, paḷḷiyeḻucci eṉpaṉa paṭukki allatu tūkkam paṟṟiya vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ.
paḷḷi paṭuttal, paḷḷipaṭi eṉpaṉa aracariyum muṉivariyum aṭakkañ ceytal paṟṟiya vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ, aṭakkañ ceytal niliyākap paṭukkip paṭuttutal pōṉṟatu.
paḷḷi kampu vittal eṉpatu, vari celuttātavaṉ vīṭṭiṉ muṉ kampu naṭṭu maṟiyal ceyyum nāñcil nāṭṭu vaḻakkup paṟṟiya col. paḷḷiyōṭam ciṟu vīṭu pōṉṟa amippuḷḷa marakkalam.
paḷḷikkaṭṭil, paḷḷikkaṭṭu, paḷḷittēvāram, paḷḷippīṭam, paḷḷivēṭṭi eṉpaṉa, araṇmaṉi allatu aracaṉ toṭarpāṉa vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ.
paḷḷiccantam eṉpatu camaṇa puttakakōvil māṉiyamum paḷḷivācal eṉpatu makamatiyar kōvilum paṟṟiya vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ.
paḷḷik kaṇakkaṉ, paḷḷik kaṇakku, paḷḷikku vittal, paḷḷikkūṭam, paḷḷikkūṭattāṉ, paḷḷittōḻami, paḷḷippiḷḷi, arippaḷḷi, uccippaḷḷi eṉpaṉa, tiṇṇip paḷḷik kūṭam allatu tuvakkak kalviccāli paṟṟiya vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ.
iṉi, vēṟu cila vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum uḷa.
ittakiya vaḻakkac coṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil illi.
paḷ - paṭu - paṭukki, paṭi. o. nō : Goth. badi, OE. bed, OS. bedi(d) E.bed. paṭu - pāṭu - pāṭi, pāṭi. paṭu - paṭi. paṭital. o. nō : OE, OS. bidan, Goth, beidan, E. bide.
paḷ - pāḷ - pāḷam = 1. parantu taṭṭiyāṉa kaṭṭi. 2. kaṉatta takaṭu.
paḷḷi - (pāḷi) - pāḻi = 1. makkaḷ tuyiliṭam. perumpāḻi cūḻnta viṭattaravi (tiv. iyaṟ. 1 : 80). 2. vilaṅku tuyiliṭam. (piṅ.). 3. kuki. (tivā.) 4. kōyil. aiyaṉ pāḻiyil āḷipōrk kurittām aṉṟu (īṭu, 1 : 1 : 3). 5. pācaṟi. (piṅ). 6. muṉivarirukki. pūtantampāṟ pāṭṭik koṇṭuṇpavar pāḻitoṟum (tēvā. 186 : 5). 7. vatiyum iṭam, iṭam. vāṉavarkōṉ pāḻi (tiv. iyaṟ. 2 : 13). 8. marutanilattūr. (cūṭā.) 9. nakaram. (piṅ.) 10. ciṟukuḷam. (tol. col. 400, uri). 11. iṟaṅku tuṟi. ivar tammit tāṉuṇarntāl ivarkkup pāḻi kiruṣṇāvatāra miṟē (tiv. iyaṟ. tiruvirut. 61). 12. elivaḷi. elippāḻi.
iṉi, pāḷ - pāḷi - pāḻi eṉṟumām.
pāḷ - pāḷi - pāḷiyam = 1. pācaṟi. 2. pōrkkuc cellum paṭi vaḻiyil taṅkiyirukkum iṭam. 3. paṭi niliyāka irukkum ūr.
pāḻi = Gk. polis, city.
E. acropolis (f. Gk. akropolis, akros, topmost, outer-most, polis, city.) citadel or elevated part of a Greek city, esp of Athens.
E. decapolis (f. Gk. deka, ten, polis, city), confederacy of ten cities in the 1st century B.C. in a region in the NE part of ancient Palestine.
E. necropolis (f. Gk. nekros, corpse, dead body, polis, city), cemetery.
E. cosmopolis (f. Gk. kosmos, universe, polis, city), a cosmopolitan city.
E. cosmopolitan, a. Belonging to all parts of the world; n, person free from national limitations.
E. cosmopolite, n. Citizen of the world; a free from national prejudices; Gk. kosmopolites, kosmos, universe, polites, citizen.
E cosmopolitical, a. Belonging to universal polity.
E. metropolis, n. Chief city of a country, capital, metropolitan bishop’s see, centre of activity. [LL. f. Gk. metropolis, meter, mother, polis, city.]
E. metropolitan, a & n. Of a or the metropolis; belonging to, forming part of, mother country as dist. from its colonies; of an ecclesiastical metropolis; metropolitan bishop; bishop having authority over bishops of a province, in the west equivalent to archbishop, in Greek church ranking above archbishop and below patriarch, whence metropolitanate; inhabitant of a metropolis.
[LL. metropolitanus f. Gk. metropolites f. metropolis.]
E. Metropolitan magistrate pid London magistrate.
E. Police. n. Civil administration, department of  government concerned with public order, civil force responsible for enforcing law and mintining public order. [F. f. med. L. politia, policy].
E. policlinic, n. Clinic in private houses; f. G. polik linik f. Gk. polis, city + clinic.
E. policy, n. Statecraft, course of action adopted by government, party. etc; political sagacity, prudent conduct. [ME f. OF. policie f. L. f. Gk. politeia, citizenship, polity, polites, citizen f polis, city.
E. politarch, n Governor of some Oriental cities. e.g. Thessalonica under Romans. [f. Gk. politarkhes, polites, citizen, arkhes, ruler.]
E. politic, a & n. (Of persons) sagacious, prudent, (of actions etc.) judicious, expedient, Scheming; n. (pl.) Science and art of government, political offairs or life, political principles. [ME. L. Of. politique f. L. f. Gk. politikos, f. politea f. polites f. polis, city].
E. political, a & n Of the State or its government, of public affairs, of politics; f. politic.
E. politician, n. One skilled in politics, statesman, one interested or engaged in politics, esp. as profession, one who makes a trade of politics; f. politic.
E. politicize, ise, v.i. & t. Act the politician; engage in, talk, polities; give political character to; f. politic + ize (sef.)
E. politico -, in comb. as politico - economical, politico - geographical, politico - social, politico - religious etc; f. politic.
E. polity, n. Condition of civil order; form, process, of civil government; orgranized society, state. [f. obs. F. politic or L. politia, policy.]
ūr kāval, pāṭi kāval eṉpaṉa pōṉṟē, police eṉṉum kāval tuṟip peyarum nakarap peyariṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyiruttali nōkkuka.



paḷḷikkūṭa viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷ
kōḻikkuñcu - 1
pala piḷḷikaḷ, oruvarpiṉ oruvarāka oruvar iṭuppi iṉ ṉoruvar cērntu kaṭṭikkoṇṭu allatu paṟṟikkoṇṭu, variciyāka niṟpar. talimiyāṉa piḷḷi mutalil niṟkum. appiḷḷikku etirē maṟṟoru piḷḷi niṟkum.
talimiyāṉa piḷḷi kōḻiyiyum, piṉṉāl niṟkum piḷḷikaḷ ataṉ kuñcukaḷiyum, etirē niṟkum piḷḷi kaḻukiyum, nikarppar. kaḻuku kaṭicik kuñcip piṭikka varum. varici vaḷintu kuñcukaḷ tappum.
kaḻuku kōḻiyi nōkki eṉakkoru kuñcu tā; iṉṟēl paṟantu vantu piṭittukkoṇṭu pōy viṭuvēṉ eṉṉum. kōḻi maṟuttu, kaḻuku kuñcip piṭikkātapaṭi maṟikkum. kaḻuku cuṟṟic cuṟṟi vantu kaṭicik kuñcip piṭittuk koṇṭu pōkum. iṅṅaṉamē, piṉpu ēṉik kuñcukaḷiyum ovvoṉ ṟāyp piṭittukkoṇṭu pōyviṭum.
kōḻi mika mika varuntum.
kōḻikkuñcu -2
eṭṭup piḷḷikaḷ cērntukoḷvar. avaruḷ oru piḷḷi kōḻiyiyum, aintu piḷḷikaḷ ataṉ kuñcukaḷiyum, maṟṟoru piḷḷi kaḻukiyum, maṟṟumoru piḷḷi nariyiyum, nikarppar.
kōḻi taṉ kuñcukaḷik kūṇṭiṟkuḷ irukkac colliviṭṭu, iritēṭa veḷiyē ceṉṟuviṭum. kuñcukaḷ tāy pēccit taṭṭik kūṇṭiṟku veḷiyē varum. kaḻuku oru kuñci eṭuttuk koṇṭupōkum. ēṉikkuñcukaḷellām kūṇṭiṟkuḷ ōṭiviṭum. piṉpu caṟṟunēram poṟuttu mīṇṭum veḷiyē varum. kaḻuku mīṇṭum oru kuñci eṭuttukkoṇṭu pōkum. iṅṅaṉam nālu kuñcu pōṉapiṉ tāy tirumpivarum. eñciyuḷḷa kuñcu naṭantatic collum.
kōḻi kaḻukiṭam ceṉṟu, eṉ kuñcukaḷiyellāṅ koṭuttuviṭu. avaṟṟiṟkup patilāka nāṉ uṉakku nāḷikkuk kaṟitaruvēṉ eṉṟu vākkaḷittu, kuñcukaḷi mīṭṭukkoṇṭu pōkum.
maṟunāḷ, kaḻukiṟkuk kaṟi eṅṅaṉam koṭuppateṉṟu kōḻi kavaṉṟu koṇṭirukkumpōtu, nari ataṉiṭam vantu, nī kavalippaṭātē. kaḻukiṟkuk kaṟikoṭukka vēṇṭiyatilli. ataṉiṉṟu tappuvataṟku uṉakkoru valakkaram (tantiram) collikkoṭuppēṉ, eṉṟu collum. kōḻi ati nampikkoṇṭu cummā iruntuviṭum. piṉpu nari kaḻukiṭam ceṉṟu, kōḻi uṉakku vākkaḷittapaṭi kaṟi koṭukkātām. nī pōy ataṉ kuñcukaḷip piṭittukkoṇṭu vantāltāṉ uṉakkuk kaṟi kiṭikkum eṉṟu mūṭṭiviṭum. kaḻuku uṭaṉē pōy, ellāk kuñcu kaḷiyum ovvoṉṟākap piṭittuk koṇṭu vantuviṭum. narikku oru kuñcu paṅku kiṭikkum.
kōḻi nariyi nampik keṭṭuppōṉati niṉittu mikat tuyaruṟum.
kuṟippu :- mēṟkūṟiya viḷiyāṭṭiraṇṭum pētip paruvap peṇkaḷuk kuriyavi. aiyāṭṭip paruvattārāyiṉ āṇpiḷḷikaḷum cērntu koḷḷalām.  (ta.nā.vi.)
paḷiṅku (t´) 
paḷiṅku - paṭika (ph, t´)
paḷ - paḷa, paḷapaḷa - paḷapaḷappu. paḷiccu. paḷīr eṉpaṉa miṉṉaṟ kuṟippukkaḷ.
paḷ - paḷiṅku = 1. paḷiṅkukkal.
aṭuttatu kāṭṭum paḷiṅkupōl  (kuṟaḷ. 706).
2. kaṇṇāṭi. 3. kaṟpūram.
paḷa - paḷitam = kaṟpūram.
pācilit tiriyalum paḷitamum paṭittu  (maṇi. 28 : 243).  (va.va. 203) (ti.ma. 748).
paḷiṅku (2) 
paḷ - paḷi - paḷiṅku = paḷiṅkukkal. kaṇṇāṭi, kaṟpūram. paḷi - paḷitam = kaṟpūram. paḷ - paṭṭu = paḷapaḷappāṉa nūl allatu tuṇi paṭṭup paṭṭeṉṟu nilā aṭikkiṟatu eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka. paḷiṅku = paṭika. (va.).  (ti.ma. : 748)
paṟṟu 
nāṉ eṉṟu taṉṉip paṟṟiya akappaṟṟum eṉatu eṉṟu taṉ uṭimi kaḷip paṟṟiya puṟappaṟṟum eṉa iruvakippaṭum.  (ti.ma : 48)
paṟṟuk kōṭu 
paṟṟuk kōṭu eṉpatu mutalāvatu koṭikaḷ paṟṟippaṭarum koḷ kom piṟkum, iraṇṭāvatu koḷ kompu pōṉṟa tāṅkalukkum peyar. paṟṟu - piṭippu. kōṭu - kompu.  (col. 14).
paṉṉiru poruttam 
1. kātaṟ poruttam. 2. uṭal nalap peruttam, 3. oḻukkap poruttam. 4. karuttup poruttam. 5. uṇṭip poruttam.  6. akavip poruttam. 7. uruvap poruttam. 8. kalvip poruttam. 9. muṟip poruttam. 10. viṉit tiṟapporuttam. 11. toḻiṟ poruttam. 12. kuṭumpa nilip poruttam.  
ta.ti : 43 - 49.
paṉṉīrkkuḷattil muḻukutal viḷiyāṭṭu
patiṉoru peṇpiḷḷikaḷ kūṭit teruvil ōriṭattil vaṭṭamāy uṭkārntapiṉ, avaruḷ talimiyāṉavaḷ ovvoruttiyiṉ muṭṭik kālkaḷiyum ovvoṉṟāka varicippaṭi cuṟṟic cuṟṟic toṭṭukkoṇṭu.
	1	2	3	4
	oruppuṭṭam	tiruppuṭṭam	oṭivā	maṅkaḷam
	5	6	7	8
	cekkit	tiruppic	cevveṇṇey	vārttu
	9	10	11	12
	māṭuṅ	kaṉṟum	varukiṟa	vēḷi
	13	14	15	16
	mañcaḷ	taṇṇīr	teḷikkiṟa	vēḷi
	17	18	19
	kāli	maṭakkaṭi	kāmāṭci
eṉṉum maraput toṭari, toṭukikkoṉṟākap pat toṉpatu cīrpaṭac colvāḷ. kāmāṭci eṉṟu muṭikiṟa peṇ uṭaṉē oru kāli maṭakki uṭkāra vēṇṭum (atāvatu maṇṭiyiṭṭukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum). iṅṅaṉamē mīṇṭum mīṇṭum ammaraput toṭar collappaṭṭu, ovvoru taṭavi yum kāmāṭci eṉṟa muṭikiṟa peṇ taṉ kāli maṭakkik koḷḷa vēṇṭum. iraṇṭān taṭaviyākak kāmāṭci eṉṟu muṭiyum peṇ, taṉ maṟu kāliyum maṭakkiṉa vuṭaṉ eḻuntu pōyviṭa vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam ēṉip patiṉ marum eḻuntu pōy ōriṭattiṟ kūṭṭamāyiruppar.
piṉpu, talimiyāṉavaḷ ōriṭattil taṉimiyāka iruntu koṇṭu, mutalil pōṉa kāmāṭci ōṭivā eṉpāḷ. avaḷ vantavuṭaṉ uṉ kuḻantiyi eṉṉa ceytāy? eṉṟu kēṭpāḷ. avaḷ, eṉ kuḻantiyik kiṇaṟṟil muṟittup pōṭṭuviṭṭuk kuḷattil muḻukiviṭṭu vantēṉ eṉṟu colvāḷ. nī entak kuḷattil muḻukiṉāy? eṉṟu talimi yāṉavaḷ kēṭpāḷ. ataṟku avaḷ, tayirkkuḷattil muḻuki ṉēṉ eṉṟu patilurippāḷ. uṭaṉē, talimiyāṉavaṉ pō pō pō, eṉ vīṭellām veḷḷiyāyp pōy viṭṭatu eṉṟu colli, avaḷit turattiviṭuvāḷ.
piṉpu, iraṇṭāvatu pōṉa kāmāṭci ōṭivā mūṉṟavātu pōṉa kāmāṭci ōṭivā eṉṟu iṅṅaṉam eñciyōruḷ ovvoruttiyiyum muṟiyē aḻittu muṉpōṟ kēṭpāḷ, ovvoru taṭaviyum muṉ coṉṉavāṟē nikaḻum. kuḷattil muḻukiṉatip paṟṟic collumpōtu, iraṇṭāvatu pōṉa kāmāṭci paruppuk kuḷattil muḻukiṉēṉ  eṉpāḷ. aṉṟu, talimiyāṉavaḷ pō pō pō, eṉ vīṭellām mañcaḷāyp pōyviṭṭatu eṉṟu collit turatti viṭuvāḷ. mūṉṟāvatu pōṉa kāmāṭci neyk kuḷattil muḻukiṉēṉ eṉpāḷ. uṭaṉē, talimiyāṉavaḷ pō pō pō, eṉ vīṭellām eṇṇeyāyp pōy viṭṭatu eṉṟu, collit turattiviṭuvāḷ. nālāvatu pōṉa kāmāṭci pavvīk kuḷattil muḻukiṉēṉ  eṉpāḷ. uṭaṉē, talimiyāṉavaḷ pō pō pō,  eṉ vīṭellām pavvīyāyp pōyviṭṭatu eṉṟu collit turattiviṭuvāḷ. iṅṅaṉam oṉpatu peṇkaḷ ēṟkāta ovvoṉṟic collit turattappaṭṭapiṉ, pattāvatu peṇ maṭṭum nāṉ paṉṉīrk kuḷattil muḻukiṉēṉ eṉpāḷ. uṭaṉē talimiyāṉavaḷ vā vā vā eṉṟu colli aviḷac cērttu aṇittuk koḷvāḷ. atōṭu āṭṭam muṭiyum.
talimiyāṉavaḷiṭam ovvoruttiyum ōṭi varum pōtu, ōr iṭakkarāṉa marapuri kūṟik koṇṭu varuvatu vaḻakkam. (ta.nā.vi.)
paṉuval 
parutti nūlukkum aṟivu nūlukkum paṉuval eṉpatu potup peyar.
pañcitaṉ collāy paṉuval iḻiyākac
ceñcol pulavaṉē cēyiḻiyā - eñcāta
kiyēvā yākak katirē matiyāka
miyilā nūṉmuṭiyu māṟu
eṉṟum,
urattiṉ vaḷam perukki uḷḷiya tīmip
purattiṉ vaḷamurukkip pollā - marattiṉ
kaṉakkōṭṭam tīrkkum nūl;  aḵtēpōl māntar
maṉakkōṭṭam tīrkkumnūl māṇpu
eṉṟum uṉṉittuk kūṟiṉār pavaṇantiyār (naṉ. 24,25)
pā eṉpatu neyavup pāviṟkum ceyyuṭpāviṟkum potup peyar.
(āṅkilattilum nūṟporuḷik kuṟittaṟku necavut toḻiliṉiṉṟu Text, yawn  eṉṉum irucoṟkaḷ eṭuttāḷap peṟukiṉṟaṉa (E. Text from L. Texre weave)  nūl maṭiyik kuṟikkum yawn eṉṉum col ōcunar (Sailors) kūṟum katikkup potup peyarākum. ati Sailor’s yawn eṉpar.
pākkiyam
paku - pāku - pākku = pakuti, naṟpakuti, naṟpēṟu pākku + iyam (īṟu) = pākkiyam.
pākku veṭṭiyik kāṇōmē . . . viḷiyāṭṭu
āṭṭiṉ peyar : pākkuveṭṭiyik kāṇōmē eṉṟu collit toṭaṅkum viḷiyāṭṭu, accolliyē peyarākak koṇṭatu:  itu vaṭakoṅku nāṭṭil paruppuc caṭṭi eṉappaṭum.
āṭuvār toki :  potuvāka aṟuvarkkuk kuṟiyāta palar iti āṭuvar.
āṭiṭam:  ūrppoṭṭal iti āṭumiṭamām.
āṭu muṟi:  talimiyāṉa iru petumpiyār1  aṇṇāviyar pōl etiretir niṉṟu koḷvar. avaruḷ oruttiyiṉ piṉṉāl, ēṉic ciṟumiyarellārum orutti ariyāṭiyi iṉṉorutti paṟṟik koṇṭu variciyāy niṟpar. iṉṉorutti, avvaricikku etir niṉṟu maṟukkāṭṭi valamum iṭamum cuṟṟic ceṉṟu, variciyāy niṟkum ciṟumiyaruḷ aṇṇāviyoḻinta piṟaruḷ oruttiyi allatu palarit toṭamuyalvāḷ. avaḷ valañ cellumpōtu iṭamum, iṭañ cellum pōtu valamumāka, variciyāy niṟkuñ ciṟumiyar vaḷintu vaḷintu iyaṅkuvar. toṭappaṭṭa peṇ nīṅkiviṭa vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam (aṇṇāviyoḻinta) ellāp peṇkaḷum toṭappaṭumvari, āṭṭut toṭaralām.
ōr āṭṭi muṭintapiṉ maṟumuṟiyum muṉ pōṉṟē āṭap peṟum.
āṭṭu nikaḻumpōtu, taṉittu niṟpavaḷum varici mutalviyumāṉa aṇṇāviyar iruvarum, piṉ varumāṟu pāṭṭuppāṭi neṭukalum uṟaḻnturippar. pāṭṭu muṭintavuṭaṉ tiruppap paṭum.
pāṇṭināṭṭup pāṭṭu
1.	ta: 	pākkuveṭṭiyik kāṇōmē.
	va:	tēṭi ōṭip piṭittukkoḷ.
2.	ta:	veṟṟilip peṭṭiyik kāṇōmē.
	va:	tēṭi ōṭip piṭittukkoḷ.
3.	ta:	āṭukiṭakkiṟa kiṭiyip pār.
	va:	āṭṭup piḻukkiyit tūrttup pār.
4.	ta:	kuṭṭi kiṭakkiṟa kiṭiyip pār.
	va:	kuṭṭip piḻukkiyit tūrttup pār.
5.	ta:	pallē valikkutē
	va:	nellik koṟittuk koḷ.
koṅkunāṭṭuppāṭṭu
1.	ta:	paruppuc caṭṭi
	va:	tiruppi nakku. 
2.	ta:	vāḻi yili.
	va:	vaḻittu nakku. 2
3.	ta:	ūciyālē kuttuvēṉ.
	va:	vīṭṭumēlē ēṟuvēṉ
4.	ta:	kiṇaṟṟilē kutippēṉ.
	va:	kalleṭuttup pōṭuvēṉ.
5.	ta:	taliyē nōkutē.
	va:	taliyaṇi pōṭṭukkoḷ.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam :- inta āṭṭu nari, āṭṭukkuṭṭikaḷiyō, paruntu kōḻik kuñcukaḷiyō, piṭip patiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiya tākat terikiṉṟatu.
pākam
pākam:  paku- pāku- pākam- pāka (va. bhaga).
pāṭivācal 
callikkaṭṭu māṭṭut toḻuvi iṉṟum pāṭi allatu pāṭivācal eṉṟaḻippatu, ivvaḻakkam paṇṭik kālattil āyarpāṭiyil nikaḻntatu eṉpati uṇarttum.  (ta.ti. 5).
pāṇṭi nāṭṭut tamiḻc ciṟappu
kaṭik kaḻakam kalintu pala nūṟṟāṇṭu kaṭanta piṉpum, pāṇṭiyavaraciṉ talimipōyp paṉṉūṟṟāṇṭu ceṉṟa piṉpum, pāṇṭiyaṉit tamiḻ nāṭaṉeṉṟu tivākaram ciṟappittaṟkum.
nallampar nalla kuṭiyuṭittu cittaṉvāḻ (vu)
illan toṟumūṉ ṟeriyuṭittu - nallaravap
pāṭṭuṭittu cōmaṉ vaḻivanta pāṇṭiyaniṉ
nāṭṭuṭittu nalla tamiḻ.
eṉṉum paḻañ ceyyuṭkum ēṟpa, pāṇṭi nāṭṭut tamiḻ iṉṉum kīḻkkāṇum palavakiyiluñ ciṟantuḷḷatu.
(1) pāṇṭi nāṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ, ciṟappāka nāṭṭuppuṟatti luḷḷavar, tamiḻ valliṉa meyyolikaḷit tiraviṭa āriya moḻikaḷiṟpōl mika valittum eṭuttum olikkātu, paḻiya muṟippaṭiyē palukki (uccarittu) varukiṉṟaṉar. vaṭa coṟkaḷiluḷḷa vallolikaḷum polivolikaḷum avar vāyil nuḻivatilli.
e-ṭu : cākṣi - cākki, jāti - cāti
(2) coṟṟūymi pāṇṭi nāṭṭut tamiḻc ciṟappukkaḷuḷ oṉṟākum.
e-ṭu:	cikicci (va.) - paṇṭuvam (ta.)
		cuttam (va.) - tuppuravu (ta.)
pāṇṭi nāṭṭār cikkiḷ (Cycle)  eṉpati mitivaṇṭi eṉṟum, nāñcil nāṭṭār puṉal (Funnel) eṉpati vittūṟṟi eṉṟum, tūya tamiḻc collāl vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar.
(3) nī, nīr eṉṉum muṉṉilip peyarkaḷiṉ muntiya vaṭivāṉa nīṉ, nīm eṉpavi, teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭilēyē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. nāmam eṉṉum tirumāliya (viṇava)k kuṟip peyariṉ mūlamāṉa irāmam eṉpatum, aṅkuttāṉ  vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. tirumaṇ kāppu irāmavaṇakkam paṟṟi irāmam eṉṟum, kōpāla vaṇakkam paṟṟik kōpālam eṉṟum peyar peṟṟuḷḷatu. kōpālam eṉpatu kōppāḷam eṉac citintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. ituvum teṉpāṇṭi vaḻakkē.
(4) tolkāppiyattiṟ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷapaṭi, paṭiyi nāḻi eṉpatum, arippaṭiyi uri eṉpatum, kāḷiyiyum āviyum viravup peyarāl cāttaṉ cāttiyeṉṟaḻippatum, iṉṟum teṉpāṇṭi vaḻakkāma.
(5) pāṇṭi nāṭṭulakavaḻakkil, vēṟeṅkum vaḻaṅkāta tūya teṉcoṟkaḷum, marapukaḷum (Idioms) vaḻakkāṟukaḷum (Usages), iṇimoḻikaḷum (words inpirs) toṭar moḻikaḷum (Phrases), paḻamoḻikaḷum (Proverbs) ērāḷamāy vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
kāṭikkaṇṇi, kutirivāli mutaliya tavacaṅkaḷum karampip payaṟu, kaṟpayaṟu mutaliya payaṟu vakikaḷum, aṅkut tāṉ viḷikkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
iḷattal, uṇattutal, iḷavaṭṭam, ēttāppu, karaṭṭi, kāṇam, kāyal, kāmputal kiṇṇutal kiḷiyañciṭṭi, kuṭimakaṉ, kuṇṭaṭiyaṉ, kuṇaṭṭutal, kutāvaṭi, kuntakkam, kentaḷippu, cavaṅkal, cavuttal, cillāṉ, ciliyōṭutal, cīyāṉ, cēṭā, takkaṉi, taṭiyam, tavval, tavattutal, tāyamaṭṭam, tikital, tuppuravu, tēri, nalavu collutal, nōṅkutal, paṇṇiyār, pattanaṭi, pataval, parital (ōṭutal), parumpu, paṟamputal, pāṭu paṭutal (payiriṭutal), putuniṟam, pulli, pūṭṭaṉ, poṇṭāṉ, mayili, māṉi, mettutal, vaṭali, vatiyaḻital, vaḷḷitāy (muḻutum), vāḻkkippaṭutal, vāḻvaraci eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷ nelli vaḻakkiṟkuc ciṟappākum, ātalāl, vaḻakki ārāytal vēṇṭum, iṟṟit nāṭṭulaka vaḻakki ārāytal vēṇṭum, iṟṟit talimit tamiḻp pērāciriyar ceṉṉit tamiḻiyum cōḻakoṅku nāṭṭut tamiḻiyumē aṟintatiṉāl, centamiḻip piṟaḻavuṇarn tuḷḷaṉar.

pāṇṭiyar kuṭi kuma


rikkaṇṭattil ki.mu. 10,000 āṇṭu kaṭku muṉ tōṉṟiyatu.  (va.va. 203).
pāṇṭiyaṉ urōma nāṭṭut toṭarpu
pāṇṭiyaṉ akattacu (Augustus- ki.mu.44- ki.pi.14) eṉṉum urōma nāṭṭup pēraracaṉukkut tūtu viṭuttatākavum, atu ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭatākavum, collappaṭukiṉṟatu. ēṟkaṉavē irunāṭukaṭkum iṭiyē irunta vāṇikat toṭar pōṭu tūtāṇmit toṭarpum ēṟpaṭṭataṉ viḷivāka, oru ciṟu urōmak kuṭiyēṟṟam maturiyil amintatākavum, pāṇṭiyaṉ mey kāvaṟ paṭi urōmap porunarik koṇ ṭiruntatākavum, terikiṉṟatu. maturi mānakarkkōṭṭi vāyiliyum, kōvalaṉ kālattil urōmap paṭiñar kāttu niṉṟati,
kaṭimatil vāyil kāvaliṟ ciṟanta
aṭalvāḷ yavaṉark kayirātu pukkāṅku  (cilap. kaca: cācā-e)
eṉpataṉāl aṟiyalām. innilimi ki.pi.ca- ām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari nīṭittatu. ataṉāl, akkālattu urōmak kācukaḷ, pāṇṭi nāṭṭil maṭṭumaṉṟit tamiḻakattiṉ palaviṭaṅkaḷilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.
yavaṉar naṉ kalan tanta taṇkamaḻ tēṟal  (puṟam. rucā: kaa)
eṉpatu, yavaṉar pāṇṭiyar vāṇikat toṭarpik kāṭṭum.
mēli yāriyak kalattuṟic coṟkaḷ
ulakil mutaṉmutal āḻkaṭaliṟ peruṅkalañ celuttiṉavarum, cuṟṟukkaṭalōṭikaḷā (circumnavigators) yiruntavarum tamiḻarē, ataṉāṟ kaṭalum kalattuṟiyum paṟṟiya pala tamiḻc coṟkaḷ, mēliyāriya moḻikaḷiṟ kalantuḷḷaṉa.

pāṇṭi viḷiyāṭṭu
(1) pāṇṭi nāṭṭu muṟi
āṭṭiṉ peyar: pala kaṭṭaṅkaḷuḷḷatāy nilattiṟkīṟappaṭṭa araṅkiṉuḷ vaṭṭeṟintu, ati noṇṭiyaṭittuk kālāl taḷḷiyāṭum āṭṭu, pāṇṭi eṉappaṭum.
ivviḷiyāṭṭu, ēṉiyiru tamiḻanāṭukaḷilum, vaṭṭu eṉṟum cilli eṉṟum cillākku eṉṟum eṟikaruviyāṟ peyar peṟṟiruppatāl, orukāl pāṇṭi eṉpatum eṟi karuvip peyarāy irukkalām.
pāṇṭil eṉṉuñ col vaṭṭam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatāl, ataṉ kaṭikkuṟiyāṉa pāṇṭi eṉpatum apporuḷ paṭalām. vaṭṭu cilli (cillākku) eṉṉum peyarkaḷ, vaṭṭam eṉṉum poruḷiyē moḻip poruṭ kāraṇamākak koṇṭiruppatupōl, pāṇṭi eṉpatum koṇṭirukkalām.
pāṇṭi viḷiyāṭṭiṟku vaṭṭu allatu vaṭṭāṭṭu eṉpatu paḻam peyar. iṉṟum paḻañcēra nāṭākiya maliyāḷattil appeyarē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
āṭuvār toki:  iti iruvar āṭuvar. itu ēṉi vaki kaṭkum okkum.
āṭu karuvi: varicikku mūṉṟu āka irunaṭṭu variciyāka āṟu kaṭṭaṅkoṇṭa oru nīḷcatura araṅkum, āṭakar ovvoru varkkum ariyaṅkulak kaṉamum īraṅkula viṭṭamumuḷḷa oru vaṭṭamāṉa kal allatu ōṭṭāñcalliyum,  iti āṭu karuviyām. araṅku, potuvāka, āṟaṭi nīḷamum nālaṭi akalamum uḷḷa tāyum, mēṟkuṟumpakkattil, camuttiram eṉṉum ōr arivaṭṭaṅ koṇṭatāyum, irukkum. avvari vaṭṭattiṉ ucciyil ciṟupāṉmi amikkappaṭum oru ciṟu vaḷivu, kumpakkuṭam eṉappaṭum. kaṭṭaṅkaṭkut taṭṭukkaḷ eṉṟu peyar. kalliṟku allatu oṭṭāñcallikku vaṭṭu eṉṟu peyar.
āṭiṭam: akaṉṟa teruvum peru muṟṟamum poṭṭalum iti āṭiṭamām. itu ēṉi vakikaṭkum okkum.
āṭumuṟi: uṭaṉpāṭṭiṉpaṭiyō tiruvuḷac cīṭṭiṉpaṭiyē yār munti yāṭuvateṉṟu tuṇintu koṇṭu iruvaruḷ oruvar āṭat toṭaṅkuvar. tiruvuḷaccīṭṭut tīrppu kācu cuṇṭi allatu vaṭṭeṟintu aṟiyappeṟum.
āṭupavar, araṅkiṟku muṉ niṉṟu, iṭavarici mutaṟkaṭṭattil tam vaṭṭiyeṟintu, noṇṭiyaṭittu orēyeṭṭil avvaṭṭi mitittu, atik kālāl muṉpuṟamāka veḷiyē taḷḷi, mīṇṭum noṇṭiyaṭittu orēyeṭṭil ati mitittal vēṇṭum.
eṟiyappaṭṭa vaṭṭu kaṭṭattiṟkappāl viḻiṉum kaṭṭattiṟkuḷ viḻātu kōṭṭiṉmēl viḻiṉum, noṇṭiyaṭittuk kaṭṭattiṟkuḷḷum veḷiyum orēyeṭṭil vaṭṭi mitikkat tavaṟiṉum, kōṭṭiṉmēl mitikkiṉum tūkkiya kāli āṭum pōtu kīḻē ūṉṟiṉum, kālāl veḷiyē taḷḷappaṭṭa vaṭṭu mutaṟkaṭṭattiṉiṉṟu orē yeṭṭil mitikka iyalātavāṟu toliviṟ ceṉṟuviṭiṉum, āṭupavar tavaṟiyavarāvar. ataṉ piṉ aṭuttavar āṭal vēṇṭum. aṭuttavarum tavaṟiṉ muṉṉavar mīṇṭum āṭavēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam māṟi māṟik kaṭipōka āṭuvar.
mutaṟkaṭṭa āṭṭut tavaṟātu muṭiyiṉ, aṭutta iru kaṭṭaṅkaḷilum, piṉpu camuttirattilum, ataṉpiṉ valappuṟa mukkaṭṭaṅkaḷilum, muṟiyē muṉpōl āṭa vēṇṭum. 2ām, 3ām, 5ām, 6ām kaṭṭaṅkaḷil āṭumpōtu, kaṭṭan toṟum mitittu noṇṭiyaṭippatum, oru kaṭṭam iṭiyiṭṭu noṇṭiyaṭippatumāka, iruvēṟu muṟiyuṇṭu. ivaṟṟuḷ piṉṉatē perumpāṉmi. vaṭṭik kālāl taḷḷuvatilum, mitikkak kūṭiya kaṭṭaṅkaṭṭamāyt taḷḷuvatum, orēyaṭiyāy veḷiyē taḷḷuvatum, eṉa irumuṟiyuṇṭu.
eṉṟum iṭavaricik kaṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻiyākavē araṅkiṟkuṭ pukavēṇṭiyiruttaliṉ, iṭavaricik kaṭṭaṅkaḷum camuttiramum muṟiyē kīḻiruntu mēlākavum, valavaricik kaṭṭaṅkaḷ muṟiyē mēliruntu kīḻākavum, āṭappeṟum eṉpati aṟital vēṇṭum.
iruvaricik kaṭṭaṅkaḷum āṭappaṭṭapiṉ, araṅkiṉ muṉ niṉṟu, āyiṉ araṅkup pakkam puṟaṅkāṭṭit talikkumēlāka, camuttirattil viḻumāṟu vaṭṭiyeṟintu, muṉpōl noṇṭi yaṭittuc ceṉṟu mitittuk kālāl muṉpuṟamāka veḷiyē taḷḷi, mīṇṭum mitittal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam iṭiyil oṉṟum, iṟutiyil oṉṟumāka, irutaṭavi camuttirattil vaṭṭeṟintu āṭappaṭum. mutal taṭavi etirnōkkiyum, iraṇṭām taṭavi puṟaṅkāṭṭiyum, vaṭṭeṟivatē vēṟupāṭām.
yārēṉum oruvar iraṇṭāntaṭavi camuttirattil tavaṟātu āṭiyapiṉ, āṭṭi muṭiyum, veṟṟi peṟṟavar, araṅkiṟku veḷiyē muṉpuṟa mūliyoṉṟi yaṭuttu, kutiṅkāli naṭuvāka vūṉṟip peruviral allatu pātattāl oru vaṭṭam varivar. ataṟku uppu eṉṟu peyar. ati varital uppu vittal  eṉappaṭum. ovvoru veṟṟikkum ovvōr uppu muṉṉēr variciyāka vikkappeṟum. munti veṉṟavar araṅkiṉ iṭappuṟa mūliyaṭuttum. pinti veṉṟavar ataṉ valappuṟa mūliyaṭuttum uppuvittal marapu. araṅkiṟku muṉṉāl taḷḷappaṭṭa vaṭṭu, araṅkiṉ mutaṟkaṭṭattiliruntu orē yeṭṭil matikka muṭiyātavāṟu eṭṭattiliruppavaṉ, āṭakar tām vitta uppuvaḻiyāka naṭantēṉum noṇṭiyaṭittōṉum ceṉṟu mitikkalām. itu uppu vittavarukkum ēṟpaṭum vacati. ivvacati uppu mikutikkut takkavāṟu mikum.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam :  orukāl, ivviḷiyāṭṭu, oru noṇṭi vaṇikaṉ palanāṭu kaṭantu arumporuḷ tēṭi vantatik kuṟittatāka irukkalām.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ: oṟṟikkāṟ celavu, kuṟitta iṭattiṟku oru poruḷi eṟital, kālāl oru poruḷik kuṟitta iṭattiṟkut taḷḷutal, piṉpuṟamākak kuṟitta iṭattiṟku oṉṟi yeṟital mutaliya payiṟcikaḷ ivviḷiyāṭṭiṉ payaṉām.
(2) cōḻa koṅku nāṭṭumuṟi
1. oṟṟic cilli
āṭṭiṉ peyar : pāṇṭināṭṭiṟ pāṇṭiyeṉa vaḻaṅkum viḷiyāṭṭu, cōḻa koṅku nāṭukaḷiṟ cilli eṉṟum cillākku eṉṟum, peyar peṟum. oṟṟikkaṭṭi araṅku kīṟi āṭappaṭum cilli oṟṟic cilli eṉappaṭum.
cillikkup pāṇṭi eṉṉum peyarum cilaviṭattu aruki vaḻaṅkum.
āṭuvār toki:  iruvar iti āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi:  nāṉku camakaṭṭaṅkaḷ koṇṭatum, ēṟattāḻa 6 aṭi nīḷamum 2 aṭi akalamum uḷḷatumāṉa, oru nīḷ catura araṅkum, āḷukkoru cilli (cillākku) eṉappaṭum vaṭṭum, itaṟkuriya karuvikaḷām. araṅkiṉ mēṟpakutiyāṉa arivaṭṭat tiṟkuc cōḻa nāṭṭil mali eṉṟum koṅku nāṭṭil karakam eṉṟum peyar. cēlam vaṭṭārattiṟ karakaṅ kīṟāmalum āṭap peṟum.
āṭumuṟi: mutalāvatu mutaṟ kaṭṭattiṟ cilliyeṟintu, noṇṭiyaṭittu akkaṭṭattit tāṇṭi aṭutta kaṭṭaṅkaḷi ovvōr eṭṭil mitittuc ceṉṟu karakattil allatu kaṭicik kaṭṭattiṟku veḷiyē kālūṉṟi, mīṇṭum muṉpōl noṇṭiyaṭittuk kīḻ vantu cilliyi mitittu veḷiyē taḷḷi, ati ōr eṭṭil mitittal vēṇṭum.
iṅṅaṉam piṟa kaṭṭaṅkaḷilum, toṭarntu cilliyeṟintu āṭal vēṇṭum.
mēṟ kaṭṭaṅkaḷil eṟinta cilliyi ovvoru kaṭṭamāyk kīḻē taḷḷik koṇṭu vantu, aṭik kaṭṭattiliruntu veḷiyē taḷḷi orē yeṭṭil mitittal vēṇṭum.
kaṭṭaṅkaḷiṉūṭu cellumpōtum mīḷum pōtum, noṇṭi yaṭikka vēṇṭiyapōtellām, noṇṭiyaṭittē ceṉṟu mīḷavēṇṭum.
eṟiyappaṭṭa cilli kaṭṭattiṟkappāl vīḻiṉum, kaṭṭattiṟkuḷ vīḻātu kōṭṭiṉmēl vīḻiṉum noṇṭiyaṭittuk kaṭṭattiṟ kuḷḷum veḷiyum orē yeṭṭil cilliyi mitikkat tavaṟiṉum, kōṭṭiṉmēl mitikkiṉum, noṇṭiyaṭikkumpōtu tūkkiya kālik kaṭṭattiṟkuḷ ūṉṟiṉum, kālāl veḷiyē taḷḷap paṭṭa cilli mutaṟ kaṭṭattiṉiṉṟu orē yeṭṭil mitikka muṭiyātavāṟu neṭuntoliviṟkuc celliṉum āṭupavar tavaṟiyavarāvar. ataṉpiṉ aṭuttavar āṭal vēṇṭum. aṭuttavarum tavaṟiṉ muṉṉavar mīṇṭum āṭal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam, āṭṭi muṭiyumvari iruvarum māṟimāṟi yāṭuvar.
nālu kaṭṭattilum cilliyeṟintāṭiyapiṉ, akaṅkiyil oru muṟiyum puṟaṅkiyil orumuṟiyum kuttukkimēl oru muṟiyum cilliyi vittuk koṇṭu, karakamvari noṇṭi yaṭittuc ceṉṟu mīḷavēṇṭum. karakattil kālūṉṟik koḷḷalām.
ataṉpiṉ, talimēl orumuṟiyum valappātattiṉ mēl orumuṟiyum cilliyi vittuk koṇṭu, ovvoru kaṭṭattiyum ovvōr eṭṭil mitittuk karakam vari naṭantu ceṉṟu naṭantu mīḷavēṇṭum.
piṉpu, araṅkiṟku muṉ niṉṟu, karakattil allatu mēṟpuṟa veḷiyil cilliyeṟintu, muṉpōl noṇṭiyaṭittuc ceṉṟu ati mitittal vēṇṭum. piṉpu aṅku niṉṟu araṅkiṟku muṉpākac cilliyeṟintu, aṅkiruntu noṇṭiyaṭittu vantu ati mitittal vēṇṭum.
piṉṉar, mīṇṭum orumuṟi karakattil allatu mēṟpuṟa veḷiyil cilliyeṟintu, kaṇṇi mūṭik koṇṭāvatu mēṉōkkik koṇṭāvatu ovvoru kaṭṭattiṟkuḷḷum ovvōr eṭṭuvittu naṭantuceṉṟu, cilliyi mitittal vēṇṭum. ovvōr eṭṭuvikkum pōtum amarēcā  eṉṟu collavēṇṭum. kāl kōṭṭil paṭātu eṭṭu cariyā yiruppiṉ, etiriyār uṭaṉ uṭaṉ rēcu (amarēcu) eṉṟu vaḻi moḻivar;  cariyāyillāviṭiṉ illi eṉpar. ataṉpiṉ etiriyār āṭal vēṇṭum.
amarēcāp pakuti tavaṟātu muṭiyiṉ; etiri yāri yāṉiyā, pūṉiyā? eṉṟu viṉavi, yāṉi eṉṟu colliṉ nimirntu niṉṟu talikku mēlākavum, pūṉi eṉṟu colliṉ kuṉintu irukāṟ kavaṭṭūṭum cilliyi oru kaṭṭattiṟkuḷ eṟiya vēṇṭum. atu ekkaṭṭattiṟkuḷ viḻukiṟatō akkaṭṭattil, mūlikkuṟukkuk kōṭukaḷ kīṟalvēṇṭum. atu paḻam eṉap paṭum. paḻam pōṭṭa kaṭṭattiṟku etiriyār kāl vittal kūṭātu;  eṉṟum atit tāṇṭiyē cella vēṇṭum. āyiṉ, etiriyāriṉ vacatikkāka, ak kaṭṭattiṟku veḷiyē valap puṟattil allatu iṭappuṟattil, kaṭṭatti yoṭṭi ōr ari vaṭṭam amittuk koṭukkappaṭum. ataṟku `yāṉikkāl eṉṟu peyar. iṅṅaṉam ovvōr āṭakarum, tattam paḻak kaṭṭattiṉ pakkamāka etiriyārkku yāṉik kāl amittuk koṭuppar.
orumuṟi paḻam pōṭṭavar maṟumuṟi muntiyāṭuvar. maṟumuṟi paḻattiṟkuc cilliyeṟiyumpōtu, atu paḻak kaṭṭattil viḻuntu viṭiṉ tavaṟām.
oruvarēyō iruvarumō ellāk kaṭṭattilum paḻam pōṭṭapiṉ, araṅku kalikkappaṭṭu mīṇṭum kīṟappaṭum.
II. iraṭṭic cilli
mutalām vaki
āṭṭiṉ peyar : iraṭṭik kaṭṭa araṅku kīṟi āṭappaṭum cilli iraṭṭic cilliyām.
āṭuvār toki: iruvar iti āṭuvar.
āṭu karuvi:  varicikku nāṉkāka iruvaricikku eṭṭuc caturak kaṭṭaṅkaḷum avaṟṟōṭu maliyuṅkoṇṭa, ōr araṅkum;  āḷukkoru cilliyum; iti āṭukaruviyām.
āṭumuṟi: itu perumpālum pāṇṭi nāṭṭu muṟippaṭi āṭappeṟum.
iraṇṭām vaki
āṭu muṟi: itu ēṟattāḻa oṟṟic cilli pōṉṟē āṭap peṟum. oṟṟic cilliyil, ovvoru kaṭṭattilum cilli yeṟintapiṉ, karakam allatu mēṟpuṟa veḷivari noṇṭi yattuc ceṉṟu mīḷa vēṇṭum. itilō, valappuṟak kaṭṭaṅ kaḷiṉ vaḻiyākac ceṉṟu iṭappuṟak kaṭṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa cilliyi mitittut taḷḷa vēṇṭum. valappuṟa uccik kaṭṭattil kālūṉṟik koḷḷalām. itik kuṟittaṟku atil mūlik kuṟukkukkōṭukaḷ kīṟappaṭṭirukkum.
naṭantu cella vēṇṭiya pakutikaḷil, orē camayattil iru kāliyum irupuṟak kaṭṭattilum vittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
mūṉṟām vaki
āṭṭiṉ peyar: itu carikip pāṇṭi, eṉappaṭum.
āṭu muṟi :  ituvum oṟṟic cilli pōṉṟatē. āyiṉ iruvaricik kaṭṭaṅkaḷilum āṭappeṟum. iṭavariciyil cilli yeṟiyumpōtu valavarici vaḻiyākavum, valavariciyiṟ cilli yeṟiyumpōtu iṭavarici vaḻiyākavum cella vēṇṭum. iṭavarici yuccikkaṭṭattil kālūṉṟik koḷḷalām.
naṭantu cella vēṇṭiya pakutikaḷ mēṟkūṟiya iraṇṭām vakiyip pōl āṭappeṟum.
III. vāṉūrtic cilli
āṭṭiṉ peyar:  vāṉūrtipōl araṅku kīṟi āṭappaṭum cilli vāṉūrtic cilliyām. ivi ērōppiḷēṉ (Aeroplane) cilli eṉa āṅkilac coṟkoṇṭē aḻippar.
mēṉāṭṭu vāṉūrti tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku varumuṉṉarē inta āṭṭu āṭappaṭṭiruppiṉ, aṉṟu itaṟku vēṟoru tamiḻp peyar vaḻaṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
āṭuvār toki:  iruvar iti āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi: mēlē kāṭṭappaṭṭapaṭi ōr araṅkum, āḷukkoru cilliyum, iti āṭukaruviyākum.
āṭumuṟi:  mutalāvatu, mutaṟkaṭṭattil cilliyeṟintu noṇṭi yaṭittu akkaṭṭattit tāṇṭi, mēṟkaṭṭaṅkaḷuḷ oṟṟik kaṭṭaṅkaḷilellām ovvōr eṭṭuvittu noṇṭi yaṭittum, iraṭṭik kaṭṭaṅkaḷil kaṭṭattiṟ koṉṟāka orē camayattil irukālum ūṉṟiyum, mēliraṭṭik kaṭṭam vari ceṉṟu, piṉpu aṅkiruntu iṅṅaṉamē mīṇṭuvantu mutaṟ kaṭṭattiluḷḷa cilliyi mitittu muṉpuṟamāka veḷiyē taḷḷi, ati orē eṭṭil noṇṭiyaṭittu mitittal vēṇṭum.
āṉāl, kaṭṭattiṉiṉṟu kutittu mēliraṭṭik kaṭṭattiṟkuk kālūṉṟumpōtu, malinōkki ūṉṟāmal, ataṟkup puṟaṅkāṭṭik kutittu muṉpuṟam nōkki ūṉṟa vēṇṭum.
iṅṅaṉam eṭṭāṅ kaṭṭamvari āṭal vēṇṭum. cilli yirukkiṟa kaṭṭam etuvāyiṉum, mēlē cellum pōtu ati mitittal kūṭātu;  kīḻē varumpōtutāṉ ati mitittal vēṇṭum. ākavē kīḻiraṭṭiyilāyiṉum mēliraṭṭiyilā yiṉum cilli irukkumpōtu, atiṟ piṟacamayampōl iru kālum ūṉṟa muṭiyātu. cilliyuḷḷa kaṭṭatti viṭṭu viṭṭuc cilliyillāta kaṭṭattit tāṉ oṟṟik kaṭṭampōl noṇṭiyaṭittu mitittuc ceṉṟu mīḷa vēṇṭum. mēṟcellum pōtum kīḻvarumpōtum, kaṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ eṇmuṟippaṭiyē ceṉṟu varavēṇṭum. kaṭṭattiluḷḷa cilliyi mitittu muṉpuṟamākat taḷḷumpōtu, kaṭṭaṅkaṭṭamākavum taḷḷalām; orēyaṭiyākavun taḷḷalām.
eṭṭuk kaṭṭaṅkaḷum iṅṅaṉam āṭappaṭṭapiṉ, oṟṟic cilliyiṟpōl, veḷḷikkiyum karuppukkiyum kuttukki yum taliyum kālum, muṟiyē āṭappeṟum. taliyuṅkālum āṭumpōtu, iraṭṭik kaṭṭattil orē camayattil iru kālum vittal vēṇṭum.
ataṉpiṉ, malikkuc cilliyeṟintu noṇṭiyaṭittuc ceṉṟu mitittu, piṉpu aṅkiruntu muṉpuṟamākat taḷḷi avvāṟē vantu mitittal vēṇṭum. ippakuti noṇṭi eṉac ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭum itilum, iraṭṭik kaṭṭattil irukālum ūṉṟa vēṇṭum.
piṉpu, malikku mīṇṭum cilli yeṟintu amarēcar āṭic ceṉṟu mitittal vēṇṭum. iraṭṭik kaṭṭattil orē camayattil irukāl vittal vēṇṭum.
amarēcā muṭintapiṉ, maliyil niṉṟu koṇṭu etiriyāri yāṉiyā, pūṉiyā? eṉṟu kēṭṭu, patilukkēṟpac cilli yeṟintu, noṇṭiyaṭittuvantu ati mitittu, cilli viḻunta kaṭṭattil oru kālāl niṉṟu koṇṭu, paḻam pōṭṭu viṭṭuc cella vēṇṭumā? vantu paḻam pōṭa vēṇṭumā? eṉṟu etiriyāri viṉavi, muṉṉatu kuṟippiṉ pōṭṭu viṭṭu noṇṭiyaṭittu muṉvaravēṇṭum;  piṉṉatu kuṟippiṉ noṇṭiyaṭittu araṅkiṟku muṉpuṟamāka vantapiṉ ceṉṟu pōṭa vēṇṭum; pōṭṭapiṉ āṭṭi muṭiyum.
noṇṭiyilum amarēcāvilum tavira, piṟa pakutikaḷil malikkuc celvatilli.
āṭṭattil tavaṟum vakiyum, ataṉpiṉ nikaḻum ceyalum, piṟa cillikaṭkuk kūṟiyaviyē.
ōr āṭṭiyil veṉṟavar aṭutta āṭṭiyil muntiyāṭuvar.
IV  vaṭṭac cilli
āṭṭiṉ peyar:  vaṭṭamāṉa araṅku kīṟi āṭum cilli vaṭṭac cilliyām.
āṭu karuvi:  cakkaravaṭimāṉa ōr araṅkum āḷukkoru cilliyum iti āṭu karuviyām.
cakkarattiṉ kuṟaṭṭil oru kuṟippiṭṭa vaṭṭat tokiyum, ataṉ ārikaṭkiṭiyil muṟiyē attokikku varici yoḻuṅkākak kīḻppaṭṭa ciṟutokikaḷum, kuṟikkappaṭum.
āṭu muṟi :  mutalāvatu kīḻttokiyuḷḷa kaṭṭattiṟ cilliyeṟintu, noṇṭiyaṭittu ati orēyeṭṭil mitittu veḷiyē taḷḷi, mīṇṭum orēyeṭṭil ati mitittal  vēṇṭum, iṅṅaṉam toṭarntu mēṉmēluyarnta toki yuḷḷa cuṟṟuk kaṭṭaṅkaḷilellām āṭiyapiṉ, naṭukkaṭṭattil āṭal vēṇṭum. naṭukkaṭṭattuḷ eṟinta cilliyi, atu aṅkirukkumpōtum ati veḷiyē taḷḷiya piṉpum, nērē orēyeṭṭil mitittal vēṇṭumēyaṉṟic cuṟṟuk kaṭṭattiṉ vaḻiyāyc ceṉṟu mitittal kūṭātu.
oruvar tavaṟiyapiṉ aṭuttavar āṭal vēṇṭum.
kuṟitta vaṭṭat tokiyi munti yeṭuttavar (atāvatu ellāk kaṭṭaṅkaḷiyum tavaṟātu muntiyāṭiyavar), kelittavarāvar, kelippataṟkup paḻam eṉṟu peyar. ovvoru paḻattiṟkum kālāl ovvōr uppu vikkappaṭum, uppukkaḷ, vaṭṭiyiṉiṉṟu toṭaṅki oṉṟiyoṉṟu toṭṭuk koṇṭu variciyāka irukkum. veḷiyē taḷḷappaṭṭa cilli orēyeṭṭil mitikka muṭiyātavāṟu toliviliruppiṉ, āṭupavar tām vitta uppu varicimēl naṭantu ceṉṟu ati mitikkalām.
V. kālippaṭṭac cilli
āṭṭiṉ peyar :  kālippaṭṭam pōl araṅku kīṟi āṭum cilli kālippaṭṭac cilliyām. 
kāṟṟiṟ paṟakkaviṭum. paṭṭattik kālippaṭṭam eṉpar vaṭārkkāṭṭu māvaṭṭattār.
āṭu karuvi:  mūlikkuṟukkuk kōṭiṭṭa oru caturamum, ati yoṭṭi paṭukkiyāṉa oru ciṟu nīḷ caturamum koṇṭa oru perunīḷcatura araṅkum;  āḷukkoru cilliyum; iti āṭu karuviyām. mūlikkuṟukkuk kōṭiṭṭa caturam nāṉku kaṭṭamāka amiyum mēluḷḷa nīḷacaturam aintāṅ kaṭṭamākum.
āṭumuṟi : mutalāvatu mutaṟkaṭṭattiṟ cilliyeṟintu, ati orēyeṭṭil noṇṭiyaṭittu mitittu veḷiyē taḷḷi, mīṇṭum ati muṉpōl mitittal vēṇṭum. piṉpu mēṟ kaṭṭaṅkaḷilum cilli yeṟintu, eṇ muṟippaṭi kaṭṭaṅ kaṭṭamāy noṇṭiyaṭittuc ceṉṟu mitittu, nēraṭiyākavō kaṭṭaṅkaṭṭamākavō veḷiyē taḷḷi, muṉpōṉṟē noṇṭi yaṭittu vantu mitittal vēṇṭum.
mali vēṇṭuvāruḷ cilar, aintāṅ kaṭṭatti maliyākak koḷvar. aṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷātār, ataṉ mēṟpuṟa veḷiyi aṅṅaṉam payaṉpaṭuttuvar. malikkuc cilli yeṟiyum pōtu etir nōkkiyum, maliyiliruntu cilli yeṟiyumpōtu puṟaṅ kāṭṭit talikku mēlākavum, eṟivatu vaḻakkam.
paḻamāṉavar uppu vippar.
iṅṅaṉam eḷiyamuṟiyil iti āṭuvatu vaṭārkkāṭṭu vaḻakkamām.
cēlam  vaṭṭārattil, mēṟkūṟiya pakutikaḷōṭu veḷḷikki karuppukki kuttukki tali kāl amarēcā eṉpavaṟṟiyum, cērttuk koḷvar.
iṉi, uttiyil niṉṟu cilliyeṟintu, 4-ām allatu 5ām kaṭṭattil viḻiṉ uṭaṉē paḻamāvatum, piṟakaṭṭaṅkaḷil viḻiṉ noṇṭiyaṭittuc ceṉṟu mitittu, uttivari noṇṭi yaṭittut taḷḷik koṇṭu pōyp paḻamāvatum;  cēlam vaṭṭārattil maṟṟoru vakiyāy iti āṭum muṟiyām.
 VI. kiccilli
taṉiyāyirukkum ciṟuvaṉ allatu ciṟumi, kīḻē uṭkārntu oṟṟic cilliyaraṅku ciṟiyatāy varintu, āṭkāṭṭi viralik kālpōṟ pāvittuk kaṭṭaṅ kaṭṭamāy vittuc ceṉṟu, āṭik koḷḷum āṭṭu kiccilliyām. itu cēlam vaṭṭārattil āṭap peṟum.
pāṇar
tamiḻnāṭṭil uḷḷa tamiḻ allatu tirāviṭak kulaṅkaḷuḷ pāṇar kulamum oṉṟu. itu mikap paḻimiyāṉa teṉpatu tolkāppiyattālaṟiyappaṭum.
pāṇar pāṇit toḻilākak koṇṭavar. pāṇ eṉpatu pāṭṭu, paṇ, pāṇ, pāṭṭu, pā eṉpavi ōriṉac coṟkaḷ. cīvaka cintāmaṇiyil pāṇiyāḻ (1500). pāṇvali(2040). pāṇuvaṇṭu (2447) eṉṉum toṭar moḻikaḷil pāṇ eṉṉum col pāṭṭu eṉṉum poruḷil vantuḷḷatu. cilappatikārattum (pa.349) pāṇ-pāṭṭu eṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār kūṟiyuḷḷār.
pāṇaruḷum icippāṇarum yāḻppāṇarum maṇṭip pāṇarumeṉap palarām  eṉṟu (tol. puṟam. 36. uri) nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟuvar. icik karuvikaḷ: tōṟkaruvi, tuḷikkaruvi, narappukkaruvi. kañcakkaruvi. miṭaṟṟuk karuvi eṉa aivakiyāyk kūṟappaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ. miṭaṟu(toṇṭi) eṉpatu iyalpāṉa vāyppāṭṭātaliṉ itaṉi nīkki ēṉiya nāṉkiyumē karuviyeṉak kūṟuvar cilar. in nāṉkaṉuḷ kañcam (veṇkalam) tāḷakkaruvi. itu mutaliṟ karuvipaṟṟi veṇkalattāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatiyum. piṉpu iṉavilakkaṇat tāṟ piṟavaṟṟiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭavaṟṟiyum kuṟikkum. tāḷak karuviyiṉṟiyum tāḷam kākkappaṭumātalāṉum. oru tāḷak karuviyum taṉittu iṉpam tārāmiyāṉum atu mukkiyamāṉa taṉṟu. mēṟkūṟiya pirivāruḷ icippāṇar vāyppāṭakarum, yāḻppāṇar narappukkaruviyiṉarum, maṇṭip pāṇar tōṟ karuviyiṉarumāvar. tuḷikkaruvi iyakkum kuḻaṟpāṇar maṇṭip pāṇaruḷ aṭaṅkuvar. maṇṭi-paṟi. kuḻaloṭu kūṭip paṟiyaṭippatē peruvaḻakku. iṉi, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar pāṇari mūvakiyar eṉṉātu palar eṉṟatāl, ataṉuṭ kuḻaṟpāṇari aṭakkiṉum amiyum. cilappatikārattil.

perum pāṇ- kuḻalar mutalōr  (pa.139) eṉa arumpatavuri kārarum kūṟiyiruppatāl kuḻaṟpāṇarumiruntaṉareṉa aṟika. kuḻalip paṟiyiṉṟit taṉittu vācippatiṉmiyiṉ, kuḻaṟ pāṇar pirittuk kūṟappaṭṭilar.
ākavē, icit toḻil muḻumiyuṅ koṇṭu ikkālattu mēḷakkārarpōla iruntavar pāṇar eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. pāṇcērip paṟkiḷakkumāṟu eṉṉum paṇṭip paḻamoḻi yum itaṉi vaṟpuṟuttum. tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇattiṟ pāṇṭiyaṉ icippulavarākak kūṟappaṭum pāṇapattirarum. tiruñāṉacampantar tēvārap patikaṅkaṭku yāḻ vācitta tirunīṇṭa yāḻppāṇarum. paṉṉīrāḻvāruḷ oruvarum yāḻaṟiñarumāṉa tiruppāṇāḻ vārum pāṇar kulattavarē, pāṇaruḷ, āṭavaṉ (puruṣaṉ) pāṇaṉ eṉṟum, peṇṭu pāṭiṉi, pāṇicci, pāṭṭi, viṟali eṉṟuṅ kūṟappaṭuvar.
vēḷāḷar kulam vēḷāṇ eṉṟum, camaṇar neṟi camaṇ eṉṟum kūṟappaṭutal pōla, pāṇar kulamum pāṇ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭuvatuṇṭu. puṟapporuḷ veṇpā māliyil.
