iruṅkaṭa luṭuttaviv viyakat taruntiṟaṟ
poṉpaṭu mālvarik kiḻava  (puṟam. 201).
eṉpaṉa, cālōmōṉ kappalkaḷ poṉ ēṟṟic ceṉṟa upparā (Ophir) eṉṉum mēlkarit tuṟimukattiṟku, aṭut tuḷḷa koṇkāṉattilum koṅkilum poṉ viḷintami kūṟutal kāṇka.
pāratak kālattil āriyap pūcārikaḷ vaṭintiyā muḻutum paraviyiruttal vēṇṭum. ataṉpiṉ annilappakutikku āriyā varttam eṉṉum peyaruṇṭāyiṟṟu. vaṅka nāṭṭiyaṭintu kāḷik kōṭṭattik kaṇṭapiṉ, kāḷivaṇakkatti mēṟ koṇṭu, kāḷi kōyiṟ pūcāriyaru māyiṉar.
pāri
pāri: para - pār - pāri. pārittal = parapputal, virittal. pāri - pari (va. sphar).
pāri kōṭu viḷiyāṭṭu
I. nālāḷam pāri
āṭukaruvi : ēṟattāḻa nāṟkacac caturamāṉa or araṅku kīṟappaṭum.
āṭuvār toki : iti āṭa eṇmar vēṇṭum.
āṭiṭam : itu poṭṭalilum akaṉṟa muṟṟattilum āṭap peṟum.
āṭumuṟi : nannāṉku pēruḷḷa irukaṭci amikkappaṭum. uṭaṉpāṭṭiṉpaṭiyō, tiruvuḷaccīṭṭiṉ paṭiyō, piṟavakit tērtaṟpaṭiyō, oru kaṭciyār araṅkiṟkuḷ niṟka, iṉṉoru kaṭciyār pakkattiṟkoruvarākak kōṭṭiṉmēl niṉṟukoḷ var. uḷniṟpār kōṭṭiṉmēl niṟpārāl toṭappaṭāmal veḷiyēṟa vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam oruvaṉ veḷiyēṟiviṭiṉum, uḷ niṟpārkku veṟṟiyāy āṭṭi muṭintuviṭum. mutalil veḷiyēṟupavaṉ kōṭṭiṉmēl niṟpāruḷ oruvaṉāl toṭappaṭṭuliṭiṉ, maṟippārkku (atāvatu kōṭṭiṉ mēl niṟpārkku) veṟṟiyāy āṭṭi muṭiyum. ataṉpiṉ, maṟippār uḷniṟpārākavum uḷniṟpār maṟippārākavum māṟavēṇṭum.
II. eṭṭāḷam pāri
itu eṇ kacac caturaṅ kīṟip patiṉāṟuvarāl āṭappaṭum. eṇmar uḷniṟka, eṇmar pakkattiṟ kiruvarākak kōṭṭiṉmēl niṉṟu maṟippar. nālāḷam pāriyum eṭṭāḷam pāriyum āṭumuṟi yoṉṟē.
pārikkōṭu (allatu pārikōṭu) eṉṉum viḷiyāṭṭu, pāriyiṉ paṟampumaliyi mūvēntarum muṟṟukiyiṭṭirunta tik kuṟikkalām.
pāl
paku - pakal - pāl = pakuti.
nūṟperum pakuti.  (ti.ma. 59).
pāliyal terippu
maṟañ ciṟanta āṭavariyē peṇṭir maṇakkum ēṟpāṭṭi, pāliyal terippu (sexual selection) eṉpar ṭārviṉ pēraṟiñar.  (ta.ti. 5).
pāvi
pāvam (va) ceytavaṉ pāvi (va)
karicu(pāvam), taricaṉ(pāvi) - āṇpāl
karici(pāvi) - peṇpāl eṉpaṉa teṉcoṟkaḷ.  (ti.ma. 1042).
pāviṉam
vaṭamoḻikkum teṉmoḻikkumuḷḷa iyipupaṟṟiya tiripuṇarccikaḷuḷ, piṟkālat tamiḻnūlkaḷiṟ peruvaḻakkāṉa pāviṉaṅkaḷ vaṭamoḻi yāppu vaḻippaṭṭaṉa veṉpatu moṉṟām. aḵtu ettuṇi uṇmi?
tuṟi tāḻici viruttameṉṉum mūvakip pāviṉaṅkaḷuḷ virutta moṉṟē vaṭamoḻip peyarāl vaḻaṅkuvatākum. aḵtūum peyarāṉ maṭṭum vaṭamoḻiyē yaṉṟi yāppā ṉaṉṟu. mūvakip pāviṉaṅkaḷuḷ tuṟi tāḻici yeṉṉu miraṇṭun tamiḻāyirukka, ēṉiyoṉṟu maṭṭum vaṭamoḻiyā yiruttal eṅṅaṉam? ēṉi moḻikaṭkellāmillāta paranta yāppilakkaṇam tamiḻilirukkavum, vaṭamoḻiyāppi vēṇṭiṟṟeṉṟal vintiyilum vintiyē, veṇpā mutaliya ceyyuḷilakkaṇamum iṉṉōraṉṉa piṟavum vaṭamoḻiyiṟ peṟappaṭāmiyāṉum eṉṟār civañāṉa muṉivararum. āṅkila yāppup palavakip pāk kūṟumēṉum tamiḻpōla attuṇip parantupaṭṭataṉṟu.
oru moḻiyāṉatu kālañ cellac cella, avvak kālattu makkaḷ iyalpiṟkum aṟiviṟku mēṟṟavāṟu ilakkiyattiṉum ilakkaṇattiṉum tirital iyalpē. aṅṅaṉam tamiḻ yāppum caṅka kālattiṟ pāvāyiruntu piṟkālattil pāviṉamākat tirintatu.
ellāp pāviṉaṅkaḷum kalippāviṉiṉṟē tōṉṟiyatākum. veṇpā, āciriyappā eṉṉu miraṇṭum perumpālum varam piṟavātaṉa. vañcippāvum maruṭpāvum kalappup pākkaḷēṉum, aviyum avaṟṟuk kōtiyavāṟu oruvaki varampupaṭṭaṉavē. āṉāl, kalippāvō oru kaṭṭiṉṟi ellāvaṭiyāṉum ellā vōciyāṉum paṟpala vuṟuppup peṟṟu varampikantu varuva tākum. ataṉuḷḷum koccakak kaliyō ēṉik kalikaṭkuriya cīrum elliyum ikantu,
taraviṉ ṟākit tāḻici peṟṟum
tāḻici yiṉṟit taravuṭit tākiyum
eṇṇiṭi yiṭṭuc ciṉṉaṅ kuṉṟiyum
aṭakkiyal iṉṟi aṭinimirn toḻukiyum
yāppiṉum poruḷiṉum vēṟṟumi uṭiyatu
āyum,
taravum pōkkum pāṭṭiṭi miṭintum
aiñcīr aṭukkiyum āṟumey peṟṟum
veṇpā viyalāṉ veḷippaṭat tōṉṟu  (tol.col.148)
vatāyum paṟpalavāṟṟāṉ mikkuṅ kuṟintun tirintum varuva tākum. koccakakkaliyuḷ oruvakiyē paripāṭaleṉka.
iṟṟit tamiḻnūlkaṭkellām muṉṉūlākiya tol kāppi yattil pāviṉam kūṟappaṭāviṭiṉum, avaṟṟiṉ tōṟṟattiṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa iyalkaḷi āṅkāṅkuk kāṇalām.
tuṟi : tuṟiyeṉpatu tolkāppiyattuḷ,
vaḻakkiyal maruṅkiṉ vakipaṭa niliip
paravalum pukaḻcciyum karutiya pāṅkiṉum
muṉṉōr kūṟiya kuṟippiṉum centuṟi
vaṇṇap pakuti variviṉ ṟāṅkē
eṉa oruvakip pāṭāṇ ceyyuṭkup peyarāka vantuḷḷatu. atu centuṟip pāṭāṇ pāṭṭeṉappaṭum. atu kaṭavuḷ vāḻttuppaṟṟi varum oruvakik kalippā. vaṇṇa meṉpatē kalippāviyuṅ kaṭavuḷ vāḻttiyu muṇarttum.
mūvā mutalā eṉṉum cīvakacintāmaṇik kaṭavuḷ vāḻttuc ceyyuḷi, muṉṉōr kūṟiya kuṟippiṉkaṇ vanta centuṟip pāṭāṇ pakuti yeṉṟaṉar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
pāṭāṇṭiṇi teyvam parāval, makkaḷēttal eṉṉum iru pāṟpaṭṭu varum. teyvam parāval perumpālum kalippā vālēyē varum. teyvattiṉ pala kuṇaṅkaḷiyum tiruviḷi yāṭalkaḷiyum vaṭivukaḷiyum vaṇṇittup pukaḻtaṟkuk kaliyuṟuppukaḷ pōlap piṟa pākkaḷ ciṟavāmi kāṇka.
ottāḻicikkali iruvakiyeṉṟu kūṟi avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu,
tēvarp parāaya muṉṉilik kaṇṇē  (tol.col.133).
eṉṟār tolkāppiyar.
cintāmaṇiyiṉuñ cilappatikārattum teyvam parāvuñ ceyyuḷ kaḷellām orupōkum koccakamumākak kalippākkaḷākavē vantaṉa. ivvaḻakkup paṟṟiyē teyvam parāvum coccakaṅkaḷ tēvapāṇi, perun tēvapāṇi yeṉappaṭuvaṉa.
kalampakattiṟ kaṭavuḷ vāḻttuk koccaka orupōkāṟ kūṟappaṭuvatuṅ kāṇka. arākam, ampōtaraṅkam mutaliya kaliyuṟuppukaḷ kaṭavuḷ vāḻttiṟkē ciṟappāyuriyaṉa.
iṉi, tuṟiyeṉpatu puṟattiṇiyiyalil tiṇip pirivākavuṅ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
ataṉuriyil,
makkaḷum māvum mutaliyaṉa ceṉṟu nīruṇṇun tuṟipōlap palavakippaṭṭa poruḷum oruvakippaṭṭu iyaṅkutalāku mārkka mātaliṟ ṟuṟiyeṉṟār, ellāvaḻiyu meṉpataṉi ellāt tuṟiyuṅ kāval pōṟṟiṉār eṉpavākaliṉ. eṉavē, tiṇiyun tuṟiyuṅ koṇṭārāyiṟṟu. akattiṇikkut tuṟi yuṭpakutikaḷellām virittuk kūṟip piṉṉum paṉmuṟi yāṟ parantupaṭṭu varampikantaṉavaṟṟiyun tokuttut tuṟip paṭuttuk kūṟuka eṉṟaṟkuc ceyyuḷiyaluḷ tuṟiyeṉpatu uṟup pākak kūṟiṉār. puṟattiṇikku aṅṅaṉam parantu virittōtātu tokuttu ilakkaṇañ ceytārāyiṉum aviyum avvāṟē pala poruṭpakutiyum uṭiyaveṉṟum uṇarttuvataṟkut tuṟi yeṉap peyarākak koṭuttār. itaṉāṉē akapporuṭ pakuti palavāyiṉum, oru ceyyuṭ pala poruḷ virāay variṉum oru tuṟiyāyiṉāṟpōlap puṟattiṇikkum avvap poruṭ pakutiyum oru tuṟiyātalum oru ceyyuḷuṭ pala tuṟi oruṅku vantum oru tuṟip paṭutaluṅ koḷka
avvam mākkaḷum vilaṅku maṉṟip
piṟavavaṇ variṉum tiṟavatiṉ nāṭit
tattam iyalāṉ marapoṭu muṭiyiṉ
attiṟam tāṉē tuṟiyeṉap paṭumē  (tol.cey. 200)
eṉpatu ceyyuḷiyal. itaṉāl tuṟiyeṉpatu potuvāyt tiṇip pirivum ciṟappāy akattiṇippirivu meṉpatu peṟṟōm.
akattiṇikkuc ciṟanta kōvi nūlkaḷuḷ akapporuṭ ṭuṟikaḷellām kalippāvālēyē kūṟappaṭuvaṉa. itaṉāṟ kalittuṟiyeṉap peyar peṟṟatu. kaliyeṉpatu ceyyuḷiyum tuṟiyeṉpatu akapporuṭ piriviyu muṇarttum. kalippā vāṟ kūṟappaṭum akapporuṭṭuṟiyeṉpatu virinta poruḷ.
kalippāvāl akapporuḷ kūṟappaṭutali.
yāppiṉum poruḷiṉum vēṟṟumi yuṭiyatu
eṉpataṟkut tēvapāṇiyum kāmamumēyaṉṟi vīṭum poruḷā meṉpatu āciriyar karuttāyiṟṟu eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟuvatāṉum.
kalittokiyāṉum, kaliyiṉ tiripākiya paripāṭal.
koccakam arākam curitakam eruttoṭu
ceppiya nāṉkum taṉakkuṟup pākak
kāmaṅ kaṇṇiya nilimit tākum
eṉappaṭutalāṉum,
talivaṉun taliviyum uṟaḻntu kūṟum uṟaḻkaliyāṉum aṟintu koḷka.
ākavē, tuṟiyeṉpataṟkuriya centuṟip pāṭāṇ pakuti, akapporuṭṭuṟi eṉṉum iruporuḷiṉum perum pāṉmi vaḻaṅkuñ ceyyuḷ kaliyēyeṉṟum, akapporuḷil varum kōvic ceyyuḷ aḷavotta neṭilaṭi nāṉkeṉṟum aṟintukoḷka.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar centuṟip pāṭāṇ pakutiyākak kūṟiya cintāmaṇik kaṭavuḷ vāḻttuc ceyyuḷum neṭilaṭi nāṉkākavē nikaḻvatu kāṇka.
tāḻici
iṉit tāḻici yeṉpatu kalippāvuṟuppeṉpatu veḷḷiṭi. atu tāḻampaṭṭa ōciyāy varutalāṟ ṟāḻiciyeṉappaṭṭatu. atu taravakappaṭṭa marapiṉatāy oru poruṇmēl mūṉṟaṭukki varum. koccakak kaliyāyiṉ taṉittum, palavāyum, piṟavuṟup piṉṟiyum varum. mūṉṟu tāḻici ottuvariṉ ottāḻicik kaliyām. ottāḻiciyi o+tāḻici yeṉṟu pirittu ot tāḻiciyeṉak kūṟātu. ottu + āḻici eṉṟu pirittu ottu āḻnta ōciyeṉṟār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
oru toṭarpupaṭṭa poruḷik kūṟumiṭattu, otta niyāyaṅ kaḷiyum nikaḻccikaḷiyuṅ kūṟutaṟkut tāḻici pōlac ciṟanta uṟuppup piṟitilatu.
viruttam
iṉi virutta meṉpatu yātōveṉiṉ atu oruvaki yāppām. atu tamiḻil maṇṭila meṉappaṭum.
ottā ḻiciyum maṇṭila yāppum
kuṭṭamum nēraṭik koṭṭiṉa veṉpa
eṉṉuñ cūttiravuriyil,
nāṟcīraṭi muraṟkipaṭat tuḷḷi varutalē yaṉṟit tāḻampaṭṭa vōci peṟṟum varumeṉṟār. iṉi nāṟcīraṭiyāy varumāciri yamum veṇpāvum īṟṟayalaṭiyum īṟṟaṭiyum muccīrāy varumeṉa muṉvitittavi orukāl nāṟcīrāyuma varumeṉṟaṟku maṇṭila yāppeṉṟār. atu maṇṭila vāciriyappāvum maṇṭila veṇpāvumām.
vēral vēli vērkkōṭ palaviṉ
cāra ṉāṭa cevviyi yākumati
yāraḵ taṟintici ṉōrē cāraṟ
ciṟukōṭṭup perumpaḻan tūṅkiyā ṅkiva
ḷuyirtavac ciṟitu kāmamō peritē  (kuṟun. 18)
eṉavum,
aṟiyaruvi yāṭāḷ tiṉippuṉamuṅ kāvāḷ
poṟiyuyar taṇcilampiṟ pūntaḻiyuṅ koyyā
ḷuṟikavuḷ vēḻamoṉ ṟuṇṭeṉṟā vaṉṉi
maṟiyaṟanīr vāḻiya miyiruṅ kuṉṟu
eṉavum maṇṭalittu vantaṉa eṉṟār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. maṇṭalittil vaṭṭamātal, atu maṇṭalam eṉṉum tokic colliṟ piṟanta viṉi. maṇṭalam maṇṭilameṉa maruviyum varum.
maṇṭalippāmpu, curamaṇṭilam, tiṅkaṇmaṇṭilam, maṇṭilac celavu (kutiriccāri) mutaliyaṉa vaṭṭameṉṉum vaṭivup poruḷil vantaṉa.
maṇṭalam, vaṭṭameṉṉum coṟkaḷ circle eṉṉum āṅkilac coṟpōla iṭappakutikaḷiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
jayaṅkoṇṭa cōḻamaṇṭalam
inta vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ avaṟketirilli
vaṭṭameṉpatu vaṭṭaki vaṭṭāra meṉṟum tiriyum. maruttuva muṟiyil nāṟpatteṇṇāṭ pakuti oru maṇṭala meṉappaṭum. kālamumiṭattō ṭokkum.
iṉi maṇṭalam, vaṭṭameṉṉum coṟkaḷ muḻumip poru ḷiṉum vaḻaṅkum. tiṅkaḷ vaṭṭamāyirukkuṅkāl patiṉāṟu kalikaḷum nirampiyiruttal kāṇka. āṅkilattum round eṉṉum col Whole, complete eṉap poruḷpaṭum. ulaka vaḻakkil muḻumip poruḷil vaḷḷitu eṉṟār col vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. atu vaṭṭam eṉṉum colpōla vaḷ eṉṉum pakutiyiṉiṉṟu piṟantatākum. vaḷḷitāyc cela vāyiṟṟu eṉṟāl muḻutum celavāyiṟṟeṉpatu poruḷ. vaḷḷicāy eṉpatu koccip pōli.
maṇṭalam eṉṉun tokiccol, maṇ + tal eṉa viriyum. paṇṭit tamiḻar kaṭaṟcelavil tērntiruntārātaliṉ. ulakeṅkum kalattiṟ ceṉṟu nilam vaṭṭamāyiruttali naṉkaṟintiruntaṉar. paṇṭiyulakatti ēḻ tīvākap pakuttuk kūṟiṉatum iti vaṟpuṟuttum. tīvu - kaṇṭam. vaṭavik kaṉali (Auora Borealis) aṟintiruntatum  maṟṟōrātāram. ilattīṉ (Latin), āṅkilam mutaliya moḻikaḷiṟ kaṭaṟṟuṟi paṟṟiya coṟkaḷ pala centamiḻāyiruppatum itaṟkuc ciṟantatōr cāṉṟām.
nāvāy	-	L.navis, E. navy = kaṭaṟpaṭi
kalam	-	E. gallecon
vāraṇam	-	L. marina. Skt. varuṇā
kari	-	E. shore. sh = ka
paṭaku		-	E. bark, r = d. c.f. Coorg = kuṭaku
kaṭṭumarama	-	catamaran
cōḻi		-	shell
naṅkūram	-	anchor
kappal	-	ship
itu niṟka. iṉi nāṟcīrāl nirampivarum aḷavaṭiyi maṇṭila yāppeṉṟār.
nāṟcīr koṇṭa taṭiyeṉap paṭumē  (tol. poruḷ. 344).
eṉpatu cūttiram. itaṉāl iyaṟcīrāl tuḷḷalicipaṟṟi aḷavaṭi nāṉkāyt taṉittu varuṅ koccakak kaliyuṟuppuk kali maṇṭilamāyiṟṟu. maṇṭilam piṟkālattil virutta meṉṉum vaṭamoḻip pariyāyap peyarāl vaḻaṅkalāyiṟṟu. ākavē tuṟi, tāḻici, virutta meṉṉum mūvakip pāviṉamum kalippāvāṉami peṟappaṭṭatu. ivaṟṟip piṟkālattār potuvākak koṇṭu, varampu kaṭantu varūum koccakak kalikaḷiyellām orupuṭi yoppumipaṟṟi ovvōr pāviṟkum mummūṉ ṟiṉamākap pakutturittār.
kalippāp palavaki yaṭikaḷālum uṟuppuk kuṟintum mikkum varumeṉpatu muṉṉarē kūṟappaṭṭatu. atiṉuṅ koccakak kaliyō kalippāviṟ kiṉṟiyamiyāta tuḷḷaliciyuṅ keṭṭu varuvatākum. kalittal tuḷḷal.
iraṇṭaṭi aḷavottuc centuṟip pāṭāṇ pāṭṭāy varuvati aṭittokipaṟṟik kuṟaṭkiṉamākki veṇ cen tuṟi yeṉṟaṉar.
e-ṭu:
ātti cūṭi yamarnta tēvaṉi
ētti yēttit toḻuvōm yāmē.
3 aṭimutal 7 aṭivari muṉnīṇṭu piṉkuṟukivarum centuṟip pāṭāṇ pāṭṭi īṟu kuṟitalpaṟṟi veṇpāviṟ kiṉamākki veṇṭuṟi eṉṟaṉar.
4 aṭiyāy eruttaṭi nintum, kuṭṭam paṭṭum, iṭi maṭakkiyum āciriya viyalāṉ varum centuṟip pāṭāṇ pāṭṭi āciriyat tuṟiyeṉṟaṉar. iṭimaṭakkal ammāṉik kiyalpeṉka.
kōviyil varum kalittuṟic ceyyuḷi eḻutteṇṇik kaṭṭaḷik kalittuṟi yeṉṟaṉar.
kuṟaḷaṭi nāṉkāy varum centuṟip pāṭāṇpāṭṭi aṭivaki paṟṟi vañcittuṟi yeṉṟaṉar.
kalippāvil ampōtaraṅka vuṟuppuc cintaṭi kuṟaḷaṭikaḷum peṟṟuvarum.
iṉit tāḻicikkuc ciṟappilakkaṇam, tāḻampaṭṭa ōciyāy aḷavottu mūṉṟu aṭukki varutal. iraṇṭaṭiyāy iṟutiyaṭi kuṟintu varuvatik kuṟaṭṭāḻici yeṉṟum. mūṉṟaṭiyāy īṟṟaṭi cintaṭiyāy varuvati veṇṭāḻici yeṉṟum, mūṉṟu nēraṭiyāy aḷavottu maṇṭila vāciriyampōl varuvati āciriyattāḻici yeṉṟum, kuṟaḷaṭi nāṉkāy varuvati vañcittāḻici yeṉṟuṅ kūṟiṉar. iviyellām oru poruṇmēl mūṉṟaṭukkiyum taṉittum varuvaṉavām.
iṉi, viruttattiṟkuc ciṟappilakkaṇam aḷavotta nālaṭiyāy maṇṭalittu varutal : atāvatu nirampi varutal.
3 aṭiyāyum 4 aṭiyāyum maṇṭalittu veṇpāviyaliṟ ṟaṉic coṟpeṟṟu varuvati veḷivirutta meṉṟum, kaliviruttamum, kalittuṟiyum aḷavaṭiyāyum neṭilaṭiyāyum varutaliṉ avaṟṟukku mēl kaḻineṭilaṭiyāy āciriyat taḷitaṭṭu varuvati āciriya virutta meṉṟum, vañcippāviṟku nirampiṉa aṭi cintaṭi yātaliṉ cintaṭiyāy varuvati vañcivirutta meṉṟuṅ kūṟiṉar.
kalippāvil arāka vuṟuppuk kaḻineṭilaṭiyāṉum varappeṟum.
aṟucī raṭiyē āciriyat taḷiyoṭu
neṟipeṟṟu varūum nēraṭi muṉṉē
eḻucī raṭiyē muṭukiyal naṭakkum
eṉpavi cūttiram. āciriya viruttam piṟkālattil piṟataḷiyum taṭṭu vantatu.
viruttaṅkaṭkellām nālaṭi yaḷavottiruttal potuvilak kaṇamām. aṭikaḷ palavakippaṭumēṉum nāṟcīraṭi aḷavāyi ṉāṟpōla, pākkaḷ paṟpala aṭittoki peṟumēṉum nālaṭic ceyyuḷ aḷavāyiṟṟu. mōṉi etuki mutaliya toṭikaṭkum iciniṟiviṟkum aṭiyuḷ nāṟcīraṭi ciṟattal pōlac ceyyuḷil nālaṭic ceyyuḷ ciṟappatākum. itaṉāṉē veṇpā palavaṭiyāy varumēṉum nālaṭi veṇpā peruvaḻak kāyiṟṟu. piṟkālattuc ceyyuḷkaḷellām perumpālum nāṭiyālēyē naṭappaviyāyiṉa. nāṟcīrāl aṭinirampi maṇṭilamāyiṉāṟpōla, nālaṭiyālum ceyyuḷ nirampi oruvaki maṇṭilamāyiṟṟeṉka.
viruttaṅkaḷil, 26 vari eḻuttuppeṟṟu varupavaṟṟi virutta meṉṟum, ataṟkumēl tāṇṭi varupavaṟṟit tāṇṭakameṉ ṟum. ivi cantamākavariṉ cantaviruttam, cantat tāṇṭakameṉ ṟuṅ kūṟiṉar.
iṅṅaṉa mellām oru pāviṟkuriya palavuṟuppukaḷip piṟa pākkaṭ kiṉamākkiyatāl, ōriṉam piṟaviṉamāyum, oru ceyyuḷ pala pāviṟkiṉamāyum varutaṟkēṟṟu, iṉṉa ceyyuḷeṉ ṟoṉṟit tuṇiyātu mayaṅkutaṟ kiṭaṉāyiṟṟu.
mūvā mutalā vulakamoru mūṉṟu mēttat
tāvāta viṉpan taliyāyatu taṉṉi ṉeyti
yōvātu niṉṟakuṇat toṇṇitic celva ṉeṉpa
tēvāti tēvaṉavaṉ cēvaṭi cērtu maṉṟē  (cīvaka. 1)
eṉṉum cintāmaṇik kaṭavuḷ vāḻttuc ceyyuḷuriyil, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
ic toṭarnilic ceyyuḷ tēvar ceykiṉṟa kālattiṟku nūl akattiyamum tolkāppiyamumālāṉum... akattiyattiṉ vaḻinūl tolkāppiya mātalāṉum, piṟar kūṟiya nūlkaḷ nirampiya ilakkaṇattaṉa aṉmiyāṉum. annūliṟ kūṟiya vilakkaṇamē itaṟkilakkaṇa meṉṟuṇarka.
icceyyuḷ muṉṉōr kūṟiya kuṟippiṉkaṇ vanta centuṟip pāṭāṇ pakutiyām.
iṉi, it toṭarnilic ceyyuḷi iṉameṉpa. an nūlkaḷ iṉameṉṟu kāṭṭiya vutāraṇaṅkaṭām avar cērtta avvap pākkaṭkē iṉamākātu. oḻinta pākkaṭkum iṉamātaṟku ēṟṟalāṉum. tuṟiyi viruttamākavum. tāḻiciyi viruttamu mākavum, ōtutaṟku aviyēṟṟamiyāṉum, mūvā mutalā eṉṉuṅ kavi mutaliyaṉa tāḻampaṭṭa ōciyāṉ virutta mākavum. cīr variyaṟiyāṉum mikat tuḷḷiya vōciyāṉum tuṟi yāyuṅ kiṭattaliṉ, itaṉi viruttak kalittuṟi yeṉṉal vēṇṭum. atu kūṟavē tuṟiyum viruttameṉap pakuttōtiya ilakkaṇam nirampātāmākalāṉum iṉameṉṟal porutta miṉṟu. ic ceyyuḷkaḷiṉ ōci vēṟṟumiyum mikkuṅ kuṟintum varuvaṉavum kalikkē yēṟṟaliṟ koccaka meṉ ṟaṭaṅkiṉa eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷār.
iṅṅaṉamē kaliviruttam tuḷḷaliciyāṟ kaliyiṉamāyum. cīrvaki yāṉum aṭivakiyāṉum, nilimaṇṭala vāciriya māyum, nālacic cīrkoḷḷiṉ, kuṟaḷaṭi vañcippāvāyum kūṟutaṟ kēṟṟuvarutalum, kaṭṭaḷik kalippāt tuḷḷaliciyāṟ  kalippāvāyum cīrvakiyāṉum aṭivakiyāṉum āciriya viruttamāyuṅ kūṟutaṟ kēṟṟuvarutalum kaṇṭukoḷka.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, pāviṉa mūṉṟun tamiḻ yāppē yeṉṟum, avi koccakakkaliyiṉ tiripeṉṟum, piṉṉūlār avaṟṟip paṉṉiru pāviṉamākap pakuttuk kāṭṭiṉareṉṟum, cāli, paṇṇi mutaliya tamiḻc coṟkaḷ maṟintu rōṭu (iṅkilīṣ), jamīṉtār (intutāṉi) mutaliya ayaṟcoṟkaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉāṟpōla, maṇṭilameṉṉum teṉcol maṟintu viruttameṉṉum vaṭacol vaḻaṅkiṟṟeṉṟum, ilakkaṇam nirampiya pāviyaṟṟum arumi nōkkic caṅkakālattiṟkup piṉṉōr eḷiya yāppāṉa iṉaṅkaḷi yiyaṟṟiṉareṉṟum, avaṟṟuṭ ciṟappuppaṟṟi maṇṭilayāppup peruvaḻakkāyiṟ ṟeṉṟum teḷḷitiṉ aṟintukoḷka.
vaṭamoḻi viruttam kuṟaḷaṭiyāṉum varumēṉum, virutta mallatu vēṟiyāppu āṇṭiṉmiyāṉum, avi perum pālum tamiḻōṭoppumi yuṭimiyāṉum, piṟṟit tamiḻ yāppil viruttamē peruvaḻak kāṉamiyāṉum, tamiḻiyaṟ kellām takātu vaṭanūlvaḻi kaṟpikkumāṟu attuṇi iyipu av virumoḻikkum āṉapiṉṉar maṇṭila meṉṉum peyar maṟintu viruttameṉṉum peyar vērūṉṟiyateṉka.  (centamiḻc celvi maṭaṅkal 1933.)
pāvi eṉṉuñ col varalāṟu
tamiḻiluḷḷa iḷamippeyarkaḷuḷ pārppu eṉpatu oṉṟākum.
māṟṟaruñ ciṟappiṉ marapiyal kiḷappiṉ
pārppum paṟaḻum kuṭṭiyum kuruḷiyum
kaṉṟum piḷḷiyum makavum maṟiyum eṉ(ṟu)
oṉpatum kuḻaviyō ṭiḷamip peyarē.  (1)
avaṟṟuḷ,
pārppum piḷḷiyum paṟappavaṟ ṟiḷami,  (4)
tavaḻpavi tāmum avaṟṟō raṉṉa,  (5)
eṉpaṉa tolkāppiya marapiyal nūṟpākkaḷ.
paṟavik kuñcum cila ūruyirikaḷiṉ (reptiles) iḷamiyum pārppeṉappaṭum eṉpatu ivaṟṟāl teriyavarum. cila vilaṅkukaḷiṉ kuṭṭiyum pārppeṉappaṭum eṉṟu piṅkala uriccoṟṟokuti kūṟum. itu attuṇic ciṟappuṭittaṉṟu. tāyiṉāl mikak kavaṉittup pārkkappaṭuvatiṉāl, paṟavik kuñcu pārppu eṉappaṭṭatu. pārttal = pēṇutal, pēṇi vaḷarttal. pārppu eṉṉuñ col poruṭkaraṇiyam paṟṟi, nurs(e)ling eṉṟu āṅkilac colli ottatākum. cila ūruyirikaḷiṉ iḷamikkum pārppu eṉṉum collit tolkāppiyar māṭṭeṟintārēṉum, atu ciṟappākap paṟaviyiṉ iḷamikkē uriyateṉpati, ciṟappuṭip poruḷi muṟpaṭak kiḷattal eṉṉum uttipaṟṟi ati mutaṟkaṇ taṉippaṭak kūṟiyatāṟ peṟavittār.
pārppu eṉṉum col poruḷ viripu muṟiyil makkaṭ kuḻaviyiyum kuḻavi pōṉṟa pommiyiyum kuṟittapōtu rakaraṅ keṭṭup pāppu, pāppā eṉat tirintatu.

pirāmaṇam
vētattiṉpiṉ, vētap poruḷi viḷakkum pirāmaṇam eṉṉum urinaṭi nūlkaḷ eḻuntaṉa. pirāmaṇaṉukkuriyatu pirāmaṇam. piramaṉi yaṟintavaṉ pirāmaṇaṉ. piramaṉ (prahmaṉ) eṉṉum col paramaṉ eṉṉum teṉcolliṉ tiripu. mā. vi. a. itip pruh. (b) eṉṉum mūlattiṉiṉṟu tirikkum. pruh eṉpatu peruku eṉṉum teṉcoṟ ṟiripu.
āraṇiyakamum upaniṭatamum
pirāmaṇattiṉpiṉ āraṇiyakam (āraṇyaka) eṉṉum upaniṭatat tōṟṟuvāy nūlkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. āraṇiyattiliruntu ārāyntatu āraṇiyakam. āraṇiyam kāṭu. āraṇiyam eṉṉum col araṇam eṉṉum teṉcoṟ ṟiripu.  va.va.
akkālattil nāṭu ciṟitākavum kāṭu peritākavum iruntati ṉāl, ūriyaṭuttē kāṭu allatu cōliyiruntatu. tamiḻa tiraviṭa muṉivar kāṭṭiluṟivatik kaṇṭa vētap pirāmaṇar, tāmum avarpōl naṭittē, ūriyaṭutta allatu taṭivaḻi (Trunk Road) mēluḷḷa cōlikaḷilum makkaḷ vaḻaṅkum maliyaṭivāraṅkaḷilum, kuṭumpattuṭaṉ vatintaṉar.
ciṟutali navvip peruṅkaṇ māppiṇi
anti yantaṇa raruṅkaṭa ṉiṟukku
muttī viḷakkiṟ ṟuñcum
poṟkōṭ ṭimayamum potiyamum pōṉṟē.
eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭup pakutiyi (2) nōkkuka.  va.va.
tamiḻ muṉivar muṟṟat tuṟantavar; āṇavam aṟṟavar;
pēypōṟ ṟirintu piṇampōṟ kiṭantiṭṭa picciyellām
nāypō larunti naripō luḻaṉṟunaṉ maṅkiyarit
tāypōṟ karutit tamarpō levarukkun tāḻmicolic
cēypō lirupparkaṇ ṭīruṇmi ñāṉan terintavarē.
kāṭē tirinteṉṉa kāṟṟē pucitteṉṉa kanticuṟṟi
oṭē yeṭutteṉṉa uḷḷaṉ pilātava rōṅkuviṇṇōr
nāṭē yiṭimaru tīcarkku meyyaṉpar nāriyarpāl
vīṭē yiruppiṉa meyññāṉa vīṭṭiṉpa mēvuvarē.
eṉṉum paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷ pāṭṭiṟ kilakkāṉavar.
vētap pirāmaṇa muṉivarō, tammip piṟappiluyarntava reṉṟum vīṭupēṟum ataṟku vāyilāṉa tuṟavum pirāmaṇarkkē uriyaṉaveṉṟum karuti, āṇavam eṉṉum kuṉṟēṟi niṉṟu, kāma viruppam nīṅkumvari maṉiviyoṭu kūṭi vāḻntavar. ātalāl, avar uṇmiyāṉa tuṟaviyarumallar; tuṟavu mutircciyāl meypporuḷ kaṇṭavarumallar. āraṇiyakattiṟkuppiṉ eḻunta upaniṭatam (upaniṣat) eṉṉum meypporuḷ nūl kaḷellām, ōttukkaḷ eṉṉum tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉ moḻi peyarppē.  (va.va.)
uyarntōrk kuriya vōtti ṉāṉa.
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (977).
upaniṣak eṉṉum vaṭacol upa+ni+sat eṉṟu piriyum. upa = uṭaṉ, aruku. ni = kīḻ. sat = kuntu. ākavē, upaniṣat eṉṉum coṟku arukaṭiyamartal eṉpatu tiraṇṭa poruḷ; āciriyaṉ kīḻiruntu meypporuḷ kaṟṟal eṉpatu virinta poruḷ. āyiṉ, paramavōtiyāl aṟiyāmiyi aṭakkutal eṉpatu āriyar kūṟum viḷakkap poruḷ (“Setting at the rest ignorance by revealing the knowledge of the Supreme Spirit”).
vēta muṉivar kāṭṭil vatintataṉāl, kaṭavuḷippaṟṟiya vuṇmikaḷit tāmē kaṇṭaṟintatupōṟ kāṭṭik koṇṭaṉar. avar muṉintatellām tamiḻiyum tamiḻaṉiyum tamiḻp paṇpāṭṭiyumē. muṉital veṟuttal. muṉintavaṉ muṉivaṉ.
upaniṭataṅkaḷ 108 eṉpar.  (va.va.)
pirāmaṇaṉ nilat tēvaṉā?
kati nīla nari, tāṉ nīlat toṭṭikkuḷ viḻuntu nīla niṟam peṟṟa taṉālēyē, arimāvum varimāvum karimāvum pōṉṟa vaṉmā vilaṅkukaḷiyum ēmāṟṟi aṭakki yāṇṭatu pōṉṟē, vētap pirāmaṇaṉum kuḷir nāṭṭu vāḻkkiyāṟ peṟṟa taṉ veṇṇiṟattit tuṇikkoṇṭu, tāṉ nilattēvaṉ eṉṟu tamiḻariyum tirāviṭariyum ēmāṟṟi aṭakkiyāṇṭāṉ. an nīla nariyiṉ ūḷiyāl ataṉ iṉam aṟiyappaṭṭatu pōṉṟē, pirāmaṇaṉum āriya māntaṉ eṉpatu, mēliyāriyattiṟku iṉamāṉa avaṉ moḻiyiṉāl aṟiyappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
ulaka varalāṟṟaṟivum moḻiyārāycciyumillāta paṇṭik kālattil, paḻaṅkuṭip pētimiyum matap pittamum koṭi maṭamuṅ koṇṭa mūvēntariyum palvakiyil vayappaṭutti, avar vāyilākap potu makkaḷiṭip pirāmaṇiyattip pukuttiṉar āriyar. annilimikaḷ iṉṟiṉmiyāl, akkālattu mūṭa nampikkikaṭkum iṉṟiṭamilli.
kaṭanta mūvarāyiram āṇṭāka, pirāmaṇar kalviyit tam kulat toḻilākak koṇṭiruntaṉāl, kalvik kēṟṟa maṉappāṉmi yum niṉivāṟṟalum avarkku mika vaḷarntu vantirukkiṉṟaṉa. āyiṉum, iv vaḷarcci kulavāri yaḷavilēyē yaṉṟit taṉip paṭṭavar aḷavil illi. ēṉeṉil, iṟivaṉ  kalviyi ellāk kulattārkkum potu vurimiyākki vittiruppa toṭu, vevvēṟu kulat taṉi makkaṭkē ciṟanta kalvit tiṟamiyi aḷittirukkiṉṟāṉ. orē kulattār kalviyit tam muṟṟiṟimiyākak koḷvatu, iyaṟkikkum iṟivaṉ ēṟpāṭṭiṟkum māṟāṉatē.
tiruvaḷḷuvar pōṉṟa aṟa nūlāciriyarō, kampariṉuñ ciṟanta pāvalarō, pirāmaṇarkkuḷ iruntatilli.
ikkālattum, vayavar (Sir) cantira cēkara veṅkaṭṭarāmaṉ pōṉṟē, kōvik kō. turiccāmi nāyakkarum (G.D. Naidu) putuppuṉi vāṟṟalar. muṉṉavar aṟiñarkkup payaṉpaṭum karut tiyal ārāycci maṭṭum ceytavar; piṉṉavarō, aṉivarkkum payaṉpaṭum kāṭciyiyaṟ karuvikaḷiyum iṟumpūtukaḷiyum ceytavar. āyiṉum, avarip pōṟṟavilli.
kuṭanti irāmāṉucam pōṉṟē, ceṅkōṭṭippar, (Dr) civa caṅkara nārāyaṇap piḷḷiyum, mēṉāṭṭār pōṟṟu maḷavu kaṇitat tiṟavōr. āyiṉum, pirāmaṇarkkuḷḷa iṉavuṇarcci tamiḻaruk kiṉmiyāl, avarukku viḷamparam illāmalē pōyiṟṟu.
mukavi māvaṭṭattic cērnta oru tamiḻar, iru kaṇṇum teriyātavar, ettaṉip pēreṇkaḷiyum uḷḷattiṟ koṇṭu nālvakik kaṇakkuñ ceyyavum, ettuṇip pēreṇṇiṟkum nāṉmaṭi vēr kāṇavum, vallavarāyiruntār.
palkalik kaḻaka āḷviṉit tiṟamiyilum āṅkilap pēc cāṟṟalilum, par. (Dr.) vayavar (Sir) ā. ilakkumaṇacāmi mutaliyāriṉum mikkavar, pirāmaṇarkkuḷ irukkavē muṭiyātu. iṅṅaṉam ēṉit tuṟikaḷilum ettaṉiyō pēr uḷḷaṉar. tamiḻariṉ taṉṉalamum poṟāmiyum iṉa veṟuppum, palar tali yeṭukka vāyppillāmaṟ ceytu viṭukiṉṟaṉa.
pirāmaṇar kalvit tiṟamiyiṟ ciṟattaṟku iṉṉoru karaṇiyamum uṇṭu. atu maṟivāṉa iṉak kalappu.
makkaḷuḷ nāl vēṟu varaṇam pōṉṟē nālvakik kalappuk kulamum uḷḷaṉaveṉṟu, maṉuviṉ kulavoḻukka nūl kūṟu kiṉṟatu. mēl varaṇa āṭavaṉukkum kīḻ varaṇap peṇṭiṟkum piṟantavaṉ aṉu lōmaṉ. kīḻ varaṇa āṭavaṉukkum mēl varaṇap peṇṭiṟkum piṟantavaṉ piratilōmaṉ. aṉulōma āṭavaṉukkum pirati lōmap peṇṭiṟkum piṟantavaṉ antarāḷaṉ. pirati lōma āṭavaṉukkum aṉu lōmap peṇṭiṟkum piṟantavaṉ virāttiyaṉ.
mēl mūvaraṇattārum tat tamakkuk kīḻppaṭṭa varaṇaṅkaḷilum allatu varaṇattilum peṇ koḷḷalāmeṉṟu, maṉu nūl kūṟukiṉṟatu. āyiṉ, kīḻ mūvaraṇattārum tattamakku mēṟpaṭṭa varaṇattiṟ peṇ koḷḷalā meṉṟu neṟiyīṭṭu muṟiyāyk kūṟavilli. āyiṉum, av vaḻakkam toṉṟu toṭṭu iruntē vantatu. iti maṉunūlun kūṟukiṉṟatu.
pirāmaṇaṉukku kṣattiriya viciyatirīkaḷiṭattiṟ piṟantavar kaḷ, eppaṭi upanayana mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ kuriyavarkaḷāyiruppatāl uyarn tirukkiṟārkaḷō, appaṭiyē viciyaṉukku kṣattiriya tirīyiṭattilum kṣattiriyaṉukkup pirāmaṇa tirīyiṭattilum piṟanta puttirarkaḷ, cūttiraṉukku viciya kṣattiriya pirāmaṇa jāti tirī yiṭattiṟ piṟantavarkaḷik kāṭṭilum uyarnta varkaḷ.  (kE.(bkhÊ peyarppu) 10:28)
mūvēntarum pirāmaṇarkku muḻu aṭimiyarāyp pōṉataṟku, pirāmaṇap peṇ nukarcciyum karaṇiyamā yiruntirukkalām.
paṇṭi muṟippaṭi tamiḻilēyē tiruk kōvil vaḻipāṭu ceytu vanta kurukkaḷ eṉṉum tamiḻa vakuppār, āriya mantiraṅ kaḷik kaṟṟuk koṇṭu camaṟkirutattil vaḻipāṭu naṭatti in nūṟṟāṇṭiṟ pirāmaṇarāka māṟi viṭṭatākavum collap paṭukiṉṟatu.
pirāmaṇar eṉṟum tam mēmpāṭṭi nili niṟutti iṉpuṟṟu vāḻa virumpiyatāl, tam miṉa vuyarttattip pēṇuvatiṟ kaṇṇuṅ karuttu māyiruntu vantirukkiṉṟaṉar.
iṟiyaṭiyā reṉṟu ciṟappittuc collap paṭupavar, ceyaṟ kariya ceyal ceyta teyvap paṟṟāḷarē.
vāḷāṉ makavarit tūṭṭaval lēṉallaṉ mātucoṉṉa
cūḷā liḷami tuṟakkaval lēṉallaṉ toṇṭuceytu
nāḷāṟiṟ kaṇṇiṭan tappaval lēṉallaṉ nāṉiṉicceṉ
ṟaḷāva teppaṭi yōtiruk kāḷatti yappaṉukkē.  (tiruttilli, 3).
eṉṟu paṭṭiṉattār maṉam varuntip pāṭiyuḷḷār.
tirut toṇṭat toki pāṭiya cuntara mūrtti nāyaṉār, tillivā ḻantaṇartam aṭiyārkkum aṭiyēṉ eṉṟu toṭaṅki yirukkavē vēṇṭiya tilli. tilli vāḻantaṇar pirāmaṇarē yaṉṟit tamiḻ muṟippaṭi antaṇaru millar; avaruḷ oruvarēṉum ceyaṟ kariya pattic ceyal ceytatu milli. mūvēntarum, ciṟappākac cōḻa vēntar, viṭṭa māṉi yaṅkaḷiyum iṭṭa kāṇikkikaḷiyuṅ koṇṭu, orē kōyilil mūvāyiravar iruntu uṇṭu koḻuttuc cōmpēṟit taṉamāy vāḻntu vantavar. poṉṉampalap pūcakarip pōṟṟuva teṉiṉ, veḷḷiyampalam ceppampalam mutaliya ēṉi yampalap pūcakariyum pōṟṟutal vēṇṭum. atu poruntākkāl, poṉṉampalap pūcakarip pōṟṟutalum poruntātu.
kōvil vaḻipāṭu ataṟkut takutiyuṭiya ellāk kulattārum ceyyalām, ceyvikkalām, tamiḻnāṭṭil tamiḻarē tamiḻil maṭṭum ceyvittal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉamē āriyar varumuṉ naṭipeṟṟu vantatu.
nīṇṭa kālamāka iruntu vanta āriya aṭimit taṉattiṉāl, aṭimit taṉattilēyē piṟantu aṭimittaṉattilēyē vaḷarntu, aṭimit taṉamē elumpuṅkurutiyumāka ūṟippōṉa tamiḻarē palar tamiḻ vaḻipāṭṭi etirkkiṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl, tamiḻ vaḻipāṭṭiyum tamiḻar pūcaka rāvatiyum pirāmaṇar vaṉmiyāka etirkkiṉṟaṉar. civakōcariyāriṉ camaṟkiruta vaḻi pāṭṭiṉum, kaṇṇappaṉāriṉ tamiḻ vaḻipāṭē civaperu māṉukkuc ciṟantatum ukantatumāyiruntatiyaṟintum, iru cārārum uṇarkiṉṟilar. ēṉi nāṭukaḷiṉ naṭappiyum ikkāla urimi vēṭkiyiyum nōkkukiṉṟilar.
āriya muṟippaṭi nōkkiṉum, pirāmaṇar iṉṟu ūrkāval tuṟiyilum paṭit tuṟiyilum āḷviṉit tuṟiyilum cērntu cattiriyarāyum, uḻavut toḻil ceytum uṇṭic cāli vittum vāṇikam mēṟkoṇṭum viciyarāyum kit toḻilum ēvalum ceytu cūttirarāyum, māṟiyuḷḷaṉar.
muṭipuri
etu eṅṅaṉamāyiṉum, pirāmaṇaṉ nilat tēvaṉallaṉ eṉpatum; ulakiyalilum mataviyalilum illaṟattilum tuṟavaṟattilum, tamiḻaṉukkillāta ēṟṟam avaṉukku immiyum illi yeṉpatum, iti uṇarātavaṉ ettuṇik kaṟṟavaṉēṉum uruvattāl māntaṉum uḷḷattāl aḵṟiṇiyumāvaṉeṉ patum; teritaru tēṟṟamām. āriyar veṇṇiṟam iṉṟu poṉṉiṟamum cenniṟamum pukarniṟamum karuniṟamumāka māṟiyuḷḷatu. tamiḻar moḻiyārāycci yum varalāṟṟārāycciyum ceytu uṇmi yaṟintu kaṇ viḻittuk koṇṭaṉar.
pirāmaṇaṉukku uriyatu evvaṟam?
aṉpuṭimi, viruntōmpal, iṉiyavi kūṟal, ceynnaṉṟi yaṟital, naṭuvu nilimi, aṭakka muṭimi, oḻukka muṭimi, piṟaṉil viḻiyāmi, poṟi yuṭimi, aḻukkāṟāmi, veḵkāmi, puṟaṅ kūṟāmi, payaṉila collāmi, tīviṉi yaccam, oppura vaṟital, īki eṉpaṉa ilvāḻkki yaṟaṅkaḷākat tirukkuṟaḷ maṟiyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ mutaṉmi yāṉatu viruntōmpal.
iruntōmpi yilvāḻva tellām viruntōmpi
vēḷāṇmi ceytaṟ poruṭṭu.  (kuṟaḷ. 81).
viruntōmpum iyalpum īkiyum pirāmaṇaṉukkiṉmiyāl, illaṟam avaṉukkuriya taṉṟu.
pirāmaṇaṉ toḻilākac collappaṭum āṟaṉuḷ, ītal eṉpatu ōtuvittalil aṭaṅkutalāl, uṇmiyil avaṉ toḻil aintē.
aruḷuṭimi, pulāṉ maṟuttal, tavam, kaḷḷāmi, vāymi, vekuḷāmi, iṉṉā ceyyāmi, kollāmi, tuṟavu, meyyuṇartal, avā vaṟuttal eṉpaṉa tuṟavu neṟiyaṟaṅkaḷākat tirukkuṟaḷ maṟiyiṟ collap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
koli vēḷvi ceytalālum, tuṟavu niliyilum akam piramam eṉṟum civōkam eṉṟum akaṅkarippatālum, tamiḻa matatti āriya mata meṉṟum taṉṉi nilat tēvaṉ eṉṟum taṉ ilakkiya moḻiyit tēva moḻi yeṉṟum colli intiyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷi ēmāṟṟuvatālum, pirāmaṇa ṉukkut tuṟavaṟamum uriyatākātu.
ākavē, pirāmaṇaṉukku nāṭum pēccu moḻiyum pōṉṟē aṟa vāḻkkiyum illiyām.
piḻā
piḻā - piṭa (t´))
piḻā - piḻavu - piṭavu - piṭaku - piṭakku = pūttaṭṭup pōṉṟa putta maṟinūl. piṭakkē yuri ceyvār  (tēvā. 245 : 10.)
piṭaku - piṭakam = 1. taṭṭukkūṭi.
2. puttamaṟi (tiripiṭakam). periyōṉ piṭaka neṟi  
(maṇi. 26 : 66)  (va.va : 209)
pīḻi, pīḻi. piḷḷutal = piṭuṅkutal. tuṉpañ ceytal, tolli koṭuttal. piyttup piṭuṅkutal eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka. piḷ - pīḷ - pīḻ - pīḻi - pīḻittal = varuttutal, tuṉpañ ceytal. pīḻi - pīṭi - pīṭ (va.) pīḻi - pīḻi - pīṭi - pīṭā (va.) pīṭittal - tuṉpañ ceytal. pīṭi tuṉpam.   (ti.ma : 748.)
piḷḷiyār vaṇakkam
piḷḷiyār vaṇakkam kaṭic caṅka kālattiṟkup piṉṉar tāṉ tamiḻakat tiṟ pukuntatu. iṟivaṉ ōṅkāra vaṭiviṉaṉ eṉṟu collappaṭṭatiṉālum, ōṅkārattiṉ varivaṭivam yāṉi vaṭivi ottiruppatālum, yāṉi vaṭivil oru teyvam āriyap pūcāriyāla putitākap paṭikkappaṭṭu, civaneṟiyi muṉṉiṉum mikutiyāy āriyappaṭuttavum cēyōṉ eṉṉum murukaṉukkuc ceyyum vaḻipāṭṭiyum avaṉukkut tantiyākac collap paṭṭa civaṉukkuc ceyyum vaḻipāṭṭiyum kuṟikkavum, tamiḻarukkuḷ maṟṟumoru matap piriviyuṇṭu paṇṇavum, pukuttappaṭṭatāka aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. civaṉ allatu tirumāl eṉṉum peyarāl ellām valla iṟivaṉ ākiya muḻu mutaṟ kaṭavuḷē vaṇaṅkappaṭutaliṉ, avaṉi vaṇaṅku vārkku, avaṉukku makaṉ allatu marukaṉ muṟippaṭṭa vēṟoru ciṟuteyvam vēṇṭiyatēyilli. mēlum, iṟivaṉ paṭippil taliciṟanta māntaṉ vaṭivil iṟivaṉi vaṇaṅku vatē pakuttaṟivuḷḷa makkaṭku iḻukkāyirukka, ōr aḵṟiṇi yuyiriyiṉ vaṭivil etteyvattiyum vaṇaṅkuvatu, iv irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭu uyartiṇiyāḷaṉukku eḷḷaḷavum poruntāteṉpatu collāmalē peṟappaṭum.  (ta.ti. 7677).
piṟappu 
piṟappu iṟappu eṉṉum coṟkaḷ muṟiyē pakarameyc cērkkiyum ataṉ nīkkamum peṟṟiruttalāl uyir uṭampoṭu kūṭutaliyum ativiṭṭu nīṅkutaliyum kuṟikkumeṉṟu kūṟuvar.
piṟappu eṉpatu piḷ eṉṉum vēriṉiṉṟum iṟappu eṉpatu iṟu eṉṉum vēriṉiṉṟum piṟantiruppatālum piṇaṅku iṇaṅku eṉap piṟa etirc coṟkaḷum piṟappu iṟappu eṉpavi pōṉṟē amintiruppatālum atupōlik kūṟṟeṉka.  (col. 33,34).
pukaḻvaki
oḷi - taṉmatippu
	peyar allatu pēr - nalla vaḻiyilum tīyavaḻiyilum paravum pēr.
ici - pulavar pāṭuvatāl uṇṭākum pukaḻ.
pukaḻ - iṟanta piṉṉum vaḻaṅkum uyartturi.
cīrtti allatu kīrtti - miku pukaḻ.  (col. 42).
pukā (uṇā)
uṇṇutal eṉpatu uṭkoḷḷutal; atāvatu, oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟit taṉṉuḷ iḻuttal allatu cērttal. vāyvaḻi vayiṟṟiṟkuḷ oṉṟi iṭutalum, nilam nīri uṟiñcutalum, pātam cempañcuk kuḻampu niṟattip peṟutalum, uṇṇutalē. ivaṟṟuḷ mutalatē makkaḷ pēcciṟ peru vaḻakkāṉatu. iṟuti yatu, cērtaliṉ allatu cērttaliṉ vakikaḷuḷ oṉṟākum.
uṇṇutal (peruvaḻakku) = I (mutaṟporuḷ) : uṇavi uṭkoḷḷutal. II (vaḻipporuḷ) : 1. nukartal (aṉupavittal). taṇkaṭaṟ cērppa nī yuṇṭaveṉ ṉalaṉē (kuṟun. 236). 2. kavartal. avuṇaṉā ruyiriyuṇṭa kūṟṟiṉi (tiv. tiruk kuṟun. 2). 3. icivātal. ōci yūṭṭiṉu muṇṇāta vāṟum (yāp. vi.pak. 97). 4. oru viṉiyiṉ payaṉiyaṭital. kōvalaṉ poṟ kollaṉāṟ koliyuṇṭāṉ (kolluṇṭāṉ).
uṇṇutal eṉpatu aṭippaṭip poruḷil uṭkoḷḷuta lātaliṉ, perumpālum uḷḷiṭatti allatu uṭpukuttalik kuṟikkuñ coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟē uṇṇutal viṉiccoṟkaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu : uḷ - uṇ - uṇā - uṇa - uṇavu.
pukuttal = 1. uṭcella viṭutal. vāyil pukuppiṉum (tol. po. 149). 2. uṭceluttutal. 3. uṇṇutal. ipporuḷil ic col vaḻakkaṟṟatu.
puku - pukavu = 1. uṭcelki. kaḻutu pukavayara (aiṅkuṟu. 314). 
2. uṇavu. paḻañcōṟṟup pukavarutti (puṟanā. 395).
puku - pukā = uṇavu. pukāak kāli (tol. po. 107).
pukā - puvā - puvvā = cōṟu, uṇavu (nāṭṭuppuṟa vaḻakku).
puka + aṭṭu (oṭṭu) = pukaṭṭu. pukaṭṭutal = 1. uṭceluttutal. 
2. ūṭṭutal. tāy kuḻavikkup pālūṭṭiṉāḷ. 3. aṟivuṟuttutal. allama tēvaṉ caritat tīmpāl ..... pukaṭṭiṉāṉāl (pirapuliṅ.tuti. 11), cevitiṟantu pukaṭṭa (tiruviḷi. viṭiyilac. 4).
oṭṭu eṉṉum tuṇi viṉiyi, tūṅkavoṭṭār, varavoṭṭār eṉṉum etirmaṟi viṉikaḷiṟ kāṇka. oṭṭutal = poruntutal, icital. oṭṭu - aṭṭu. e - ṭu : varaṭṭu (vara + aṭṭu), pōkaṭṭu (pōka aṭṭu). ivi orumi yēval. varaṭṭum, pōkaṭṭum eṉpaṉa paṉmi yēval.
pukaṭṭu - pōṭṭu (u. va.). kuḻantikku maruntu pōṭṭu.
puka + viṭu = pukaṭu. o. nō : pōka viṭu - pōkaṭu - pōṭu. pukaṭutal = vīciyeṟital. itaṟku ūṭṭutaṟ poruḷilli.
puku - Gk. paku (phag) phagin, to eat, devour; phagema, food; phagas, phagon, glutton.
phage (phag), combining form meaning ‘eater’ as in xylophage.
phagedena, gangrene, L. phagedena fr. Gk. phagedina, cancer, lit. ‘voracity’ fr. phagein, to eat.
phago, combining form meaning ‘eating’ as in phagocyte.
phagous, combining form meaning ‘eating, feeding on’ as in creophagous. xylophagous Gk. phagos, ‘eater of’.
phagus, combining form meaning ‘eating’, as in sarco-phagas, L. fr. Gk. sarkophagos, orig. flesh-consuming (stone).
phagy, combining form meaning ‘eating of ’ (something specified), as in anthropophagy, geophagy.
phagia, same as ‘phagy’.	
ukaramutal akaramutalākat tirivatu perumpāṉmi. tamiḻil mūccoli (aspirate) illi. tamiḻ meykaḷ piṟa moḻikaḷil mūccoli meyyākat tirivatuṇṭu.
e-ṭu : kal - khal (pirākuvī).
iṅṅaṉam tamiḻccol tamiḻukku akappuṟamāṉa tiraviṭatti lēyē mūccoli peṟumpōtu. ataṟkup puṟamāṉa āriyattil atip peṟuvatu iyaṟkikku māṟāṉataṉṟu.
ph eṉṉum iṇivari allatu kūṭṭeḻuttu, āṅkilattil ‘f’ eṉṟolippiṉum, kirēkkattil mūccoli kūṭiya pakaramē (pha) eṉpati aṟital vēṇṭum.
puku eṉṉum tamiḻccol, kirēkkattil paku allatu paka eṉṟu tirintapiṉ, ataṟkiṉamāṉa intiya āriya muṉṉi moḻiyil (Pre - Indian East Aryan) puj (bhuj) eṉṟu tirintuḷḷatu. 
o. nō : paku - va. paj. (bhaj).
camaṟkirutattiṟku muntiṉa vēta moḻiyilēyē it tiripu kāṇappaṭuvatāl, itu vēta āriyar intiyāviṟkuṭ puku muṉṉarē nikaḻntirukka vēṇṭum. miṭaṟṟoli aṇṇa voliyākat tiriyumpōtu, eṭuppilā voli (voiceless sound) eṭuppilā voliyākavum eṭuppoli (voice sound) eṭuppoliyākavum tirivatē potuviyalpu.
e - ṭu :	ka - ca : kuṭiki - kuṭici.
		g-j : L. ago - Skt. aj (to drive).
im muṟippaṭi, puku eṉpataṉ tiripāṉa paku (phag) eṉṉum mēliyāriyak kirēkkac col kīḻiyāriyattil puj (bhuj) eṉṟu tirintuḷḷatu. eṭuppilāvoli eṭuppoliyākat tirivatum iyalpē.
e - ṭu :	E. acre - L. ager, Gk. agros.
		E. triple - E. treble.
bhuj, to eat, eat and drink, enjoy a meal, consume, enjoy, use, possess, make use of, utilize, exploit, suffer, experience, undergo, RV., AV., Up., MBh., KaÍv.
bhukta, enjoyed, eaten, made use of, possessed etc.
bhukti, eating, enjoyment, consuming, frution, possession, usufruct.
bhuktvaÍ having enjoyed or eaten or possessed.
bhuji, the granting of enjoyment, favour, RV.
bhujishya, granting food, useful, AV.
bhoktru, one who enjoys or eats, Maitr Up.
bhoÍga, enjoyment, eating, feeding on, RV.
bhoÍgin, enjoying, eating, having, wealthy; a king, a concubine.
bhoÍgya, to be enjoyed, to be used.
bhoÍja, bestowing enjoyment, bountiful, liberal, RV leading a life of enjoyment, BhP., a king.
bhoÍjaka, cating, being about to eat; giving to eat, nourishing.
bhoÍjana, feeding, giving to eat; the act of enjoying, using, RV., the act of eating; a meal, food.
bhoÍjaniya, to be eaten, eatable; to be fed, to be made to eat, Mn.; one to whom enjoyment is to be afforded or service to be done, Nir.; food (esp. what is not masticated, as opp. to khaÍdaniya) MBh.
bhoÍjin, enjoing, eating.
bhoÍjya, to be enjoyed or eaten, what is enjoyed or eaten, anything to be enjoyed or eaten, a festive dinner.
bhoÍktavya, to be enjoyed or eaten, to be used or employed; to be possessed or governed or ruled, to be utilized or exploited, to be fed.
ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻaka akaramutaliyit tokuttavarkaḷ, puku (uṇ) eṉṉum teṉcoṟkum puj (bhuj) eṉṉum vaṭa coṟkum uḷḷa toṭarpi aṟintē, pukavu, pukā eṉṉum iru coṟkaḷiyum puj eṉṉum vaṭacolloṭu oppu nōkkak kuṟittirukkiṉṟaṉar. āyiṉ teṉcollē vaṭacolliṉ mūlam eṉṟu kāṭṭuvatu avar karuttaṉṟu. vaṭamoḻi tēva moḻi eṉṉum aṭāvaṭittaṉa ēmāṟṟi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu, vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa teṉcol lellām vaṭa colleṉṟu collāmaṟ colvatē, av voppunōkkak kuṟippiṉ nōkkam. tamiḻi vaṭamoḻit tiripāka allatu kiḷiyākak kāṭṭavēṇṭumeṉpatē ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻaka akaramutalit tokuppāḷariṉ aṭippaṭikkoḷki yātalāl, vaṭamoḻikku aṭippaṭiyāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷi yellām vaṭacol leṉṟu kāṭṭuvatoṭu, uṇmiyil toṭarpiṉṟi oru maruṅku oliyotta pōliyoppumic coṟkaḷiyum vaṭacoṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu tirippatilum avaṟṟoṭu oppunōkku vatilum, kaṇṇuṅ karuttumāyiruntirukkiṉṟaṉar. e - ṭu : āriyaṉ - aiyaṉ, cyāmā - cāmi. vēntaṉ - o.nō : tēvēntiraṉ, maruntu - o.nō : amruta.
itaṉāl, teṉmoḻi vaṭamoḻikku mūlamāyirukkalām eṉṉum ōr accam avar uḷḷattiṟ kuṭikoṇṭiruntateṉa uyttuṇarak kiṭakkiṉṟatu.
puku eṉṉum teṉcol puj (bhuj) eṉṉum vaṭacoṟku mūlamāyiruppatoṭu, tōṟṟamum vaḻimuṟit tiripuṅ kūṟum teḷivāṉa taṉvaralāṟuṅ koṇṭuḷḷatu. ivviyalpu ataṉ tirivāṉa āriyaccoṟkaṭkilli.
ul - uḷ - uḷu = marattit tuḷikkum puḻu. uḷuttal = marattip puḻu arittut tuḷittal.

pul - puḷ - (puḷu) - puḻu = kāykaṟikaṉikaḷit tuḷikkum uḷu.
puḻuttal = puḻu uṭpukutal allatu arittut tuḷittal.

potuvāka, mēliyāriyattiluḷḷa eṭuppolik kakaram (g) kīḻiyāriyat til aikaramākat tirikiṉṟatu.

pukiyilik kaṭṭi yuruṭṭal viḷiyāṭṭu (1)
itu oruvāṟu kaṇṇāmpotti pōṉṟatē. āyiṉ itaṟku oru vaṭṭam pōṭappaṭum; atōṭu muntit toṭuvatu yār eṉṟu tīrmāṉittaṟku, ellāriyum variciyāyk kuṉiya vittu, avarkaḷ niḻalil pukiyilik kaṭṭiyi oruvar uruṭṭuvar. atu yār niḻaliṟpōy niṟkiṉṟatō, avar ēṉiyōrit toṭutal vēṇṭum.
piṟarellām oṭi oḷintukoḷḷutaṟku iraṇṭoru nimiyaṅ koṭukkappaṭum. toṭum piḷḷi toṭavarumpōtu, ellārum ōṭippōy vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ niṉṟukoḷvar. vaṭṭattiṟkuṭ ceṉṟapiṉ toṭutal kūṭātu. vaṭṭattiṟkuṭ cellumuṉ toṭappaṭṭa piḷḷi aṭuttamuṟi toṭutal vēṇṭum. oruvarum toṭappaṭāviṭiṉ, muṉpu toṭamuyaṉṟa piḷḷiyē mīṇṭum toṭutal vēṇṭum.
oruvar toṭarntu mūvāṭṭi oruvariyuṅ toṭāviṭiṉ, avarmītu ēṉiyōr ciṟitu ciṟitu kutiriyēṟuvatuṇṭu. ataṉpiṉ, mīṇṭum pukiyilik kaṭṭi uruṭṭappaṭum.
pukiyilik kaṭṭiyeṭuttal viḷiyāṭṭu (2)
ituvum oḷintu viḷiyāṭum viḷiyāṭṭē.
āṭuvārellām uttikaṭṭi irukaṭciyākap pirintu koṇṭapiṉ, oru kaṭciyār oṭi oḷintukoḷvar. iṉṉoru kaṭciyār. kaṭikaṭkuc ceṉṟu pukiyilik kaṭṭi (allatu veṟṟilikkāmpu) eṭuttuvantu, oḷintavarit tēṭippiṭippar. yāriyēṉum kaṇṭupiṭittapōtu, kaṭikkuc ceṉṟu vantamikku aṭiyāḷamākap pukiyilik kaṭṭiyik kāṭṭalvēṇṭum. kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭavar muṉpu ellārum niṉṟa iṭattiṟku vantuviṭuvar. ellāruṅ kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭapiṉ viṉimāṟi viḷiyāṭuvar.
oḷintiruntavarik kaṇṭupiṭikkumpōtu pukiyilik kaṭṭiyik kāṭṭāviṭiṉum, yāriyēṉuṅ kaṇṭupiṭikka muṭiyāviṭiṉum, muṉpu kaṇṭupiṭittavarē mīṇṭuṅ kaṇṭu piṭittal vēṇṭum. itu pāṇṭināṭṭu viḷiyāṭṭu. itaṉ cōḻanāṭṭu vaki varumāṟu:
āṭuvārellārum kipōṭṭu ovvoruvarākap pirittu, iṟutiyilakap paṭṭuk koṇṭavar ēṉiyārip piṭittal vēṇṭum. piṭikkavēṇṭiyavar caṟṟuk toliviluḷḷa oru kuṟippiṭṭa taḻiyik koṇṭuvanta piṉputāṉ piṭittal vēṇṭum. ataṟkuḷ ēṉiyarellām maṟivāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil oḷintukoḷvar. kaṇṭupiṭippavar oḷintiruppavarik kiyiṉāṟ piṭittukkoḷḷalām, allatu avar peyari maṭṭum piṟarkkuk kēṭkumāṟu urakkac collalām. ivviraṇṭil etu ceyvateṉṟu muṉṉarē tīrmāṉikkap paṭṭirukkum. kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭavar taḻiyik kāṭṭac collumpōtu, kaṇṭupiṭittavar kāṭṭal vēṇṭum; illāviṭiṉ, maṟu muṟiyum avarē kaṇṭupiṭittal vēṇṭum. taḻi kāṭṭappaṭiṉ, kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭavar maṟumuṟi kaṇṭu piṭittal vēṇṭum. ivviḷiyāṭṭu kulīmtār eṉṉum urutup peyarāl vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. itit taṉittamiḻil taḻipaṟittal eṉalām.  (ta.nā.vi.)

putu maṇippavaḷap puṉmiyum purimiyum.

ākavē, coṟṟūymi pōṉṟē olittūymiyum tamiḻukku iṉṟiyamiyāta paṇpām.
veḷināṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu vanta poruḷkaḷellām centamiḻp peyar peṟṟaṉa. uruḷikkiḻaṅku, ēḻilikkiḻaṅku, karumpu, cāttukkuṭi, centāḻi (aṉṉāci), pukiyili, miḷakāy mutaliya nilittiṇip poruḷkaḷum, oṭṭakam, oṭṭakac civiṅki, kaḻuti, kōvēṟu kaḻuti (attiri), kutiri, varik kutiri, nīryāṉi mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷum; tīkkōḻi, vāṉkōḻi mutaliya paṟavikaḷum; mitivaṇṭi, pukivaṇṭi, cūḻcciya vaṇṭi mutaliya ūrtikaḷum; kuṇṭukkuḻāy, vittūṟṟi, maṇ ṇeṇṇey mutaliya palvakip poruḷ kaḷum, veḷi nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu vantaviyē.
kutiri oṉṟē paṉṉiru vakiyāka vakukkappaṭṭu vevvēṟu peyar peṟṟuḷḷatu. kuccukkiḻaṅku iṭantoṟum peyar vēṟupaṭṭup paṉṉiru coṟkaḷāṟ kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ātalāl, veḷināṭṭup poruḷkaṭkellām veḷināṭṭup peyarkaḷē yiruttal kūṭumeṉpatu poruḷaṟṟa uṟaḻāṭṭē.
piṟamoḻikaḷellām, tamiḻpōl colvaḷamum collākka vāyppum tūymi marapum uṭiyaṉavalla. āṅkilar kaṭanta munnūṟṟāṇṭukaḷākap putup puṉivukaḷāl tam aṟivip perukkik koṇṭaṉar. tam puttaṟivuk karuttukaḷik kuṟikkat tam moḻiyiṟ colliṉmiyāl, ilattīṉ kirēkka moḻikaḷiṉiṉṟu ērāḷamāyk kaṭaṉkoṇṭu tam karuttiṟ kēṟpat tirittukkoṇṭaṉar.
e-ṭu:
putukkaruttu	kaṭaṉcol	tirippu
cūḻacciyappoṟi	L. ingenium	engine
	(cūḻcci)
miṉ	Gk. electron	electricity
	(ampar)
iṅṅaṉam āṅkilar tam karuttiyuṇarttap piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiyatu, paṅkīṭṭuk kālattil mikutiyāy arici kiṭikkumiṭattiṟ piyiyum, uḻavaṉ tāṉ viḷitta kūlattic cantikkuk koṇṭupōka vaṇṭiyiyum, iraval peṟṟatokkum.
āṅkilattil maṭṭumaṉṟi ilattīṉ kirēkka moḻikaḷilum pala aṭippaṭic coṟkaḷ tamiḻāyiruppatāl, iṟṟi āṅkila aṟiviyaṟ kammiyak kalik kuṟiyīṭukaḷiṟ pala tamiḻc coṟkaḷiyē aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭum uḷḷaṉa. mēlum, āṅkilak kalikkuṟiyīṭukaḷellām palvēṟu vakiyil mika eḷiya muṟiyil amintuḷḷaṉa. ammuṟikaḷik kiyāḷiṉ, ellāk kalikkuṟiyīṭukaḷiyum tamiḻiṟ cevviyāka moḻi peyarttuk koḷḷalām.
tamiḻnāṭṭaracu caṭṭattuṟi tavira vēṟetilum kuṟiyīṭukaḷi moḻipeyarkka ituvari ettaki muyaṟciyum ceyyavilli. kālañ ceṉṟa i.mu. cuppiramaṇiyap piḷḷi pōṉṟa taṉip paṭṭa tamiḻp pulavarum, kiṇṭi, kōvi, kārikkuṭi aṇṇā mali nakar mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa kallūri māṇavarumē, tam aḷaviṟanta tamiḻp paṟṟiṉāl, āṭcic coṟkaḷiyum palvēṟu aṟiviyaṟ kuṟiyīṭukaḷiyum tokuttum moḻipeyarttum uḷḷaṉar. aracu avaṟṟiyellām tokuttu aḷavip paṭutti, āṭcic coṟkaḷip pōṉṟē piṟavaṟṟiyum āṭcikkuk koṇṭuvarutal vēṇṭum. itaṟkākat takkārik koṇṭa oru niliyāṉa kuḻuviyum aracu amarttutal vēṇṭum.
tamiḻ mummoḻik kalaviyāyiṉ, ennili yaṭiyumō, iṟivaṉukkut tāṉ veḷiccam.
oppuyarvaṟṟa taṉṉantaṉit tūya tamiḻi, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ pōṉṟa niṟi pulavarkkum māṟṟavum citikkavum atikāra milli. aṅṅaṉamirukka, tamiḻp pulamiyum tamiḻārāycciyum tamiḻp paṟṟumillāta orucilar tamiḻi urutteriyātu māṟṟa urimiyuṭiyarō?
āṭpeyar, iṭappeyar mutaliya moḻipeyarkkak kūṭāta ciṟappuc coṟkaḷiyum, tamiḻeḻuttilēyē eḻututal vēṇṭum. e-ṭu : cēkkacuppiyar, āppirikkā, piṟa coṟkaḷi yellām moḻipeyarttēyātal vēṇṭum.
pura
purattal = kāttal. pura - puram = kāppu. pura - puravu = kāppu. puravalaṉ = kāvalaṉ. purantā - puṟantā, puṟantarutal = pātukāppaḷittal, kāttal. purantara - purantaraṉ = kāvalaṉ, aracaṉ. purantaraṉ - purantara (va.) = intiraṉ, viṇṇōr kāvalaṉ.  (ti.ma. 749).
puraṭci
tamiḻ camaṟkirutap piṇippiṉiṉṟum, tamiḻaṉ āriya aṭimit taṉattiṉiṉṟum viṭutali peṟumvari, tamiḻ vaḷarccikkum tamiḻaṉ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkum iṭamiṉmiyāl,
	tamiḻāynta tamiḻaṉtāṉ tamiḻnāṭṭil
	mutalamiccāy varutal vēṇṭum.
eṉṟu pāṭiṉār puraṭcip pāvēntar. āyiṉ, atu pōtātu, tamiḻāynta taṟukaṇmit tamiḻaṉtāṉ tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ mutalamiccāy varutal vēṇṭum. pētikkilli ūtiyam; pēṭikkilli paṭikkalam; aṭimikkilli viṭutali; añcikkilli aṭikkalam.
ulakavaralāṟu (World History), kumukaviyal paṇpāṭṭiyal māntaṉūl (Social and Cultural Anthropology), oppiyaṉ moḻi nūl (Comparative Philology) eṉṉum mūvaṟiviyal tamiḻ viṭu taliyic cārntiruppatāl, tamiḻiyakkatti moḻi veṟiyeṉṟō iṉaveṟiyeṉṟō evaruñ collamuṭiyātu.
puraṭcip pāvēntaṉ kaṉavu
tamiḻi mūlamākak koṇṭa mēliyāriya vakiyāṉa kirēkkat tiṟku nerukkamāyiruntu, intiyāviṟku vantavuṭaṉ vaḻakkaṟṟup pōy vaṭatiraviṭa mākiya pirākirutattuṭaṉ iraṇṭaṟak kalantu vētamoḻiyāki, piṉṉart tamiḻi yaṇṭi ataṉāṟ peritum vaḷampaṭuttappaṭṭu aric ceyaṟki ilakkiya naṭimoḻi yāṉa camaṟkirutatti, ulaka aḷavil talimiyākkutaṟ poruṭṭu, oru putiya camaṟkiruta āṅkilap pērakara mutali uruvākkutaṟku, intiya naṭuvaṇaracum, marāṭṭira aracum, palkalik kaḻaka nalkik kuḻuvum, pūṉāp palkalik kaḻakamum, oṉṟiya nāṭṭiṉaṅkaḷiṉ amippum (U.N.O.), iru kōṭikku mēl otukkiyuḷḷaṉa.
tamiḻ nāṭṭilō, ulaka mutaṟṟāy uyartaṉic cemmoḻiyāṉa centamiḻc coṟpiṟappiyal akaramutali uruvākkavō, camaṟ kirutak kiḷiyeṉṟum paṉmoḻik kalaviyeṉṟum pirāmaṇap pulavar kāṭṭiyuḷḷa naccut taṉmi niṟinta ceṉṉip pal kalikkaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutaliyit tiruttavō, iṟṟiyaracu oru ciṟu tokiyum otukkavilli.
tamiḻnāṭṭit tamiḻnāṭeṉṟu araciyalēṭṭiṟ kuṟittata ṉālum, araciṉar cālik kaṭattap pēriyaṅkikaḷil (Govt. Road Transports Buses) ovvoru tirukkuṟaṭ palakiyi āṇi yaṟintu vittataṉālum, tamiḻ vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟum intiyi ṉiṉṟum viṭutali peṟṟu vaḷarntu viṭātu.
tamiḻaṉ uṇmiyāṉa iyalpi yaṟintu ati añcātu pōṟṟik kāppavaṉ tamiḻnāṭṭu āṭcit talivaṉākum pōtē, tamiḻāynta tamiḻaṉtāṉ tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ mutalamic cāy varutal vēṇṭum. eṉṉum pāratitācaṉ kaṉavu niṟi vēṟiyatākum.
puram
puram - va. pura (i. vē.).
puram = uyarcci, uyarnta kaṭṭiṭam, mēṉmāṭam, kōpuram, kōpura nakar. (piṅ.). maturiyantaṇ purattiṉkaṇ (tiruvālavā. 39 : 1). kōpuramuḷḷakōyil, kōyil, mūvarkkōtiya puramum (kallā. 24 : 23).  (va.va : 206 - 207).
purāṇam
purāṇam eṉpatu paḻimi. ātalāl paḻañceytikaḷip paṟṟik kūṟum nūl potuvākap purāṇam eṉappaṭum. māpurāṇam, pūtapurāṇam eṉpaṉa teṉṉāṭu vantu taṅkiya āriyar iyaṟṟiya paḻantamiḻ ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ.
piṟkālattil, (1) ulakap paṭippu, (2) ulaka aḻivum maṟu tōṟṟamum, (3) teyvaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻimarapu, (4) manukkaḷ āṭci, (5) katirava tiṅkaṭkula aracar varalāṟu ākiya aintiyuṅ kūṟuvatu purāṇam eṉa ilakkaṇam vakukkappaṭṭatu.
taruma cātiraṅkaḷ tamiḻari aṭimippaṭuttiṉaveṉiṉ, purāṇaṅkaḷ avari maṭamippaṭuttiṉa.
vaṭamoḻip purāṇaṅkaḷ muṉṉeḻuntavi 18; piṉṉeḻun tavi 18. piṉṉeḻuntavi upapurāṇam eṉappaṭum.

kirēkkamum āṅkilamum
Gk. pref. peri, round about.
perianth, floral envelope.
pericardium, membranous sac enclosing the heart.
pericarp, seed-vessel, wall of ripened ovary of plant.
perichondrium, membrane enveloping cartilages (except at joints).
periclinal (geol.), sloping in all directions from central point.
perigynous (of stamen), situated around pistil or ovary.
periosteum, membrane enveloping the bones,
peripteral (of temple), surrounded by single row of pillars.
perisperm, mass of albumen outside embryo-sac in some seeds.
peristome (bot.), fringe of small teeth around mouth of capsule in mosses, (zool). parts around mouth in various invertebrates.
perity phlitis, inflammation of some part around the caceum, e.g. appendicitis.
pericranium, membrane enveloping skull.
periapt, thing worn about the person as charm, amulet.
perigee, that point in planet’s (esp. moon’s) orbit at which it is nearest to earth.
perihelion, that point in planet’s orbit at which it is nearest to sun.
perimeter, circumference, onthire of closed figure.
period, round of time marked by recurrence of astronomical coincidences.  Gk. peri, round. hodos, way.
periodical, magazine published at regular intervals, e.g. monthly.
peripatetic, a. & n. Aristotelian, so called from Aristotle’s custom of walking about in Lyecum while teaching.
periphery, bounding line, esp. of round surface, external boundary or surface. Gk. periphercia, circumference, peri round, phero, bear.
periphrasis, roundabout way of speaking, roundabout phrase, circumlocution.
periscope, kinds of tube and mirror apparatus by which one in a trench or in a submarine can see things above the parapet or water respectively. periscopic, enabling one to see distinctly for some distance round axis of vision.
perispomenon (Gk. gram). word with circumflex accent on last syllable.
peristaltic (pysiol). applied to the automatic muscular movement consisting of wave-like contractions in successive circles, by which contents of alimentary canal etc. are propelled along it.
peristyle, row of columns surrounding temple, court, cloister etc, space so surrounded.
peritoneum (anat.), double serous membrane lining cavity of abdomen.
tiruntiya vaḻakkilum kocci vaḻakkilum akara mutal ekara mutalākat tirivatu iyalpē.
e-ṭu : paru - peru, araṅkaṉ - raṅkaṉ - reṅkaṉ. im muṟiyil pari eṉṉum tamiḻccol kirēkkattil peri eṉṟu tirintatu.
pari eṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭup peṟṟa cila coṟkaḷum tamiḻt tiri coṟkaḷāyiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
e - ṭu :	period = peri + hodos (pāti).
		Periphery = peri + phero (poṟu.) poṟuttal, 			cumattal.
puri eṉṉum tūya tamiḻccolliṉ teṉmiyum toṉmiyum muṉmiyum, ura(vu), kura(vu), curi, tiri, neri, muri eṉṉum piṟa mutaṉiliyeḻuttukkaḷik koṇṭa orupōkaṭikaḷālum uṇarappaṭum.
puru
puru - va. pruṇa (bh) - i. vē. L. embryo.
puru = kuḻanti. puru - piru - pirukku = iḷampiñcu.
o. nō : uru - aru - arumpu.
kuru - karu = cūl, pīḷ, muṭṭikkaru, muṭṭi, kuḻanti, kuṭṭi.
nuru = taḷir. nuru - noru = iḷampiñcu.
muru - muruku = iḷami.  (va.va : 207).
puruvam
puruvam - va. pru (bh) - i. vē.
purital = vaḷital. puri - purivu - (purivam) - puruvam. koṭum puruvaṅ kōṭā maṟippiṉ (kuṟaḷ. 1086).
ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutali vaṭacolli bhruva eṉṟa vaṭivilēyē kuṟikkiṉṟatu.  (va. va : 207). (ti.ma. 748.)

puṭi - puṇi - paṇi. paṇittal = 1. paruttal. paṇitta vemmuli (kamparā. eḻucci. 33). 2. ceḻittal (nālaṭi. 251).
paṇi = 1. perumi. paṇiyē piḻittal peruppumākum (tol. uri. 41). 2. parutta mūṅkil. perum paṇittōḷ (pu.ve. 11, āṇpāṟ. 1). 3. periya muracu. viyaṉpaṇi yurumeṉa vatirpaṭṭu (patiṟṟup. 39 : 5).
putu - potu = 1. palar kūṭiya kūṭṭam, maṉṟu, ampalam, makkaṭṭokuti. 2. maṉṟu kūṭum iṭam. 3. palarkkum allatu ellārkkum uriya nilimi. potu nōkkāṉ vēntaṉ variciyānōkkiṉ (kuṟaḷ. 528). 4. ciṟappiṉmi. potuk koṇṭa... vatuvi (kalit. 66). 5. iyalpu allatu vaḻakkam. uri potuvē (kamparā. mutaṟ. 172). 6. ellārkkum otta naṭuvunili.
ma. potu, te. potu (pottu).
potu - poruḷ. potuḷutal = 1. neruṅkutal. cikiyaṉal potuḷiyatāl (kamparā. intiracit. 20). 2. niṟital. ñāṉaṅ kuṇaṅkalvi nūl potuḷi (ari camaya. parakāl. 7). 3. taḻittal. iruḷpaṭap potuḷiya (tirumuruku. 10).
potu - potumpu = 1. cōli. kāntaḷam potumpil (akam. 18). 2. kuṟuṅkāṭu. mulliyam potumpiṉ (cīvaka. 3042).
potumpu - potumpar = 1. maracceṟivu. poḻiliṉ viyaṉ potumpariṉ (tirukkō. 39). 2. iḷamarakkā. veyiṉuḻi paṟiyāk kuyiṉuḻi potumpar (perumpāṇ. 374). 3. cōli.
potu - poti = 1. tokuti. peykaṇip potikaḷālē vaḷarntatu piṟanta kōpam (kamparā. nākapā. 114). 2. kottu. ampotit tōri (malipaṭu. 121). 3. uṭal. potiyē cumantuḻalvīr (tēvā. 1154 : 9). 4. parumaṉ. āḷ potiyāyirukkiṟāṉ. (u.va.). 5. ampalam. maṉṟam potiyiṉu māmayil cērtañci vāṇaṉ (tañcivā. 34). 6. potiyamali. potimāmuṉiva (civataru. pāva. 23).
poti - potiki = potiyamali.
poti - potiyam = potiyil. poṟkōṭṭimayamum potiyamum pōṉṟē (puṟam. 2).
poti + il - potiyamali = 1. ampalam. tamaṉiyap potiyilum (maṇimē. 28 : 66). 2. potiyamali. āay maḻitavaḻ potiyil (kuṟun. 84).
poti - potir. potirtal = 1. vīṅkutal. puṉitār pora nontu potirntaveṉ (cīvaka. 1380). 2. mikutal.
potirttal = paruttal. potirnta muliyiṭi (paripā. 
21 : 25).
potu - pottu - potti = parumiyāṉatu. pottikkāl = yāṉikkāl. potti miḷakāy = taṭitta miḷakāy. pottiyaṉ = taṭittavaṉ (yāḻ. aka.).
pottu - potti = 1. maṭalviriyā vāḻippū (yāḻp.). 2. cōḷakkatir (W.). 3. viti (aṇṭam). 4. varālmīṉ. mattiyuñ ciṟu pottiyum (kurukūrp paḷḷu).
pottup potteṉal = taṭittiruttaṟ kuṟippu. kuḻanti pottup potteṉṟirukkiṟatu (u.va.).
pottu - pontu. pontu ponteṉal = taṭittiruttaṟ kuṟippu.
pontaṉ = taṭittavaṉ. pontantaṭi = peruntaṭi (nelli). pontattaṭi = peruntaṭi (nāñcil).
pontāṉ potukkeṉal = taṭittum cati taḷarntum iruttaṟ kuṟippu.
pontāṉ - pōntāṉ = peruṅkōḻi.
pontu - ponti = parumi (caṅ. aka.).
ponti - pōnti = vīkkam (yāḻ. aka.). te. pōt.
pōntikkāl = yāṉikkāl. te. pōtakkālu.
potu - poti = ceṭiyaṭartti.
potu - pōtu - pōtā = perunāri (cilap. 10 : 117, uri).
poti - pōti = mali (piṅ.).
pul - pol - poli. polital = 1. perukutal (W.). 2. mikutal. kaḻunīr policta kaṇṇakaṉ poyki (maturik. 171). 3. ciṟattal. polinta varuntavat tōṟkē (puṟam. 1). 4. ceḻittal. poliyumālvari (tēvā. 236 : 8). 5. nīṭuvāḻtal. vaḻivaḻi ciṟantu polimiṉ (tol. cey. 109).
poli - polici = 1. ūtiyam. poṉpeṟṟa polici peṟṟār piṇiyaṉār (cīvaka. 2546). 2. vaṭṭi. policikkuk koṇṭavūrum (S.I.I. ii, 82). ma. polica.
poliyūṭṭu = policiyūṭṭu. (S.I.I. III, 84 : 88).
polici - palici = 1. ūtiyam. paliciyāṟ paṇṭam pakarvāṉ. (pu.ve. 12, veṉṟip. 2). 2. vaṭṭi. ip poṉ patiṉeṇ kaḻañcē mūṉṟu mañcāṭi kuṉṟikkum paliciyāṟ cantirātittaval ...... aṭṭuvōmāṉōm. (S.I.I.(i), 116). ma. palica.
poli - (poḷi) - poḻi. poḻital = (ce. kuṉṟāvi.) 1. tiraṇṭu peytal. kaḻintatu poḻinteṉa vāṉkaṇ māṟiṉum (puṟam. 203). 2. mikac celuttutal. iḷaṅkōḷari poḻintāṉ (kamparā. nikumpali. 118). 3. mikutiyāyk koṭuttal (cūṭā.). 4. taṭṭut taṭaṅkaliṉṟu virintu pēcutal.
(ce. ku. vi.). 1. niṟital. poḻimaṇit taṇṭūṇ (peruṅ. uñcik. 47 : 110). 2. niṟintoḻukutal purappōṉ pāttiram poḻintūṇ curantu (maṇimē. 14 : 49).
poḻittal = tiraṭṭutal.
poḻippu = tiraṭṭu. poḻippuri = tiraṭṭuri.
pol - polku - poluku. polukutal = atikappaṭutal. 
te. polacu (Z).
poḻi - poḻil = 1. perumi (piṅ). 2. cōli (piṅ.). kāvatap poḻiṟkappuṟam (kamparā. vaṉampuku 52). 3. pūntōṭṭam (tivā.). aṇimalarp pūmpoḻi lakavayiṉ (maṇimē. pati. 38). 4. ñālam. poḻil kāvalaṉ (pu. ve. 6 : 6). 5. ulakam. nalkittāṉ kāt taḷikkum poḻilēḻum (tiv. tiruvāy. 1 : 4 : 5). 6. nāṭu (cūṭā.). 7. nāṭṭiṉ kūṟu. nāvalan taṇpoḻil (perumpāṇ. 465).
kuṟippu :
1.paruttal eṉpatu oṉṟaṟkum palaviṉ īṭṭattiṟkum potuvām.
2.paruttal, mikutal, tokutal, ceṟital, niṟital eṉpaṉa ōriṉak karuttukaḷ.
pul3 (tiraṭṭaṟ karuttuvēr)
paruttaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu tiraṭṭaṟ karuttup piṟakkum.
poḻittal = tiraṭṭutal. poḻippu = tiraṭṭu, piṇṭam.
poḷi = tiraṇṭa varappu. poḷiyi veṭṭi iraṇṭu taḷiyum oṉṟākkiṉāṉ (W.).
varampu kaṭṭutal, varampu tiraṭṭutal eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka.
poḷi - poḻi = 1. kaṇu. poṟkuṭam poruntiya poḻiyami maṇittūṇ (peruṅ. uñcik. 48 : 87). 2. vayalvarampu, varappu.
pul - puḷ - piḷ - piḻampu = 1. tiraṭci. kaṭuntaḻaṟ piḻampaṉṉa (tiruvāca. 29 : 7). 2. vaṭivu. uṇmi yuṇarttip piḻampuṇarttap paṭātaṉa (iṟi. kaḷa. pa. 15). 3. uṭal. piḻampunaṉi yularttal (tol. puṟat. 20, uri.
piḷ - piṇṭu = 1. tiraṭci. 2. uṭampu. piṇṭālam vittiṉ (tiruman 3025).
o.nō : kaḷ - kaṇṭu, naḷ - naṇṭu, cuḷ - cuṇṭu, vaḷ - vaṇṭu.
piṇṭu - piṇṭam = 1. tiraḷi. 2. cōṟṟuttiraḷi. piṇṭa mēya peruñcōṟṟu niliyum (tol. puṟat. 8). 3. uṇṭi (piṅ.). 4. teṉpulat tārkkup paṭikkum cōṟṟuṇṭi. 5. catittiraḷ. uṟuppil piṇṭamum (puṟam. 28). 6. uṭampu. uṇṭi mutaṟṟē uṇaviṉ piṇṭam (puṟam. 18). 7. tokuti. parivutapa veṭukkum piṇṭa nacciṉ (puṟam. 184). 8. nūṟpirivup poruḷip potuppaṭat tokuttuk kūṟum nūṟpā. piṇṭan tokivaki kuṟiyē ceyki (naṉ. 20). 9. mūvuṟuppaṭaṅkiya nūl. mūṉṟuṟup paṭakkiya piṇṭattāṉum (tol. cey. 170).
o.nō : kaṇṭu - kaṇṭam, tuṇṭu - tuṇṭam.
te. piṇṭali, va. piṇṭa, ka. peṇṭṭe, heṇṭṭe, heṇṭe.
piṇṭakkaru = uṟuppaṟṟa catittiraḷāṉa karu (embryo, foetus).
piṇṭak kāppu = cōṟu. piṇṭak kāppukkut tāḷam pōṭukiṟāṉ (u.va.).
piṇṭak karumam = teṉpulattārkkup piṇṭam paṭikkum caṭaṅku.
piṇṭakaṉ = piṇṭam paṭippōṉ.
piṇṭa nūṟpā = atikārap poruḷi allatu nūṟpirivup poruḷit tokuttuk kūṟum nūṟpā.
piṇṭatāṉam = piṇṭap paṭippu.
piṇṭap poruḷ = karuttu (W.).
piṇṭap poḻippu = piṇṭavuri (ci.pō. pā. 1, pa. 26, cuvāminā).
piṇṭapākam = aiyam (picci) (aka. ni.).
piṇṭam piṭittal = (ce. kuṉṟāvi.). 1. uṇṭi tiraṭṭutal. 
2. uruccitittu uruṇṭi yākkutal.
(ce. ku. vi.). karu uṇṭātal (u.va.).
piṇṭam viḻutal = kāy viḻutal (abortion).
piṇṭavuri = poḻippuri (W.).
piṇṭu - piṇṭi. o.nō : kaṇṭu - kaṇṭi, tuṇṭu - tuṇṭi.
piṇṭi = 1. vaṭivam (cilap. 3 : 26, uri). 2. kūṭṭam (W.). 3. ēḻām nāḷ (puṉarpūcam) (cūṭā.). 4. iṇiyā viṉikki vaki (cilap. 3 : 18, uri).
piṇṭittal = (ce. kuṉṟāvi.). tiraḷi yākkutal. piṇṭittu vitta vuṇṭiyi (tol. puṟat. 8, uri). 2. tokuttal.
(ce. ku. vi.). tiraḷutal. piṇṭittup perukiṟ ṟeṉpār (cīvaka. 1276).
piṇṭi - piṭi. o. nō : kaṇṭi - kaṭi, taṇṭi - taṭi.
piṭittal = (ce. kuṉṟāvi). 1.kiyāl iṟukkit tiraṭṭutal allatu tiraḷiyākkutal. poriviḷaṅkāy piṭikka vēṇṭum. (u.va.). 2. kiyāl iṟukkit tiraṭṭip paṭimiyamittal. piḷḷiyār piṭikkak kuraṅkāy muṭintatu (paḻa.). 3. kiyāl iṟukkit tiraṭṭutal pōl oṉṟi iṟukap paṟṟutal. kuṭi piṭittāṉ (u.va.). 4. oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟip paṟṟutal. pūṉi eliyip piṭikkum, iṉṟikkuk kuḷattil mīṉ piṭikkiṟārkaḷ. ōṭukiṟa māṭṭip piṭittukkoṇṭu vantāṉ. ūrkāvalar kuṟṟavāḷiyip piṭittukkoṇṭārkaḷ. intap piḷḷiyik koñcanēram piṭi (u.va.). 5. kōṇiyip piṭittu vāṅkutal. tirunelvēlikku arici piṭikkap pōyirukkiṟārkaḷ. (u.va.). 6. kayiṟṟip piṭittu vāṅkutal. kāṅkēyattiliruntu ōru cōṭi māṭu piṭittu vantirukkiṟāṉ (u.va.). 7. kalattil nīr koḷḷutal. kuḻāyil nīr piṭittuvā (u.va.). 8. kaḻaṅku viḷiyāṭutal. taṭṭāṅ kal piṭittu viḷiyāṭukiṟārkaḷ. oṟṟiyā iraṭṭiyā piṭikkalāmā? (u.va.). 9. poruttutal. nāṟkālik kālukku vāṅku piṭikka vēṇṭum (u.va.). 10. aḻuttit taṭavutal. uṭampu piṭikkiṟavaṉ iṅkē irukkiṟāṉā? 11. cuḷukkutal. kālil narampu piṭittukkoṇṭirukkiṟatu (u.va.). 12. iṟukkamāyiruttal. caṭṭi mutukiṟ piṭikkiṟatu (u.va.). 13. uḷḷaṭakkutal. antap peṭṭi ellār uṭuppiyum piṭikkum (u.va.). 14. oṭṭikkoḷ ḷutal. pēṉ piṭitta tali (u.va.). 15. tāṅkutal. muṭṭukkaṭṭi cuvarip piṭittuk koṇṭirukkiṟatu (u.va.). 16. oḷippaṭam eṭuttal. paṭam piṭikkum iṭam (u.va.). 17. paṟṟik koḷḷutal. yār cēliyiliruntō cāyam piṭittuviṭṭatu (u.va). 18. paṟṟi yerital. kuṭiciyil tīppiṭittuviṭṭatu. 19. aṭittal. viti piṭitta kāḷi. 20. kuṟittal. campaḷattiṟ pattu rūpā piṭittuk koṇṭārkaḷ (u.va.). 21. niṟutti yaṭakkutal. callikkaṭṭil māṭu piṭippārkaḷ (u.va.). 22. aṭital. perukiya celvanī piṭi (kamparā. kiḷikāṇ. 23). 23. aṭin tēṟutal. maturi pōy vaṇṭiyip piṭittāṉ. 24. collip payaṉpaṭuttal. avaṉ coṉṉa collip piṭittukkoṇṭāṉ. 25. akappaṭuttu tal. avaṉ piṭikoṭukka māṭṭāṉ (u.va.). 26. vacappaṭuttutal. avaṉip piṭittāl itu eḷitāy muṭiyum (u.va.). 27. paṟṟuk kōṭākak koḷḷutal. añceḻuttiṉ puṇipiṭittuk kiṭakkiṉṟēṉi (tiruvāca. 5 : 27). 28. pukalaṭital. uṉṉi nāṉ piṭittēṉ koḷ (tiv. tiruvāy. 2 : 6 : 1). 29. mēṟ koḷḷutal. vicuva rūpam piṭittāṉē (irāmanā. cuntara. 19). 30. uṇarntu koḷḷutal. āciriyaṉ kaṟpittati māṇavar piṭittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum (u.va.). 31. kuṟikkoḷḷutal. piṭittatu muṭikkavalla vicciyar (pirapōta. 24 : 52). 32. kaṭippiṭittal. perumāṉuri piṭittēm (kamparā. nikum. 143). 33. oṭṭāram paṇṇutal. orē piṭiyāyp piṭikkiṟāṉ (u.va.).
(ce. kuṉṟiya vi.) 1. oṭṭikkoḷḷutal. aḻukkup piṭitta tuṇṭu. 2. tōṉṟutal. maḻiyiṟ pācam piṭikkum (u.va.). 3. viruppamātal. eṉakkum avaṉukkum piṭikkātu (u.va.). 4. ēṟṟatātal. antavūrt taṇṇīr eṉakkup piṭikkavilli (u.va.). 5. celavātal. tirumaṇattiṟku āyiram urupā piṭikkum (u.va.). 6. nikaḻtal. malinakaril mālitoṟum maḻi piṭikkum (u.va.).
k., ka. piṭi.

ka. hiṭitta.
piṭitta = (pe.) oru kippiṭip poruḷ. piṭitteruvum vēṇṭātu cālappaṭum (kuṟaḷ. 1037). 2. toṭaṅki. eṉ piṟappē piṭittu (īṭu, 2 : 1 : 1).

k., ka. piṭippu.
piṭimāṉam = 1. piṭittam. 2. piṭippu. 3. kippiṭi. 4. uṟuti.

k., bj., ka. piṭi.
piṭikkompaṉ (ciṟu kompuḷḷa vilaṅku). piṭikkoḻuk kaṭṭi, piṭikayiṟu, piṭikal, piṭikācu, piṭikāraṉ, piṭikiḻaṅku, piṭittarāvi, piṭitta vērppaṅku, piṭittāṭṭikkaḻi, piṭicciṟṟuḷi, piṭiccīli, piṭicāmpal, piṭicuṭṭup paṭittal, piṭicuvar, piṭicuṟṟutal, piṭicempu, piṭicōṟu, piṭittapiṭi, piṭittāṭi (palakaṟi), piṭitaṇṭam, piṭitam (aiyam), piṭināḷ (naṉṉāḷ), piṭinel, piṭippiṭṭu, piṭipāṭu, piṭimaṇ, piṭimīci, piṭiyarici, piṭiyal (ciṟutukil), piṭiyaṉam (kuṟitta aḷavu koḷḷum pāṉi), piṭiyāḷ, piṭiyeḻuttāṇi mutaliya coṟkaḷ toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varuvaṉa.
kipiṭittal (kikkoḷḷutal). kippiṭi (kiyiṟ peṟṟuk koṇṭa poruḷ), kipiṭicuvar mutaliya kūṭṭuc coṟkaḷum, arivāṭpiṭi, aṟuvāṭpiṭi, uḷippiṭi, kattippiṭi, kuṭip piṭi, koṭṭuppiṭi mutaliya piṭivakip peyarkaḷum; uṭumpup piṭi, kaṭippiṭi, kuraṅkuppiṭi, kuruṭṭuppiṭi, viṭāppiṭi mutaliya piṭippup peyarkaḷum toṉṟutoṭṭaṉavē.
piṇṭam eṉṉum teṉcol, vaṭamoḻi marapiṉpaṭi īṟukeṭṭup piṇṭa eṉa irukku vētattil niṉṟu, tiraḷi, uṇṭi eṉṉum poruḷkaḷik kuṟikkiṉṟatu. ataṉiṉṟu, piṇṭatva, piṇṭak, piṇṭaṉa mutaliya taṉic coṟkaḷiyum; piṇṭakanta, piṇṭakaraṇa, piṇṭatarkkaka mutaliya kūṭṭuc coṟkaḷiyum, ēṟattāḻa eḻupatuvari tirittuḷḷaṉar.
aṅṅaṉamē, piṇṭi eṉṉum teṉcol vaṭiviṉiṉṟu, piṇṭita, piṇṭiṉ, piṇṭī, mutaliya taṉiccoṟkaḷiyum; piṇṭitamūlya, piṇṭitārtta, piṇṭīkaraṇa, sapiṇṭīkaraṇa, piṇṭīpāva (bh) mutaliya kūṭṭuc coṟkaḷiyum; ēṟattāḻa muppatu vari tirintuḷḷaṉar.
āriyattiṟkut tamiḻoṭu allatu tiraviṭattoṭu toṭar pilliyeṉṟum, camaṟkirutam ēṟattāḻa ainnūṟu coṟkaḷi maṭṭum tiraviṭattiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ koṇṭuḷḷa teṉṟum, kūṟum parō eṉṉum camaṟkirutap pērāciriyar kūṭa, piṇṭam eṉṉum col teṉcollē yeṉṟu tam camaṟkiruta moḻi (Sanskrit Language) eṉṉum nūliṟ piṉvarumāṟu kuṟittiruttal kāṇka.
“Pinda lump, clod’: Ka, petta, pette, pente, hente, hende, clod, lump of earth’, Te, pedda, pella ‘id’, pendali ‘a lump or mass’, Ka., Te, pindu to squeeze together”.
o. nō : piḻi. piḻital = uḷḷirukkum nīrpporuḷ vaṭiyumāṟu, oṉṟi iṟukkip piṭittal.
piṇṭam allatu piṇṭi eṉpatu vaṭacollāyiṉ, piṭi eṉṉum viṉiccollum, ataṉ nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa tiri coṟkaḷum kūṭṭuccoṟkaḷum vaṭacollām. piṭi eṉpatu, kumari nāṭṭil tōṉṟiya aṭippaṭit tamiḻccoṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟātalālum, irukkuvētam mikap piṟkālattatātalālum, piṇṭam allatu piṇṭi eṉpatu teṉcollē yeṉpatu teritaru tēṟṟamām.

o.nō : peṭṭu = viruppam E. pet.
“pet, n., & v.t. (-tt-). 1. Animal tamed & kept as favourite, or treated with fondness; darling, favourite, (often attrib); one’s ... aversion, what one specially dislikes; ... cock, small stop-cock for drining, letting out steam, etc; - name, one expressing fondness or familiarity.
2. v.t. Treat as a ..., fondle; ... ting party (colloq.), social gathering of young people at which hugging, kissing, etc., are indulged in. (16th C.Sc. & north., orig. unkn.)”  (The Concise Oxford Dictionary.)

pul5 (vaḷitaṟ karuttuvēr)
pul - pur - piraḷ. puraḷutal = 1. uruḷutal. aṭittup puraṇṭu viḻuntāṉ. kaḻuti puraṇṭa kaḷamāyp pōyviṭṭatu (u.va.). 2. cuṟṟutal (W.). 3. alimaṟital. puraṇeṭun tirikaḷam (kamparā. vipīṭaṇa. 27). 4. kari kaṭantōṭutal. āṟu karipuraṇṭōṭu kiṟatu (u.va.). 5. māṟi māṟi varutal. veyilkaḷum nilākkaḷum puraḷa (kamparā. piramā. 99). 6. māṟupāṭaṭital. 7. coṟpiṟaḻtal. 8. cātal. kaḻuttilē puṇṇākip puḻuttup puraṇṭāṉ (kuruparam. 165. āṟā.). 9. paṇam virintu tokutal allatu kimāṟutal.
ma. puraḷuka, ka. poraḷ, te. peralu.
puraḷ - puraḷi = 1. poy. 2. vañciṉi. 3. kuṟumpu (W.). 4. kalakam (W.).
puraḷ - puraṇṭi - piraṇṭi = tirukalāṉa koṭivaki.
ma. piraṇṭa.
puraḷ - puraṭṭu. puraṭṭutal = 1. uruṭṭutal. muṭiyuṭik karuntali puraṭṭu muṉṟā ḷukiruṭi yaṭiya (paṭṭiṉap. 230). 2. kīḻmēlākat tirupputal. 3. kaṟi mutaliyavaṟṟik kiṇṭi vatakkutal. kaṟiyip puraṭṭu, kattirikkāyip puraṭṭiyiruntatu (u.va.) 4. uṇṭapiṉ vayiṟṟiṟ kōḷāṟu nērtal. vayiṟṟip puraṭṭukiṟatu (u.va.). 5. coṟporuḷ tirittal. 6. vañcittal (W.). 7. nilattiṟ paṭivittu aḻukkākkutal. putuc cēliyik kīḻum mēlumākap puraṭṭukiṟāḷ (u.va.).
ma. puraṭṭuka, ka. poraḷcu.
puraṭṭu - puraṭṭal = kuḻampuṅ kūṭṭumiṉṟik kaṭṭiyākac camitta kaṟivaki.
puraṭṭu = 1. kīḻmēlākat tiruppuki. 2. coṟporuḷ tirippu. puṟappaṭṭār puraṭṭup pēci (tiruvālavā. 38 : 14). 3. vañcakam. 4. kumaṭṭal. 5. vayiṟṟuvali. 6. kaṟic camiyal vaki.
puraṭṭaṉ = 1. coṟporuḷ tirippōṉ. 2. māṟāṭṭakkāraṉ.

(aka. ni.).
ka. pari. pari - va. pari.
“Pari, ind. round, around, about, round about; fully, abundantly, richly (esp.) ibc. (where also pari) to express fullness (or high degree), R.V. & c. & c. as a prep. (with acc.) about (in space and time), R.V. , A.V., aginst opposite to, in the direction of, towards, to, ib. (of. Pam. i, 4, 90; also at the beginning of a comp. mfn. cf. ib. ii, 2, 18, Vartt. 4 Fat. and Pary adhyayana), beyond, more than, A.V., to the share of (with as or bhu, to fall to a person’s lot.). Pam i, 4, 90, successively, severally (e.g. vriksham pari sincati, he waters tree after tree), ib; (with abl.) from, away from, out of, R.V.; A.V.; SBr. (cf. Pam, I, 4.93); outside of, except, Pam, 1,4,88, Kat. (often repeated, ib. viii, 1,5; also at the beginning of or the end of am ind. Comp., ib. II, 1, 12); after the lapse of Mu. III, 119; MBh. XIII, 4672 (some read parisamvatsarat); in consequence or on account or for the sake of, R.V; A.V.; according to (esp. dharmanas pari, according to ordinance or in conformity with law or right),  R.V.; (cf. Zd. piri, Gk. pari)”.
- A Sanskrit English Dictionary by Sir Monier Monier Williams, p. 591.
“peri, pref. = Gk. peri round, about, as : perianth, floral envelope;- carditis n., inflammation of - cardium; - cardium, membranous sac enclosing the heart, so - cardiac, - cardial, aa.; pericarp, seed-vessel, wall of ripened ovary of plant........”
- The Concise Oxford Dictionary

2. vaṭṭak karuttiṉiṉṟu muḻumik karuttup piṟakkum. e-ṭu : kaṭaṉi vaḷḷitāyk koṭuttut tīrttuviṭṭāṉ. vaḷḷitu = vaṭṭamāṉatu, muḻumiyāṉatu.
E. roundly = in thorough - going manner.

piṭuku - piṭuṅku. piṭuṅkutal = (ce.ku.vi.). 1. uṭital. veḷḷattāl kuḷakkari naṭuviṟ piṭuṅkiviṭṭatu (u.va.). 2. aṭippu vilakutal, taṇṇīrt toṭṭiyil aṭippup piṭuṅki viṭṭatu (u.va.). 3. veṭittal. kuḻiyil allatu kuḻāyil veṭimaruntu piṭuṅki viṭṭatu (u.va.). 4. veḷippaṭutal. veḷikkuppōkātu nīṇṭa nēram aṭakki vittataṉāl piṭuṅkiviṭṭatu (u.va.).
(ce.kuṉṟāvi.). 1. paṟittal. kīri piṭuṅkutal (u.va.). 2. valintu kavartal. avaṉ kiyilirunta paṇappiyip piṭuṅkik koṇṭāṉ (u.va.). 3. peyarttal. āṟu aṇiyip piṭuṅkik koṇṭu pōkiṟatu. yāṉi maratti vērōṭu piṭuṅkiviṭṭatu (u.va.). pallip piṭuṅkip paruntāṭṭa māṭṭi (taṉippā.). 
4. kottit tiṉṉutal. piṇam piṭuṅkik kaḻuku (u.va.). 5. kaṭittal. antak kuḷattiṟ kuḷittāl mīṉ piṭuṅkiviṭum. 6. iḻuttal allatu piyttal. uṭumpuk kaṟi tiṉṟavaṉ vāyāleṭuttāl uṭcatiyip piṭuṅkik koṇṭu varum (u.va.). 7. paci vāṭṭutal. paci vantu piyttup piṭuṅkukiṟatu (u.va.). 8. tontaravu ceytal. vīṭṭiṟkup pōṉāl maṉivi makkaḷ piṭuṅkal veḷiyē vantāṟ kaṭaṉkāraṉ piṭuṅkal (u.va.).

kuṟippu : tuḷittal eṉpatu, kuḻittal, kuṭital, tōṇṭutal, tuḷiyiṭutal, turuvutal eṉṟum; kuttutal, veṭṭutal, kiḷḷutal, kīṟutal, cīvutal, citittal, aṟuttal, kiḻittal, piḷattal, uṭittal, pakuttal (pirittal), tuṇṭittal, (tuṇṭupaṇṇutal), poṭittal eṉṟum; kulittal, kalittal eṉṟum; viṉikkum karuvikkum ēṟṟavāṟu palatiṟappaṭum.
māḻiyum (metal) kallum maramum pōṉṟa vaṉporuḷkaḷ karuvikaḷālum, kaṉikaḷum uṇṭi vakikaḷum kaḷimaṇṇum pōṉṟa meṉporuḷkaḷ karuviyālum kiyālum tuḷikkap paṭum.
cila poruḷkaḷ, takka nilimi variṉ, nilam veṭippatum kaṉipiḷappatum pōla, tāmākavē tuḷiyuṇṇum.
kuḻittal kuṭital pōṉṟaṉa āḻavāṭṭil tuḷittal; veṭṭiṭutal, aṟuttal pōṉṟaṉa nīḷavāṭṭil tuḷittal.
kuḻittaluṅ kīṟutaluṅ kuṭitalum pōṉṟaṉa kuṟiyat tuḷittal; turuvutalum veṭṭutalum piḷattalum pōṉṟaṉa niṟiya allatu muṟṟat tuḷittal.
muṟṟat tuḷittalāl pakuttal, pirittal, tuṇittal, poṭittal mutaliyaṉa ēṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

paṇṇi2 = 1. marutanilam. vaḷanīrp paṇṇiyum vāviyum (cilap. 11 : 13). 2. vayal (piṅ.) paṇṇicūḻn tilakun tirumuṉip paḻiya nāṭu (pārata. pāyi. 6). veḷḷanīrp paṇṇiyum virinī rēriyum (cilap. 13 : 192). 3. nīrnili (piṅ.). 4. marattiṟku aṭiyil nīr pāyccumāṟu cuṟṟivarat tōṇṭi vikkum paḷḷam. marattiṟkup paṇṇi piṭikka vēṇṭum (u.va.). 5. nilakkiḻārē ceyyum payirttoḻil (C.G.). 6. vārakkuṭi. 7. neṟkuttumāṟu naṭuviṟ paḷḷañceytu nilattiṟ patikkappaṭṭa vaṭṭakkal. 8. pallāṅkuḻi viḷiyāṭṭiṟku nilattil tōṇṭappaṭum kuḻi.
ka. paṇṇeya.

paṇṇi - paṇi1 = 1. vilaṅku tuyiliṭam (piṅ.). 2. yāṉi kutirikaḷ taṅkumiṭam. paṇinili muṉiik kaḷiṟupaṭin tuṇṭeṉa (puṟam. 23), paṇiyami pāymāṉ (kalit. 57).
paṇi2 = 1. marutanilam. perun taṇpaṇi pāḻāka (puṟam. 16). 2. vayal. irumpaṇi tiraṅka (patiṟ. 43.12). 3. nīrnili (piṅ.).
paṇ - paṇi. paṇital = 1. tāḻtal. paṇiyiya rattiniṉ kuṭiyē (puṟam. 6), 2. cerukkiṉṟiyaṭaṅkutal. ellārkku naṉṟām paṇital (kuṟaḷ. 125). 3. iṟaṅkutal (W.). 4. tāḻvātal. 5. kuṟital. 6. eḷimiyātal. 7. vaṇaṅkutal. uṭiyāṉ kaḻal paṇintili (tiruvā. 4 : 35). paṇintavaṉ = kuḷḷaṉ (W.).
ma. paṇiyuka.
paṇittal = 1. tāḻttutal. vēntaṉ vēṟṟavarp paṇippa (pu.ve.4 : 9, koḷu). 2. kuṟittal (W.). 3. mitittal, nantu māmiyum paṇittu (cīvaka. 2109). paṇiviṭi = toṇṭu, kuṟṟēval. tiruppaṇi = teyvat toṇṭu.
paṇi - paṭi. paṭital = 1. aṭiyil taṅkutal. vaṇṭal paṭintirukkiṟatu (u.va.). 2. taṅkutal. paṟavi paṭivaṉa vīḻa (neṭunal. 10). 3. vayamātal. aṭiyāta māṭu paṭiyātu (paḻa.). 4. kīḻppaṭital. periyōrkkup paṭintu naṭattal vēṇṭum (u.va.). 5. kuḷittal. taṭaṅkaṭaliṟ paṭivām (tiruvāca. 38 : 9). 6. amuṅkutal. eṇṇey tēyttuc cīviṉāl mayir paṭiyum (u.va.). 7. taṇital, veḷḷam paṭintatu (u.va.). 8. kīḻē viḻuntu vaṇaṅkutal. cirantalattuṟap paṭintu (upatēcakā. civattu. 344).
paṇi ceyvōṉ = 1. vēlikkāraṉ. 2. viḻākkālattil tāriyum, iḻavunāḷil caṅkum ūtupavaṉ. kīḻppaṭital = coṉṉapaṭi ceytu paṇivāy naṭattal.
paṇi - pāṇi, pāṇittal = 1. kālat tāḻttal. pāṇinī niṉcūḷ (paripā. 8 : 56). 2. kāttiruttal. pāṇiyē meṉṟār (kalit. 102). 3. piṉvāṅkutal. camariṟ pāṇiyāṉ (kantapu. mūvāyirar. 59).
pāṇi = paḷḷamāṉa iṭattil taṅkum allatu paḷḷam nōkki ōṭum nīr. viṇṇiyal pāṇiyaṉ (patiṉo. poṉvaṉ. 30).
ka. paṇi, marā. pāṇi, i pāṇī.
nīrik kuṟikkum pāṇi eṉṉuñcol vaṭamoḻiyil illi. nīram eṉṉum tamiḻccol vaṭamoḻiyil nīra eṉṟu kaṭik kuṟintu vaḻaṅkumpōtu, pāṇi (nīr) eṉṉum col intiyil pāṇī eṉṟu tirintu vaḻaṅkuvatu iyaṟkikku māṟaṉṟu.
paṇ - paṇṇal = 1. vayalit tiruttutal. 2. payir ceytal. paṇṇiya payiril puṇṇiyan teriyum (paḻa.). 3. vēli ceytal. 4. cevviyā yamittal. 5. aḻakupaṭuttutal. 6. aṇiya mākkutal (āyattañ ceytal). 7. iciyeḻuvum eṇvakiyuḷ oṉṟu. paṇṇal.... eṇvakiyā liciyeḻīi (cilap. 7 : 5-8).
paṇ - paṇṇu. paṇṇutal = 1. payir ceytal. 2. vēli ceytal. 3. ceytal umpark kiṭantuṇṇap paṇṇappaṭum (nālaṭi. 37). 4. cevvippaṭuttutal. 5. aḻakupaṭuttutal. paṭṭamo ṭilaṅkap paṇṇi (cūḷā. kalyā. 14). 6. aṇiyamākkutal. pūnutal yāṉiyoṭu puṉitēr paṇṇavum (puṟam. 12). 7. yāḻ narampil alaku (curuti) amittal. 8. paṇṇiṟkēṟpa yāḻ narampamittal. marutam paṇṇiya karuṅkōṭṭuc cīṟiyāḻ (malipaṭu. 534). 9. camittal. pālu mitaviyum paṇṇātu peṟukuvir (malipaṭu. 417). 10. uṇṭākkutal. pāṉi caṭṭi paṇṇukiṟavaṉ kuyavaṉ. (u.va.).

kiyik kuṟikkum pāṇi eṉṉuñ col vaṭamoḻiyiṟ ciṟappāka vaḻaṅkiṉum, ataṟku mūlamākak kāṭṭappaṭum paṇ eṉṉuñ colliṟku, paṇṭamāṟṟutal, paṇiyam vittal, pēram pēcutal eṉṉum poruḷkaḷē uḷa. avi poruntu vaṉavalla.
entap poruḷiyum toḻiliyuñ ceytaṟkuk ki iṉṟiyamiyāta uṟuppātalāl, ceytalik kuṟikkum colliṉiṉṟu kippeyar amivatē iyaṟkikkup poruttamām. paṇ (paṇṇu) - pāṇ - pāṇi = (paṇṇi).

karu - va. kru - kara = ki. karuttal ceytal. ivviṉiccol kaṭikkaḻakak kālattiṟku muṉpē vaḻakkaṟṟup pōyiṟṟu. karumam, karuvi, karaṇam mutaliya coṟkaḷi nōkkuka.

paṇṇal - pāṭa niṉinta paṇṇukku iṇi kiḷi paki naṭpāṉa narampukaḷ peyarun taṉmi māttiri yaṟintu vīkkutal (cīvaka. 657, nac. uri).
o. nō : cey - ceyyuḷ.
vallōr aṇipeṟac ceyvaṉa ceyyuḷ  (naṉ.268).
ceyyuḷ icippāviṟku iṉamāṉatē. pāṭṭu, pāṇi, tēvapāṇi, peruntēvapāṇi eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ, ceyyuṭkum icippāviṟkum potuvāka vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.

6. perumpoḻutu. ennila maruṅkiṟ pūvum puḷḷum annilam poḻutoṭu vārā vāyiṉum (tol. akat. 19). 7. nēram. niṟparavu pūciyiṉum poḻutu pōkkeṉak karuḷ purivāy (cilap. pirapan. cōṇa. 29). 8. vēḷi. kalanta poḻutuṅ kāṭciyum aṉṉa (tol. akat. 16). 9. amiyam. nāṉ niliyatti aṭintapoḻutu vaṇṭi varavilli (u.va.). 10. noṭi. orupoḻutum vāḻva taṟiyār (kuṟaḷ. 337). 11. taṭavi, muppoḻutu maruntu (u.va.). 12. takka camiyam allatu kālam. ikalvellum vēntarkku vēṇṭum poḻutu (kuṟaḷ. 481). poḻutoṭu puṇartal (piṅ.). 13. pakal allatu iravu. oru poḻutu vēli, orupoḻutu uṟakkam.

poku - paku.
kālṭuvelār tam tiraviṭa oppiyal ilakkaṇattiṉ iṟutip pakutiyil, colliyal uṟavukaḷ (Glossarial Affinities) eṉṉum talippiṉ kīḻ,
“bhaj, to share.
bhajga, a portion. I am doubtful whether to regard these words as derived from the Tamil pag-u, to divide, to share, or to suppose both the Sanskrit and the Tamil to be derived from a common and earlier source. Probably the former supposition is in this case the more correct. At all events the Tamil - Malayalam pag-u is a pase, underived Drividian root. A noun former from it, signifying a share, is pang-u (ng for g, as is after the case); and a collateral root is pag-ir, meaning also to share. The Sanskrit word pangu means lame, and is altogether unconnected with the Tamil one. Other derived nouns are pagal, a division daylight; pal (=pagal), a portion; and padi (pagudi). half” - (Dravidian Comparative Grammar, p. 573). eṉṟu kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.

(ce. kuṉṟāvi.). pākam pirittal pakuntuṉakku vittakō laṟikku (tiruvālavā. 30 : 50).

paku - pakavu = 1. piḷavu. 2. veṭippu (yāḻ. aka.). 3. paṅku (yāḻ. aka.). 4. tuṇṭu, tuṇikki. eṭpaka vaṉṉa ciṟumittē yāyiṉum (kuṟaḷ. 889). 5. pakuppu.
pakavu - pakavaṉ = ellā vuyirkaṭkum uṇavip pakuttaḷikkum iṟivaṉ. 2. civaṉ (tivā.). 3. tirumāl (piṅ.). 4. teyvamāka vaṇaṅkappaṭum katiravaṉ (piṅ.). 5. āriyat teyvamākiya piramaṉ (piṅ.). 6. camaṇarkkut tēvaṉ pōṉṟa arukaṉ (tivā.). 7. putta neṟiyārkkut tēvaṉ pōṉṟa puttaṉ (tivā.). 8. iṟivaṉiṭat tiṟku vaḻikāṭṭum matakkuru (piṅ.). 9. iṟivaṉaṭiyārum muṟṟat tuṟanta muḻu muṉivarum. pattarkaḷum pakavarkaḷum (tiv. periyāḻ. 4 : 9 : 6).
tirukkōvilkaḷilum maṭaṅkaḷilum paṇṇiyāriṭattum vēli ceyvārkkellām, campaḷamāka nelli aḷantu koṭuppatē paṇṭi nāḷ vaḻakkam. atu paṭiyaḷattal eṉṟu collappaṭum. ataṉāl, vēliyilamartti vāḻkkikku vēṇṭum campaḷam koṭuppārk kellām, paṭiyaḷappār eṉṉum peyar uṇṭāyiṟṟu. ellā vuyirkaṭkum uṇavip pakuttaḷippavaṉ eṉṉuṅ karuttil iṟivaṉum paṭiyaḷappāṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. namakkup paṭiyaḷappār nāriyōr pākar (taṉippā. 1, 121 : 5).
ellāmvalla oru taṉi iṟivaṉukkuriya pakavaṉ eṉṉum peyar, ciṟuteyvaṅkaṭkum ciṟanta makkaṭkum nāḷaṭivil vaḻaṅkat talippaṭṭuviṭṭataṉāl, tiruvaḷḷuvar tam tirukkuṟaṭ kaṭavuḷ vāḻttil, muḻumutaṟkaṭavuḷik kuṟikka ātipakavaṉ eṉṟu aṭi koṭuttuk kūṟa vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. āti eṉpatu mutal eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum vaṭanāṭṭuc col. atu muntu tiraviṭamākiya pirākirutac collāyirukkalām. ātipakavaṉ eṉṟatu, ātiyeṉṉum pulicciyi maṇanta oru pirāmaṇaṉi eṉiṉ, āti caṅkarāccāriyār eṉpatum ātiyeṉṉum pulicciyi maṇanta caṅkarāccāri yāri eṉṟa kūṟal vēṇṭum. atu poruḷaṉmi aṟika.
kārkālam, muracukaṭṭil, kōvūrkiḻār eṉpaṉa pōla, ātipakavaṉ eṉpatum valimikātu vaḻaṅkiya orucila puṇarc coṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟākum. ataṉāl, āti yeṉpatu vaṭacol (camaṟkirutac col) eṉṟu koḷḷa vēṇṭuvatilli. mēliyāriya moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟilum āti eṉṉuñ colliṉmiyiṉ, atu vaṭacollākātu.
pakavaṉ - va. pakavāṉ (bhagavan).
paku - pakal = 1. pakukki (piṅ.). nerunip pakaliṭaṅ kaṇṇi (puṟam. 249). 2. (oru poruḷ iru camappākamākap pakum) naṭu (tivā.). 3. naṭuvunilimi. akalviyattup pakalāṟṟi (patiṟ. 90 : 9). 4. naṭu nukattāṇi. neṭunukattup pakalpōla (paṭṭiṉap. 206). 5. irunāḻiki koṇṭa muḻuttam (mukūrttam). oru pakalkāṟu niṉṟāṉ (cīvaka. 2200). 6. naṇpakal, uccivēḷi. 7. naḷḷiruḷ. yāmattum pakalun tuñcār (cilap. 81). 8. kāli mutal māli variyuḷḷa kālam. pakal viḷaṅkutiyāṟ palkatir virittē (puṟam. 8). 9. aṟupatu nāḻiki koṇṭa nāḷ. olva koṭāa toḻinta pakalum (nālaṭi. 169). 10. katiravaṉ. paṉmalarp pūmpoḻiṟ pakaṉmuḷit tatupōl (maṇimē. 4 : 92). 11. oḷi viḷakkam (tivā.).
pakal - pakar. pakartal = oḷiviṭutal. pakkaṅ karuñciṟup pāṟi mītē yaruvikaḷ pakarntaṉiya (tiv. periyāḻ. 1 : 7 : 8).
pakar = oḷi. cintāmaṇikaḷ pakarallip pakalcey tiruvēṅkaṭat tāṉē (tiv. tiruvāy. 6 : 10 : 9).
pakal - pāl. 1. pirittuk koṭukki. pāluṅ koḷālum vallōy (patiṟ. 16 : 19). 2. pakuti aṟiyeṉa varūum pālvari kiḷavikku (tol. toki. 23). 3. pāti. pāṉā ḷiravil (kalit. 90). 4. pakkam. orupāṟ kōṭāmi (kuṟaḷ. 118). 5. toḻil vakuppu. vēṟṟumi terinta nāṟpā luḷḷum (puṟam. 183).

pakir - vakir = 1. piḷavu. 2. kīṟu (W.). 3. piḷanta tuṇṭu. māviṉ vaṭuvaki raṉṉa kaṇṇīr (tiruvāca. 9 : 2). 4. ucciyeṭuppu. vārnta neṭuṅkūntal vakiriṉāḷ (veṅkiyu. 304). 5. tōlvār (yāḻ. aka.). 6. vārkkaccu (W.). 7. narampu (yāḻ. aka.). 8. vaḻi (piṅ.).

tiripāki (muppāki) = mūveḻuttukaḷ koṇṭa oru col, ataṉ mutaleḻuttiyum iṟuti yeḻuttiyuñ cērkka maṟṟoru collākiyum, iṭiyeḻuttiyum kaṭi yeḻuttiyum cērkka maṟṟumoru collākiyum vantu, vevvēṟu poruḷ tarumāṟu pāṭappaṭum collaṇi (taṇṭi).
itu muppākiyeṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum. tiri eṉṉum vaṭamoḻi eṇṇup peyar, muppuriyākat tirikkappaṭum nūl allatu kayiṟu eṉṉuṅ karuttiṉiṉṟum tōṉṟiyirukkalām. ēka eṉṉum vaṭamoḻi mutaleṇṇup peyar, okka eṉṉum teluṅkuc colliṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyiruttal kāṇka.

patti (varici) - va. paṅkti (pankti).
patti (periyōr māṭṭup paṟṟu) - va. pakti (bhakti).
iṅṅaṉam iruvēṟu vakiyil vaṭamoḻiyil eḻutap paṭuvatāl, patti eṉpatu iruvēṟu collō eṉṟu aiyuṟaṟka. pakti eṉṉum colliṟkum pākam eṉṉum colliṟkum vaṭamoḻiyāḷar paj (bhaj) eṉṉum orē mūlaṅkāṭṭuvatiyum, paku eṉṉum mutaṉiliyiṉiṉṟē tirintuḷḷa pakkam eṉṉum colli avar pakṣa eṉṟu tirippatiyum, nōkkuka.
iṅṅaṉam, orē mutaṉiliyiṉiṉṟu tirintuḷḷa palvēṟu tiri coṟkaḷi, eḻuttumāṟṟi vevvēṟu mūlattiṉiṉṟu tirinta coṟkaḷākak kāṭṭuvatu, vaṭamoḻiyāḷar vaḻakkam.
muttam eṉṉum teṉcolli mukta eṉṟum tirittatu pōṉṟē, patti eṉṟa teṉcolliyum pakti eṉṟu tirittuḷḷaṉar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar.
patti - panti = makkaḷ uṇṭaṟku amarum varici allatu varicit tokuti. pantikku muntu, paṭikkup pintu (paḻa.). 2. vilaṅkukaḷi niṟuttum oḻuṅku. paṇṇami vayapparikaḷ pantiyi ṉirittār (kamparā. varikkāṭci. 13). 3. kutiric cāli. ivuḷikaḷ kaṇṇu pantiyiṟ kaṭṭat takumeṉa (tiruvālavā. 27 : 16). o. nō : kottu - kontu.
panti - pirā. pantti - va. paṅkti.
