pū : pūtam = peruveḷiyil tōṉṟiya nālvaki allatu aivakip poruḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.
vaḷiyeṉa varūum pūtak kiḷaviyum  (tol. 242).
pālvari teyvam viṉiyē pūtam
pālpirin ticiyā vuyartiṇi mēṉa.  (tol. 541).
pū : pū, (va. bhu), iccolliṉiṉṟē puvana, pūmi, paviṣya, pāvana. mutaliya muṉṉoṭṭup peṟāc coṟkaḷum, anupava, anupūti, utpava, svayampū mutaliya muṉṉoṭṭup peṟṟa coṟkaḷumāka nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa vaṭacoṟkaḷ tōṉṟumeṉa aṟika.  (ti.ma : 749).
pūkkāmpu vaki
kāmpu ciṟiyatu;
tāḷ allatu taṇṭu, parumiyum, meṉmiyumuḷḷatu.
nāḷam, uruṭṭuḷiyuḷḷatu.  (col. 67).
pūk kutiri viḷiyāṭṭu
āṭṭiṉ peyar : oru pūp peyaric colli, oruvaṉmē loruvaṉ kutiriyēṟi viḷiyāṭuvatu pūkkutiri.
āṭuvār toki : potuvāka, aivarkku mēṟpaṭṭa palar iti āṭuvar.
āṭiṭam : muṟṟattilum teruvilum piṟa veḷiyiṭaṅkaḷilum iti āṭalām.
āṭu muṟi : ētēṉumoru tērnteṭuppu vakiyil, tavaṟi viṭṭa oruvaṉ, aṇṇāvipōl niṟkum oruvaṉiṭam maṟivāka oru pūppeyaric colliviṭṭuk, kuṉintu niṟka vēṇṭum. piṟar ovvoruvaṉāy avaṉ mēṟ kutiriyēṟumuṉ, ovvoru pūppeyari aṇṇāviyiṭam veḷippaṭiyāyc colla vēṇṭum. yārēṉum oruvaṉ coṉṉa pū kuṉintavaṉ coṉṉatā yiruppiṉ piṉpu avaṉ aṇṇāviyiṭam oru pūp peyari maṟivākac colliviṭṭut tāṉ kuṉiya vēṇṭum. muṉpu kuṉintu niṉṟavaṉ, piṉpu piṟaroṭu cērntu muṉ coṉṉavāṟu viḷiyāṭuvāṉ. kuṉintavaṉ collāta vēṟu pūp peyar collik kutiriyēṟiṉavaṉ, oru nimiyattiṟku iṟaṅkiviṭal vēṇṭum. kuṉintavaṉ coṉṉa pū varumvariyum, ovvoruva ṉākavum māṟi māṟiyum ēṟi yiṟaṅkik koṇṭē yiruppar.  (ta.nā.vi.)
pūcci viḷiyāṭṭu
pūcci eṉpatu āḷniḻal. nilavoḷiyiṟ pūcci teriyumpōtu oruvarit toṭum viḷiyāṭṭu, pūcci allatu pūcci viḷi yāṭṭu. āṭuvāruḷ ētēṉumoru vakiyil akappaṭṭuk koṇṭa oruvar, nilavoḷiyiṭattil niṟka, ēṉiyarellām arukēyuḷḷa ōr iruṇṭa iṭattil niṉṟukoḷvar. iruṇṭa iṭattil niṟpavar oḷiyiṭattiṟku variṉ, avarit toṭalām; illāviṭiṉ toṭal kūṭātu. oḷiyiṭattil toṭappaṭṭavar piṉpu piṟarit toṭutal vēṇṭum.  (ta.nā.vi.)
pūcu
pūcu - va. pūj, pūci - va. pūjā
pūcutal = kaḻuvutal. nīruṇṭār nīrāṉvāy pūcupa (nāṉ maṇik.35).
mukampūcutal eṉpatu teṉpāṇṭi vaḻakku.
pūcu - pūci = teyvap paṭimiyi nīrāṭṭuc ceyyum vaḻipāṭu.
pūcu - pūcuṉi. ciṟappoṭu pūcuṉi cellātu (kuṟaḷ. 18).
pūci - pūciyāri - pūcāri. taliyāri eṉṉuñ colliṟpōl āri eṉpatu ōr īṟu.
pūci
pūcutal = kaḻuvutal. teyvap paṭimiyi nīrāl tuppuravākkutal. 
pūcu - pūci. pūcittal = pūccāttiyum tēṅkāy paḻam mutaliyaṉa paṭittum vaḻipaṭutal. uḻavu eṉpatu payirt toḻiliṉ piṉ viṉikaḷiyum kuṟittal pōl pūcittal eṉpatum vaḻipāṭṭiṉ piṉviṉikaḷiyum kuṟittatu.
pūci - pūci. o.nō : ācu (paṟṟu) - āci (aḷavu) - āci (avā).
pūci - pūcaṉi, pūcaṉam. ai, aṉi, aṉam eṉpaṉa tamiḻ īṟukaḷē.
pūci - pūcāri. āri - taliyāri eṉpatil pōl ōr īṟu.
pūcāccāri (pūci + āccāri) eṉṉum vaḻakku vaṭamoḻiyilum illi.
pū cey eṉpatu pūci eṉṟāyiṟṟeṉpatu poruntātu.
pūci - pūj (va.). pū - pūjā (va). pūcaṉam - pūjaṉa (va.).
pūcaṉi - pūjaṉā (va.).
vēta āriyarkku vēḷḷi vēṭṭalēyaṉṟip paṭimappūciyum kōvil vaḻipāṭum illi. pūj eṉṉum collum vētattil illi. pāratam mutaliya piṟkāla vaṭapaṉuvalkaḷilēyē atu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.	(ti.ma. 47).
pūtam
pūtam : pūttal = tōṉṟutal.
aviyañci meyvitirppār kalviyuṅ kallār
aviyañcā vākulac cellum - naviyañci
īttuṇṇār celvamu nalkūrntā riṉṉalamum
pūttaliṟ pūvāmi naṉṟu.  (nītineṟi. 5).
ippāviluḷḷa pūttaṟ col vēṟu; malartalik kuṟikkum pūttaṟ col vēṟu. malartalik kuṟippatu polivi yuṇarttum pol eṉṉum aṭiyiṉiṉṟu tirintatākum.  (ti.ma. 749).
pūtam1 - va. pūta (bh) - i.vē.
pūttal = tōṉṟutal. pū - pūtu - pūtam = tōṉṟiyatu, aimpūtaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.
vaḷiyeṉa varūum pūtak kiḷaviyum  (tol. 242).
pūtam2 - va. pūta (bh)
ūtāṅkuḻalāl neruppūtuvatip pūttup pūtteṉa ūtukiṟāṉ eṉpatu vaḻakku. kāṟṟūtuvatāl turuttiyum ūttāmpiyum puṭikkum.
ūtutal = vīṅkutal, paruttal.
ūtu - pūtu - pūtal - pūtali. pūtalittal = paruttal, taṭittal.
pūtu - pūtam = paruttatu, parutta pēy.
pūtakkāl = yāṉikkāl. pūtakkaṇṇāṭi = perukkikkāṭṭum. kaṇṇāṭi.  (va.va.)
iṉi, pottu - potu - putu - pūtu - pūtam eṉṟumām. putā = perunāri. pottu - potti = parumiḷakāy.
putal - putar = aṭar ceṭi. puti = aṭarkāṭu, ampukkaṭṭu.
puti - pūti = ampuk kaṭṭu. pūti = potu. potu - potumpu - potumpar.
mā. vi. a. vum ce. pa. ka. ka. ta. a. vum iruvēṟu pūtac colliyum oṉṟākak kūṟiyiruppatu tavaṟām.  (va.va : 208 - 209).
pūti
pūti - va. pūti (bh)
puḻuti - pūti = 1. puḻuti (cūṭā). 2. cāmpal. 3. tirunīṟu (piṅ.). pūtiyaṇi poṉṉiṟattar (tēvā. 592 : 2). 
o.nō : koḻutu - kōtu, poḻutu - pōtu.  (va.va : 209).
pūppaṟikka varukiṟōm viḷiyāṭṭu
potuvākap patiṉmarukkuk kuṟiyāta pala piḷḷikaḷ uttikaṭṭic camamāṉa irukaṭciyākap pirintu, or akaṉṟa teruvilēṉum ūrppoṭṭa lilēṉum iru kaṭcikkum potuvāka oru naṭukkōṭu kīṟi, ataṟku ippālum appālum niṉṟukoḷvar. piṉpu irukaṭciyārum kaṭcivāriyākak kikōttu muṉpiṉṉākap piṉvarumāṟu pāṭikkoṇṭu, māṟimāṟi naṭukkōṭu vari ceṉṟu mīḷvar.
mutaṟ kaṭciyār : pūppaṟikka varukiṟōm. pūppaṟikka varukiṟōm, inta nāḷilē.
iraṇṭāṅ kaṭciyār : yāraṉuppap pōkiṟīr? yāraṉuppap pōkiṟīr? inta nāḷilē.
mu : kamalā aṉuppap pōkiṟōm,
	kamalā aṉuppap pōkiṟōm, innāḷilē.
i : entappū vēṇṭum?
	entappū vēṇṭum? inta nāḷilē.
mu : mallikippū vēṇṭum,
	mallikippū vēṇṭum, inta nāḷilē.
iṉṉapū vēṇṭumeṉṟu colli mutaṟkaṭciyār pāṭi muṭin tatum, irukaṭciyiṉiṉṟum ovvoru piḷḷi muṉceṉṟu naṭukkōṭṭi yaṭuttavuṭaṉ, iruvarum oruttiyi yorutti piṭittiḻuppar. kōṭṭiṟkappāl iḻuttuk koḷḷappaṭṭa piḷḷi etirkkaṭciyic cērum. piṉpu muṉpōṟ pāṭi, vēṟiruvar iḻuppar. iṅṅaṉam ivviruvarāy ellārum iḻukkappaṭṭapiṉ, mikutiyākap piḷḷikaḷ cērntirukkiṟa kaṭciyār veṉṟavarāvar.
pūmaṭal vaki
pū : vāḻi maṭal;
maṭal : tāḻi, vāḻi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ maṭal;
pāḷi : teṉṉi, paṉi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ maṭal.	(col. 66).
pūviṉ nilikaḷ
arumpu : pūviṉ tōṟṟanili;
pōtu : pū viriyat toṭaṅkum nili;
malar (alar) : pūviṉ malarnta nili;
vī : marañceṭiyiṉiṉṟu pū kīḻē viḻunta nili;
cemmal : pū vāṭiṉa nili.  (col. 67).
pūṉippeyarkaḷ
(1) pūci
pūṉi eṉpatu pūci eṉṉum colliṉ tiripē. pūcutal = kaḻuvutal, cilikaḻuvutal. nelli māvaṭṭattiṉ orupakutiyil, mukam pūcutal eṉpatu iṉṟum ulaka vaḻakkāyirukkiṉṟatu. pūcikkoḷiṉum irumpiṉkaṇ mācoṭṭum (nāṉmaṇi. 94).
pūṉi aṭikkaṭi taṉ eccili muṉṉaṅkālāl toṭṭu mukattip pūcikkoḷvatāl, atu pūciyeṉap peyar peṟṟatu. vevvāy vekukiṉip pūciyeṉṟalum (tol. mara. 69). vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa mārjāla eṉṉum pūṉip peyarum ikkāraṇam paṟṟiyatē. meḻuku - va. mruj (mrj) = pūcu, kaḻuvu. mruj - mārj - mārjāra - mārjāla.
pūṉiyip pūcupūcu eṉṟaḻippatu peṇṭir vaḻakkam. pūci - pūcci (nāñcilnāṭṭu vaḻakku) pūcci - ma. pūcca. pūci - pūṉi. ca - ña, pōlittiripu. o.nō : pici - piṉi.
pūṉikkaṇ = pūṉikkaṇ pōṉṟa niṟamuḷḷa oḷikkal (viṭūriyam). āṅkilattilum itu cat’s eye eṉṟu peyar peṟṟiruttali nōkkuka. viṭūriyam eṉṉum vaṭacollum ik kāraṇam paṟṟiyatē.
pūṉi - pūñi o. nō : aṉṉi - aññi. iṉi, pūci - pūñi eṉṟalumām. o. nō : kuṭici - kuṭiñi. cilampi ciccili pūñi kirumi (tattuvap. 19).
āṅkilac col (The Oxford English Dictionary and Klein’s Comp. Ety. Dy. of the E. Language).
E. puss, pus, pusse. (A word common to several Teutonic languages, usually as a call-name for the cat).
Du. poes, LG. puus, puuskatte, puus-man, Sw. dial. pus, katte-pus, Norw. puse, puus, Lith. puz, puiz, lr. and Gael. f. us.
Alb piso, Ruman pisicaÍ.
‘Puss in the corner’, a game of children; Pussy - wants - a - corner, an American name of the game.
E. pussy, a pet name for a cat. Y dim suffix.
Pussy - cat, a nursery word for a cat.
Pussyfoot, to tread stealthily like a cat.
Pussy - willow, the American glucous willow, in reference to its silky catkins.
Puss - Etymology unknown eṉpatu O.E.D.; Imitative of the spitting of a cat eṉpatu K. C. E. D. E. L.
2. piḷḷi (pūṉippiḷḷi)
piḷḷiyillātavarkaḷ, cila uyiriṉaṅkaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟip piḷḷipōl vīṭṭil vittu vaḷarppatu paṇṭi vaḻakkam. ataṉāl, irukkum piḷḷi mūṉṟu, ōṭum piḷḷi mūṉṟu, paṟakkum piḷḷi mūṉṟu. eṉṟu oru paḻamoḻi yoḻuntatu. ōṭumpiḷḷi mūṉṟaṉuḷ, aṇiṟpiḷḷi kīrippiḷḷi eṉpaṉa iraṇṭu. mūṉṟām piḷḷi pūṉippiḷḷi yāyiruk kalām.
piḷḷi kuḻavi kaṉṟē pōtteṉak
koḷḷavum amiyum ōraṟi vuyirkkē.  (tol. mara. 24).
nāyē paṉṟi pulimuyal nāṉkum
āyuṅ kālik kuruḷi yeṉpa.  (tol. mara. 8).
nariyum aṟṟē nāṭiṉar koḷiṉē.  (tol. mara. 9).
piḷḷip peyarum piḻippāṇ ṭilli
koḷḷuṅ kāli nāyalaṅ kaṭiyē.  (tol. mara. 11)
kōṭuvāḻ kuraṅkum kuṭṭi kūṟupa.  (tol. mara. 13).
mukavum piḷḷiyum paṟaḻum pārppum
aviyum aṉṉa appā lāṉa.  (tol. mara. 14).
pārppum piḷḷiyum paṟappavaṟ ṟiḷami.  (tol. mara. 4).
eṉṟu tolkāppiyaṅ kūṟuvatāl, palvēṟu uyiriṉaṅkaḷilum ciṟcila uyirikaḷ piḷḷi yeṉṟum iḷamip peyar peṟṟiruntami aṟiyappaṭum.
vīṭṭup pūṉikkuṭṭi pālūṭṭi vaḷarkkappaṭuvatu perum pāṉmi. ātalāl, pūṉippiḷḷiyeṉpatu, peruvaḻakkup paṟṟi, piḷḷiyeṉṉum potup peyarālēyē kuṟikkappaṭ ṭiruppatu iyalpu. piḷḷipōl vaḷarkkap paṭuvatu, peritāṉa piṉpum appeyar peṟum. aṇiṟpiḷḷi kīrippiḷḷi mutaliya peyarkaḷi nōkkuka.  (kuṭṭi eṉpatu āṭṭuk kuṭṭiyiyum, kaṉṟukkuṭṭi eṉpatu māṭṭuk kaṉṟiyum, ciṟappākak kuṟittaliyum nōkkuka).
piḷḷi yeṉṉuñcol teluṅkiṟ pilliyeṉṟu tiriyum. 
o.nō : taḷḷi (tāy) - talli. pilli - in. pillī (billi). pilli yeṉpatu ilattīṉil felis allatu feles eṉṟu vaḻaṅkum. uyartiṇiyil irupāṟkum potuvāṉa piḷḷi yeṉṉum iḷamippeyar, ilattīṉil filius eṉṟu makaṉiyum, filia eṉṟu makaḷiyum kuṟittu vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka. piḷḷi eṉpatu, vaṭārkkāṭṭu āmpūr vaṭṭattil āṇpiḷḷiyiyum, pāṇṭināṭṭup pakutiyiṟ peṇpiḷḷiyiyum, ciṟappākak kuṟippatu, vaḻakku vēṟupāṭu paṟṟiyatē.
pūci allatu pūṉi yeṉpatu vīṭṭup pūṉiyiyē. kāṭṭup pūṉi veruku eṉappaṭum. veruvattakka tōṟṟamum valimiyum uṭiyatu veruku. veruvutal - añacutal. veruku vīṭṭil vaḷarkkappaṭṭa piṉ pūciyeṉap peyar peṟṟatu. vevvāy verukiṉip pūci yeṉṟalum eṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟutal kāṇka. vīṭṭuppūṉi ilattīṉil felis domes tiea eṉappaṭum.
Feline (adj) = catlike. Felid = one of the Felidoe or cat-tribe.
(3) viṭarakaṉ
viṭutal = piḷattal, viṭu - viṭar = piḷavu, piḷappu, viṭaravaṉ = (pakaliṟ kaṇpiḷavuḷḷa) pūṉi (yāḻ. aka.). viṭaravaṉ - viṭarakaṉ - viṭarakam - viṭarukam - viṭarūkam = pūṉi(nāmatīpa. 203)
viṭarakam - viṭārakam - va. viṭāraka = pūṉi.
viṭārakam - viṭālakam = pūṉi.
viṭālakam - viṭālam = pūṉi (caṅ. aka.).
viṭālam - va. viṭāla - viṭāla = pūṉi.
viṭārakam - viṭāram - va. viṭāra - viṭūrya = pūṉikkaṇ pōṉṟa oḷikkal.
viṭūrya - ta. viṭūryam.
tamiḻiṟ pūṉikkaṇ eṉṟum āṅkilattil cat’s eye eṉṟum uṟuppit teḷivākak kuṟippiṭṭa uvamiyākupeyar tōṉṟiyuḷḷatu. camaṟkirutac collō pūṉippeyariṉiṉṟē tirinta tiricollākavuḷḷatu. va. viṭāraka - piṭāraka (b), va. viṭāla - piṭāla (b). vakaramutal vaṭamoḻiyiṟ pakaramutalākat tirital iyalpē. o. nō : valam - va. pala (bala).
(4) ka. kotti = pūṉi.
ma. koṟṟi, kuṟiññi, (kuṟuññi) = peṇpūṉi.
tamiḻil iccol  iṟantupaṭṭatu. paḻañcēra nāṭṭut tamiḻiṉ (vaṭacoṟ ceṟinta) tiripē maliyāḷamātalāl, koṟṟiyeṉ patit tamiḻākak koḷḷalām.
kuṟu, kuṟukuṟu, koṟukoṟu (ma. kuṟuṭṭuka - kuruṭṭuka, kuṟummuka; ka. kor, kora, korakora) mutaliya olikkuṟippukkaḷi yotta pūṉiyaṭittoṇṭi yolik (purr) kuṟippiṉiṉṟu kuṟuññi (- kuṟiññi) eṉṉum pūṉippeyar tōṉṟiyirukka lām. itaṟku koṟṟi yeṉṉuñ colloṭu toṭarpilli.
tamiḻiṟ kuruṭaṉik kuṟikkak kotti eṉṟoru col uḷatu. kottikku mūṅkar vaḻikāṭṭuvittu (tēvā. 1040 : 2), iṉṉāṉ eṉpatu, kaṇṇāṟ kotti, kālāṉ mūṭavaṉ eṉpaṉa. (tol. vēṟ. 11, iḷam. uri). kottiyeṉṉuṅ kaṉṉaṭaccol kottiyeṉṉum tamiḻccollip peritum ottuḷatu. pūṉi pakalil muḻukkaṇṇum teriyāmal arik kuruṭāyiruppataṉāl, ataṟkuk kottiyeṉṉum peyar ēṟpaṭṭirukkalām. appeyar oru tiraviṭa moḻiyiṟ kottiyeṉṟu tirintiruppatum, uttikkum iyaṟkikkum poruttamāṉatē. koṟṟiyeṉpatu kottiyeṉpataṉ tiripāyirukkalām.
pūṉikku mēlē moḻikaḷellāvaṟṟilumperuvaḻakkāy vaḻaṅkum peyar, palvēṟu vakikaḷil tirintiruppiṉum kotti eṉṉum colliyē ottuḷḷatu.
Gk. katta, kattos; mod. Gk. gata; L. catta, catus, caÍtus, cattus, Late Latin. cattus, catta.
It. gatto; Sp., Pg. gato; Cat. gak; Pr. Cat; ONF. cat; F. chat (fem. gatta, gata, cata, cate, chate, chatte);
E. cat; OE., ME. cat, catt, catte., ON. Kott-r, kattus; keta, fem; Sw. katt, katta; Da kat; MLG. katte; MDu. katte, kat; D. kat; OHG. kazza, chazza, chataro; MHG., Mod. G. katza; MHG. katero, kater; mod. G. and Du. kater = he cat.
OIr. cat (masc); Gael. cat com. W. kath; Welsh and Cornish. cath; Breton kaz; Vannes kac’h m. OCo. kat.
Nubian kadis; Arab qitt, tomcat, qitta, cat.
Slavonic kot; OSlav. kot’ka; Bulg. kotka; Slovenish kot m; Russ. kot m. kotchka, koshka; Pol. kot (koczur m.); Boh. kot m. kotta f.; Sorabian kotka; Lith. kate; Finnish katti.
Derivatives.
Chatoyant, of a changeable colour, shining like the eyes of a cat.
caterpillar fr. LL. catta pilose = hiry cat.
caterwaul fr. LG. caterwanlen = to cry like cats.
meerkat fr. MDu. meer catte = sea cat.
catteny = place where cats are bred.
kitten, catkin, cattish, catishly, catishness, catling, catty, cattily, catliness etc.
airōppāvil ki.pi. 3ām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari pūṉiyirunta tilliyeṉṟum, atu ekipatu nāṭṭiliruntu aṅkuk koṇṭu varappaṭṭateṉṟum, cat eṉṉum ataṉ peyar ekipatiyac colleṉṟum, mākkacu mullar kūṟukiṉṟār. pūṉi nīṇṭa kālamāka ekipatu nāṭṭil vaḷarkkappaṭṭu vantataṉālum, ōr ekipatiyap peṇ teyvam pūṉittali koṇṭiruntata ṉālum, airōppāviṟku ekipatu aṇmiyiliruppataṉālum, avar kūṟṟup poruttamākavē tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. āyiṉ, kumari nāṭē māntaṉ piṟantakamātalālum, tamiḻakattiṟku ekipati ṉōṭu nīṇṭa kālamāka nīr vāṇikamum nilavāṇikamum iruntu vantataṉālum, veruku tamiḻakak kuṟiñcinilak karup poruḷāta lālum, pūṉi tamiḻakattiṉiṉṟē ekipatuviṟkuc ceṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum. ataṉāl ataṉ peyarum tamiḻc collāyē yiruttal vēṇṭum.
mākkacu mullar pūṉip peyariyum pūṉik kaṇṇiyum paṟṟik kūṟiyiruppatiṉ (On the Name of the Cat and the Cat’s eye) cila pakutikaḷ varumāṟu:-
“Our domestic cat came to us from Egypt, where it had been tamed by a long process of kindness, or,  it may be, of worship. If no classical writer, Greek or Roman, do we find the cat as a domestic animal before the third century A.D. It is first mentioned by Caesarius, the physician, brother of Gregory, the theologian of Nazianzus, who died 369 A.D. He speaks of  ‘Katti endrumo’, About the same time Palladius ............ writes “contra ... ponunt” .... it is clear that when Palladius wrote (fourth century A.D.) tame mustele were still more common than cats, whether called cati or catti.
“Evagrius scholasticus (Hist. Eccl. 17, 23), about 600 A.D., speaks of katta as the common name of ‘ailouros’, here meant therefore, for cat....
“And Isidorus, his contemporary, expresses himself in the same sense when saying (12, 2, 38). ‘hune ............ catum ............ vocant.
“If we admit, in the absence of evidence to the contrary effect, that the tame cat came from Eqypt to Greece and Italy in the fourth century A.D., and that the shrewd little animal was called by the Romans catus, everything else becomes intelligible.
“In the ruins of Pompeii, where the bones of horses, dogs and goats have been found, no bones of cats have hitherto been discovered.....
“In the language of Romania no traces exist of the word catus, probably becuse at the time when that Romanic dialect became settled in Dacia, catus did not yet exist as a Latin name for cat.
The Romans did not transfer the name of Mustela to the cat, but by a kind of popular etymology, changed cattus into catus, and these two names, katta and catus, found their way afterwards into nearly all the languages of Europe”.
“We now come to the question, whether cat was known at an early time in India. The two principal words in Sanskrit for cat are ‘maÍrjaÍra’ and ‘vidaÍla’.
“MaÍrjaÍra means the cleaner, the cat being well known for its cleanliness.
“The second name for cat in Sanskrit is vidaÍla or bidaÍla....”
“It is difficult to analyse this word. I thought at first that it might be connected with vidaÍla (bidaÍla, in the At, AÍr. III. 1, 2, 6) which means cut in half, split in the middle, which would be a very appropriate term for a caÍt’s eye. But this would leave the lingual of  unaccounted for. In the UnaÍdi-Sutras (I. 117) it is derived from vid, to shout with the suffix aÍla. This suffix shows a certin analogy with aliga in marjaliya, another name for cat....”
“VaiduÍrya and VaiduÍrya, the very form that would best correspond to the Greek ailouros means in Sanskrit the cat’s eye. The cat is called man´i vidurya lokana, ie. having eyes like the Vaid´uÍrya jewel. It is true that so ancient a grammarian as PaÍn´ini (IV. 3, 84) derives Vaid´uÍrya from viduÍra, ‘very distant,’ and that accordingly it is often spelt with a dental d. But this seems an after-thought. The transition of Vaid´aÍrya into Vaid´uÍrya is not impossible, even in Sanskrit, if we remember such parallel forms as dura and daviyas, sthuÍla, stahviÍyas, & c.....”
“It was objected by KaÍtyayana that PaÍn´ini rule (PaÍn. IV. 3, 84), according to which Vaid´uÍrya is formed from ViduÍra, must be wrong, becuse the Vaid´uÍrya jewel does not come from ViduÍra, but from BaÍlavaÍya, and is only cut or polished at ViduÍra....”  (What Can India Teach Us? - pp. 261 - 268.)
itiṉiṉṟu viṭūrya eṉpatu vitūra (mikat tolivāṉa) eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyil pāṇiṉiyāl tirikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu tavaṟu eṉpatu, teḷivākum. vaṭamoḻic ceṉṟa teṉcoṟkaḷiyellām, poruntap poyttalāka vēṟu mūlaṅkāṭṭi maṟippatu, vaṭamoḻiyāḷar vaḻakkamē.
viṭāraka eṉpatu piṭāraka (b) eṉṟum viṭāla eṉpatu piṭāla (b) eṉṟum, pakara mutaṟ collākat tirikkappaṭṭiruppatē vaṭacolliṉ piṉmiyik kāṭṭum.
piṭāla (bid´aÍla) eṉṉum colliṉkīḻ, “also written vid´aÍla, of doubtful origin” eṉṟu māṉiyar villiyamcu tam camaṟkiruta āṅkila akaramutaliyiṟ kuṟittiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.  (ta.ta.nā.co.)
peṭṭakam (1)
peṭṭakam - pēṭṭaka
piḷḷutal = piḷattal, virital, akalutal.
piḷ - piḻā = 1. vāyakaṉṟa ōlikkuṭṭāṉ. (nelli vaḻakku). malarvāyp piḻāviṟ pulara vāṟṟi (perumpāṇ.276). 2. iṟikūṭi. ōṅkunīrp piḻāvum (cilap. 10 : 111).  (va.va : 209).
peṭṭakam(2)
peṭṭakam - pēṭṭaka
peḷ - pēḻ = akaṉṟa. pēḻvāy = akaṉṟavāy. pēḻvāy...... pēymakaḷ (tirumuka. 47).
pēḻ - pēḻi = 1. peṭṭi. aruṅkalap pēḻi (cīvaka. 557).
2. kūṭi. 3. marakkalam (Arc).  (va.va : 210).
peṭṭi
peṭṭi - pēṭṭ
vaṭamoḻiyil ekaramiṉmiyāṟ peṭṭi pēṭṭi eṉṟāyiṟṟu.
peṭṭi - peṭṭakam = 1. peṭṭi. āṅkilaṅku maḷapparum peṭṭakam (tiruvālavā. 27 : 22). 2. kaṭṭuppeṭṭi.  (va.va : 209).
peyar
peyar eṉṉum col mutalāvatu ōr āṉi viḷittaṟkō oru poruḷic cuṭṭuvataṟkō iṭappaṭṭa collip potuppaṭak kuṟippatākum. atu piṉṉar ākupeyarāy oru peyarāl viḷikkappaṭum, āḷiyum kuṟikkum.
iruvar mūvar mutaliya eṇṇaṭi uyartiṇip peyarkaḷi iraṇṭu pēr mūṉṟu pēr eṉṟu colvatu ulakavaḻakku. pēr eṉpatu peyar eṉpataṉ marūu.  (col. 35).
peyar viṉi
peyar viṉi iṭi uri eṉṉum nālvakic coṟkaḷuḷ mutaliraṇṭē talimiyāṉavi.
peyar eṉpatu āḷiyum viṉi eṉpatu  āḷiṉ ceyaliyum kuṟippaṉa.  (col. 34).
periya purāṇam
piṟappāṟ ciṟappilli : ellāvakuppārum vīṭupēṟṟiṟkuriyar; illaṟattālum vīṭu peṟalām eṉṉum tamiḻk koḷki kaḷiyum kaṟṟataṉā lāya payaṉeṉkol vālaṟivaṉ, naṟṟāḷ toḻāar eṉiṉ eṉṉum tirukkuṟaṭkaruttiyum; aṟivu, aṉpu, toṇṭu īkam (tiyākam) eṉpaṉavē vīṭupēṟṟu nālvāyil eṉpatiyum naṭimuṟic ceytiyāl viḷakkik kāṭṭuvatum nāṭṭuvatum periyapurāṇam oṉṟē.  (ta. i. va. 81).
periyōric cuṭṭāmi
periyōrik kiyiṉāṟ cuṭṭuvatu avamatippeṉṟu karuti aṅṅaṉam ceyvatilli. cuṭṭa nērnta viṭattuk kiyāṟ cuṭṭātu collāṟ cuṭṭuvatu vaḻakkam. aṅṅaṉam collāṟ cuṭṭum pōtu avarkaḷ ivarkaḷ eṉṉum paṭarkkic collālaṉṟi, nīr nīṅkaḷ eṉṉum muṉṉilic collāṟ cuṭṭak kūṭāteṉṟu koṇṭu, tāṅkaḷ eṉṉum paṭarkkic collāṟ cuṭṭuvar. atuvum nāḷaṭivil muṉṉilit taṉmiyaṭintu viṭṭateṉṟum, mikap periyōrkku accol pōtāteṉṟum karuti, maṭattampirāṉāri aṅkuttiyeṉṉum paṭarkkic collāl muṉṉilip paṭuttik kūṟuvatu civamaṭa marapu.
aṅkutti (aṅkuṟṟi) yeṉpatu avviṭam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum col. tampirāṉoṭu uriyāṭupavar nīṅkaḷ eṉṉum poruḷil iccolli vaḻaṅkuvar.  (col. 113).
peru
peru (ku) - va. prah (b) - i. vē.
pal - paru - peru - peruku.
peruṅkati - pruhat katā.
peruvuṭiyār = pruhat īsvara  (va.va : 210).
peruntiṇi
āṭavaṉ peṇṭu ākiya iruvarukkum kātal illātiruppatu allatu oruvariyoruvar valintu koḷvatu. ....... poruntāk kāmam eṉṉum collap peṟum.
peruntiṇi perumpakuti. makkaḷ maṇa muṟikaḷuṭ perum pālāṉavaṟṟit taṉṉuḷ aṭakki niṟpatāl, poruntāk kāmam peruntiṇiyeṉappaṭṭatu. kavarvu maṇaṅkaḷum, iyaṟ kikku māṟāṉa ellāp puṇarcci vakikaḷum peruntiṇiyē. kātaloṭu peruntāmiyum neṟiyoṭu poruntāmiyum paṟṟi, poruntiṇi poruntākkāmam eṉappaṭṭatu.  (ta.ti.16).
perun tēva matam
aintiṇit teyva vaṇakkaṅkaḷuḷ, iraṇṭē matamāka vaḷarcci yaṭintaṉa. cēyōṉ vaṇakkattiṉiṉṟu civa matamum, māyōṉ vaṇakkattiṉiṉṟu tirumāl matamum, tōṉṟiṉa. viṇṇulaka vēntaṉ koḷkiyiṉpiṉ eṅkum niṟinta iṟivaṉ koḷki ēṟpaṭṭapōtu, tīyiṉ kūṟākac civaṉ eṉṟum nīriṉ kūṟāka māl (māyōṉ) eṉṟum, peyariṭṭu iṟivaṉi vaḻipaṭṭaṉar.
(1) civamatam
iṟivaṉ peyar
cēyōṉ, civaṉ eṉṉum iru coṟkaḷum, orē mūlattiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟic civantavaṉ eṉṉum poruḷik koṇṭaṉa.
cul - cuḷ. cul - culli = aṭuppu, aṭukkaḷi. cuḷ - cuḷḷi = kalamuñ ceṅkalum cuṭum aṭuppup pōṉṟa kāḷavāy.
cul - cel - cēl = cenniṟak keṇṭi mīṉ.
cēlviḻi = cēl mīṉ pōlum cevvari paranta peṇṇiṉ kaṇ.
neruppiṉ niṟam civappātalāl, neruppiṉ peyar cenniṟattik kuṟittatu.
o. nō : eri = neruppu, civappu. erimalar = 1. civanta murukka malar. erimalarp pavaḷac cevvāy (cīvaka. 602). 2. centāmari. celva ṉerimalarc cēvaṭiyi (cīvaka. 2741).
cel - ceḷ - ceṭṭu - ceṭṭi = 1. civanta aṭiyiyuṭiya veṭcic ceṭi. ceṅkāl veṭci (tirumuru. 21). 2. murukaṉ.
ceḷ - cey - ceyyavaṉ = 1. civantavaṉ. 2. katiravaṉ. 3. cevvāy. ceyyaṉ = murukaṉ.
ceyyāḷ (ceyyavaḷ) = ceṅkōlat tirumakaḷ.
ceyyāṉ = civantavaṉ, cempūrāṉ.
cey - cēy = civappu, cevvāy, murukaṉ.
cēy - cēyatu - cēytu = civantatu. cēytu - cētu.
cētu + ā = cētā. cētu + āmpal = cētāmpal.
cēy - cēyaṉ - cēyāṉ = cenniṟattāṉ.
cēyavaṉ = cevvāy, murukaṉ.
cēyōṉ = murukaṉ, civaṉ.
cēy - cē. cēttal = civattal.
cē = civappu, cēṅkōṭṭi.
cē - cēttu = civappu.
cēttu - cēntu = civappu, tī, acōku.
cēntu - cēntaṉ = civantavaṉāṉa murukaṉ.
cēntaṉ - cēntu.
o.nō : vēntaṉ - vēntu, murukaṉ - muruku.
cēntu + il = cēntil - centil.
cēntu + ūr = cēntūr - centūr (tiruccentūr).
cey - ceymmi - cemmi - cevvi.
cem - cev - ceva - cevappu.
ceva - civa - civappu - cikappu.
civa - cival - civali.
civa - civam - civaṉ = civantavaṉ, neruppiṉ kūṟāka niṉṟu ulakatti iyakkum iṟivaṉ.
tīvaṇṇaṉ, antivaṇṇaṉ, aḻalvaṇṇaṉ, māṇikkak kūttaṉ mutaliya civaṉ peyarkaḷi nōkkuka.
civaṉ māli
kuṟiñci nilattiṟkuriya koṉṟi māli. koṉṟi vēyntōṉ - koṉṟi vēntaṉ.
civaṉūrti
kuṟiñci nilattiṟkuriya (veṇ) kāḷi. ataṉ veṇṇiṟam tūymi kuṟittatu.
civaṉ paṭikkalam
muk kavarc cūlam. ataṉāṟ civaṉukkuc cūli eṉṟu oru peyar. kaṇicciyum (maḻuvum) civaṉ paṭi. ataṉāl avaṉukkuk kaṇicciyāṉ (maḻuvāḷi) eṉṟum peyar.
civaṉirukki
vīṭṭulakamum veḷḷimaliyum tirukkōvilkaḷum toṇṭaruḷḷamum.
civaṉ kuṇam
taṉvayattaṉātal, tūya vuṭampiṉaṉātal, iyaṟkiyuṇar viṉaṉātal, muṟṟu muṇartal, iyalpākavē kaṭṭukkaḷiṉ (pācaṅkaḷiṉ) nīṅkutal, pēraruḷuṭimi, muṭivilāṟṟa luṭimi, varampiliṉpa muṭimi eṉṉum eṭṭu.
civaṉ toḻil
paṭippu kāppu aḻippu eṉṉum mūṉṟu.
civaṉ vaṭivam 
ai vaki.
(1)	koṉṟi māli yaṇintu cūla mēntik kāḷi yūrntu cellum cemmēṉiyaṉ.
(2)	ammiyappaṉ
ellā vuyirkaṭkum tāytanti pōṉṟavaṉ. valappuṟam tantikkūṟum iṭappuṟam tāykkūṟum koṇṭataṉāl, maṅki paṅkaṉ allatu mātoru pākaṉ eṉṟu collappaṭupavaṉ.
	tantikkūṟṟup peyar	tāykkūṟṟup peyar
	civaṉ	civi
	iṟivaṉ	iṟivi
	tēvaṉ	tēvi
	paraṉ	pari
	appaṉ	ammi
	aiyaṉ	aiyi
	malimakaṉ	malimakaḷ
	cūli	cūliṉi
malimakaṉ malimakaḷ eṉṉum irupeyarum, malivāḻ teyvam eṉṟē poruḷpaṭuvaṉa.
ammiyappaṉ vaṭivam, ilaṅkam (liṅkam) eṉṉum uru vaṭivilum, ōm eṉṉum oli vaṭivilum, piḷḷiyār cuḻiyeṉṉum ukara vari vaṭivilum, kuṟikkap peṟum.
ilakku = kuṟi. ilakku - ilakkam - ilaṅkam.
ammiyappaṉ vaṭivu, iṟivaṉiṉ uṇmi vaṭivik kāṇa muṭiyāta illaṟavāṇarāṉa potu makkaṭkē. uyarnta aṟivupaṭitta cittarum muṉivarum, iṟivaṉiṉ āṟṟaliyē peṇ kūṟāka uruvakippar.
ilaṅka vaṭivu niliyil, iṟivaṉāṟṟalik kuṟikkum aṭittaḷattiṟku āvuṭiyāḷ eṉṟum, mēl niṟkum ilaṅkattiṟku āvuṭiyappaṉ eṉṟum, peyar. āvuṭiyāḷ eṉpatu āvuṭiyammi, āvuṭiyācci eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum.
(3) kuravaṉ
takutiyuḷḷavarkku, antaṇa (aruḷ muṉivaṉ) vaṭivil vantu uyaraṟi vuṟuttum parama āciriyaṉ.
muṉivaṉ kōlattiṟ caṭiyuṭimiyāl, civaṉ caṭiyaṉ, caṭiyappaṉ eṉṟum peyar peṟṟāṉ. puram = uyarnilik kaṭṭiṭam, aḵtuḷḷavūr. puram - param = mēlulakam, vīṭṭulakam. param - paramaṉ = mēlōṉ, iṟivaṉ. param - varam - varaṉ 
(kuṟaḷ. 24).
(4) eṇ vaṭivaṉ (aṭṭamūrtti)
nilam, nīr, tī, vaḷi, veḷi, katiravaṉ, tiṅkaḷ, ātaṉ (āṉmā) eṉṉum eṇ poruḷ vaṭiviṉaṉ. eṅkum niṟintiruppatu paṟṟi eṇṭiciyum civaṉukku eṇ kiyākac collappaṭum. ataṉāl eṇṭōḷaṉ (piṅ.) eṉṟu peyar. katiravaṉ tiṅkaḷ tī eṉṉum muc cuṭarum civaṉukku mukkaṇṇākak kūṟappaṭum. ataṉāl avaṉukku mukkaṇṇaṉ eṉṟu oru peyar.
(5) naṭavaracaṉ
uyirāṉatu niṉivu colvu ceyal eṉṉum muttoḻiṟ paṭumāṟu, uṭampiṉ naṭuvuḷ toṅki niṉṟu iyaṅkum neñcat tuṭippi naṭamāka vuruvakittu, atu pōṉṟu ellā uyiriṉaṅkaḷum (paṭippu kāppu aḻippu eṉṉum) tōṉṟal vāḻtal maṟital ākiya muttoḻiṟpaṭumāṟu, pērulakap paraveḷiyuṭampiṉ naṭuvil niṉṟu iṟivaṉ neñcam iyaṅkuvatākak kōṭittu (pāvittu), avaṉ mut toḻiliyum iṉpa naṭamāka vuruvakittu, avaṉi naṭaruḷ talivaṉākki, naṭavaracaṉ (naṭanāyakaṉ, āṭavallāṉ) eṉṟu kuṟittaṉar. ivvuruvakam, aṇṭattiṟ kottatu piṇṭattiṟkum. eṉṉum uṇmiyi, piṇṭattiṟ kottu aṇṭattiṟkum eṉak kāṭṭiya vāṟām.
kumarināṭirunta paṇṭik kālattil, kumarimalikkum paṉi malikkum naṭuviṭattilirunta tilli nakarip pāṇṭiyaṉ pārukku neñcat tāvākak koṇṭu, aṅku naṭavaracaṉ tirup paṭimi niṟka ampalam amittāṉ. naṭavaracap paṭimikaḷ niṟkum kōvilkaḷellām, ampala meṉṟu peyar peṟṟiruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu. ampalam āṭaraṅku. ampalak kūttaṉ, maṉṟāṭi eṉpaṉa tillic civaṉ peyarkaḷ. pēram palam ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ, āṭampalam ciṟṟampalam eṉappaṭṭatu. appeyarē iṉṟu citamparam eṉat tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. ciṟṟampalam poṉṉāl vēyappaṭṭapiṉ, poṉṉampalam eṉappaṭṭatu. ataṉ piṉṉarē maṇiyampalam veḷḷiyampalam ceppampalam mutaliyavi tōṉṟiṉa.
kaṭpulaṉākak kāṇum naṭavaracaṉ paṭimi, akak karaṇa vaḷarcci yaṭiyāta potu makkaṭ kuriyatē. ciṟanta aṟivarāṉa aṭiyār, tiṟanta veḷiyiyē ampalamākak karutuvar. appara veḷiyampalamē tiri nīkkik kāṭṭappaṭum. atuvē ciṟṟampala marumam (citampara rakasiyam) eṉa vaḻaṅkuvatu.
naḷi - naṭi - naṭam, naṭaṉam. naṭam - naṭṭam - naṭṭuvaṉ.
naṭṭam - va. nrutta, nāṭya,
ummutal = kūṭutal. um - am - ami. amital = 1. neruṅkutal, aṭartal. vaḻiyami cāral (malipaṭu. 161). 2. kūṭutal. ami - avi = kūṭṭam, kuḻām. 3. poruntutal. pāṅkami patali (kantapu. tiruppara. 9). 4. niṟital. uṟup pamintu (kuṟaḷ. 791).
am - ampu - ampal = 1. kūṭutal, kūṭṭam. o. nō : um - kum - kumpu - kumpal. ampal - ampalam = kūṭṭam, avi, kūṭumiṭam, maṉṟam.
2. kuvital, kuvinta arumpu allatu moṭṭu. ampal eṉpatu mukiḻ mukiḻttal. (iṟi. 22, uri). o. nō : kum - kumpu - kūmpu. kum - kumi - kuvi.
3. arumpu pōṉṟa cila ruri paḻi. ampalum alaruṅ kaḷavu veḷippaṭuttaliṉ (tol. kaḷa. 46). ampalum alarum kaḷavu. (iṟi. 22).
ma. ampalam, ampala, tu. ampila, va. ampara.
vaṭacollil lakaram rakaramākat tirintiruttal kāṇka. at tiricolliyē ampalam eṉṉum iyaṟ colliṟku mūlamākac ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻaka akaramutaliyiṟ kāṭṭi yiruppatu, iṟṟit tamiḻariṉ iḻivāṉa aṭimit taṉattiyē kāṭṭum.
civa vaḻipāṭṭu vaṭivam
civaṉuṭiya ai vaki vaṭivukaḷuḷḷum, potu makkaḷ vaḻipāṭṭiṟ kēṟṟatu ammiyappa vaṭivamē.
civa vaḻipāṭṭu muṟi
kāliyiṟ kuḷittu, uṇṇumuṉ, tīya niṉiviṉṟi aminta vuḷḷattuṭaṉ akka māli yaṇintu tirunīṟupūcic civap paṭimi muṉ niṉṟu, iyalum pōtellām tēṅkāyuṭittu vāḻip paḻattuṭaṉ paṭittu, naṟumpuki kāṭṭip pūccāttik ki kuvittu, (ōm eṉṉum muḷi mantiratti muṉṉiṭṭa) civa pōṟṟi eṉṉun tiruvin teḻutti ōti, palvēṟu pōṟṟit toṭarkaḷāl vaḻutti, neṭuñcāṇkiṭi vaṇakkañ ceytu eḻuntiruppatē ciṟanta muṟippaṭṭa civa vaḻipāṭākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu.
uḻavarum uḻippāḷikaḷum toḻilāḷarum kāliyil civa vaṇakkam maṭṭum ceyya muṭiyum. tirunāṭkaḷilum tiruviḻāk kālattilum ellārum kōvil vaḻipāṭu ceyvar.
civaṉi yaṭintavariṉ mum mācum erintu cāmpalāy viṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpati uṇarttaṟkē, tirunīṟu pūcappaṭṭatu. atu pūti (piṅ.). eṉṟum collappaṭum. puḻuti - pūti = tūḷ, nīṟu, tirunīṟu.
tēṅkā yuṭittu ataṉ nīric cinti muṟiyip paṭippatu, vaḻipaṭuvōṉ taṉ tīviṉi niṉintu maṉamuṭintu kaṇṇīr cintit tūymippaṭa vēṇṭumeṉpatiyum; vāḻip paḻattip paṭippatu, ataṉ catipōla uḷḷam kaṉintu meṉmiyum iṉimiyum peṟa vēṇṭumeṉpatiyum, kuṟippāka vuṇarttum.
civaṉ kōviṟ pūcakar, kurukkaḷ, paṇṭāram, ōtuvār, pulavar, pōṟṟi eṉap pala peyar peṟṟaṉar.
mūvēntarum mutaṟkaṇ civaṉaṭiyārāyiruntu, piṉṉar āriyar (pirāmaṇar) vantu muttirumēṉik koḷki pukuttiya piṉ, iṭiyiṭi orōvoruvar māliyattiyum (viṇavatti yum) taḻuviṉar. tammip pōṉṟē tām vaḻipaṭu teyvamum ēṟṟamāka iruntu iṉpuṟa vēṇṭumeṉṟu, tamakkuriya ciṟappi yellām tam teyvattiṟkum ceytaṉar. acciṟappukkaḷ teyvattiṉ oppilā vuyarvu nōkkip paṉmaṭiyuyarvākac ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.
vāṉaḷāvum eḻunilik kūṭa kōpuramum māṭa maṇṭapaṅkaḷum cuṟṟu matiluṅ koṇṭa tiruvuṇṇāḻikit tirukkōvil, ūrvalattiṟkuc ciṟanta yāṉi kutiriyoṭṭaka veṇ kāḷikaḷ, kuṭi koṭi mutaliya ciṉṉaṅkaḷ, karuvūla kaḷañciya paṇṭacālikaḷ, palvaki aṇikaṅkaḷ (vākaṉaṅkaḷ), capparaṅkaḷ, vili yuyarvum ōviya vēlippāṭṭuc ciṟappumuḷḷa poṉṉāṭi poṉmaṇi yaṇikaḷ, tirukkuḷam, pūṅkā, tiruppaḷḷiyeḻucci yiṉṉiyam, tirumuḻukkāṭṭu, tiruviṉṉamutu paṭippu, tirunāḷ āravāra ūrvala ulākkaḷ, āṇṭu tōṟum (mut taṭṭu mutal eḻu taṭṭu vari koṇṭa) tērōṭṭa teppat tērt tiruviḻākkaḷ, nila māṉipaṅkaḷ, iyavar, kāvalar ēvalar mēṟpārvalarākiya paṇimakkaḷ, mutaliya pal vakic ciṟappum vēntarālum maṉṉarālum peruñ celvarālum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. iciyālum naṭattālum iṟi vaṉi iṉpuṟutta nālvakippaṭṭa ellāk karuviyiciyum āṭṭum pāṭṭum kōvilkaḷilum tiruvulākkaḷilum nikaḻntaṉa. itaṟkeṉṟē pāṭakarum kaṇikiyarum naṭṭuvarum muṭṭuvarum amarttappaṭṭaṉar.
koṇ muṭipu (cittāntam)
talivaṉ taḷiyaṉ taḷi eṉṉum mūṉṟum toṭakkamilā mupporuḷkaḷ. kāmam (kāmiyam) vekuḷi (āṇavam) mayakkam (māyi) eṉat taḷi mūvakittu. terintum teriyāmalum ceyyum iruvakit tīviṉiyum nīkki iṟivaṉi vaḻipaṭiṉ, illaṟattālum tuṟavaṟattālum irupālārum vīṭu peṟalām. vīṭṭulakam civaṉulakam (civa vulakam) eṉappaṭum. vīṭu peṟum vari ātaṉ piṟavik kaṭaluḷ aḻuntum. nilittiṇi, nīrvāḻi, ūri, paṟavi, vilaṅku, māntaṉ, tēvaṉ eṉap piṟavi eḻu vaki.
ituvē civak koṇ muṭipu.
aṉṉīṟum āṉīṟum oṉṟaṉ pāliyum uṇarttum.
e - ṭu : taṉiyaṉ (taṉippāṭal), taṭiyaṉ (pūcaṇikkāy), alavaṉ, maṭiyāṉ, kuṇṭaṭiyaṉ, kaṭuvaṉ.
kāmam vekuḷi mayakka mivimūṉṟaṉ
nāmaṅ keṭakkeṭum nōy.  (kuṟaḷ. 370).
eṉpatil, mum mācu kuṟikkappaṭṭiruttal kāṇka.
kāmaṅ keṭutal eṉpatu, illaṟattiṟkup piṟaṉil viḻiyā miyum piṟaṉ poruḷ veḵkāmiyum; tuṟavaṟattiṟku āci aṭiyōṭoḻital.
teriyātu mitippiṉum tīccuṭutal pōla, teriyātu ceyyum tīviṉiyum tīṅku viḷikkum eṉpatu koḷki. nalviṉi yāṟ kēṭilli.
nōyellām nōyceytār mēlavām nōyceyyār
nōyiṉmi vēṇṭu pavar.  (kuṟaḷ. 320).
iruḷcēr iruviṉiyum cērā iṟivaṉ
poruḷcēr pukaḻpurintār māṭṭu.  (kuṟaḷ. 5).
eṉpatil, iruviṉiyeṉṟatu terintum teriyātum ceyyum tīviṉikaḷiyē.
tacarataṉ vēṭṭiyāṭiya pōtu teriyātu koṉṟa ciṟuvaṉiṉ kuruṭarāṉa peṟṟōr iṭṭa vivē (cāpamē), piṉṉar irāmaṉik kāṭṭiṟ kēkac ceytatu eṉpatu, naṭuniliyaṟiñar karuttu.
aṟattāṟṟiṉ ilvāḻkki yāṟṟiṉ puṟattāṟṟiṟ
pōoyp peṟuva tevaṉ.  (kuṟaḷ. 46).
eṉpataṉāl, illaṟattālum vīṭupēṟuṇṭeṉpatē tamiḻar koḷkiyām.
āṟṟiṉ oḻukki aṟaṉiḻukkā ilvāḻkki
nōṟpāriṉ nōṉmi yuṭittu.  (kuṟaḷ. 48)
ātalāl, tuṟavaṟattāṉ poṟiyiṉum illaṟattāṉ poṟiyē peritām.
pēriṉpa vīṭṭip peṟṟatākap periya purāṇaṅ kūṟum civaṉaṭiyāruṭ perumpālār, illaṟattil niṉṟavarē.
iruvaki yaṟa vāḻkkiyiyum iṟivaṉ ēṟkiṉṟāṉ eṉpati yuṇarttaṟkē, avaṉukku ammiyappa vaṭivum antaṇa vaṭivum kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
	meyp poruḷiyal
cuviyoḷi yūṟōci nāṟṟameṉ ṟintiṉ
vakiterivāṉ kaṭṭē yulaku.  (kuṟaḷ. 28).
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar. ākavē,
nilam nīr tī vaḷi veḷi eṉṉum pūtaṅkaḷ aintum, avaṟṟiṉ ciṟappiyalpukaḷāṉa nāṟṟam cuvi oḷi ūṟu ōci eṉṉum aintum, avaṟṟi yaṟiyum karuvikaḷāṉa mūkku nāvu kaṇ mey cevi eṉṉum aṟivup poṟikaḷ aintum, palvēṟu viṉi ceyyum ki kāl vāy eruvāy karuvāy eṉṉum karumap poṟi kaḷ aintum, mati uḷḷam (cittam) maṉam nāṉuṇarvu eṉṉum akak karaṇaṅkaḷ nāṉkum, avaṟṟik koṇṭu poruḷkaḷi āyntaṟiyum ātaṉum, ataṉ viṉikaṭ kellām iṭam pōṉṟa nilikkaḷamākiya kālamum tūṇṭukōlāṉa ūḻum ākiya iraṇṭum, ellāvaṟṟiyum iyakkum iṟivaṉum, āka mottam meyp poruḷkaḷ (tattuvaṅkaḷ) irupat teṭṭām.
civamata virivaḷarcci
civa matam, nāḷaṭiviṟ palvēṟu vaṇakkaṅkaḷiyum, iṟutiyil tirumāliyattiyum taṉṉuṭ koṇṭatu.
nāka vaṇakkattār nāka vuruvit tam taliyucciyil aṇintiruntaṉar. avaric civaṉiyarākkaṟku, civaṉ muṭimītum nāka vuruviruppatākap paṭimi yamittu viṭṭaṉar. atōṭu, civaṉ pāmpukaḷiyē palvēṟu aṇikaḷāka aṇin tiruppatākavum kāṭṭi viṭṭaṉar. ataṉāl, nākappaṉ, pāmpaṇiyaṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷum tōṉṟiṉa.
pāmpalaṅ kārap paraṉ  (tirukkō. 11).
tamiḻakam muḻuvatum vēntarālum toḻappaṭṭa kāḷi, civaṉ tēviyākkappaṭṭāḷ. atu tiruvālaṅkāṭṭut tirunaṭap pōril avaḷ tōlviyuṟṟataṉ viḷivākak kāṭṭappaṭṭatu. āṇpāṟ kēṟṟa ūrtta naṭaṉam peṇpāṟ kēṟkāmiyāl, kāḷiyaṭiyārum ati ottuk koḷḷa vēṇṭiya tāyiṟṟu.
uvar - ivar. uvar - ūr. ūrtal - ēṟi naṭattutal allatu cellutal, uyartal. ūr - ūrttam - va. ūrttavam (uÍrdhva).
civiyuṅ kāḷiyum oṉṟāṉatiṉāl, civi nīli (karuppi) yeṉavum, kāḷi iṟivi (iṟivaṉ tēvi) yeṉavum, peyar peṟṟaṉar. ammi aiyi eṉpaṉa iruvarukkum potup peyarkaḷ. ammi - ammā - va. ampā.
kāḷi civaṉ tēviyāṉa piṉ, kāḷiyappaṉ, pēycciyappaṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷ civaṉukkut tōṉṟiṉa.
viṇṇaka vāḻvu niliyaṟṟatāy eḻu piṟaviyuḷ aṭaṅkiṉatiṉālum, viṇṇaka vēntaṉukku maḻi peyvikkum atikāramē yiruntataṉālum, niliyāṉa vīṭṭulakat talivaṉum ellām vallavaṉumāṉa civaṉi vaḻipaṭum vaḻipāṭṭuḷ, vēntaṉ vaṇakkam maṟin toḻintatu.
iṟutiyil, tirumālum peṇṇāka māṟic civa perumāṉiṉ iṭappākat tamarntāṉ eṉṉum kati yeḻuntatu. ari eṉṉum tirumāl peyar itaṉāl tōṉṟiyirukkalām. aram = civappu. araṉ = civaṉ. araṉ - ari (peṇ pāl.). pacciyaṉ eṉṉum poruḷ piṟkālattatu.
tirumāl matamum oru taṉi matamākat toṉṟutoṭṭu iruntu vantiruppiṉum, civaṉukkē iṟivaṉ eṉṉum peyaruṇmi yum, piṟavā yākkip periyōṉ kōyilum eṉṉum cilappatikārak kūṟṟum (5 : 179), civa matattiṉ talimiyi yuṇarttum. ulaka mellām uṭiyavar eṉpatu paṟṟi, civaṉukkup peruvuṭiyār eṉṟum peyar. ēḻuṭiyāṉ poḻil (tirukkō. 6).
	(2) tirumāl matam
tirumāl niṟamum peyarum
ellām valla iṟivaṉiyē, mulli nilat teyva aṭippaṭiyil nīriṉ kūṟākak koṇṭu, nīrip poḻiyum mukil niṟam paṟṟi māyōṉ (māyaṉ, māyavaṉ) eṉṟum, mālōṉ (māl, mālaṉ, mālavaṉ) eṉṟum, kār vaṇṇaṉ (kariyaṉ, kariyavaṉ) eṉṟum, mukil kūṭum vāṉiṟam paṟṟi nīla vaṇṇaṉ (maṇi vaṇṇaṉ, kaṭal vaṇṇaṉ) eṉṟum, peyariṭṭu vaḻipaṭṭu vantuḷḷaṉar orucār tamiḻar. māl eṉṉum peyar aṭiyaṭuttut tirumāl, perumāl eṉa vaḻaṅkum. perumāl - perumāḷ.
mukilukkum atu niṟkum vāṉiṟkum viṇ eṉṉum peyaruṇmiyāl, appeyari aṭiyākak koṇṭa viṇṭu eṉṉum peyarum tirumālukku ēṟpaṭṭatu.
viḷḷutal = virital, tiṟattal, veḷiyātal.
viḷ - viṇ = veḷi, vāṉam, mukil, mēlulakam.
viṇ - viṇṭu = veḷi, vāṉam, mukil, tirumāl, mēlulakam.
pacci niṟam nīlaniṟattiṟku iṉamātalāl, pacciyaṉ, pacciyappaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷum tirumāliṟkut tōṉṟiṉa.
tirumā lirukki
viṇṭu + nakar = viṇṇakar = 1. tirumāl kōvil, tirumāl vīṭṭulakam.
viṇṇakar - viṇṇakaram.
parama patam = paramaṉ patintirukkum iṭam, vīṭṭulakam, tirumālulakam.
tirumāl malaraṇi
mulli nilattiṟkuriya tuḻāy māli.
tirumāl ūrti
mulli nilattiṟkuriya kaluḻaṉ.
kaluḻtal = kalattal. kaluḻ - kaluḻaṉ = veṇṭaliyum cevvuṭampumāka iruniṟaṅ kalanta paṟavi.
kaluḻaṉ - va. karuṭa (garud´a).
tirumāl paṭi
caṅku, cakkaram, vil, vāḷ, taṇṭam eṉṉum aintu.
aimpaṭi yuruvākac ceyta aimpaṭit tāliyeṉṉum aṇiyi, mata vēṟupāṭiṉṟic ciṟu piḷḷikaṭkut toṉṟu toṭṭuk kāppāka aṇintu vantirukkiṉṟaṉar.
tāli kaḷintaṉṟu milaṉē  (puṟam. 77).
poṉṉuṭit tāli yeṉmakaṉ  (akam. 54).
amaḷit tuñcu mimpaṭit tālik
 kutalic cevvāyk kuṟunaṭip putalvar  (maṇi. 7 : 56-7)
aḻakiya vimpaṭiyu māramuṅ koṇṭu  (periyāḻ. tiru. 1. 4 : 5)
aimpaṭi cataṅki cātti  (periya pu. taṭuttāṭ. 4).
taṉpaṭika ḷāṉa tiru vimpaṭi tarittē  (kaliṅ. ava. 9)
tāli yimpaṭi taḻuvu mārpiṭi  (kampa. nāṭu. 58).
aimpaṭi mārpiṟ kāṇēṉ  (tiruviḷi. 39 : 25).
tirumāl nilikaḷ
vāṉiṟ kaluḻaṉūrtalum, nilattil niṟṟaliruttal kiṭattalum.
kiṭattaṟkuc ciṟanta iṭam tiruvaraṅkam.
tiruvaraṅkam - va. srīraṅka.
tirumāl toḻil
paṭippu kāppu aḻippu eṉṉum mūṉṟu.
ulakam yāviyum tāmuḷa vākkalum
nilipe ṟuttalum nīkkalum nīṅkalā
alaki lāviḷi yāṭṭuṭi yāravar
talivar aṉṉavark kēcaraṇ nāṅkaḷē  (kampa. ciṟappup pā. 1).
tirumāl vaḻipāṭu
akka mālikkut tali māṟākat tuḷacimaṇi māli yaṇintu, tirunīṟṟiṟkut talimāṟākat tirumaṇ kāppuc cātti, civa pōṟṟi eṉpataṟkut talimāṟāka māl pōṟṟi allatu māya pōṟṟi eṉṟu ōti, piṟavaki kaḷillām civaṉiyar pōṉṟē ceytu vaḻipaṭal.
civaṉiya aracar civaṉ kōvilkaṭkuc ceytatu pōṉṟu, māliya aracarum tirumāl kōvilkaṭkuc ciṟappuc ceytu māṉiyam viṭṭaṉar. iru cārāruḷḷum oru cilar, camayap potu nōkkarāyiruntu iru matak kōvilkaṭkum iyaṉṟatu ceytaṉar. tirumāl kōviṟ pūcakar tiruvaṭi piṭippāṉ, nampi eṉap peyar peṟṟaṉar.
koṇ muṭipu
perumpālum civaṉiyatti yottatē. iṟivaṉ peyarum vīṭṭulakap peyarum cila coṟkaḷum maṭṭum vēṟṟumi.
meyp poruḷiyal
civaṉiyatti yottatē.
civaṉiyamum māliyamum, ellām valla iṟivaṉāṉa orē teyvatti vevvēṟu peyarāl vaṇaṅkum mataṅkaḷātalāl, civaṉun tirumālum vevvēṟu teyvameṉṟō, avariṭi ēṟṟat tāḻvuṇṭeṉṟō, kumari nāṭṭār koḷḷa villi.
ariyuñ civaṉum oṇṇu, aṟiyātavaṉ vāyil maṇṇu. eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
araṉatika ṉulakaḷanta variyatika
 ṉeṉṟurikku maṟivilōrkkup
 parakaticeṉ ṟaṭivariya paricēpōṟ
 pukalariya paṇpiṟṟāmāl  (kampa. nāṭaviṭ. 24).
eṉṟār kampar. iru matattār piḷḷikaṭkum aimpaṭittāli aṇiyappaṭṭu vantatum, iti valiyuṟuttum.
muṟkālattil, āṭavarum peṇṭir pōṉṟē talimuṭiyi nīḷa vaḷarttuk koṇṭi muṭittaṉar. nōyillā niliyil muṭi veṭṭuvatu, avamāṉat tōlvikkum tuyarattiṟkum aṭi yāḷamākak karutappaṭṭatu. iṟivaṉ muṉ tammit tāḻttu vataṟkē, teyvap paṟṟāḷar tirukkōvilkaṭkuc ceṉṟu tam taliyi maḻittuk koṇṭaṉar. avvaḻakkam iṉṟum ōraḷavu iruntu varukiṉṟatu.
iṉi, aṭimikaṭkuk kātil tuḷiyiṭuvatu akkālattu vaḻakkam. teyvappaṟṟu mikka celvarum, tammi iṟiyaṭimi yarākak koṇṭu, tam kātil tuḷiyiṭṭaṉar. atu tūrntu pōkāmip poruṭṭē, kaṭukkaṉ kuṇṭalam mutaliya kātaṇi kaḷi aṇintu vantaṉar.
mantira veḻuttum cakkaramum poṟitta takaṭṭi uṭkoṇṭa, kuḷicam pōṉṟa teyvak kāppāṉa muṉkiyaṇiyē, iṉṟu kāppu eṉa vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
nākamum nākappaṭamum pōṉṟa uruvaminta ellā aṇikaḷum, toṭakkattil nāka vaṇakkattik kāṭṭiyaviyē.
paṇṭi aimpaṭittāliyum caṅkuttāliyum āmittāliyum pōṉṟu, iṉṟu kuṟukki (ciluvi)t tāliyum mūvilit tāliyum kiṟittavap peṇṭirāl aṇiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
iṅṅaṉam teyvap paṟṟāl aṇiyappaṭum urukkaḷellām, poṉṉālum maṇiyālum iyaṉṟu, aḻakiṟkāka aṇiyum aṇikalaṅkaḷākavē māṟiviṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉṟum iyaṟki vaṭivilēyē aṇiyappaṭa vēṇṭiya akka maṇiyum, orō oruvariṭip poṟ pūccup peṟṟuviṭukiṉṟatu.
(3) kaṭavuṭ camayam
ūr pēr kālam iṭam vaṇṇam vaṭivam pāl paruvam mutaliya variyaṟi yiṉṟi, eṅkum niṟintu, ellārkkum ellā vaṟṟiṟkum potuvāy, ellām vallatāy, eṉṟum māṟā tirukkum param poruḷi uḷḷattil akak kaṇṇāṟ kaṇṭu toḻutu, mukkaraṇat tūymiyuṭaṉ oḻukuvatē kaṭavuṭ camayamām.
pēr eṉṟatu, iyaṟ peyarum paṇpu paṟṟiya ciṟappup peyarum pōṉṟavaṟṟi.
kaṭavuṭ camayam, illaṟam tuṟavaṟam eṉṉum iruvaki aṟa vāḻkkikkum potuvām. ataṉāṟ peṟum pēriṉpamum, immi maṟumi yiraṇṭiṟkum potuvām. immiyiṟ peṟuvatu uṭalirunta vīṭu eṉṟum, maṟumiyiṟ peṟuvatu uṭaliṟanta vīṭu eṉṟum peyar peṟum.
ellāvaṟṟiyum kaṭantu niṟpavaṉ kaṭavuḷ. ellā vulakaṅkaḷi yum āḷvatāl āṇṭavaṉ eṉṟum, eṅkun taṅkiyiruppatāl iṟivaṉ eṉṟum, ellā vuyirkaṭkum uṇavip pakuttaḷippa tāl pakavaṉ eṉṟum, peyar peṟuvaṉ. āṇṭavaṉ mutaliya piṟa peyarkaḷ perun tēva mataṅkaḷilum vaḻaṅkuvatāl, kaṭavuṭ camayattiṟkut taṉic ciṟappākavuriya teyvap peyar kaṭavuḷ eṉpatē.
teyvam eṉṉum peyar, iṭattiṟkēṟpa mūvakit teyvatti yuṅ kuṟikkum. teyvam uṇāvē (tol. 964). eṉpatiṟ ciṟu teyvattiyum, naṇṇiyapoṉ ṉampalattē naṭampuriyun teyvam (irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷ pāṭal) eṉpatiṟ perun tēvaṉiyum, teyvam niṉṟu kollum eṉṉum paḻamoḻi yiṟ kaṭavuḷiyum, kuṟittal kāṇka. vāṉuṟiyun teyvattuḷ vikkappaṭum (kuṟaḷ. 50) eṉpatil, ciṟu teyvattiṉ pāṟpaṭṭa tēvarulaka vāḻiyik kuṟittatu.
taṉvayatta ṉātal, eṅkum niṟital, ellām vallaṉātal, ellām aṟital, eṉṟu muṇmi, iyal māṟāmi, muḻut tūymi, oppuyar viṉmi, pēraruḷuṭimi, varampiliṉpa muṭimi eṉpaṉa kaṭavuḷiṉ iyalkaḷākac collappeṟum.
ellām valla iṟi vaṇakkattiyē koṇṭa civaṉiyam, māliyam, yūtam, kiṟittuvam, icalām mutaliya ellā mataṅ kaṭkum, kaṭavuṭ camayam potuvām.
icumavēla icuravēlariṉ muṉṉōṉāṉa āpirakāmiṟku muṉpē, nōvā kālattup peru veḷḷattiṟkum muṉṉarē, kaṭavuḷi ulakil mutaṉ mutalākak kaṇṭavar kumari nāṭṭut tamiḻarē. kaṭavuḷ eṉṉuñ col tolkāppiyattil āḷap paṭṭiruppatē, ataṟkuc cāṉṟām.
kāmap pakuti kaṭavuḷum variyār.  (tol. puṟat. 27).
kaṭavuḷ vāḻttoṭu kaṇṇiya varumē.  (tol. puṟat. 33)
kaṭavuḷ eṉṉuñ col, ellāvaṟṟiyuṅ kaṭantu eṇṇiṟkum eṭṭāta param poruḷiyē kuṟikkum. ciṟu teyvavaṇakka āriyar (pirāmaṇar), piṟkālattil atip piḻipaṭa āṇṭu viṭṭaṉar.
kaṭavuṭku uruvamiṉmiyāl, uruva vaṇakkamu milli. evarum eṅkum eṉṟum tamittum piṟaroṭu kūṭiyum, kaṭavuḷit toḻalām.
tiruvaḷḷuvar kaṭavuṭ camayattār. ataṉāl, mutaṟ pakavaṉ, vālaṟivaṉ, malarmici yēki, viruppu veṟuppillāṉ, iṟivaṉ, aimpulam veṉṟāṉ, uvamiyili, aṟavāḻi yantaṇaṉ, eṇ kuṇattāṉ, piṟavi nīkki eṉak kaṭavuṭkuriya pal camayap potup peyarkaḷiyē tam kaṭavuḷ vāḻttil āṇṭār.
aṅkiṅ keṉātapaṭi yeṅkumpira kācamāy
	āṉanta pūrttiyāki
aruḷoṭu niṟintatetu taṉṉaruḷ veḷikkuḷē
	akilāṇṭa kōṭiyellām
taṅkum paṭikkicci vittuyirk kuyirāyt
	taḻittatetu maṉavākkiṉil
taṭṭāmal niṉṟatetu camayakō ṭikaḷelām
	tanteyvam enteyvameṉ
ṟeṅkun toṭarntetir vaḻakkiṭavum niṉṟatetu
	eṅkaṇum peruvaḻakkāy
yātiṉum vallavoru cittāki yiṉpamāy
	eṉṟikku muḷḷatetumēṟ
kaṅkulpaka laṟaniṉṟa elliyuḷa tetuvatu
	karuttiṟ kicintatuvē
kaṇṭaṉa velāmōṉa vuruveḷiya tākavum
	karutiyañ caliceykuvōm.  (tāyu. paraciva. 1).
paṇṇē ṉuṉakkāṉa pūciyoru vaṭivilē
	pāvitti ṟiñcavāṅkē
pārkkiṉṟa malarūṭu nīyē yiruttiyap
	paṉimala reṭukkamaṉamum
naṇṇē ṉalāmaliru kitāṉ kuvikkaveṉiṉ
	nāṇumeṉ ṉuḷaniṟṟinī
nāṉkumpi ṭumpō tarikkumpi ṭātalāl
	nāṉpūci ceyyalmuṟiyō
viṇṇē viṇātiyām pūtamē nātamē
	vētamē vētāntamē
mētakka kēḷviyē kēḷviyām pūmikkuḷ
	vittē yavittiṉ muḷiyē
kaṇṇē karuttē eṉ eṇṇē yeḻuttē
	katikkāṉa mōṉavaṭivē
karutariya ciṟcapiyi lāṉanta niruttamiṭu
	karuṇākarak kaṭavuḷē.  (tāyu. karuṇā. 6).
eṭṭut ticiyum patiṉāṟu kōṇamu meṅkumoṉṟāy
muṭṭit tatumpi muḷittōṅku cōtiyi mūṭarellām
kaṭṭic curuṭṭittam kakkattil vippar karuttilviyār
paṭṭap pakali yiraveṉṟu kūṟiṭum pātakarē.  (paṭṭiṉat. potu. 30).
uḷiyiṭṭa kalliyum oppiṭṭa cāntiyum ūttiyaṟap
puḷiyiṭṭa cempiyum pōṟṟuki lēṉuyar poṉṉeṉavē
oḷiyiṭṭa tāḷiraṇ ṭuḷḷē yiruttuva tuṇmiyeṉṟu
veḷiyiṭ ṭaṭittuvit tēṉiṉi mēloṉṟum vēṇṭilaṉē. 
(paṭṭiṉat. potu. 61).
muppāḻum pāḻāy mutaṟpāḻuñ cūṉiyamāy
appāḻum pāḻā yaṉpuceyva tekkālam.  (pattira. 113).
veṭṭa veḷikkuḷḷē viḷaṅkuñ catācivattik
kiṭṭa varattēṭik kirupiceyva tekkālam.  (pattira. 199).
naṭṭa kallit teyva meṉṟu nālu puṭpañ cāttiyē
cuṟṟi vantu muṇamu ṇeṉṟu collu mantiram ētaṭā
naṭṭa kallum pēcu mōnam nāta ṉuḷḷi rukkiyil
cuṭṭa caṭṭi caṭṭu vamka ṟiccu viya ṟiyumō.  (civa. vāk. 518).
koṇ muṭipu
civa matattiṟkuk kūṟiya koṇmuṭipē kaṭavuṭ camayattiṟkum. talivaṉ taḷiyaṉ taḷi eṉṉum mupporuḷi; kaṭavuḷ kaṭṭuṇi kaṭṭu eṉṟu coṉ māṟṟiyuṅ kūṟalām.
illaṟa vāyilākavum iṟivaṉ tiruvaṭi yaṭiyalā mātalāl, nal viṉiyāṟ kēṭilli.
ceykuvaṅ kollō nalviṉi yeṉavē
aiyam aṟāar kacaṭīṇṭu kāṭci
nīṅkā neñcattut tuṇivil lōrē
yāṉi vēṭṭuvaṉ yāṉiyum peṟumē
ataṉāl,
uyarnta vēṭṭat tuyarntici ṉōrkkuc
ceyviṉi maruṅkiṉ eyta luṇṭeṉiṉ
toyyā vulakattu nukarcciyuṅ kūṭum
toyyā vulakattu nukarcci yilleṉiṉ
māṟip piṟappiṉ iṉmiyuṅ kūṭum
māṟip piṟavā rāyiṉum imayattuk
kōṭuyarn taṉṉa tammici naṭṭut
tītil yākkiyoṭu māytaltavat taliyē.  (puṟam. 214).
piṟavārāyiṉum eṉpatil ummi etirmaṟi. aci nili eṉṟu paḻiya vuri kūṟuvatu poruntātu.
illaṟattālum vīṭu peṟalām eṉpatē kumari nāṭṭut tamiḻar koḷki. cuntara mūrtti nāyaṉār caṅkili paravi yārākiya tēva kaṇikiyaroṭu iṉpam nukarntatu, iṟivaṉuk kuṭampāṭeṉṟu collappaṭum pōtu, iṟivaṉ vakutta muṟippaṭi karaṇattoṭu maṇanta kaṟpuṭi maṉiviyoṭu illaṟam naṭattuvatu, eṅṅaṉ iṟivaṉukku ēṟkā tirukkum?
	meyp poruḷiyal
civa matattiṟkuk kūṟiyatē kaṭavuṭ camayattiṟkum.
kaṭavuṭ camayam tuṟaviyarkkē uriya teṉṟum, illaṟattārkku uruva vaṇakkam iṉṟiyamiyātateṉṟum, taṉṉalap pirāmaṇap pūcāriyarāl, oru tavaṟāṉa karuttu iṟṟit tamiḻa ruḷḷattiṟ pukuttappaṭṭuḷḷatu. kiṟittavarum mukamatiyarum uruvat tuṇi yiṉṟiyē iṟivaṉi vaḻipaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. kaṟṟāruṅ kallārumāṉa tamiḻarum, vīṭṭilum kāṭṭilum vaḻip pōkki lum tuṉpam nērnta viṭattu, aṇṇāntu vāṉi nōkki āṇṭavaṉē! allatu kaṭavuḷē eṉṟu viḷittu vēṇṭutal ceyvatu; allatu oruvar aṟamuṟi tavaṟi aṭṭūḻiyañ ceyyumpōtu, vāṉi nōkki, atō pōkiṟāṉē, avaṉ kēṭpāṉ. eṉṟu muṟiyiṭuvatu, iṉṟum vaḻakkamāyiruk kiṉṟatu. ātalāl, kallārkkō, potu makkaṭkō, illaṟat tārkkō, kaṭavuḷ vaṇakkamum vaḻipāṭum ēṟkā teṉpatu poruntātu.
uruva vaṇakkap paḻiya ēṟpāṭu (Old Testament) nīṅki, uruvilā vaṇakkap putiya ēṟpāṭu (New Testament) kumari nāṭṭilēyē aṟiñariṭit toṭaṅki viṭṭateṉṟum, atu īraṟattārkkum ēṟku meṉṟum, uṇmi yaṟika.
perumippeṇ
ammāṉ makaḷum atti makaḷumpōla muṟikārap peṇ ṇāyiṉ urimip peṇ eṉṟum, vēṟu celvar vīṭṭup peṇṇāyiṉ perumippeṇ eṉṟum aḻippatu vaḻakkam.  (ta. ti. 19).
peṟṟi
iṟumpūtu āṟṟaṟ pēṟṟic citti eṉṟum, ati aṭintavaric cittar eṉṟum vaṭamoḻiyāḷar colvar. citti eṉṉum colliṟkuk kikūṭal allatu muyaṟci veṟṟi eṉpatē poruḷ. ātalāl cittiyip peṟṟi eṉṉum cittarip peṟṟiyar eṉṟum tamiḻiṟ kūṟalām.
peṟu - peṟṟi - peṟṟatu, peṟṟa taṉmi, pēṟu; peṟṟiyār = ciṟanta pēṟu peṟṟavar. pēṟu eṉṉum potuc coṟkum ciṟanta pēṟu eṉṉum poruḷ iruttalāl. cittarip peṟṟiyar eṉṟu colvatu poruttamē.  (ta. i. va. 95).
peṟṟiyar ākār (cittar ākār)
piṟappāl ēṟṟat tāḻvu vakuttup piṟari ikaḻvōr, uḷḷat tūymiyum illār; ulakat tuṟavum koḷḷār. oruvakip peṟṟiyum (cittiyum) ollār.  (ta. i. va. 95).
peṟṟōr
peṟṟōr maram pōlvar. piḷḷikaḷ kiḷikaḷum koḻuntum pōlvar. ataṉāl makaṉik kompaṉ eṉṟum makaḷik kompu eṉṟum iruvariyum potuppaṭak kulak koḻuntu eṉṟum kūṟuvatu vaḻakkam.
oru marattiṉ aṭiyil muḷikkum muḷiyip pōṉṟu makaṉ iruttaliṉ avaṉukkuk kāṉmuḷi eṉṟum peyar.  (col. 3).
pē
pē - va. pī (bh)
pēpē eṉpatu accak kuṟippu.
pē = accam. pēemutir kaṭavuḷ  (kuṟun. 87).
pē - pēm = accam.
pēmnām urumeṉa varūuṅ kiḷavi
āmuṟi mūṉṟum accap poruḷa.  (tol. 848).
pē - pēy = accam, añcappaṭum āvi.
pē - pī - paya = accam.  (va. va : 210).
pēcu(1)
pēcu - va. pāṣ (bh)
pē - pēcu. pēcutal = utaṭacittuc collutal, moḻital, uriyāṭutal. pakaram itaḻp piṟappiṉatu.
pēcu - pēccu = col, uri, coṟpoḻivu, moḻi.  (va. va : 210 - 211).
pēcu(2)
poḷḷutal = tuḷittal. poḷḷal = tuḷi. poḷ = tuḷi, uṭṭuḷi (piṅ.).
poḷ - poy. o. nō : toḷ - toy.
poy = 1. tuḷi, uṭṭuḷi. poyporu muṭaṅkuki (cilap. 15 : 50) 2. marappontu (piṅ.). 3. uḷḷīṭaṟṟatu. 4. viḷicca lillātatu. poyppiñcu, poyccīppu, (u.va.). 5. uṇmi yillātatu. poruḷ keṭuttup poymēṟ koḷīi (kuṟaḷ. 938). poykkati. 6. māyi. poynnīr, (W.). 7. niliyāmi. puṟputamē yaṉṉa poykkuṭi vāḻkkiyi (tirunūṟ. 3). 8. pōliyāṉatu. 9. ceyaṟkiyāṉatu. poykkāl. 10. ēmāṟṟuvatu, poymmāṉ, poykkuḻi.
poytal = (ce. ku. vi.). 1 tuḷipaṭutal. 2. tuḷikkappaṭutal. poytaka ṭoṉṟu porunti (kamparā. pañca. 49). 3. tuḷiyiṉiṉṟu oḻukutal - (ce. kuṉṟāvi.). 1. corital.. ikaliṉ poyta (kamparā. kiṅkarar. 50). 2. vīḻttutal. avi pārcōrap poytāṉ (kamparā. aṭca. 33).
poy - pey. peytal = (ce. ku. vi). mēṉiṉṟu oḻukutal. peyyeṉap peyyum maḻi (kuṟaḷ. 53) - (ce. kuṉṟāvi.). 1. vārttal. pāṇar maṇṭi niṟiyap peymmār (puṟanā. 115). 2. kala mutaliyavaṟṟil iṭutal. ulippey taṭuvatu pōlun tuyar (nālaṭi. 114). 3. ciṟunīr kaḻittal. 4. tūvutal.
pey - pēy. pēytal = peytal (u.va.). pēyntuṅ keṭṭatu, ōyntuṅ keṭṭatu (paḻa.). ip paḻamoḻiyiṟ pēyntum eṉṉum col ōyntum eṉpataṉoṭu etuki nōkkit tirintati, pāṭṭuri nūlē eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpā (cey. 78) nīṭṭum vaḻi nīṭṭal eṉṉum ceyyuḷ tiripāka amikka iṭantarumēṉum, kocciyeṉṟu karutappaṭum iraṇṭōr ulaka vaḻakkuc col vaṭiviṉiṉṟum ilakkiyac coṟkaḷ amintiruppatāl pēytal eṉṉum collit tiripiṉ pāṟpaṭṭatākak koḷvatē takkatām.
e - ṭu : koṇṭuvā - koṇṭā - koṇā - koṇar.
iccol vaṭivukaḷuḷ, koṇṭā eṉpatu vaḻuniliyākavum, ataṉ vaḻippaṭṭa koṇā, koṇar eṉpaṉa vaḻānili yākavum, iruttali nōkkuka.
pēy - pēyu - pēcu. o. nō : tēy - tēyu - tēcu.
pēcutal = vāṉ nīri oḻuka viṭuvatu pōṉṟu vāy colli oḻuka viṭutal.
oḻukutal ōṭṭiyiṉiṉṟu viḻutal allatu cintutal, kalattiṉiṉṟu nīr vārttal eṉpatu, ataṉ vāyākiya ōṭṭi vaḻi oḻuka viṭutal. vāṉ maḻi peyvatu kalattiṉiṉṟu nīr vārttal pōṉṟiruttalāl, vāṉiṉ ṟamiyā toḻukku (kuṟaḷ. 20) eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
maḻi tūṟaluṅ koṭṭutalumiṉṟi naṭuttiṟamākap peyyiṉ peytaleṉṟum, koṭṭutalum ūṟṟutalum pōlak kaṉattup peyyiṉ poḻital eṉṟum, collappaṭum.
poḻital - tiraḷutal. poḻittal - tiraṭṭutal. poḻippuri - tiraṭṭuri. poḻil - tiraṇṭa cōli.
oruvar iyalpākac collāṭal maḻi peytal pōṉ ṟiruttalāṟ pēcutal eṉṟum, virivākac collāṭal maḻi poḻital pōṉṟiruttalāṟ poḻital eṉṟum, collappaṭu tal toṉṟu toṭṭa vaḻakkamāyiruntu varukiṉṟatu. oru maṇi nēram poḻintu taḷḷi viṭṭār eṉṉuṅ kūṟṟu, kallā makkaḷ vāyiṉiṉṟu varuvatiyuṅ kāṇalām. iv vaḻakki ṉiṉṟē, coṟpoḻivu, coṟpoḻivāḷar eṉṉuṅ kūṭṭuc coṟkaḷ ikkālattil tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. itaṉāl, poḻital eṉṉum coṟ pōṉṟē pēcutal eṉṉum collum maḻi viṉiyoppumi paṟṟit tōṉṟiyateṉa uṇaralām.
camaṟkirutac coṟkaḷ
pēcu eṉṉun teṉcolliṉiṉṟē, pāṣ (bhaÍsh, eṉṉum vaṭacollum tirintuḷḷatu. iti yuṇarntē ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻakarātiyit (Tamil Lexicon) tokutta vaṭamoḻi veṟiyarum, pēcu eṉṉum taliccolliyaṭuttu “cf. bhaÍs´” eṉṟu kuṟittirukkiṉṟaṉar.
bhas, to speak, talk, say, tell.
bhashaka, speaking, talking about, KaÍv.
bhashana, the act of speaking, talking, speech, talk, Nir; Mn; MBh.
bhasha, Speech, language (esp. common or vernacular Speech as opp. to Vedic or in later time to Sanskrit) Nir; PaÍn; Mn; MBh; any PraÍkit dialect or a particular group of 5 of them. (Maharashtri, SØauraseniÍ, MaÍgadhiÍ, PraÍcya and Avanti, also called PanÃca VidhaÍ bhaÍshaÍ.
bhashika, belonging to the common or vernacular speech, Nir.
bhashita, spoken, uttered, sid, spoken to, addressed, Mn; MBh.
bhashitarya, to be spoken to or addressed, R.
bhashitri, a speaker, talker, SBr; MBh; KaÍv.
bhashin, saying, speaking, loquacious, MBk; KaÍv.
bhashya, speaking, talking, Susr; any work in the common or vernacular speech, VPraÍt. GrS; Hariv; an explanatory work, exposition, explanation, commentary esp. on technical sutras, Name of PatanÃjali’s commentary on the sutras of PaÍn´ini, mahaÍ bhaÍshya.
sam-bhash, to speak together, converse with, speak to, join in a conversation.
sam-bhashana, conversation, discourse with.
subhashana, Name of a person.
subhashita, spoken well or eloquently.
subhashitam, good or eloquent speech, witty saying.
Gk. phasis, speech, utterance, expression.
aphasia, loss of speech, or of understanding of language, owing to brin damage; f. mod. L. f. Gk. aphatos, speechless (a, not, pha, to speak).
A allatu a eṉṟa (kirēkka) eḻuttiṟkuk kirēkkattil a, ā eṉṟa olikaḷē uḷa. ātalāl, pēcu eṉṟa collē, intiya āriyariṉ muṉṉōr kirēkka nāṭṭiṟkup pakkattil vāḻntu vantapōtu, avar moḻiyil pāṣ (bhaÍsh) eṉṟum, kirēkka moḻiyiṟ pā (phaÍ) eṉṟum, tirintiruttal vēṇṭum.
kiḷēṉ (Klein) āṅkilac coṟpiṟappiyal akaramutalic coṟkaḷ
“aphasis, n., loss of the faculty of speech (med.) - Medical L., fr. priv. pref. a - and phasis, speech, from the base of phani, to speak, which is rel. to phame, voice, report, rumour, and cog. with L. fama, talk, report, rumour” (p. 42).
“aphemia, n, a kind of aphasis (med.) - Medical L. coined by the French surgeon and anthropologist Paul Broca (1824 - 80) in 1860 fr. priv-pref. 
a - and Gk. pheme, voice, which is rel. to phani, to say, speak”. (p. 42).
“fama, n., the personification of rumour in Roman mythology.” (p. 273).
“fame, n. F. fame, fr. L. fame, talk, report, rumor, tradition, reputation, lit, ‘saying’, from the stem of for, faÍri, pp. fatus, to speak, rel. to fateÍri, to confess, fabula, narrative, account, tale, story, fas, divine law (lit. utterance, fr. I. E. base, bhaÍ, to speak, tell, say, whence also OI bhanati, speaks, Arm. bay, gen. bayi, word, term, bey, he sid, ban, gen. bani, word, speech, judgement, Gk. phemi, I say; pheme, Dor. phaÍmaÍ, voice, report, rumour, phatis, saying, speech, report, OSlav bajo, bajati, to talk, tell, basni, fable, tale, charm, OE. boian, to boast, ON. boÍn, OE. beÍn, prayer, request. Cp. phone, speech sound, Cp. also abandon, affable, aphasla, aphemia, apophasis, ban, to prohibit, ban, ediet. bandit, banish, bifarious, blame, blaspheme, boon, confabulate, confess, defamation, defame, euphemism, fabulous, famous, fascinate, fate, infamous, infamy, infant, infantry; multifarious, nefarious, - phasia, - phemia.  Poly-phemns, preface, prefatory, profess, prophet, trifarious”. (p. 273).
iṅṅaṉam, pēcu eṉṉum col pē - phe - fe eṉṟum, pācu (bhaÍsh) - pā (bhaÍ) - bhaÍ - phaÍ - faÍ eṉṟum, piṟavāṉum, tirintu palvēṟu coṟkaḷi mēliyāriyattil tōṟṟuvittuḷḷatu.
āyiṉ, ban, ben, bon mutaliya coṟkaḷ paṉ eṉṉum teṉcolliṉiṉṟu tirintiruttal vēṇṭum. mēliyaṟiñar tam moḻiyiṉ tamiḻ mūlattiyum tamiḻiṉ toṉmiyiyum iṉṉum aṟiyāmiyāl, ittakiya paṟpala tavaṟukaḷ nērntuḷḷaṉa.
paṉṉutal = 1. collutal, pēcutal, paṉṉiyirakkum (kamparā. kikēyi, 41). 2. oṉṟit tirumpat tirumpac collutal allatu eḻututal. 3. niṉṟu niṉṟu pēcutal allatu paṭittal.  4. vācittal allatu paṭittal ōli..... vāṅkip paṉṉuvaṉō (piramōt. 
13 : 50). 5. pukaḻtal. eṉṉāviṉāṟ paṉṉa vempirāṉ varuka (tiruvāca, 5 : 99). 6. pāṭutal. palkītamum paṉṉiṉār (tēvā. 408 : 3).
ca - ṉa(na), ṉa - ma, pa- ph, ph - f, ph - bh mutaliya tiripukaḷ iyalpē yātalāl, pēcu eṉṉum colliluḷḷa pakaramum cakaramum mēliyāriya moḻikaḷil mēṟkāṭṭiyuḷḷavāṟu palvēṟu eḻuttukkaḷākat tirintuḷḷaṉa.

pēraṉ
oruvaṉukku makaṉiṉ makaṉ pēraṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. peyaraṉ eṉpatu pēraṉ eṉa maruviṟṟu. pāṭṭaṉ peyarip pēraṉuk kiṭuvatu paṇṭi vaḻakkam. ātalāl pēraṉukku appeya rāyiṟṟu. cilar pāṭṭaṉiyum pēraṉ allatu pēraṉār eṉpar. aracakēcari, parakēcari eṉṟa paṭṭaṅkaḷ, piṟkālac cōḻarkku oṉṟuviṭṭu vaḻaṅkiyatum immuṟi paṟṟiyē.  (col. 28).
pottakam
pottakam - putaka
pullutal = poruntutal. pul - pol - poru - poruntu - poruttu - pottu - poṭṭu.
pottutal = poruttutal, cērttal, tittal, mūṭṭutal, mūṭutal.
pottu - pottakam = pottiya (cērtta) ēṭṭuk kaṟṟi, eḻutiya eṭṭut tokuti.
cuvaṭi. cērttal eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
cummi = tokuti. cum - cuv - (cuvaḷ) - (cuvaṇ) - civaṉ.
civaṇutal = poruntutal, ottal. cuvaṇ - cuvaṭu = pātam nilattiṟ poruntiya taṭam. cuvaṭu - cōṭu = iṇi, iraṭṭi, iṇiyuḷ oṉṟu, oppu, ceruppiraṭṭi. cuvaṭu - cuvaṭi = iṇi, kaṟṟi, kaṭṭu, pottakam.
kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyum cuvaṭiyāyp pōkiṟārkaḷ eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka. cuvaṭi - cōṭi = iṇi.
cuvaṭittal = porutti aḻakupaṭuttutal. cuvaṭi - cōṭi - cōṭaṇi = aḻakupaṭuttal (alaṅkarippu).  (va. va.)
poṭṭu = maṇṭiyōṭṭup poruttu. poṭṭutal = poruttutal, kaṭṭutal. poṭṭu - poṭṭaṇam.
pottakam = 1. nūṟ puttakam.
niṟinūṟ pottakam neṭumaṇi yēṟṟi  (peruṅ. uñcik. 34:26)
2. kaṇakkup puttakam.
pottakappaṭi kuḻi  (S.I.I. iii, 80).
pottakam - puttakam.
puttakamē cālat tokuttum  (nālaṭi. 318).
vaṭavar kāṭṭum mūlam put eṉpatu. ataṟku mā. vi. a. mati allatu avamati (?) eṉṟum, tātu pāṭam (DhaÍtupaÍt´ha) kaṭṭu eṉṟum, poruḷ kūṟiyuḷḷaṉa. pintiya poruḷil atu pottu eṉpataṉ tiripē.
āriyarkku mutalnūl vētam eṉṟum, atu nīṇṭa nāḷ eḻutappaṭātiruntu eḻutāk kiḷavi eṉṟum kēḷvi eṉṟum peyar peṟṟateṉṟum, aṟika.  (va. va : 211).
poruṭ pākupāṭu
poruḷkaḷic ciṉi, kuṇam, toḻil mutaliyaṉapaṟṟip pala vakiyāka vakuttuk kūṟal poruṭ pākupāṭākum. ituvē entak kaliyaṟiviṟkum ārāyccikkum toṭakkamām. oru poruḷippaṟṟic ciṟappāyārāyumuṉ, atu enta iṉattic cērntateṉṟu aṟintukoḷvatu iṉṟiyamiyātatākum.
taṟkālattil, poruḷkaḷippaṟṟiya ārāycciyil mēṉāṭ ṭār ciṟantu viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl, muṟkālattilō in niliyik kīḻ nāṭṭār, ciṟappākat tamiḻar, aṭintiruntaṉar.
paṇṭit tamiḻar, poruḷkaḷellāvaṟṟiyum mika nuṭpamāka ārāyntu avaṟṟi vakuttuk kūṟal, ōriṉappaṭuttal, vēṟiṉappaṭuttal eṉṉum mūvakiyilum vakiyīṭu ceytatu mikap pōṟṟattakkatākum.
1. vakuttuk kūṟal
(1) iruvakip poruḷ - iyaṅkutiṇi, nilittiṇi.
ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum iṭam peyarvatum peyarāmiyum paṟṟi iyaṅkutiṇi nilittiṇi eṉa iraṇṭāy vakuttuk kūṟiṉar. ivaṟṟi muṟiyē caram acaram eṉṟum caṅkamam tāvaram eṉṟum kūṟuvar vaṭanūlār. iyaṅkutiṇi uyiruḷḷatu maṭṭum. nilittiṇiyil uyiruḷḷatum illatum aṭaṅkum.
(2) mūvakipporuḷ - uyir, mey, uyirmey.
ellāp poruḷkaḷum taṉiyuyir, taṉiyuṭampu, uyiruḷḷa uṭampu eṉa mūvakippaṭum. ivaṟṟi muṟiyē uyir, mey, uyirmey eṉṟaṉar paḻantamiḻar. kaṭavuḷum pēyum uṭalip pirinta āviyum taṉiyuyir; piṇamum uyiraṟṟa aḵṟiṇi yuṭampum uyiraṟṟa poruḷum taṉimey; uṭampoṭu kūṭi vāḻum makkaḷ, vilaṅku, paṟavi mutaliyaṉa uyirmey. uyirmey eṉṉum col iṉṟu vaḻakkaṟṟu, ataṟkup patilākap pirāṇi eṉṉum vaṭacol vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. pirāṇaṉiyuṭiyatu pirāṇi, uyiriyuṭiya mey uyirmey.
uyir, mey, uyirmey eṉṉum mūvakip poruṭ peyarkaḷiyē, mūvaki yeḻuttukkaḷukkum avaṟṟōṭoppumi karutiyiṭṭaṉar ilakkaṇikaḷ eṉka.
mūvakip poruḷkaḷilum uyirum uyirmeyyum iyaṅku tiṇiyum mey nilittiṇiyumākum.
uyirmeyyic curukkampaṟṟi uyiri eṉṟu kūṟalām. piṟkālattil mata nūlār poruḷkaḷellāvaṟṟiyum kaṭavuḷ āṉmā kaṭṭu eṉa mūvakiyākap pakuttaṉar.
(3) aṟuvaki yuyir
ellā vuyirikaḷiyum, aimpulaṉum pakuttaṟivumākiya āṟaṟivu paṟṟi, ōraṟiyuyir mutal āṟaṟivuyir vari āṟākap pakuttiruntārkaḷ.
oṉṟaṟi vatuvē uṟṟaṟi vatuvē
iraṇṭaṟi vatuvē ataṉoṭu nāvē
mūṉṟaṟi vatuvē avaṟṟoṭu mūkkē
nāṉkaṟi vatuvē avaṟṟoṭu kaṇṇē
aintaṟi vatuvē avaṟṟoṭu ceviyē
āṟaṟi vatuvē avaṟṟoṭu maṉaṉē
nēritiṉ uṇarntōr neṟip paṭuttiṉarē  (572)
pullum maraṉum ōraṟi viṉavē
piṟavum uḷavē akkiḷip piṟappē  (573)
nantum muraḷum īraṟi viṉavē
piṟavum uḷavē akkiḷip piṟappē  (574)
nantu = natti, muraḷ = caṅku.
citalum eṟumpum mūvaṟi viṉavē
piṟavum uḷavē akkiḷip piṟappē  (575)
cital = kaṟiyāṉ
naṇṭum tumpiyum nāṉkaṟi viṉavē
piṟavum uḷavē akkiḷip piṟappē  (576)
māvu mākkaḷum aiyaṟi viṉavē
piṟavum uḷavē akkiḷip piṟappē  (577)
makkaḷ tāmē āṟaṟi yuyirē
piṟavum uḷavē akkiḷip piṟappē  (578)
ivi tolkāppiya nūṟpāk(cūttiram)kaḷ. tol kāppiyat tiṉ kālam ki.mu.2000 ātalālum, atu oru vaḻinūlātalālum, nēritiṉ uṇarntōr neṟippaṭuttiṉarē eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppa tālum, mēṟkūṟiya ceytikaḷ toṉṟu toṭṭu neṭuṅkāla māka vaḻaṅki aṭippaṭṭa vaḻakkukaḷik kūṟum marapiyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭiruppatālum, tamiḻariṉ āṟuyirp pākupāṭu eṇṇukku meṭṭāta toṉmit teṉṟaṟiyalām.
iṉi, oraṟivuyiriyum maṉitariyum ivviruvakiyākap pakuttatu mika viyakkattakkatākum.
pullu maraṉum oraṟi viṉavē eṉṉumpōtē ōraṟivuyir iraṇṭākap pakukkappaṭṭuviṭṭatu. itaṉāl, pullukkum marattukkum iṭiyil uḷḷa pūṇṭu, putar, ceṭi, koṭi mutaliyavum pulliṉ vakiyāyaṭaṅkum eṉpatu pulaṉākum. āṉāl, mūṅkilāṉatu uṟuppāl pullāyum uyarattāl maramāyumiruppatāl, atu evvakiyuḷ aṭaṅkum eṉṉum aiyatti nīkkuvataṟku
puṟakkā ḻaṉavē pulleṉa moḻipa  (tol. 626)
akakkā ḻaṉavē marameṉa moḻipa  (tol. 627)
eṉṟaṉar.
ivaṟṟuḷ, moḻipa eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatāl ivi muṉṉōr kūṟṟeṉṟaṟital vēṇṭum. puṟakkāḻ veḷivayiramuḷḷatu. akakkāḻ uḷvayiramuḷḷatu. ākavē, mūṅkil pulliṉ vakiyeṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
ōraṟivuyir pōṉṟē māntariyum makkaḷ mākkaḷ eṉa iru vakiyākap pirittaṉar. maṉita vaṭivukoṇṭa māttirattilēyē oruvaṉ maṉitaṉākamāṭṭāṉeṉṟum piṟavuyirkaḷiṉiṉṟu avaṉip pirittuk kāṭṭakkūṭiya aṟivum oḻukkamum aṭintapōtē avaṉ maṉitaṉāvaṉeṉṟum tamiḻ muṉṉōr kaṇṭuṅkoṇṭu miruntaṉar. ataṉāl, aṟivoḻukka muḷḷavari makkaḷeṉṟum aviyillātavari mākkaḷ (vilaṅkukaḷ) eṉṟum kūṟiṉar. iti.
māvu mākkaḷum aiyaṟi viṉavē  (tol. 1531)
makkaḷ tāmē āṟaṟi vuyirē  (tol. 1532)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷālum,
ceviyiṟ cuviyuṇarā vāyuṇarviṉ mākkaḷ
aviyiṉum vāḻiṉum eṉ  (kuṟaḷ. 420)
makkaḷē pōlvar kayavar avaraṉṉa
oppāri yāṅkaṇṭa til  (kuṟaḷ. 1071)
vilaṅkoṭu makka ḷaṉiyar ilaṅkunūl
kaṟṟārō ṭēṉi yavar  (kuṟaḷ. 410).
eṉṉum kuṟaṭpākkaḷālum aṟiyalākum. makkaḷukku nūlaṟi villā viṭṭālum oḻukkattiṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa pakuttaṟi viruntāṟ pōtumeṉpatu muṉṉōr karuttu. ākavē, makkaḷukku oḻukkam iṉṟiyamiyātatu. makkaḷ eṉṉum peyarukku makaṉ eṉpatum mākkaḷ eṉṉum peyarukku mā eṉpatum orumi yākum. makaṉ makaḷ eṉṉum orumip peyarum makkaḷ eṉṉum paṉmip peyarum, maṉitap poruḷum muṟimip poruḷum oruṅku koṇṭiruttaliṉ, avaṟṟāl mayakkamuṇṭākāmip poruṭṭu, maṉitap poruḷiyē tarutaṟku māntaṉ eṉṉum peyar piṟkālattil tōṉṟiṉatākat terikiṉṟatu. māntaṉukkup palarpāl māntar; peṇpāl māntiyākat tōṉṟukiṟatu.
māntar makkaḷ eṉṉum peyarum...
appatiṉintum avaṟṟōraṉṉa  (tol. 647)
eṉṟu māntar eṉṉum peyari uyartiṇiyākat tol kāppiyattiṟ kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.
	(4) irutiṇi - uyartiṇi, aḵṟiṇi.
tamiḻilakkaṇattil, poruḷkaḷellām uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi eṉa irutiṇiyāka vakukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. itu tamiḻariṉ ciṟanta aṟiviyum nākarikattiyum kāṭṭuvatākum. tiṇi = kulam.
pakuttaṟivuḷḷa allatu oḻukkamuḷḷa mēṉmakkaḷ uyartiṇi yākavum, avarallāta kīḻmakkaḷum uyiruḷḷaṉavum uyirillaṉa vumākiya piṟaporuḷkaḷum aḵṟiṇiyākavum kūṟappaṭṭaṉar. oruvaṉukkup pakuttaṟiviruntum oḻukkamillā viṭiṉ avaṉ uyartiṇiyākāṉ. ākiyāl, pakuttaṟi veṉpatu eppōtum oḻukkattiyum taṉṉuṭaṉ uḷappaṭuttum.
al + tiṇi = aḵṟiṇi, uyarvallāta tiṇi aḵṟiṇi yeṉappaṭṭatu.
uyartiṇi yeṉmaṉār makkaṭ cuṭṭē
aḵṟiṇi yeṉmaṉār avarala piṟavē  (tol. col. 1)
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam.
itil makkaḷ eṉṉum vakuppāriyē uyartiṇiyākak kūṟiyuḷḷatu. āṉāl, piṟkālattil 13ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiya pavaṇantiyār,
makkaḷ tēvar narakar uyartiṇi
maṟṟuyir uḷḷavum illavum aḵṟiṇi
eṉa, uyartiṇiyuṭaṉ tēvar narakariyum cērttatumaṉṟi, tolkāppiyar vilakkiya kīḻmakkaḷiyum uyartiṇiyākak kūṟiviṭṭār.
ulakil tōṉṟiya makkaḷ, immiyiṟ ceyta nalviṉi mikuti yāl maṟumiyil tēvarāyp piṟappareṉpatum, tēvarulakil vīṭupēṟṟu muyaṟciyēyilliyeṉpatum, koḷkikaḷātaliṉ tēvappiṟappu makkaṭ piṟappiṉuñ ciṟantataṉṟeṉpatum, tēvarum makkaḷāyaṭaṅkuvar eṉpatum peṟappaṭum. āṉāl, narakarō veṉiṉ muṟpiṟappil mikutiyum tīmi ceytu ataṉāl taṇṭaṉiyaṭiyum kīḻmakkaḷātaliṉ, avariyum uyartiṇi yākak kūṟiyatu tamiḻilakkaṇattiṟkē muṟṟum māṟāṉatākum.
iṉi, makkaḷ tēvar, eṉṉum naṉṉūl nūṟpāvil, narakar eṉpati nākar eṉṟu māṟṟikkoṇṭu, makkaḷ, tēvar nākar eṉpavar muṟiyē tamiḻarum, āriyarum, nākarumāvar eṉakkūṟiya tāṉtōṉṟit tamiḻp pakivarumuḷar.
(5) aṟuvakip poruḷ
tamiḻilakkaṇattil, peyariyalil, maṟṟoruvakiyil poruḷ kaḷellām poruḷ, iṭam, kālam, ciṉi, kuṇam, toḻil eṉa aṟuvakiyākap pakukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ poru ḷeṉṟatu iṭampeyarakkūṭiya kaṭpulap poruḷi. ciṉi uṟuppu.
iṉi, nūṟporuḷkaḷi, akam puṟam eṉṟiraṇṭākavum mutal, karu, uri eṉa mūṉṟākavum vakuppatu poruḷilakkaṇa marapum, aṟam poruḷ iṉpam vīṭeṉa nāṉkāka vakuppatu nītinūṉ marapum, poruḷ kuṇam karumam potu ciṟappu oṟṟumi iṉmi eṉa ēḻāka vakuppatu tarukka nūṉmarapumākum.
ivaṟṟuḷ, akam eṉpatu kātal; puṟam eṉpatu aḵtallāta piṟa poruḷkaḷ; mutal eṉpatu kālamum iṭamum; karu eṉpatu ōriṭattil tōṉṟum poruḷkaḷum kātalallāta viṉikaḷum; uri eṉpatu kātal allatu kātaṟpakuti.
6. ōriṉappaṭuttal
ōriṉappaṭuttalāvatu vevvēṟiṉattic cērnta pala poruḷkaḷi ōr oppumipaṟṟi oriṉamākak kūṟal.
yāṉikkum paṉṟikkum kaḷiṟu kēḻal eṉpaṉa potup peyarkaḷ. ivviru vilaṅkum ōriṉam eṉpatu avaṟṟiṉ vaṭivālum niṟattālum viḷaṅkum. kaḷiṟu eṉpatu yāṉiyāl paṉṟikkum kēḻal eṉpatu paṉṟiyāl yāṉikkum ēṟpaṭṭa peyar. kaḷippatu kaḷiṟu. kaḷittal mataṅkoḷḷutal. mataṅ koḷvatu āṇyāṉi. yāṉiyōṭoppumipaṟṟip paṉṟi yiṉ āṇum kaḷiṟeṉappaṭṭatu. kiḷippatu, kīḻvatu kēḻal, kiḷittal nilatti muṇṭippōṭutal. paṉṟiyōṭoppumi paṟṟi yāṉiyum kēḻal eṉappaṭṭatu.
yāṉikkum oru vaki vaṇṭiṟkum tumpi eṉappeyar. tumpi yuṭiyatu tumpi. tumpu eṉpatu tūmpu. tūmpāvatu uṭṭuḷi yuḷḷatu allatu kuḻāy. kuḻāypōṉṟa oru muṉṉūṭṭi (proboscis) yāṉikkum tumpiyeṉṉum vaṇṭukku miruttal kāṇka. yāṉikkut tutikkiyum vaṇṭukkut tēṉuṟiñcum neṭu mūkkum muṉṉūṭṭiyākum. yāṉiyiṉ tutikki tumpikki yeṉappaṭukiṟatu. itu mutalāvatu tumpiyiṉ ki eṉap poruḷpaṭum. iṉṟu poruḷ maṟintu. akkikkē ciṟappup peyarāy allatu taṉippeyarāy vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
aracu āṟṟaracu pūvaracu eṉṉum maraṅkaḷ orē peyarip peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. im mum maraṅkaḷum neñcāṅkuli vaṭivāṉa (cordate) iliyuṭiyaṉavākum. aracamaram veḷip paṭiyākap pūkkātatu. itaṉāl atu pūvillātateṉṟē karutappaṭṭatu.
pūvātu kāykkum maramuḷa
eṉṟār auviyār.
pūppatāy aracu pōṉṟa iliyuṭiyatu pūvaracu. aracu pōṉṟa iliyuṭiyatāy āṟṟōrattil vaḷarvatu āṟṟaracu.
7. vēṟiṉappaṭuttal
vēṟiṉappaṭuttalāvatu orēyiṉattic cērnta poruḷkaḷi nuṭpa vēṟupāṭupaṟṟi vēṟupaṭuttik kūṟul.
vaṇṭun tēṉu ñimiṟuñ curumpu
 mumiḻnaṟa varunti yuṟaṅku ceñcaṭiyōṉ  (55)
eṉpatu kallāṭam.
vaṇṭu tēṉ ñimiṟu curumpu vaṇṭiṉ vakikaḷ eṉṟār. ataṉ uriyāciriyar.
mutaliyum iṭaṅkarum karāmum (kuṟiñcippāṭṭu. 267) eṉa mutali mūvakiyākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu. mūṅkilil, uṭṭuḷiyuḷḷatu vēy eṉṟum uṭṭuḷiyillātatu ami eṉṟum muḷḷuḷḷatu kaḻi eṉṟum muḷḷillātatu paṇi eṉṟum piṟa piṟavāṟum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivaṟṟuḷ, vēyum amiyum ciṟu mūṅkilum, kaḻiyum paṇiyum peru mūṅkilu mākum. ippōtu vēy tuḷimūṅkileṉṟum ami kalmūṅkileṉṟum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. ilikaḷ vaṭivum kaṉamumpaṟṟi, ili tāḷ tōki ōli eṉa nālvakiyākak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
iṅṅaṉamē piṟavum. itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, tamiḻ muṉṉōr mika nuṇṇaṟivuṭiyavarāyiruntaṉar eṉpatum poruḷkaḷi naṉṟāyārāyntu avaṟṟip palavakiyiṟ pākupaṭuttiṉar eṉpa tum, oru poruṭ pala peyarkaḷil ovvoṉṟum ovvoru nuṭpa vēṟupāṭṭik kāṭṭumeṉpatum aṟiyappaṭum.  (The Putturian Vol VIII Feb. 1942)
poruṭ peyarkaḷ
pala poruṭpeyarkaḷ uvami aṭippaṭiyāka apporuṭ kaḷiṉ paṇpu kuṟittup peyareccamāka vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
ciṟumi	-	aricippal, kuruvittali, kiḷivāyakal, narikkeḷiṟu, eriyā maṇakku.
nuṇciṟumi	-	ūci miḷakāy, ūciveṭi.
kūrmi	-	ūcik kātu.
parumi	-	āṉi neruñcil, kaṭā nāratti.
iṭimi	-	(ciṟuparumi) kutiri veṭi.
neṭumi	-	oṭṭakac civiṅki.
nīṇmi	-	pāmpuk keṇṭi, nākap pūcci.
tāḻvu	-	nāykkaṭuku, nāy vēmpu.
iḻivu	-	kaḻutip poṉvaṇṭu, kaḻutik kūttu.
tīmi	-	pēykkarumpu, pēy veḷḷari.
vaṉmi	-	kalvirucu, kalvāḻi.
karumi	-	kākkip piciṉ, kākkip poṉ.
nīlmi	- 	mayil tuttam.
mañcaṇmi	-	taṅkamēṉi, poṉvaṇṭu.  (col. 15-16).
porunaṉ
pōr maṟavaṉ allatu paṭittalivaṉ.  (ti.ma. 6).
poruḷ tiri coṟkaḷ ainnili
1) uyarvu, 2) iḻipu, 3) potu, 4) variyaṟuppu, 5) virippu.
1. uyarvu (Elevation).
aracaṉē teyvamāka vaṇaṅkappaṭṭa muṟkālattil, iṟivaṉ eṉpatu aracaṉiyum kōvil (allatu kōyil) eṉpatu kaṭavuḷuṇarcci yēṟpaṭṭapiṉ avi iraṇṭum muṟiyē kaṭavuḷiyum avarirukkiyākak karutappaṭum vīṭṭiyum kuṟittaṉa.
toṇṭaṉ aṭiyāṉ eṉṟa peyarkaḷum, mutalāvatu makkaṭkut toṇṭum aṭittoḻilum ceytavarkaḷik kuṟittu, piṉṉar, kaṭavuḷ toṇṭariyum, kaṭavuḷaṭiyāriyum kuṟittaṉa. toṇṭaṉ eṉṉum peyar, iṉṟu potunala ūḻiyaṉiyum kuṟikkum.
kaḷippu eṉṉum col, mutalāvatu, kaḷḷuṇṭaliyē kuṟittu, piṉpu kaḷḷuṇṭu makiḻtaliyum, ataṉpiṉ iṉpattāl makiḻtaliyum, kuṟittatu.
mēṟkāṭṭiya aiñcoṟkaḷum, mutalāvatu tāḻnta poruḷ kaḷik kuṟittu, piṉṉar uyarnta poruḷkaḷik koṇṭami kāṇka. iṅṅaṉam coṟporuḷ uyarvatē uyarvu eṉappaṭum.
2. iḻipu (Degradation)
uyarvukku nēr etir iḻipu eṉappaṭum.
kaṭavuḷ eṉṉum peyar, maṉamoḻi meykaḷiyum ellāvaṟṟi yuṅ kaṭanta muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷiyē kuṟikka eḻunta colleṉ patu, ataṉ pakutiyālēyē viḷaṅkum, kaṭavuṭṭaṉmiyaṭinta muṉivarkaḷi illaṟap potumakkaḷ kaṭavuḷ eṉṟaḻittapiṉ, kaṭavuḷ eṉṉum coṟporuḷ iḻintuviṭṭatu. pakavaṉ eṉṉum collum iṅṅaṉamē kaṭavuḷi maṭṭum kuṟitta niliyil uyarntatāyiruntatu. piṉṉar muṉivaṉi allatu muṉivaṟ pōliyik kuṟikka āḷappaṭṭapiṉ, iḻivaṭintu viṭṭatu. itaṉālēyē, muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷi āti eṉṉum aṭikoṭuttu ātipakavaṉ eṉṟaḻittār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
tampirāṉ eṉṉum kaṭavuṭpeyar, tuṟavi mēṟkoṇṭa maṭattalivarik kuṟippatum mēṟkūṟiya muṟiyē.
antaṇar eṉpatu aḻakiya kuḷirnta aruḷiyuṭiya muṉivar peyar.
antaṇar eṉpōr aṟavōrmaṟ ṟevvuyirkkum
centaṇmi pūṇṭoḻuka lāṉ.
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvarum. iccol antaṇar pōlvāriyum, aṭiyāriyum, pūcārikaḷiyum paṭippaṭiyāyk kuṟittu, iṉṟu oru kulattāriyuṅ kuṟikkiṉṟatu.
paṇṭāram eṉpatu pala poruḷkaḷ niṟinta carakkaṟip peyar. atumuṟiyē, pala aṟivup poruḷkaḷi uḷḷattil tokuttu vitta pēraṟiñaṉiyum, appēraṟivu kāraṇamāka ulakappaṟṟit tuṟanta tuṟaviyiyum, attuṟavipōlak kōlam pūṇṭavaṉiyum, akkōlam pūṇṭa irappōṉiyum kuṟittatu.
eṅkaḷ pāṇṭippirāṉ taṉ aṭiyārkku mūlap paṇṭāram vaḻaṅkukiṟāṉ vantu muntumiṉē eṉṉum tiruvācakat toṭari lum (36, 5) paṇṭāraṅ kāmaṉ paṭiyuvaḷkaṇ kāṇmiṉ eṉṉum paripāṭal toṭariṉum (11, 123) paṇṭāram eṉṉum col pokkicattiyum pokkica cāliyiyum kuṟittami kāṇka.
kaḷañciyattip paṇṭāram eṉpatum, nūlniliyattik kalimakaḷ paṇṭāram eṉpatum ulakavaḻakku.
civamaṭat talivarip paṇṭāram eṉpatu, avar pēraṟivu vāynta tuṟavi eṉṉum kāraṇam paṟṟiyē.
paṇṭāram paratēci eṉṉum iṇimoḻi vaḻakkil paṇṭāram eṉpatu tuṟavuk kōlam pūṇṭa irappōṉik kuṟittal kāṇka.
āḷvāṉ, āḷuṭiyāṉ, āṭkoṇṭāṉ, āḷavantāṉ, āṇṭavaṉ, āṇṭāṉ, āṇṭi, āṇṭi eṉpaṉa orē peyariṉ palvēṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷākum. āṇṭi eṉṉum col, mutalāvatu kaṭavuḷiyum, piṉpu avaṉaruḷ peṟṟa tuṟaviyiyum, ataṉpiṉ pōlit tuṟaviyāṉa irappōṉayum, ataṟkum piṉ ēḻiyiyum kuṟittatu. paḻaṉiyāṇṭi, maṭattāṇṭi, kōvaṇāṇṭi, ōṭṭāṇṭi mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka.
paratēci
paratēci eṉpatu cutēci (cuvatēci) eṉpataṟku etir. atu mutalāvatu ayal nāṭṭāṉik kuṟittatu. piṉpu ayalnāṭṭu allatu ayalūr ēḻiyik kuṟittatu. iṉṟu irappōṉik kuṟikkiṉṟatu. paṇṭāram paratēci aratēci paratēci mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka.
pattiṉi
pattiṉi eṉpatu pattaṉ eṉpataṉ peṇpāl vaṭivam. pattaṉ eṉṉum āṇpāl vaṭivam pōṉṟē, pattiṉi eṉpatum mutalāvatu tēvapattiṉiyik kuṟittatu. piṉṉar, kulamakaṭkuk koḻunaṉē teyvam eṉṉum puripaṭṭakaruttu makkaḷ uḷḷattiṟ pukuntapiṉ, māntaṉāṉa kaṇavaṉmāṭ ṭaṉpuṭi yāḷik kuṟittu varukiṉṟatu.
āṇṭilum aṟivilum tāḻnta ciṟuvaṉ ciṟumiyip payalum ciṟukkiyum eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku.
payal eṉpatu piyal (piyaṉ) eṉpataṉ maṟuvaṭivam. ciṟukki eṉpatu, ciṟukkaṉ (ciṟuvaṉ) eṉpataṉ peṇpāl. ivviru peyarkaḷum, mutalāvatu iḷamiyeṉṟē kuṟittaṉavāyiṉum, iṉṟu iḷamiyōṭu iḻivum cērttuk kuṟippaṉavākum.
paṭṭappeyar
paṭṭappeyar eṉpatu, mutalāvatu, aruṇmoḻit tēvaṉ uttama cōḻap pallavaṉ tarumacēṉaṉ mutaliya paṭṭaṅkuṟitta uyarvup peyarkaḷiyē kuṟittatu. āyiṉ, iṉṟō, atu nakiyāḷarum pakiyāḷarum paḻippataṟkiṭum iḻivup peyarkaḷiyum kuṟikkiṉṟatu.
tiruvāḷaṉ
tiruvāḷaṉ eṉpatu tirumāliṉ peyarkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. atu piṉpu celvamuḷḷavaṉiyum matippuḷḷavaṉiyuṅ kuṟittatu. iṉṟō atu Mr. eṉṉum āṅkila matippaṭipōla ovvoruvaṉ peyarkku muṉṉarum cērkkappaṭukiṟatu.
ellā
ellā eṉṉum viḷi irupāṟ potuvākavē vaḻaṅki vantateṉiṉum, ataṉ tiripu niliyil eluvaṉ eṉṉum āṇpāl vaṭivamum, eluvi eṉṉum peṇpāl vaṭivamum tōṉṟiṉa.
ellā eṉṉum col, iṉṟu teṉṉāṭṭil ēla ēlā ēlē eṉṉum vaṭivukaḷil āṇpāl viḷiyākavum, ēḻā eṉṉum vaṭivil peṇpāl viḷiyākavum, vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
ēḻu - ēṭaṉ - tōḻaṉ, ēṭi - tōḻi; ēṭaṉ eṉṉum vaṭivam, ēṭa ēṭā ēṭē aṭa aṭā aṭē eṉṟu tirintu āṇpāl viḷiyākavum; ēṭi. ēṭī aṭi aṭī eṉat tirintu peṇpāl viḷiyākavum; tamiḻ nāṭeṅkum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. aṭaṭā aṭaṭa eṉpaṉa aṭukku.
aṭa eṉpatu arā rā eṉṟum, aṭē eṉpatu arē rē eṉṟum, teluṅkil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. ivaṟṟuḷ rā rē eṉaṉum citivukaḷ intiyilum urutuvilum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
iṅṅaṉam tamiḻiṉ tāymiyi yuṇarttum ellā eṉṉum col, mutalil talivaṉum taliviyum oruvariyoruvar matippāy viḷikkum viḷiyāyiruntatu, iṉṟu aṭimiyariyum kīḻmakkaḷiyum viḷikkum iḻivuc collāy vantuḷatu.
cēri
cēri eṉpatu pala vīṭukaḷ oṉṟākac cērntirukkum kuṭiyiruppu. mutaṟ kālattil mulli nilattūrkaḷ cēri eṉṉum potuppeyar peṟṟaṉa. mulli nila makkaḷ iṭiyarātaliṉ, marutanila nakarppuṟattuḷḷa iṭiyar kuṭiyiruppu puṟañcēri eṉappaṭṭatu. maruta nilattūrkaḷ pērūrum nakarumāki, tiṇi mayakkamum toḻilpaṟṟiya kulappākupāṭum uṇṭāṉa piṉ, ovvoru pērūrilum ovvoru kulattāruṭiya taṉik kuṭiyiruppum cēri eṉppaṭṭatu. iṅṅaṉam iṭiccēri paṟiccēri pārppaṉac cēri eṉṉum vaḻakku eḻuntatu.
3. potu
cila coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷkaḷ, uyarpaṭiyāmalum iḻipaṭi yāmalum potumuṟiyākat tirintuḷḷaṉa.
paḷḷi eṉpatu mutalāvatu, paṭukkiyi yuṇarttum col. paḷḷi koḷḷutal, paḷḷi koṇṭāṉ, paḷḷiyeḻucci mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka. paḷḷi eṉpatu, kīḻiṭatti allatu tāḻiṭatti yuṇarttum paḷ eṉṉum vēriṉiṉṟu piṟantatākum. paḷ + am = paḷḷam. paḷ - paṭu, paṭuttal - kīḻē kiṭattal.
paṭukkiyiyuṭiya nilamum aṟiyum vīṭum tāṉiyāku peyar eṉṉum iṭaṉākupeyarāyp paḷḷi eṉappaṭum. paṭukkum aṟiyip paḷḷi aṟi eṉpar.
irappōrum tuṟavikaḷum vaḻippōkkarum tikkilikaḷum paḷḷi, koḷvataṟkeṉṟu, aḵtāvatu paṭuppataṟkeṉṟu, ēṟpaṭṭa maṭamum paḷḷi eṉappaṭum.
kalviccālikaḷ perumpālum maṭaṅkaḷiliruntu vantami yāl, paḷḷi eṉṉum col kalviccāliyiyuṅ kuṟittatu. paḷḷikkūṭam paḷḷip piḷḷi paḷḷikku vittal mutaliyaṉa ulaka vaḻakku. eḻuttup paḷḷi eṉpatu maliyāḷa (cēra) nāṭṭu vaḻakku.
maṭaṅkaḷ perumpālum kōvilkaḷik cērntiruntami yālum, aṅṅaṉam cērāta maṭaṅkaḷuḷḷum kōvil iruntami yālum, kōvil kaṭavuḷ vīṭātalālum, paḷḷi eṉṉum col kōviliyuṅ kuṟikkum.
camaṇa puttamatak kōvilkaḷ maṭattōṭu cōntiruntami yālēyē, potuvāyp paḷḷiyeṉappaṭṭaṉa. macūtikaḷum maṭam pōṉṟavi eṉṉuṅ kāraṇattāl paḷḷivācal eṉappaṭum.
kaḻāakkāl paḷḷiyuḷ vittaṟṟāl cāṉṟōr
kuḻāattup pēti pukal
eṉṉum kuṟaṭku, (840) cāṉṟōr aviyiṉkaṇ pētiyā yiṉāṉ pukutal, tūyavalla mitittakāli iṉpantarum amaḷik kaṇṇē vittāṟpōlum.
kaḻuvākkāl eṉpatu iṭakkaraṭakku; ataṉāl avvamaḷiyum iḻikkappaṭu māṟu pōla, ivaṉāl avvaviyum iḻikkap paṭum eṉpatām eṉṟu uri kūṟiṉār parimēlaḻakar.
cāṉṟōr avi tirumuṉṉilipōlat teyvattaṉmi yuṭiyatāyk karutappaṭuvatāl, paḷḷi eṉpataṟkup paṭukki eṉṟu poruḷ koḷḷāmal kōvil eṉṟu poruḷ koḷvatē poruttamām.
paṭukkum vīṭē oruvaṉukku niliyāṉa iṭamātalāl paḷḷi eṉpatu niliyāṉa iṭattik kuṟittup piṉpu ak karutti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu iṭattiṟkē potuppeyarāyiṟṟu.
ellā eḻuttum veḷippaṭak kiḷantu
colliya paḷḷi eḻutaru vaḷiyiṉa
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟutal kāṇka; (piṟappiyal, 20).
turi eṉpatu, tura eṉṉum pakutiyaṭiyākap piṟantu, talivaṉ allatu ciṟaṟaracaṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum koṭun tamiḻc col. turattal celuttutal allatu naṭattutal. mēṉāṭukaḷiliruntu iṅku mutaṉ mutalil vantavarkaḷ talivarākavum araciyali mēṟkoṇṭavarākavumiruntatiṉāl, avarkaṭkut turi eṉpatu potuppeyarāka vaḻaṅkat talippaṭṭatu.
peṇṇi allatu peṇṭirik kuṟikkum mātar eṉṉum col, kātaṟ poruḷit taruvataṟkuk kāraṇam peṇṭiriṉ kātalikkappaṭum taṉmiyākum. mātar kātal eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (uriyiyal 30).
cēvakaṉ (va) eṉṉum peyar, ūḻiyaṉ ēvalaṉ maṟavaṉ mutaliya poruḷkaḷit tarum. ūḻiyaṉum ēvalaṉum maṟavarāyiruntāl tāṉ uṇmiyākavum ciṟappākavum ūḻiyañ ceyya muṭiyum. paṇṭik kālattil ēvalar iḷiyar eṉap paṭṭatum, avar iḷamiyōṭu kūṭiya maṟamum valiyum uṭiya rāyiruntami paṟṟiyē.
vaṇṇam
vaṇṇam eṉpatu niṟam. atu niṟampōṉṟa vakiyiyum, ceyyuḷiṉ ōci vakiyiyum, ōciyiṟ ciṟanta ceyyuḷ allatu pāṭṭiyum kuṟittatu. avvaṇṇam ivvaṇṇam eṉpaṉa vakiyiyum, neṭuñcīr vaṇṇam kuṟuñcīr vaṇṇam eṉpaṉa ōci vakiyiyum, vaṇṇaka vottāḻicik kali nāṭṭi vaṇṇam eṉpaṉa ceyyuḷ allatu pāṭṭiyum kuṟittal kāṇka. vaṇṇatti varṇam eṉpar vaṭamoḻiyār.
veḷḷi niṟamāṉatu teḷivākavum kaḷaṅkamaṟṟumiruppatāl veḷḷi eṉṉum col teḷiviyum kaḷaṅkamiṉmiyiyum kuṟikkum. eḷiya naṭiyi veḷḷi naṭi eṉpar.
cūtuvātaṟṟavaṉi veḷḷi eṉṟum veḷḷanti, eṉṟum aḻippar. veḷḷik killi kaḷḷac cinti, eṉṟār auviyākum. kuṟṟamaṟṟa pāvaki veḷḷi eṉappaṭṭatu. veḷḷiyāṉa pā veṇpā.
pakkuvam ceyyappaṭāta mūliki pacciyāyiruppatāl pacci eṉṉum col, pakkuvam ceyyappaṭāta aḵtāvatu aṭakkic collappaṭāta iṭakkarik kuṟikkum. paccip pēccu, pacci pacciyāyp pēcutal eṉpaṉa ulaka vaḻakku.
eḷiyāriṭattum valiyāriṭattum oppa niṉṟu oṟukkum taṉmipaṟṟit teyvac cāṉṟākak koḷḷappaṭuvatu neruppu. ataṉ niṟam cemmi. ataṉāl cemmi eṉṉum col nērmiyi uṇarttum.
tiṇi
tiṇi eṉpatu, tiraṭciyik kuṟikkum tiṇṇi eṉṉuñ colliṉ tokuttal tiripu (vikāram). atu muṟiyē, tiraṇṭa kuḻuviyum, peruṅkuḻuvāṉa kulattiyum, kulattiṟkuc ciṟanta oḻukkattiyum, kulampōṉṟa pākupāṭṭiyum, aivaki marapiṉar vacitta nila vakiyiyum avaṟṟiṉ ceytikaḷiyum kuṟippatāyiṟṟu.
paḻaṅkālattil, oruvar iṉṉoruvarukkuk koṭukkuṅ koṭiyi uṟutippaṭuttuvataṟku, koṭuppār koḷvār kiyil nīri vārppatu vaḻakkam. itaṟkut tārivārttal eṉṟu peyar. ittoṭar iṉṟu atik koṭuttuviṭuvatu pōliruttalāl atuvuṅ koṭic collāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa teṉka.
4. variyaṟuppu (Restriction):
pala poruṭkup potuvāṉa colli avaṟṟuḷḷa orē poruḷil vaḻaṅkutal variyaṟuppākum.
piḷḷi: eṉṉum potup peyari vēḷāḷarkku maṭṭum kulappaṭṭamāka vaḻaṅkuvatum, āṇpiḷḷiyi allatu peṇpiḷḷiyi maṭṭum kuṟikka āḷvatum kaṟi (itu Meat eṉṉum āṅkilac colli yottatu) eṉṉum potuc collāl ūṉ kaṟiyi maṭṭum uṇarttuvatum; kuṭṭi eṉṉum iḷamip peyari āṭum makkaḷumākiya īriṉattiṟku maṭṭum ciṟappāyp payaṉpaṭuttuvatum; variyaṟuppiṟ keṭuttuk kāṭṭām.
iḻittal eṉṉum viṉi, potuvāyt tīmi ceytaliyē kuṟikkum.
5. virippu (Extension) :
oru poruṭṭuc ciṟappāṉa colli, ataṟkiṉamāṉa piṟaporuṭkum vaḻaṅkuvatu virippām.
kimpeṇ: kimmi nōṉpu nōṟṟavaḷi maṭṭum kuṟikka vēṇṭiya kimpeṇ (kimpeṇṭu, kimpeṇṭāṭṭi) eṉṉum peyar, iṉṟu kaṇavaṉiyiḻanta peṇṭir ellārk kum potuppeyarāy vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
poṉ : poṉ eṉpatu palavakit tātukkaḷiyum potu vākac cuṭṭuvatum, cempu eṉpatu palavakit tātukkaḷālum ceyyappaṭum oru vakik kalattik kuṟippatum, tōṭu eṉpatu ōliyāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa kātaṇiyi maṭṭum kuṟikkātu piṟa karuvikaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭṭavaṟṟiyum kuṟippatum, puḷ eṉpatu paṟavi nimittatti maṭṭumiṉṟip palvēṟu nimittaṅkaḷiyum potuvākak kuṟippatum virippiṉ vakiyām.
irumpiṟkuk karumpoṉ eṉṟum, veḷḷikku veṇpoṉ eṉṟum peyar.
tūṇṭiṟpoṉ mīṉ viḻuṅkiyaṟṟu  (kuṟaḷ. 931)
eṉṉum kuṟaḷ toṭaril, poṉ eṉpatu irumpik kuṟittatu.. vāyc collāṟ kuṟikkappaṭum kuṟiyi vāyppuḷ eṉpar.  (col. 92-102).
poruḷ vaki
	poruḷ	-	kāṭci karuttu ākiya iraṇṭiṟkum potuvāṉa
			poruḷ.
	paṇṭam	-	kaṭpulaṉāṉatum kaṉavaṭivuḷḷatumāṉa uyiraṟṟa
			poruḷ.
	carakku	-	kāynta poruḷ.
	tāram	-	iyaṟki viḷiporuḷ.
	ākkam	-	ceyaṟki viḷiporuḷ.
	ceyporuḷ	-	kiyāṟ ceyyappaṭum poruḷ.
	uru	-	kaṉavaṭivap poruḷ.
	uruvam	-	peruṅ kaṉavaṭivap poruḷ.
	uruppaṭi	-	taṉippaṭṭatum uyiraṟṟatum oṉṟaṉpaṭiyu māṉa
			kaṉavaṭivap poruḷ (Article).
	uṭimi	-	uṭampiluḷḷa āṭiyaṇipporuḷ.
	mati	-	aḷaviṭappaṭṭu varivitikkappaṭum kaṭal vāṇikap poruḷ (e-ṭu : ēṟṟumati, iṟakkumati).
	com	-	contap poruḷ.
	cottu	-	contap poruṭ ṭokuti (Property).
	celvam	-	vili matippuḷḷa poruṭṭokuti.
			(celvōm eṉṟu colvatu celvam eṉṟum kiyi viṭṭuc celvatu celvam eṉṟum cilar poruṭ kāraṇam kūṟuvatu poruntātu. cellum matippuḷḷatē celvam. ikkācu cellumā cellātā? eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka)
	veṟukki	-	ceṟinta celvam
	kācu	-	taṉi nāṇayam (coin)
	paṇam			-	kācut tokuti (Money).  (col. 38, 39).
